(i-ḷ.) uriyacik kiḷavikku varūum - tāṉ kūṟum poruḷik kōṭaṟku oruvaṉi etirmukamākkuñ colliṟkup poruntavarum, āvayiṉ - am miyā eṉṉum iṭiccollic collumiṭattu, yāveṉ ciṉimici makaram ūrntu - yāveṉṉum uṟuppiṉ mēlatāy mutalāy niṉṟa makaravoṟṟiṉi yēṟi, kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum - kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kēṇmiyā, ceṉmiyā eṉa varum.
kēḷ eṉṟatu uriyacik kiḷavi. ataṉic cārntu taṉakku  iyalpiṉṟi niṉṟatu miyā eṉṉum iṭiccol. avviṭic col mutalum, ataṉiṟ piriyum yā ataṟku uṟuppumām eṉṟu karuti ‘yāveṉ ciṉi’ eṉṟār. miyā iṭam; makaram paṟṟukkōṭu. yāvum, ikaram ari māttiriyātaṟkuc cārpu. ivviṭiccol taṉittu niṟṟal āṟṟāmiyiṟ kēḷ eṉpataṉoṭu cārntu oru collāyē niṉṟuḻi iṭiniṉṟa ikaram orumoḻiyiṭattuk kuṟṟiyalikaramāy varutalāṉum āṇṭu uṇarttaṟkuc ciṟappiṉmiyāṉum īṇṭup pōttantu kūṟiṉār. ūrntu eṉavē kuṟṟiyalikaramum uyireṉpatu peṟṟām, uyirkkallatu ēṟutaliṉmiyiṉ.	(1)
puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram
35.	puṇariyal niliyiṭik kuṟukalum urittē
	uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum.
itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) puṇariyal niliyiṭiyum kuṟukal urittē - ak kuṟṟiyalikaram oru moḻikkaṇṇaṉṟip puṇariyaluḷ irumoḻi tammiṟ puṇartaliyaṉṟa nilimikkaṇṇuṅ kuṟukutalurittu; uṇarakkūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum - ataṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarak kūṟat toṭaṅkiṉ avi kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷḷē kūṟappaṭum e-ṟu.
‘kuṟukalum’ eṉṉumiṭattu ummiyi ‘niliyiṭiyum’ eṉa māṟik kūṭṭuka. ‘yakaram varuvaḻi’ (eḻut. 410) eṉṉuñ cūttirattu yakaram iṭam, ukarañ cārnta valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu.
e-ṭu:	nākiyātu, varakiyātu, teḷkiyātu, eḵkiyātu, kokki yātu, kuraṅkiyātu eṉa varum.
itu ‘moḻivām’ eṉṉum utti.	(2)
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram
36.	neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ
	kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.
iḵtu orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaram vallāṟu ūrntē - kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttukkaḷ āṟiṉiyum ūrntu, neṭṭeḻuttimparun toṭar moḻi īṟṟum - neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum aivakit toṭarmoḻiyiṉ iṟutiyiṉum niṟṟal vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
neṭṭeḻuttiṉatu piṉ, toṭarmoḻiyiṉatu īṟeṉpaṉa ‘nilatta takalam pōla’ oṉṟiyaṟkiḻamippaṭṭu niṉṟaṉa, am moḻiyiṟ ṟīrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram nillāmiyiṉ. vallāṟu: paṇputtoki. muṟṟummi tokku niṉṟatu. ‘atikāra muṟimi’ eṉṉum uttiyāṉ ‘niṟṟal vēṇṭu’meṉpatu varuvikka.
e-ṭu:	nāku, varaku, teḷku, eḵku, kokku, kuraṅku eṉa varum.
iv vāṟu vakiyum iṭam; valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu. eṉavē, moḻikku īṟātalum peṟṟām. perumuracu tirumuracu eṉpaṉa iru moḻikkaṇ vanta kuṟṟukaram. paracu, iṅku, ētu eṉṉum muṟṟukaraṅkaḷ, vaṭamoḻic citivu. tarukku, aṇuku eṉpaṉa viṉikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram.
kuṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vanta uyirēṟi muṭiya ari māttiriyāy niṟṟalum, muṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vanta uyirēṟi muṭiyāmiyun tammuḷ vēṟṟumi.	(3)
puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram
37.	iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟukum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
	kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.
itu kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻikkaṇ taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟuki varum eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟukum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē - avvukaram orumoḻiyuḷaṉṟip puṇarmoḻi yiṭippaṭiṉ taṉ arimāttiriyi ṉuṅ kuṟukum iṭaṉum uṇṭu; kaṭappāṭu aṟinta puṇariyalāṉa - ataṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum yāṇṭup peṟuvaveṉiṉ, ataṉ puṇarcci muṟimi aṟiyuṅ kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ e-ṟu.
‘valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi’ (eḻut. 409) eṉpataṉuḷ val leḻuttut toṭarmoḻiyum valleḻuttu varum vaḻiyum iṭam; īṟṟu valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu.
e-ṭu:	cekkukkaṇi, cukkukkoṭu eṉa varum.
ivi arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟukiyavāṟu ēṉiyavaṟṟoṭu paṭuttu uṇarka. ‘iṭaṉum’ eṉavē itu ciṟupāṉmiyāyiṟṟu.	(4)
orumoḻi āytam
38.	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar āytap puḷḷi
	uyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micittē.
itu niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē āytam orumoḻiyuḷ varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āytap puḷḷi - āytamākiya oṟṟu, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micittu - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉatāy uyiroṭu kūṭiya valleḻuttuttāṟaṉ mēliṭattatāy varum e-ṟu.
‘vallāṟaṉ micittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉum, īṇṭup ‘puḷḷi’ eṉṟata ṉāṉum, ‘āytat toṭarmoḻi’ (eḻut. 409) eṉa mēṟkūṟutalāṉum, uyireṉṟatu īṇṭup perumpāṉmiyum kuṟṟukaramēyām; ciṟupāṉmi ēṉi uyirkaḷiyuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	eḵku, kaḵcu, kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu, aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉa varum.
kaḵṟītu muḵṭītu eṉpaṉavaṟṟi ‘meypiṟitākiya puṇarcci’ eṉṟataṉāṉum, īṇṭup ‘puḷḷi’ eṉṟataṉāṉum āytamum meyyāyiṟṟu. aḵkāmi veḵkāmi aḵki veḵki aḵkam eṉap piṟavuyirkaḷoṭum vantatu. kaḵciyātu eṉat tirintatuvuṅ kuṟṟiyalukarattoṭu puṇarntatām.(5)
puṇarmoḻi āytam
39.	īṟiyal maruṅkiṉum icimi tōṉṟum.
iḵtu avvāytam puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) īṟiyal maruṅkiṉum - nilimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu puṇarntu naṭakkum iṭattum, icimi tōṉṟum - ataṉ ari māttiriyē icikkun taṉmi tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaḵṟītu, muḵṭītu eṉavarum.
ivvīṟu iyalumāṟu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluṭ (369, 399) peṟutum. īṇṭum iṭam, kuṟṟeḻuttumēl varum valleḻuttu.	(6)
āytam curuṅkātaviṭattu amiyum coṟkaḷ
40.	uruviṉum iciyiṉum arukit tōṉṟum
	moḻikkuṟip pellām eḻuttiṉ iyalā
	āytam aḵkāk kāli yāṉa.
iḵtu etiratupōṟṟal eṉṉum uttiyāṟ ceyyuḷiyali nōkki āytattiṟku eytiyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uruviṉum iciyiṉum arukit tōṉṟuṅ kuṟippu moḻiyum - niṟattiṉkaṇṇum ōciyiṉkaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmi āytan tōṉṟum poruḷ kuṟittaliyuṭiya collum, ellāmoḻiyum - aviyoḻinta ellā moḻikaḷum, eḻuttiṉiyalā - oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ pōla arimāttiriyiṉkaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmi mikkum naṭantu, āytam aḵkāk kāliyāṉa - āytañ curuṅkāta iṭattāṉa coṟkaḷām e-ṟu.
eṉavē, īṇṭu ārāycciyiṉṟēṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaliṟkūṟum, ‘oṟṟaḷa peṭuppiṉum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa’ (cey. 18) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuk ‘kaṇṇ ṭaṇṇeṉak kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum’ (malipaṭu. 352) eṉpuḻik kaṇṇṇeṉpatu cīrnili eytiṉāṟ pōlak ‘kaḵḵ ṟeṉṉuṅ kallata rattam’ eṉa niṟattiṉkaṇṇum, ‘cuḵḵ ṟeṉṉun taṇṭōṭṭup peṇṇi’ eṉa iciyiṉkaṇṇum vanta āytam oru māttiri peṟṟuc cīrnili yeytuṅkāl, āṇṭup peṟukiṉṟa oru māttirikku īṇṭu etiratu pōṟṟi viti kūṟiṉār, āytam atikārappaṭṭami kaṇṭu. ‘eḵḵ kilaṅkiya kiyarā yiṉṉuyir, veḵḵku vārkkilli vīṭu’ eṉṟu ēṉiyiṭattum vantaṉa.
oṟṟaḷapeṭukkumāṟu ivvatikārattuk kūṟiṟṟilar, aḵtu uyiraḷa peṭipōlac cīrnili yeytutalum aciniliyān taṉmiyu muṭiyatāyc ceyyuṭkē varutaliṉ. itaṉāṉē oṟṟaḷapeṭiyum oru māttiri peṟumeṉpatu peṟṟām.
‘eḻuttiṉ’ eṉṟa iṉ uvamapporuḷ. ‘iyalā’ eṉṟatu ceyyā veṉṉum viṉiyeccam.
ivvāṟaṉṟi ikkuṟippuccoṟkaḷ āytam iraṇṭiṭṭu eḻutap paṭā veṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṟ ceyyuḷiyaloṭu māṟupaṭṭu ‘māṟu koḷak kūṟal’ eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟan taṅkumeṉṟu uṇarka.	(7)
uyiraḷapeṭi āmāṟu
41.	kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟici niṟikkum
	neṭṭeḻut timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.
iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟal eṉṉum utti paṟṟic ceyyuḷiyali nōkki ‘nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ’ (eḻut. 6) eṉa muṟkūṟiya aḷapeṭiyāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟu iciniṟikkum - aḷa peṭuttuk kūṟākkāṟ kuṉṟuvatāṉa ōciyiyuṭiya avvaḷa peṭic coṟkaṇṇē niṉṟu avvōciyi niṟikkum; avi yāviyō veṉiṉ, neṭṭeḻuttimpar otta kuṟṟeḻuttē - neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṉṉākat tamakku iṉamotta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āa, īi, ūu, ēe, ōo eṉa varum.
‘kuṉṟici moḻi’ eṉṟataṟku icikuṉṟu moḻi eṉṟumām. iṉamottalāvatu piṟappum puṇarcciyum ōciyum vaṭivum ottal. īṇṭu ‘moḻi’ yeṉṟatu ‘aḷapeṭi yacinili’ (cey. 17) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyal cūttirattu eṭṭu iyaṟcīriṉpāṟ paṭukiṉṟa eṇvaki aḷapeṭic coṟkaḷiyum. avi āa, kaṭāa, āaḻi, paṭāaki, āaṅku, āavatu, pukāarttu, virāayatu eṉpaṉavām. kaṭṭaḷi koḷḷā āciriyar ivaṟṟit taṉinili mutaṉili iṭinili iṟutinili yeṉṟum aṭakkupa.
iṉi ‘moḻi’ yeṉṟataṟkut taṉinili ēḻaṉiyumē koḷḷiṉ, oḻinta iyaṟcīrppāṟpaṭum aḷapeṭi kōṭaṟku iṭamiṉmi uṇarka.	(8)
aikāra aukāraṅkaṭku aḷapeṭik kuṟṟeḻuttu āvaṉa
42.	aiau eṉṉum āyī reḻuttiṟ
	kikara ukaram iciniṟi vākum.
iḵtu otta kuṟṟeḻuttu illātaṉa aḷapeṭukkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
ituvum etiratu pōṟṟal.
(i-ḷ.) ai au eṉṉum āyīreḻuttiṟku - tamakku iṉamillāta aikāra aukārameṉṟu kūṟappaṭum avviraṇṭeḻuttiṟku, ikara ukaram iciniṟivākum - īkāra ūkāraṅkaṭku iṉamākiya ikara ukaraṅkaḷic cārttikkūṟa, avi akkuṉṟici moḻikkaṇ niṉṟu ōciyi niṟippaṉavām e-ṟu.
aii, auu eṉa niraṉiṟiyākak koḷka. ivaṟṟi muṟkūṟiya iyaṟcīreṭṭiṟkum ēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭikkoḷka.
ittuṇiyum nūṉmarapiṉ oḻipu.	(9)
uyirneṭil ōreḻuttorumoḻi ātal
43.	neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi.
iḵtu ōreḻuttorumoḻi uṇarttutal nutaliyavaṟṟuḷ neṭṭeḻuttāṉām moḻiyākkaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē - neṭṭeḻuttākiya uyirkaḷēḻum, ōreḻuttorumoḻi - ōreḻuttāṉākum oru moḻiyām e-ṟu.
muṟṟummi tokuttu īṟṟaci ēkāram virittār.
e-ṭu:	ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō eṉa varum.
aukāram uyirmeykkaṇṇallatu vārātu. ū eṉpatu taci. iḵtu uyirkkum uyir meykkum viti. kā tī pū cē ti kō ku eṉa varum. ivi tammi yuṇaraniṉṟavaḻi eḻuttām; iṭiniṉṟu poru ḷuṇarttiyavaḻic collām. neṭṭeḻuttēṟiya mey neṭṭeḻut tāyuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttēṟiya mey kuṟṟeḻuttāyum niṟṟalēyaṉṟi meykku neṭumiyum kuṟumiyum iṉmi uṇarka.	(10)
uyirkkuṟil cila ōreḻuttorumoḻi ātal
44.	kuṟṟeḻut tintum moḻiniṟi pilavē.
itu kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum moḻiyākā; avaṟṟuṭ cila moḻi yākumeṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum - kuṟṟeḻuttākiya uyirintum, moḻiniṟipu ilavē - tāmē niṟintu niṉṟu moḻiyātal ila; cila meyyoṭukūṭi  niṟintu niṉṟu moḻiyām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tu, no eṉa varum.
ivi uyirmeykkaṇṇallatu vārāmiyāṉum, uyirkkaṇ ṇum, ēṉi akara ikara ukaramum ekaramum akkoṟṟaṉ ik koṟṟaṉ ukkoṟṟaṉ epporuḷ eṉat taṉittu niṉṟu uṇartta lāṟṟātu iṭiccollāyp peyaric cārntu niṉṟu cuṭṭup poruḷum viṉāpporuḷum uṇarttutalāṉum, ‘niṟipila’ veṉṟār.
muṟṟummi īṇṭu eccappaṭṭu niṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka.	(11)
eḻuttāṉām moḻiyiṉ peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum
45.	ōreḻut torumoḻi īreḻut torumoḻi
	iraṇṭiṟan ticikkun toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa
	mūṉṟē moḻinili tōṉṟiya neṟiyē.
itu muṉṉar meymmayakkam uṭaṉilimayakkaṅ kūṟalāṉum, īṇṭu ‘neṭṭeḻut tēḻē’ (eḻut. 43) eṉpataṉāṉum, eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu kūṟiṉār. ammoḻikku iccūttirattāṟ peyarum muṟiyun tokiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟār.
(i-ḷ.) ōreḻut torumoḻi īreḻut torumoḻi iraṇ ṭiṟantu icikkun toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa - ōreḻuttāṉākum oru moḻiyum iraṇṭeḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyum iraṇṭiṉi iṟantu palavāṟṟāṉ icikkun toṭarmoḻiyuṭaṉē kūṭa, moḻi nili mūṉṟē - moḻikaḷiṉ nilimi mūṉṟēyām, tōṉṟiya neṟiyē - avi tōṉṟiya vaḻakkuneṟikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ā kā nā: ōreḻuttorumoḻi. maṇi varaku koṟṟaṉ: īreḻuttorumoḻi. kuravu aravu: mūveḻuttorumoḻi. kaṇaviri: nāleḻuttorumoḻi. akattiyaṉār: aiyeḻuttorumoḻi. tirucciṟṟampalam: āṟeḻuttorumoḻi. perumpaṟṟappuliyūr: ēḻeḻuttorumoḻi.
ōreḻuttorumoḻiyun toṭarmoḻiyum eṉṉātu īreḻut torumoḻiyum ōtiṉār, cila pala eṉṉun tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkki.
āciriyar oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum eḻutteṉṟu koṇṭaṉarātaliṉ, mā kā eṉa niṉṟa coṟkaḷ māl kāl eṉa oṟṟaṭuttuḻi oṟṟiṉāṉ vēṟuporuḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟi īreḻuttorumoḻi yeṉṟum, nāku varaku eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṟ kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ col loṭu kūṭip poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟi, īreḻuttorumoḻi mūveḻuttorumoḻi yeṉṟuṅ kōṭum eṉpārkku, āciriyar poruḷik karutātu māttiri kuṟintami paṟṟi ‘uyiri leḻuttu meṇṇap paṭāa’ (cey. 44) ‘kuṟilē neṭilē kuṟiliṇi’ (cey. 3) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ ivaṟṟi eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟā eṉṟu vilakkuvarātaliṉ, avaṟṟāṉ īṇṭu īreḻut torumoḻiyum mūveḻuttorumoḻiyum koḷḷiṉ ‘māṟukoḷak kūṟal’ eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟan taṅkumeṉṟu maṟukka. iṉi ‘neṭṭeḻut tēḻē yōreḻut torumoḻi’ (eḻut. 43) ‘kuṟṟeḻut tintu moḻiniṟi pilavē’ (eḻut. 44) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ meykkuk kuṟumi neṭumi yiṉmiyāṉ uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya neṭiluṅ kuṟilumē moḻiyā meṉṟu kūṟi, mīṭṭum ataṉiyē iccūttirattāṉ ōreḻuttoru moḻi yeṉṟeṭuttu, ataṉōṭē īreḻuttiyum iraṇṭiṟantaṉiyuṅ kūṭṭi moḻiyākak kōṭaliṉ, oṟṟiṉik kūṭṭi eḻuttākak kōṭal āciriyarkkuk karuttaṉmi yuṇarka. aṉṟiyum ‘moḻippaṭut ticip piṉum’ (eḻut. 63) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟukiṉṟa vāṟṟāṉum uṇarka.
‘akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy muppaḵ teṉpa’ (eḻut. 1) eṉa oṟṟiṉi yum eḻutteṉṟatu eḻuttiṉ taṉmi kūṟiṟṟu. īṇṭu moḻiyān taṉmi kūṟiṟṟu.	(12)
taṉimeykaḷi akaram iyakkutal
46.	meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum.
itu taṉimeykaḷi akaram iyakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) meyyiṉiyakkam - taṉimeykaḷiṉatu naṭappu, akaramoṭu civaṇum - akarattoṭu porunti naṭakkum e-ṟu.
eṉavē oruvaṉ taṉimeykaḷi nāvāṟ karuttup poruḷākiya uruvāka iyakkum iyakkamum, kiyāṟ kāṭcip poruḷākiya vaṭivāka iyakkum iyakkamum akarattoṭu porunti naṭakkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa’ (eḻut. 19) ‘kakāra ṅakāra mutaṉā vaṇṇam’ (eḻut. 89) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa nāvāṉ iyakkiyavāṟu kāṇka. eḻutik kāṭṭumiṭattuk kakaram mutaliyaṉa uyir peṟṟu niṉṟa vaṭivāka eḻutip piṉṉart taṉi meyyākkutaṟkup puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭukiṉṟavāṟṟāṉ vaṭivi iyakkumiṭattum akaraṅ kalantu niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉam meykkaṇ akaraṅ kalantu niṟkumāṟu kūṟiṉāṟ pōlap patiṉōruyirkkaṇṇum akaraṅ kalantu niṟku meṉpatu āciriyar kūṟā rāyiṉār, annilimi tamakkē pulappaṭutalāṉum piṟarkku ivvāṟu eṉṟu uṇarttutal aritākalāṉumeṉṟu uṇarka. iṟivaṉ iyaṅku tiṇikkaṇṇum nilittiṇikkaṇṇum piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum avaṟṟiṉ taṉmiyāy niṟkumāṟu ellārkkum oppa muṭintāṟpōla, akaramum uyirkkaṇṇun taṉimeykkaṇṇuṅ kalantu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmi yāyē niṟku meṉpatu cāṉṟōrkkellām oppamuṭintatu. ‘akaramutala’ eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷāṉ akaramākiya mutaliyuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷellām; atupōla iṟivaṉākiya mutaliyuṭittu ulakameṉa vaḷḷuvaṉār uvami kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum, kaṇṇaṉ ‘eḻuttukkaḷil akaramākiṉṟēṉ yāṉē’ eṉak kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum, piṟanūlkaḷāṉum uṇarka.
itaṉāṉ uṇmittaṉmiyuñ ciṟitu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. itaṉi nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟātu īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār, ‘valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa’ (eḻut. 19) eṉṟa iṭattut tāṉ iṭiniṉṟu oṟṟeṉpatoru poruḷi uṇartti moḻiyāntaṉmi eyti niṟṟaliṉ.	(13)
meyyiṉ taṉmiyil uyiratu taṉmi mayaṅkumāṟu
47.	tammiyal kiḷappiṉ ellā eḻuttum
	meynnili mayakkam māṉa milli.
itu muṉṉar meykkaṇ uyir niṉṟavāṟu kūṟi avvuyir meykkaṇ ēṟi uyirmeyyāy niṉṟa kālattu ammeyyāṟ peyar peṟumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum - paṉṉīruyirum, meynnili tam iyal mayakkaṅ kiḷappiṉ - meyyiṉ taṉmiyilē tammuṭiya taṉmi mayaṅkiṟṟākap peyar kūṟiṉ, māṉamilli - kuṟṟamilli e-ṟu.
meyyiṉ taṉmiyāvatu vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi; tammiyalāvatu uyirttaṉmi; eṉṟatu, valleḻuttu melleḻuttu iṭiyeḻuttu eṉa uyirmeykkum peyariṭṭāḷutal kūṟiṟṟu. avi ‘valleḻut tiyiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē’ (eḻut. 296) eṉavum, ‘melleḻut tiyiyiṉ iṟutiyō ṭuṟaḻum’ (eḻut. 342) eṉavum, ‘meṉmiyum iṭimiyum’ (eḻut. 130) eṉavum, piṟāṇṭum āḷpa. eḻutti vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi yeṉa vicēṭitta ciṟappāṉ ippeyar kūṟiṉār.
iḵtaṉṟip patiṉeṭṭu meyyun tammik kūṟumiṭattu meym mayakkaṅ kūṟiya vakiyāṉaṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu mayaṅkumeṉṟu kūṟi ‘avaṟṟuḷ laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar’ (eḻut. 24) eṉpataṉik kāṭṭiṉ, aḵtu irumoḻikkaṇṇateṉa maṟukka.	(14)
īroṟṟuṭaṉili āmāṟu
48.	yaraḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟum oṟṟak
	kacatapa ṅañanama īroṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ya ra ḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟum oṟṟa - yaraḻaveṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu puḷḷiyum oṟṟāy niṟpa, ka ca ta pa ṅa ña na ma īroṟṟākum - ka ca ta pa k kaḷum ṅa ña na ma kkaḷum vantu īroṟṟāy niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vēykka vāycci pāyttal vāyppu eṉavum, pīrkku nērcci vārttal ārppu eṉavum, vāḻkki tāḻcci tāḻttal tāḻppu eṉavum, kāyṅkaṉi tēyñcatu kāyntaṉam kāympuṟam eṉavum, nērṅkal nērñcili nērntili nērmpuṟam eṉavum varum.
ḻakārattiṟku vāḻntaṉam eṉa ikkālattu nakaravoṟṟu varum. ēṉiya mūṉṟum ikkālattu vaḻaṅkumeṉiṉ uṇarka.
iṉit tāḻṅkuli tāḻñciṉi tāḻntiraḷ vīḻmpaṭi eṉa akkālattu vaḻaṅkumeṉṟu ittokic coṟkaḷ kāṭṭalum oṉṟu. uriyāciriyar irumoḻikkaṭ kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiṟku avvīṟukaṭōṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷ piṉṉar vēṇṭāmi uṇarka. iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyātaliṉ īṇṭu vittār.
iṉi neṭiṟkīḻē yaṉṟip pala veḻuttun toṭarntu niṉṟataṉ piṉṉum īroṟṟu varutal koḷka. avi vēntarkku aṉṉāykku eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavām.	(15)
kuṟṟoṟṟu ākātaṉa
49.	avaṟṟuḷ
	rakāra ḻakāraṅ kuṟṟoṟ ṟākā.
iḵtu eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ  - muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ, rakāra ḻakāram - rakāramum ḻakāramum, kuṟṟoṟṟākā - kuṟiṟkīḻ oṟṟākā, neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟām, kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyām e-ṟu.
kīḻeṉṉum urupu tokuttuk kūṟiṉār. ākātaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇa miṉṟu.
e-ṭu:	kār vīḻ eṉa neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟāy vantaṉa. karu maḻu eṉak kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyāy vantaṉa.
ivaṟṟi vilakkavē, yakaram poy eṉavum nōy eṉavum iraṇṭiṭattum oṟṟāy varutal peṟṟām.
pukar pukaḻ pulavar eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavō veṉiṉ, moḻikku mutalām eḻuttiṉic cērvaṉavaṟṟiṟkē īṇṭu ārāycci yātalāṉ avi vēṇṭiyavāṟē varumeṉṟu uṇarka. aṉṟiyuṅ kuṟṟoṟṟeṉṟē cūttirañ ceytaliṟ kuṟiliṇi yoṟṟiṉik kāṭṭik kaṭāvalākāmi uṇarka.
itu variyaṟiyiṉṟi uyirmeyyoṭu taṉimey mayaṅkuvaṉa vaṟṟiṟ cilavoṟṟiṟku variyaṟi īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu.	(16)
aḷapeṭikkaṇ neṭṭeḻuttē māttiri mikutal
50.	kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ
	toṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻut tiyala.
iḵtu ‘aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum’ (eḻut. 33) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyali nōkkiya nūṉmarapiṟ cūttirattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭiyāy ataṉkaṇ nikaḻva tōr aiyam akaṟṟukiṉṟatu; eṉṉi? uyirum meyyum aḷapiṟantu icikkuṅkāṟ kuṟilō neṭilō icippateṉa māṇākkarkku nikaḻvatōr aiyam aṟuttaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum -  eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kuṟiya taṉmiyum neṭiya taṉmiyum, aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ - māttiri yeṉṉum uṟup piṉic cevi karuviyāka aḷakkiṉṟa aḷavu toḻilālē ceyyuṭkuk koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ, toṭarmoḻi yellām - ammāttiri tammuḷ toṭarntu niṟkiṉṟa coṟkaḷellām, neṭṭeḻut tiyala - neṭ ṭeḻuttu māttiri mikku naṭakkumpaṭiyākat toṭarnta collām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘varuvarkol vayaṅkiḻāay’ (kali. 11) eṉavum, ‘kaṭiyavē kaṉaṅkuḻāay’ (kali. 11) eṉavuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām neṭṭeḻuttiṉi māttiri mikuttaṟkuk kūṭiyavāṟu uṇarka. ēṉic ceyyuṭkaḷiyum ivvāṟē kāṇka.
eṉavē, māttiri aḷakkuṅkāl neṭṭeḻuttē māttiri peṟṟu mikku niṟkum eṉṟamiyāṉ, ‘etiratu pōṟṟal’ eṉṉum utti paṟṟic ceyyuḷiyali nōkkik kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār, neṭṭeḻuttu iraṇṭu māttiriyiṉ ikantu varum eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku.
‘aḷapu’ eṉṟu māttiriyik kūṟātu ‘aḷavu’ eṉac cūttirañ ceytamiyāṉ aḷavu toḻiṉmēl niṉṟatu. atu ceyyuḷiyaluḷ ‘māttiriyaḷavum’ (cey. 2) eṉpataṉāṉum uṇarka. ‘iyala’ veṉṟataṉic ceyaveṉecca mākkip paṭuttalōciyāṟ kūṟuka.
iṉit ‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, oṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. ‘kuraṅṅkuḷip polinta koycuvaṟ puravi’ (akam. 4) eṉṟa kuṟuñcīrvaṇṇattiṟku uriya kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām iṭiyiṉiṉṟa oṟṟeḻutti māttiri mikuttaṟkuk kūṭiniṉṟavāṟu uṇarka. eṉavē, kuṟaṟeḻuttukkaḷellām oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷoṭum neṭṭeḻuttukka ḷoṭuṅ kūṭi avaṟṟiyē ōci mikuttuniṟkum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē, ‘oṟṟici nīṭalum’ (eḻut. 33) eṉṟa oṟṟici nīḷuṅkāṟ kuṟṟeḻuttāy nīḷumeṉṟār.
iṉi, uriyāciriyar pukar pukaḻ eṉak kuṟiliṇikkīḻ rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ vanta toṭarmoḻikaḷellān tār tāḻ eṉṟāṟ pōla ōciyottu neṭṭeḻuttiṉ taṉmiyavām eṉṟārāleṉiṉ, pukar pukaḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟi neṭṭeḻutteṉṟē evviṭattum āḷāmi yāṉum, neṭṭeḻuttākak kūṟiya ilakkaṇattāṉ oru payaṉ koḷḷāmiyāṉum, ceyyuḷiyaluḷ ivaṟṟik kuṟiliṇi oṟṟaṭutta niriyaciyākavun tār tāḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟi neṭṭeḻuttu oṟṟaṭutta nēraciyākavuṅ kōṭalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmi uṇarka.	(17)
ṉakāra makāram īroṟṟām iṭaṉ
51.	ceyyuḷ iṟutip pōli moḻivayiṉ
	ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum.
itu ceyyuṭkaṇ īroṟṟilakkaṇamāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuṭ pōli moḻi iṟutivayiṉ - ceyyuṭkaṇ pōlumeṉṉuñ colliṉ iṟutikkaṇ, ṉakāram makāram īroṟṟākum - ṉakāramum makāramum vantu īroṟṟu uṭaṉiliyāy niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘annūli, munnūlāk koḷvāṉum pōṉm’ (kali. 103), ‘citiyuṅ kalattip payiṉāṉ tiruttit, ticiyaṟi mīkāṉum pōṉm’ (pari. 10:55) eṉa varum.
‘pōlum’ eṉṉuñ ceyyumeṉṉum muṟṟu īṟṟumici ukaram meyyoḻittuk keṭṭu lakāran tirintu niṉṟatu. iḵtu iṟutiyil muṟṟu; iṭiyiṟ peyareccamākiya uvamavurupu. īṇṭu muṟṟeṉpār ‘iṟuti moḻi’ eṉṟār.	(18)
ammakaram kuṟukumāṟu
52.	ṉakāri muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum.
iḵtu ariyaḷapu kuṟuku meṉṟa makarattiṟkuk kuṟukum iṭam itu veṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ṉakāri muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum - muṟkūṟiya ṉakārattiṉ muṉṉar vanta makaran taṉ arimāttiriyiṟ kuṟuki niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: pōṉm eṉa muṉṉark (51) kāṭṭiṉām.
‘ṉakāri’yeṉa iṭiccol īṟu tirintu niṉṟatu. iṉit ‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ṇakāravoṟṟiṉ muṉṉum makāraṅ kuṟukutal koḷka. ‘maruḷiṉu mellā maruṇm’ eṉa varum.	(19)
oṟṟum kuṟṟukaramum īriṭattum eḻuttām taṉmi tiriyāmi
53.	moḻippaṭut ticippiṉun terintuvē ṟicippiṉum
	eḻuttiyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum īṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷoṭu kūṭṭi eṇṇappaṭṭu niṟkumeṉpatūuñ ceyyuḷiyaluḷ eṇṇappaṭātu niṟku meṉpatūuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) terintu - oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷ taru nilimiyi ārāyntu, moḻippaṭuttu icippiṉum - collākac cērttuc colliṉum, vēṟu icippiṉum - ceyyuḷiyaluḷ oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷ tarumēṉum māttiri kuṟintu niṟkum nilimiyi nōkki eḻutteṇṇappaṭā veṉṟu āṇṭikku vēṟākak kūṟiṉum, eḻut tiyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar - avviraṇṭiṭattum arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkum oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum muṟkūṟiya eḻuttān taṉmi tiriyāveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
itaṉāṉ oṟṟum kuṟṟukaramum eḻuttāki niṉṟu poruḷ tantum, eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟā veṉpatu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. terintu vēṟicittal kuṟṟukarattiṟku iṉṟātaliṉ ‘ēṟpuḻik kōṭalāṉ’ oṟṟiṟkuk koḷka.
e-ṭu:	al il uṇ eṇ ol eṉavum, kal vil muḷ cel col eṉavum, āl īr ūr ēr ōr eṉavum, kāl cīr cūl tēṉ kōṉ eṉavum uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya kuṟṟeḻutti yum neṭṭeḻuttiyum oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷtantavāṟu kāṇka. kaṭam, kaṭām, uṭiyāṉ, tiruvārūr, akattiyaṉār eṉa īreḻuttiyum mūveḻutti yum nāleḻuttiyum aiyeḻuttiyum iṟutiyilum iṭiyilum oṟṟaṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷtantavāṟu kāṇka. eḵku, teḷku, kokku, kuraṅku eṉpaṉavum eḻut teṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟāta kuṟṟukaramum aṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷ tantavāṟu kāṇka.
‘uyiril eḻuttum eṇṇap paṭāa, uyirttiṟam iyakkam iṉmi yāṉa’ (cey. 44) eṉpatu eḻuttu eṇṇap peṟāmikku viti.
iṉi, iccūttirattiṟku eḻuttukkaḷic collākkik kūṟiṉum piṟitākak kūṟiṉum māttiri tiriyāteṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, akaram eṉpuḻi yum a eṉpuḻiyum, ālam eṉpuḻiyum ā eṉpuḻiyum, kakaram eṉpuḻiyum ka eṉpuḻiyuṅ, kālam eṉpuḻiyum kā eṉpuḻiyum ōci ottu niṟku meṉṟāl atu muṉṉark kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāṟ peṟappaṭutaliṟ payaṉil kūṟṟāmeṉka.	(20)
eḻuttuppōli āmāṟu
54.	akara ikaram aikāra mākum.
itu, cilaveḻuttukkaḷ kūṭic cilaveḻuttukkaḷ pōla icikkumeṉa eḻuttuppōli kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) akara ikaram aikāram ākum - akaramum ikaramuṅ kūṭṭic colla, aikāram pōla icikkum, atu koḷḷaṟka e-ṟu.
pōla eṉṟatu tokkatu.
e-ṭu:	aivaṉam, aivaṉam eṉa varum.
‘ākum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ iḵtu ilakkaṇamaṉṟāyiṟṟu.	(21)
55.	akara ukaram aukāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) akara ukaram aukāram ākum - akaramum ukaramum kūṭṭic colla aukārampōla icikkum, atu koḷḷaṟka e-ṟu.
pōla eṉṟatu tokkatu.
e-ṭu:	auvi, auvi eṉa varum.	(22)
56.	akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
	aieṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum - akarattiṉ piṉ ikaramē yaṉṟi yakaramākiya puḷḷi vantālum, aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉi meypeṟat tōṉṟum - aiyeṉappaṭṭa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ vaṭivu peṟat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e- ṭu:	aivaṉam, ayvaṉam eṉa varum.
‘meypeṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ akarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaramē yaṉṟi vakarappuḷḷiyum aukāram pōla varumeṉṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	auvi, avvi eṉa varum.	(23)
aikāra aukāraṅkaḷatu māttiric curukkam
57.	ōraḷa pākum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
	tēruṅ kāli moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu atikārattāṉ aikārattiṟkum aukārattiṟkum etiratu pōṟṟal eṉpataṉāṟ ceyyuḷiyali nōkki māttiric curukkaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) moḻivayiṉāṉa - oru colliṭattē niṉṟa aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ, tēruṅkāli - ārāyumiṭattu, ōraḷapākum iṭaṉu māruṇṭē - oru māttiriyāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu e-ṟu.
ummiyāṉ iraṇṭu māttiri peṟutalē valiyuṭit tāyiṟṟu. ‘iṭaṉum’ eṉṟatu, oru colliṉ mutaliṭikaṭi yeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattuṅ kuṟukum, atu ceyyuṭkaṇ ōci iṭarppaṭ ṭolikku miṭattuk kuṟukumeṉṟaṟku. ‘uriyiṟ kōṭalāṉ’ aikāram mutaliṭi kaṭi yeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattuṅ kuṟukum; aukāram mutaṟkaṇ kuṟukumeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	aippaci, kippi, iṭiyaṉ, kuvaḷi eṉa varum. ‘aṭippiyiyāy kōltā’ eṉavum, ‘puṉiyiḷaṅ koṅkiyiyāy varum’ eṉavum piṟavāṟum varuvaṉa ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kāṇka. auvi, keḷavi eṉa varum.
aukāram ‘kavvinīr vēli’ (pu.ve.mā. 4 - 23) eṉat toṭi nōkkik kuṟukiṉavāṟuṅ kāṇka. ‘tēruṅkāli’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ ōreḻut torumoḻiyuṅ kuṟukum.
e-ṭu:	ki, pi eṉa varum.	(24)
moḻiyiṟutikkaṇ ikara yakaraṅkaḷ ōci viravivarutal
58.	ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum.
ituvum pōli kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum - ikaramum yakaramum orumoḻi yiṉ iṟutikkaṇ ōci viravi varum, avvikaraṅ koḷḷaṟka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāy, nāi eṉa varum.	(25)
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum moḻimutalātal
59.	paṉṉī ruyirum moḻimuta lākum.
itu mēl eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu uṇartti am moḻikku mutalā meḻuttu iviyeṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉṉīruyirum - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum, moḻi mutal ākum - moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aṭi, āṭi, ili, īyam, uḷi, ūrti, eḻu, ēṇi, aivaṉam, oḷi, ōṭam, auviyam eṉa varum.	(26)
taṉimey moḻimutal ākāmi
60.	uyirmey allaṉa moḻimuta lākā.
iḵtu uyirmey moḻikku mutalām eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirmey allaṉa moḻimutal ākā - uyiroṭu kūṭiya meyyallātaṉavākiya taṉimeykaḷ moḻikku mutalākā e-ṟu.
eṉavē, uyiroṭu kūṭiya meykaḷē moḻikku mutalāvaṉa eṉṟavāṟām. īṇṭu uyirmey yeṉṟatu, vēṟṟumi nayaṅ karutiṟṟu; oṟṟumi nayaṅkarutiṉ mēlic cūttirattu uyiroṭu kūṭi āmeṉṟal payaṉiṉṟām.	(27)
taṉimeykaḷ tamakkēṟṟa uyiroṭu kūṭi mutalāmāṟu
61.	katana pameṉum āvin teḻuttum
	ellā uyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē.
itu mēṟ potuvakiyāṉ eytuvitta irunūṟṟorupattāṟu eḻuttukkaḷic ciṟappuvakiyāṉ variyaṟuttu eytuvikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) katanapama eṉum āvinteḻuttum - ka ta na pa ma eṉṟu kūṟap paṭṭa avvintu taṉimeyyum, ellā uyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyiroṭum moḻikku mutalātaṟkuc cellum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kali, kār, kiḷi, kīri, kuṭi, kūṭu, keṇṭi, kēḻal, kiti, koṇṭal, kōṭi, keḷavi eṉavum; tanti, tāy, titti, tīmi, tuṇi, tūṇi, teṟṟi, tēṉ, tiyal, toṇṭi, tōṭu, teḷavi eṉavum; nantu, nāri, nilam, nīlam, nukam, nūl, neytal, nēmi, nivaḷam, nocci, nōkkam, nuvi eṉavum; paṭi, pāl, piṭi, pīṭu, pukaḻ, pūmi, peṭi, pēṭi, pital, poṉ, pōtu, puvam eṉavum; maṭi, māli, miṭaṟu, mīḷi, mukam, mūppu, melivu, mēṉi, miyal, moḻi, mōtti, muval eṉavum varum.	(28)
62.	cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
	aaiau eṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟōraṟṟē - cakaramākiya taṉi meyyum muṟkūṟiyavi (61) pōla ellā uyiroṭuṅ kūṭi moḻikku mutalām; a ai au eṉum mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē - akara aikāra aukāra meṉṟu collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu uyirum allāta iṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cāntu, ciṟṟil, cīṟṟam, curi, cūral, cekku, cēval, col, cōṟu eṉa varum.
caṭṭi, cakaṭam, camaḻppu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ‘kaṭicol illi’ (col. 452) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. ciyam curiyam eṉpavaṟṟi vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka. (camaḻppu - nāṇam)	(29)
63.	uū oō eṉṉum nāṉkuyir
	veṉ eḻuttoṭu varuta lilli.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ū o ō eṉṉum nāṉkuyir - u ū o ō eṉṟu collappaṭṭa nāṉku uyirum, va eṉ eḻuttoṭu varutalilli - va eṉṟu collappaṭun taṉimeyyeḻuttoṭu kūṭi moḻikku mutalāy varutalilli e-ṟu.
eṉavē, oḻintaṉa moḻikku mutalām eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	vaḷi, vāḷi, viḷari, vīṭu, veḷḷi, vēṭki, viyam, vuvutal eṉa varum.	(30)
64.	ā e
	oeṉum mūvuyir ñakārat turiya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ā e o eṉṉum mūvuyir - ā e o eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu uyirum, ñakārattu uriya - ñakāra oṟṟoṭu kūṭi moḻikku mutalātaṟku uriya e-ṟu.
eṉavē, ēṉiya uriyavalla eṉpatām.
e-ṭu:	ñālam, ñeṇṭu, ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉavarum.
‘ñamali tanta maṉavuccū luṭumpu’ (perumpāṇ. 132) eṉpatu ticiccol. ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa iḻivaḻakku.	(31)
65.	āvō ṭallatu yakara mutalātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āvōṭu allatu yakaram mutalātu - ākārattoṭu kūṭi yallatu yakaravoṟṟu moḻikku mutalākātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉi, yāṭu, yāmam eṉa varum.
yavaṉar, yutti, yūpam, yuvaṉam eṉpaṉa vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka.	(32)
mutalākā meyyum uyirum, tattam peyar kūṟutaṟkaṇ mutalātal
66.	mutalā ēṉa tampeyar mutalum.
itu, moḻikku mutalākātaṉavum orōvaḻi āmeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalāvum - moḻikku mutalākā eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum, ēṉavum - moḻikku mutalā meṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭuyirum, tam peyar mutalum - tattam peyar kūṟutaṟku mutalām e-ṟu.
‘mutalāvum’ ‘ēṉavum’ eṉṟa ummikaḷ tokku niṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:	ṅakaramum ṭakaramum ṇakaramum rakaramum lakaramum ḻakaramum ḷakaramum ṟakaramum ṉakaramum eṉa moḻikku mutalākāta oṉpatum mutalāmāṟu, ṅakkaḷintār, ṭapperitu, ṇannaṉṟu eṉavarum, ivvāṟē ēṉiyavaṟṟiyum oṭṭuka.
iṉi ‘ēṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ kakkaḷintār tapperitu akkuṟitu āneṭitu eṉa moḻikku mutalāmavaṟṟiyum tam peyar kūṟutaṟku mutalāmāṟu oṭṭikkoḷka. variyaṟukkappaṭṭu moḻikku mutalākātu niṉṟa meykkum ivviti koḷka. avi cakarattu mūṉṟum, vakarattu nāṉkum, ñakarattoṉpatum, yakarattup patiṉoṉṟumām.	(33)
moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram
67.	kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ
	oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum.
iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷi moḻikku mutalāmāṟu kūṟiya muṟiyē kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ - kuṟṟiya lukaramāṉatu muṉṉili muṟippeyariṭattu, oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum - taṉimeyyāy niṉṟa nakarattumēl niṉṟa nakarattoṭu kūṭi moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nunti eṉa varum.
itaṉāṉē muṟippeyar iṭamum, nakaram paṟṟukkōṭumāyiṉa. īṇṭuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyppiṉṉar niṉṟatēṉum oṟṟumi nayattāṉ moḻikku mutaleṉṟār. itu ceyyuḷiyali nōkkik kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu.	(34)
akkuṟṟukaram muṟṟiyalukarattoṭu poruḷ vēṟupaṭāmi
68.	muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāatu
	appeyar maruṅkiṉ niliyiya lāṉa.
itu mēlataṟkoru puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) appeyar maruṅkiṉ niliyiyalāṉa - ammuṟip peyariṭattē niṟṟalilakkaṇamāṉa kuṟṟiyalukaram, muṟṟiyalukaramoṭu poruḷ vēṟupaṭāatu - itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟumvaḻi varum muṟṟukarattōṭu avviṭattuk kuṟṟukaram poruḷ vēṟupaṭumāṟu pōla īṇṭup poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu nillātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kātu, kaṭṭu, kattu, murukku, teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟāṟ pōla vēṟupaṭātu nunti yeṉṟu itaḻ kuvittu muṟṟak kūṟiyaviṭattum, itaḻ kuviyāmaṟ kuṟiyak kūṟiyaviṭattum oru poruḷē tantavāṟu kāṇka.
nuntāy eṉpatō veṉiṉ, aḵtu itaḻ kuvittē kūṟa vēṇṭu taliṟ kuṟṟukaramaṉṟu. ‘iyal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum iraṇṭaṟkum vēṟupāṭila eṉṟu koḷka.
itaṉāṉē, moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttut toṇṇūṟṟu nāṉku eṉṟu uṇarka.	(35)
moḻikku īṟām uyirkaḷ
69.	uyiru eñciya iṟuti yākum.
iḵtu uyir moḻikku īṟāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyiru eñciya iṟuti yākum - uyirkaḷuḷ aukāram oḻintaṉa vellām moḻikku īṟām e-ṟu.
eṉavē aukāravuyir īṟākātāyiṟṟu. iḵtu uyirkkum uyir meykkum potu.
ā ī ū ē ai ō eṉa ivi tāmē īṟāyiṉa. āa īi ūu ēe ōo eṉak kuṟilintum aḷapeṭikkaṇ īṟāyiṉa. kā tī pū cē ki kō eṉavum, viḷa kiḷi maḻu eṉavum varum. ekara okaram mēlē (71, 72) vilakkupa. ‘aḷapeṭi mikūu mikara viṟupeyar’ (col. 127) eṉparātaliṉ, aḷapeṭip piṉ vanta kuṟṟeḻuttuṅ koḷvar āciriyareṉṟu uṇarka. neṭṭeḻuttēḻum mutaṉmoḻiyām eṉṉun tuṇiyē muṉ ṉuṇarttutaliṉ īṇṭu avi īṟāmeṉṟum uṇarttiṉār.	(36)
aukāram kava-vōṭiyintu īṟātal
70.	kavavō ṭiyiyiṉ auvu mākum.
iḵtu īṟākāteṉṟa aukāram iṉṉuḻiyām eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) auvum - muṉ īṟākāteṉṟa aukāramum, kavavōṭu iyiyiṉ ākum - kakara vakarattōṭu iyintavaḻi īṟām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ku, vu eṉa varum.
eṉavē, oḻinta uyirellān tāmē niṉṟum, meykaḷoṭuṅ kūṭi niṉṟum īṟātal itaṉāṟ peṟṟām. itaṉāṉē, aukāram ēṉi meykkaṇ vārāteṉa vilakkutalum peṟṟām. uyir ṅakarattoṭu kūṭi moḻikku īṟā meṉpatu itaṉāṉ eytiṟṟēṉum, atu moḻikku īṟākāmi ‘tantupuṇarn turittalāṉ’ uṇarka.
itu variyaṟi kūṟiṟṟu.	(37)
ekaram meyyōṭu īṟākāmi
71.	eeṉa varumuyir meyyī ṟākātu.
iḵtu ekaran tāṉē niṉṟavaḻi yaṉṟi meyyoṭu kūṭiṉāl īṟākā teṉa vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) e eṉa varum uyir mey īṟākātu - e eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum uyir tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi yāṇṭum meykaḷoṭu iyintu īṟākātu e-ṟu.	(38)
okaram nakarameyyōṭaṉṟi īṟākāmi
72.	ovvum aṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭiyē.
itu vilakkum variyaṟiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ovvum aṟṟu - okaramum muṉ coṉṉa (71) ekaram pōlat tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi meykaḷōṭu iyintu īṟākātu, navvalaṅkaṭiyē - nakaravoṟṟōṭu allāta iṭattil e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	no koṟṟā, ‘noaliya ṉiṉṉāṭṭi nī’ eṉa varum.	(39)
ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ñakārattōṭu īṟākāmi
73.	ēō eṉumuyir ñakārat tilli.
itu cila uyir cila uṭalōṭēṟi muṭiyāveṉa vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ē ō eṉum uyir ñakārattilli - ē ō eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa iraṇṭuyirun tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum īṟātalaṉṟi ñakārattōṭu īṟāta lilli e-ṟu.
 eṉavē, ēṉiyuyirkaḷ ñakārattōṭu īṟāmeṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriña, uriñā, uriñi, uriñī, uriñu, uriñū ivi eccamum viṉippeyarum paṟṟi varum. aññi maññi ivi peyar. ēṉiyintum vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. uriñō eṉpatu ‘kaṭicol lilli’ (col. 452) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.	(40)
ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ nakaravakaraṅkaḷōṭu īṟākāmi
74.	uū kāram navavoṭu navilā.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ūkāram - ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum payilvataṉṟi, navavoṭu navilā - nakara voṟṟoṭum vakara voṟṟoṭum payilā e-ṟu.
eṉavē, ēṉi yuyirkaḷ nakara vakaraṅkaḷoṭu varumāyiṉa.
e-ṭu:	nakaram, poruna eṉa viṉippeyarākiyum, nā nī nē eṉap peyarākiyum, ni no nō eṉa viyaṅkōḷākiyum varum. poruni eṉṟuṅ kāṭṭupa. vakaram, uva vē eṉa viyaṅkōḷāyum, uvā cevvi vī vi eṉap peyarāyum varum. oruva oruvā oruvi oruvī auvi eṉṟuṅ kāṭṭupa.
īṇṭu vilakkāta ēṉi yuyirkaḷoṭu vanta nakaravakaraṅkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉṟu kōṭum ivvitiyāṉ. iṉi ‘navilā’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, vakaravukaram katavu, turavu, kuvavu, puṇarvu, nukarvu, novvu, kavvu eṉap payiṉṟu varutaluṅ koḷka.	(41)
cakara ukaram irumoḻikkē īṟātal
75.	ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē.
itu cakāra ukāram palacoṟku īṟāy vārātu irucoṟku īṟāmeṉṟu variyaṟi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāram - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya cakāram, iru moḻikkē urittu - iraṇṭu moḻikkē īṟām e-ṟu.
eṉavē, paṉmoḻikku īṟākātu eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. ‘urittē’ yeṉṉum ēkāram ‘moḻikkē’ yeṉak kūṭṭuka.
e-ṭu:	ucu; iḵtu uḷuviṉ peyar. mucu; itu kuraṅkiṉuḷ oru cāti.
pacu eṉpatōveṉiṉ, aḵtu āriyac citivu. kaccu kuccu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram. ukaram ēṟiya cakaram iru moḻikku īṟāmeṉavē ēṉi uyirkaḷ ēṟiya cakaram paṉmoḻikku īṟāmāyiṟṟu. uca, ucā, vici, cē, kacci, cō eṉap peyarāyum; tuñca, eñcā, eñci, mūci, mūcū eṉa eccamāyum varum. ‘accō’ eṉa viyappāyum varum. iṉṉum ivi vaḻakkiṉkaṭ palavāmāṟum uṇarka.	(42)
pakara ukaram orumoḻikkē īṟātal
76.	uppa kāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa
	iruvayiṉ niliyum poruṭṭā kummē.
iḵtu oru col variyaṟiyum, aḵtu ōci vēṟṟumiyāṉ iru poruḷ tarumeṉpatum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya pakaram orumoḻikkallatu paṉmoḻikku īṟākāteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; iruvayiṉiliyum poruṭṭākummē - atutāṉ taṉviṉi piṟaviṉi yeṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattum nilipeṟum poruṇmittām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tapu eṉa varum.
itu paṭuttuk kūṟa, nī cā eṉat taṉviṉiyām. eṭuttuk kūṟa, nī oṉṟaṉic cāvappaṇ eṉap piṟaviṉiyām.
uppu kappu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram. ukarattoṭu kūṭiya  pakaram oṉṟeṉavē, ēṉi uyirkaḷoṭu kūṭiya pakaram paṉmoḻikku īṟāyp pala poruḷ tarum eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. maṟantapa tappā eṉa eccamāyum, nampi cempū pē petumpi eṉap peyarāyum, pō eṉa ēvalāyum varum. ivaṟṟip piṟa coṟkaḷōṭum oṭṭuka. ēṉi īkāra pakaram iṭakkarāy vaḻaṅkum.	(43)
eñciniṉṟaṉavum, moḻikku īṟākā eṉṟaviyum,
tampeyar kūṟutaṟkaṇ īṟātal
77.	eñciya vellām eñcutal ilavē.
itu muṉṉar moḻikku īṟām eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ eñci niṉṟaṉa moḻikku īṟāmāṟum, moḻikku īṟākā eṉṟavi tampeyar kūṟuṅkāl moḻikku īṟāmāṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eñciyavum eñcuta lila - ‘kavavō ṭiyiyiṉ’ (eḻut. 70) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ patiṉōruyirum patiṉeṭṭu meykkaṇṇum vantu moḻikku īṟā meṉṟa potuvitiyiṟ piṉṉi vicēṭittuk kūṟiyavaṟṟi oḻintaṉavum moḻikku īṟātaṟku oḻivila, ellām eñcutalila - moḻikku īṟākā veṉṟa uyirmeykaḷun tampeyar kūṟumvaḻi īṟātaṟku oḻivila e-ṟu.
‘ellām’ eṉṟatu ‘colli ṉeccañ colliyāṅ kuṇarttal’ eṉṉum utti. ummi virikka. īṇṭu ‘eñciya’ veṉṟatu, muṉṉar utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiya ñakaramum nakaramum vakaramuñ cakaramum pakaramum oru moḻikkum īṟākāta ṅakaramum oḻinta paṉṉiraṇṭu meykkaṇṇum, ekaramum okaramum aukāramum oḻinta oṉpatuyirum ēṟi, moḻikku īṟāy varuvaṉavaṟṟi yeṉṟu uṇarka.
e-ṭu:	varuka pukā vīkki pukī ceku pukū īṅkē maṅki eṅkō eṉavum, kaṭṭa kaṭā maṭi maṭī maṭu paṭū paṭi eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), maṇṇa eṇṇā kaṇṇi uṇī kaṇu naṇṇū paṇṇi eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), ata putā pati vatī atu kitū tanti antō eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāram ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), kama neṉmā ammi mī cemmu koṇmū yāmi ‘kāttumvammō’ (puṟam. 281) eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāram ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), ceya kāyā koyyū aiyi aiyō eṉavum (itaṟku ikara īkāra ukara ēkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), vara tārā pari kurī karu verū nāri eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), cila palā vali valī valu kollū vallē kali eṉavum (itaṟku ōkāram ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), toḻa viḻā nāḻi vaḻī maḻu eḻū tāḻi eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), uḷa uḷḷā veḷḷi kuḷī uḷu eḷḷū kaḷi eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), kaṟṟa kaṟṟā uṟi uṟī maṟu uṟū kaṟṟi eṟṟō eṉavum (itaṟku ēkāram ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka), naṉa kaṉā vaṉṉi tuṉī muṉṉu tuṉṉū eṉṉē aṉṉi aṉṉō eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟuṭ peyarāyum viṉiyāyum varuvaṉa uṇarka. ivaṟṟuṭ kakara ṉakaraṅkaḷ vilakkāta oṉpatum vantaṉa.
āka īṟu, nūṟṟu nāṟpattu mūṉṟum, utāraṇamillāta patiṉeṭṭum āka nūṟṟaṟupattoṉṟu.
ṅakaram moḻikku īṟākā teṉpatu perumpāṉmiyātaliṟ kūṟiṟṟilar. iṉi ṅakaramum, aukāram ēṟāta mey patiṉintum, ekaramum okaramum ēkāramum ōkāramum ukaramum ūkāramum ēṟāta meykaḷun, tam peyar kūṟuṅkāl moḻikku īṟāmāṟu: ṅapperitu, cu aḻakitu, ñutītu eṉa varum. ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum ivvāṟē oṭṭuka. kekkuṟin tatu, kottītu, ñevvaḻakitu, ñottītu, nunnaṉṟu, nūpperitu, vucciṟitu, vūpperitu eṉa ellāvaṟṟiyum ivvāṟē oṭṭuka.
iṉṉum ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, kannaṉṟu ānaṉṟu eṉa moḻikku īṟāvaṉavun tampeyar kūṟumvaḻi āmeṉṟu koḷka.	(44)
puḷḷiyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ īṟāvaṉa
78.	ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷa eṉṉum
	appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti.
itu muṉṉar uyir īṟāmāṟu uṇarttip puḷḷikaḷuḷ īṟāvaṉa ivi yeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum appatiṉoṉṟē puḷḷiyiṟuti - ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa veṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa patiṉoṉṟumē puḷḷikaḷil moḻikku īṟāvaṉa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñ, maṇ, porun, tirum, poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vēḷ eṉa varum.
ṉakaram īṟṟu viyātu makarattoṭu vittatu vaḻakkup payiṟciyum mayakka iyipum nōkki.	(45)
nakaravoṟṟu irumoḻikkē īṟātal
79.	ucca kāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum.
itu mēṟ potuvakiyāṉ īṟāvaṉavaṟṟuḷ variyaṟippaṭuvatu itu veṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya cakaram irumoḻik kīṟāyavāṟu pōla (75) nakaravoṟṟum irumoḻikkallatu īṟākātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	porun, verin eṉa varum.	(46)
ñakaravoṟṟu orumoḻikkē īṟātal
80.	uppa kāramoṭu ñakāriyum aṟṟē
	apporuḷ iraṭṭā tivaṇi yāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāramoṭu ñakāriyum aṟṟē - ukārattoṭu kūṭiya pakarattoṭu ñakaramum ottu oru moḻikku īṟām; ivaṇiyāṉa apporuḷ iraṭṭātu - ivviṭattu ñakārattiṉkaṇṇāṉa apporuḷ pakarampōla iruporuṭpaṭātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñ eṉa varum.
ñakāram oru moḻikku īṟākaliṉ nakarattiṉ piṉ kūṟiṉār. ‘ivaṇi’ eṉṉum aikāram aci.	(47)
vakaramey nāṉku moḻikkaṇ īṟātal
81.	vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu - vakaramākiya eḻuttu nāṉku moḻiyiṉ īṟṟatām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	av, iv, uv, tev eṉa varum.
‘kiḷavi’ ākupeyar, eḻuttuk kiḷaviyātaṟku urittāmātaliṉ.	(48)
makarīṟṟoṭu mayaṅkā ṉakarīṟṟut toṭarmoḻi
aḵṟiṇikkaṇ oṉpatu eṉal
82.	makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta
	ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa
	pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇi mēṉa.
ituvum atu, variyaṟi kūṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇi mēṉa - kuṟṟamaṟac collap paṭṭa aḵṟiṇip peyariṭattu, makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵtu eṉpa - makara īṟṟut toṭar moḻiyoṭu mayaṅkā veṉṟu variyaṟippaṭṭa ṉakara īṟṟut toṭarmoḻi oṉpateṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
āytam vikāram.
e-ṭu:	ekiṉ, cekiṉ, viḻaṉ, payiṉ, kuyiṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, kaṭāṉ, vayāṉ eṉa varum.
ekiṉ ekiṉam eṉṟāṟpōla vēṟoru peyarāyt tirivaṉavum cantiyāṉ tirivaṉavumāya ivaṟṟuḷ, tiripuṭiyaṉa kaḷintu oṉpatum varumēṟ kaṇṭu koḷka. nilam nilaṉ, pilam pilaṉ, kalam kalaṉ, valam valaṉ, ulam ulaṉ, kulam kulaṉ, kaṭam kaṭaṉ, polam polaṉ, pulam pulaṉ, nalam nalaṉ, kuḷam kuḷaṉ, vaḷam vaḷaṉ eṉa ittoṭakkattaṉa tammuḷ mayaṅkuvaṉa. vaṭṭam, kuṭṭam, ōṭam, pāṭam ivi pōlvaṉa mayaṅkātaṉa. variyaṟi ṉakarattiṉmēṟ cellum. mayaṅkāveṉavē mayakkamum peṟṟām.	(49)
moḻimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.

3
piṟappiyal
eḻuttukkaḷatu potuppiṟavi : nilikkaḷaṉum muyaṟciyum āmāṟu
83.	unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit
	taliyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum niliip
	pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum
	aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ
	uṟuppuṟ ṟamiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
	ellā eḻuttum colluṅ kālip
	piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
	tiṟappaṭat teriyuṅ kāṭci yāṉa
eṉpatu cūttiram.
ivvōttu eḻuttukkaḷiṉatu piṟappu uṇarttutaliṟ ‘piṟappiyal’ eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu eḻuttun taṉimeyyum moḻiyiṉaṉṟi uṇarttalākāmiyiṉ, avi piṟakkum moḻiyi moḻimarapiṭi uṇarttip piṟappu uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ, nūṉmarapiṉ piṉṉar viyātu itaṉi moḻimarapiṉ piṉṉar vittār.
ic cūttiram eḻuttukkaḷiṉatu potuppiṟavi ittuṇi nilikkaḷattu niṉṟu pulappaṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum piṟappiṉ ākkañ colluṅkāli - tamiḻeḻuttu ellāvaṟṟiṟkum āciriyaṉ kūṟiya piṟappiṉatu tōṟṟaravi yāṅ kūṟumiṭattu, unti mutalāt tōṉṟi muntuvaḷi - koppūḻaṭiyākat tōṉṟi muntukiṉṟa utāṉaṉeṉṉuṅ kāṟṟu, taliyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilii - taliyiṉkaṇṇum miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum neñciṉ kaṇṇum nilipeṟṟu, pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ uṟuppuṟṟu - pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamumeṉṟa aintuṭaṉē akkāṟṟu, niṉṟa taliyum miṭaṟum neñcuṅ kūṭa eṭṭākiya muṟimiyiyuṭiya taṉmiyoṭu kūṭiya uṟuppukkaḷōṭu oṉṟuṟṟu, amiya - iṅṅaṉam amitalāṉē, vēṟu vēṟu iyala - avveḻuttukkaḷatu tōṟṟaravu vēṟuvēṟu pulappaṭa vaḻaṅkutaliyuṭiya, kāṭciyāṉa nāṭi neṟippaṭa - ataṉi aṟivāṉ ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē maṉampaṭa, tiṟappaṭat teriyum - appiṟappu vēṟupāṭukaḷellāṅ kūṟupaṭa viḷaṅkum e-ṟu.
colluṅkāli vaḷi nilipeṟṟu uṟuppukkaḷuṟṟu iṅṅaṉam amitalāṉē avi vaḻaṅkutaliyuṭiya; avaṟṟiṉ vaḻakkam avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē maṉampaṭat teriyumeṉak kūṭṭi urittuk koḷka. iṅṅaṉam kūṟavē, muyaṟciyum muyaluṅ karuttāvum uṇmi peṟṟām.	(1)
uyireḻuttiṉatu potuppiṟavi : nilikkaḷaṉ
84.	avvaḻip
	paṉṉī ruyirum tannili tiriyā
	miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icikkum.
iḵtu uyireḻuttiṟkup potup piṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉṉīruyirun tannili tiriyā - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirun tattam māttiri tiriyāvāy, avvaḻip piṟanta - avvuntiyiṭattup piṟanta, miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyiṉ icikkum - miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇ nilipeṟṟa kāṟṟāṉ olikkum e-ṟu.
eṉavē, kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramun tannili tiriyu meṉṟārāyiṟṟu. avveḻuttukkaḷik kūṟi uṇarka.	(2)
uyireḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu
85.	avaṟṟuḷ
	aā āyiraṇ ṭaṅkān tiyalum.
iḵtu avvuyirkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟappup piṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirkaḷuḷ, a ā āyiraṇṭu - akara ākāraṅkaḷākiya avviraṇṭum, aṅkāntu iyalum - aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṟ piṟakkum e-ṟu.
muyaṟci uyirkkiḻavaṉ kaṇṇatu. a ā eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(3)
86.	iī eē aiyeṉa icikkum
	appāl aintum avaṟṟō raṉṉa
	avitām
	aṇpal mutalnā viḷimpuṟal uṭiya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) i ī e ē ai eṉa icikkum appālintum - i ī e ē ai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum akkūṟṟu aintum, avaṟṟōraṉṉa - akara ākāraṅkaḷ pōla aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṉ piṟakkum; avitām aṇpal mutal nā viḷimpu uṟal uṭiya - avitām aṅṅaṉam piṟakkumāyiṉum aṇpallum aṭinā viḷimpum uṟap piṟakkum vēṟupāṭuṭiya e-ṟu.
aṇpal: viṉittoki. eṉavē, nāviḷimpu aṇukutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa palleṉṟu ataṟkoru peyarāyiṟṟu. i ī e ē ai eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(4)
87.	uū oō aueṉa icikkum	
	appāl aintum itaḻkuvin tiyalum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ū o ō au eṉa icikkum appālintum - u ū o ō au eṉṟu collappaṭum akkūṟṟu aintum, itaḻ kuvintu iyalum - itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu.
u, ū, o, ō, au eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(5)
uyirkkum meykkum ciṅkanōkkākap potuviti
88.	tattam tiripē ciṟiya eṉpa.
itu muṟkūṟiya uyirkkum mēṟkūṟum meykkum potu viti kūṟic ciṅkanōkkākak kiṭantatu.
(i-ḷ.) tattam tiripē ciṟiya eṉpa - uyirkaḷum meykaḷum ovvoru tāṉattuṭ piṟappaṉavaṟṟik kūṭṭik kūṟiṉēmāyiṉum nuṇ ṇuṇarvāṉ ārāyumiṭattut tammuṭiya tammuṭiya vēṟupāṭukaḷ ciṟiyavāka uṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
avi eṭuttal paṭuttal nalital vilaṅkal eṉṟavāṟṟāṉun talivaḷi neñcuvaḷi miṭaṟṟuvaḷi mūkkuvaḷi eṉṟavāṟṟāṉum piṟavāṟ ṟāṉum vēṟupaṭumāṟu nuṇṇuṇarvuṭiyōr kūṟi uṇarka. ai vilaṅka luṭiyatu. valliṉan talivaḷiyuṭiyatu. melliṉam mūkkuvaḷi yuṭiyatu. iṭiyiṉam miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyuṭiyatu. ēṉiyavuṅ kūṟik kaṇṭu uṇarka.	(6)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu
kakāra ṅakāram
89.	kakāra ṅakāra mutalnā aṇṇam.
itu meykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟappuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kakāra ṅakāram mutal nā aṇṇam - kakāramum ṅakāramum mutalnāvum mutalaṇṇamum uṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu.
uyirmeyyākac cūttirattuk kūṟiṉun taṉimeyyāk kūṟik kāṇka. mutali iraṇṭaṟkuṅ kūṭṭuka. ka ṅa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. 	(7)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu
cakāra ñakāram
90.	cakāra ñakāram iṭinā aṇṇam.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) cakāra ñakāram iṭinā aṇṇam - cakāramum ñakāramum iṭināvum iṭiyaṇṇamum uṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu.
iṭiyi iraṇṭaṟkuṅ kūṭṭuka. ca ña eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(8)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu
ṭakāra ṇakāram
91.	ṭakāra ṇakāram nuṉinā aṇṇam.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṭakāra ṇakāramum nuṉi nā aṇṇam - ṭakāramum ṇakāramum nuṉināvum nuṉiyaṇṇamum uṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu.
nuṉiyi iraṇṭaṟkuṅ kūṭṭuka. ṭa ṇa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(9)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu 
aiyam akaṟṟutal
92.	avvā ṟeḻuttum mūvakip piṟappiṉa.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟkōr aiyam akaṟṟiyatu.
(i-ḷ.) avvāṟeḻuttum mūvakip piṟappiṉa - akkūṟappaṭṭa āṟeḻuttum mūvakiyākiya piṟappiṉi uṭiya e-ṟu.
eṉavē, avi kakāram mutal nāviṉum, ṅakāram mutal aṇṇat tiṉum piṟakkumeṉṟu ivvāṟē niraṉiṟivakiyāṉ aṟuvakip piṟappiṉa alla eṉṟār.	(10)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu
takāra nakāram
93.	aṇṇam naṇṇiya palmutal maruṅkiṉ
	nānuṉi parantu meyyuṟa oṟṟat
	tāmiṉitu piṟakkun takāra nakāram.
itu meykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇṇam naṇṇiya palmutal maruṅkiṉ - aṇṇattic cērnta palliṉatu aṭiyākiya iṭattē, nānuṉi parantu meyyuṟa oṟṟa - nāviṉatu nuṉi parantu ceṉṟu taṉ vaṭivu mikavum uṟumpaṭi cēra, takāra nakāran tām iṉitu piṟakkum - takāra nakāram eṉṟavitām iṉitākap piṟakkum e-ṟu.
ta na eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. muṉṉar ‘uṟuppuṟṟu amiya’ eṉṟu kūṟi, īṇṭu ‘meyyuṟa oṟṟa’ eṉṟār, ciṟitu oṟṟavum varuṭavum piṟappaṉa uḷavākaliṉ.	(11)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu
ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ
94.	aṇari nuṉinā aṇṇam oṟṟa
	ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
i-ḷ: nuṉi nā aṇari aṇṇam oṟṟa  - nāviṉatu nuṉi mēṉōkkic ceṉṟu aṇṇatti aḻuttit taṭava, ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum - ṟakāra ṉakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum e-ṟu.
itu mutalāka neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyaṉṟi nāvatikāram paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟār.
ṟa ṉa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(12)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu
rakāra ḻakāram
95.	nuṉinā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa
	rakāra ḻakāramā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nuṉi nā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa - nāviṉatu nuṉi mēṉōkkic ceṉṟu aṇṇattit taṭava, rakāra ḻakāram āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum - rakāra ḻakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum e-ṟu.
ra ḻa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(13)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu
lakāra ḷakāram
96.	nāviḷimpu vīṅki yaṇpal mutaluṟa
	āvayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟavum varuṭavum
	lakāra ḷakāramā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nā vīṅki viḷimpu aṇpal mutaluṟa - nā mēṉōkkic ceṉṟu taṉ viḷimpu aṇpalliṉ aṭiyilē uṟāniṟka, āvayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟa lakāramāy - avviṭattu avvaṇṇatti annā aḻuttit tīṇṭa lakāramāyum, āvayiṉ aṇṇam varuṭa ḷakāramāy - avviṭattu avvaṇṇatti annāt taṭava ḷakāramāyum, iraṇṭum piṟakkum - ivviraṇṭeḻuttum piṟakkum e-ṟu.
la ḷa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.
ittuṇiyum nāvatikāram kūṟiṟṟu.	(14)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu
pakāra makāram
97.	itaḻiyintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) itaḻ iyintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram - mēlitaḻuṅ kīḻitaḻuṅ tammiṟ kūṭap pakāramum makāramum piṟakkum e-ṟu.
pa ma eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(15)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu vakāram
98.	pallitaḻ iyiya vakāram piṟakkum.
itu vakāram piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) pal itaḻ iyiya vakāram piṟakkum - mēṟpallik kīḻitaḻ kūṭa vakāramāṉatu piṟakkum e-ṟu.
va eṉa varum. itaṟkum itaḻ iyitaliṉ makarattiṉ piṉṉar vittār.	(16)
meyyeḻuttukkaḷatu ciṟappuppiṟavi : muyaṟci vēṟupāṭu yakāram
99.	aṇṇam cērnta miṭaṟṟeḻu vaḷiyici
	kaṇṇuṟ ṟaṭiya yakāram piṟakkum.
itu yakāram piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eḻuvaḷi miṭaṟṟuc cērnta ici - untiyileḻunta kāṟṟu miṭaṟṟiṭattuc cērnta ataṉāṟ piṟanta ōci, aṇṇaṅ kaṇṇuṟṟu aṭiya - aṇṇatti aṇintu uralāṇi iṭṭāṟpōlac ceṟiya, yakāram piṟakkum - yakāra oṟṟup piṟakkum e-ṟu.
āṇi - maram. ya eṉa varum.	(17)
melleḻuttu mūkkiṉ vaḷiyāṉ icittal
100.	melleḻut tāṟum piṟappiṉ ākkam
	colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa vāyiṉum
	mūkkiṉ vaḷiyici yāppuṟat tōṉṟum.
itu melleḻuttiṟkuc ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu āṟum piṟappiṉ ākkañ colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa āyiṉum - melleḻuttukkaḷ āṟun tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkañ colliya iṭattē nilipeṟṟaṉa vāyiṉum, mūkkiṉ vaḷiyici yāppuṟat tōṉṟum - ōci kūṟuṅkāl mūkkiṉkaṇ uḷatākiya vaḷiyiṉ iciyāṉ yāppuṟat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
avi aṅṅaṉamātal kūṟikkāṇka.
‘yāppuṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ iṭiyiṉattiṟku miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyum valliṉat tiṟkut talivaḷiyuṅ koḷka.	(18)
cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu mūṉṟum piṟakkumāṟu
101.	cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat
	tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉi mūṉṟum
	tattam cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
	otta kāṭciyiṉ tammiyal piyalum.
itu cārpiṉ tōṟṟaṅkaḷ piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cārntuvariṉ allatu - cila eḻuttukkaḷic cārntu tōṉṟiṉallatu, tamakku iyalpu ila eṉa - tamakkeṉat tōṉṟutaṟku ōriyalpilaveṉṟu, tērntu veḷippaṭutta tammiyalpu mūṉṟum - ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ tammuṭiya piṟappiyalpu mūṉṟaṉiyuṅ kūṟuṅkāl, tattam cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi iyalum - tattamakku uriya cārpākiya meykaḷatu ciṟappup piṟappiṭattē piṟatta loṭu porunti naṭakkum; ēṉi otta kāṭciyiṉ iyalum - oḻinta āytan taṉakkup poruntiṉa neñcuvaḷiyāṟ piṟakkum e-ṟu.
‘kāṭci’ eṉṟatu neñciṉi.
e-ṭu:	kēṇmiyā nāku nunti eṉavum, eḵku eṉavum varum.
āytattiṟkuc cārpiṭaṅ ‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar’ (eḻut. 38) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār.
iṉi āytan talivaḷiyāṉum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉum piṟakku meṉpārum uḷar.
‘moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, aḷapeṭiyum uyirmeyyun tammi ākkiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappiṭamē iṭamāka varumeṉṟu uṇarka.	(19)
poruḷpaṭum olikkē māttiri kūṟal
102.	ellā eḻuttum veḷippaṭak kiḷantu
	colliya paḷḷi eḻutaru vaḷiyiṟ
	piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi uṟaḻcci vārattu
	akatteḻu vaḷiyici ariltapa nāṭi
	aḷapiṟ kōṭal antaṇar maṟittē
	aḵtivaṇ nuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticikkum
	meyteri vaḷiyici aḷapunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.
iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷtam piṟappiṟkup puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttuṅ kiḷantu veḷippaṭa - āciriyaṉ ellā eḻuttukkaḷum piṟakkumāṟu muntunūṟkaṇṇē kūṟi veḷippaṭukkiyi ṉāṉē, colliya paḷḷi piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi - yāṉum avvāṟē kūṟiya eṇvaki nilattum piṟakkiṉṟa piṟappōṭē avveḻuttukkaḷik kūṟumiṭattu, eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ uṟaḻccivārattiṉ aḷapu kōṭal - yāṉ kūṟiyavāṟu aṉṟi untiyil tōṉṟuṅ kāṟṟiṉatu tiritaruṅkūṟ ṟiṉkaṇṇē māttiri kūṟikkōṭalum, akattu eḻu vaḷiyici ariltapa nāṭik kōṭal - mūlātārattil eḻukiṉṟa kāṟṟiṉōciyik kuṟṟamaṟa nāṭikkōṭalum, antaṇar maṟittē - pārppāratu vētattu uḷavē; annilimi āṇṭu uṇarka; aḵtu ivaṇ nuvalātu - aṅṅaṉam kōṭali īṇṭuk kūṟalākāmiyiṉ innūṟkaṭ kūṟātē, eḻuntu puṟattu icikkum - untiyiṟṟōṉṟip puṟattē pulappaṭṭu olikkum, meyteri vaḷiyici aḷapu nuvaṉṟiciṉē - poruḷteriyuṅ kāṟṟiṉatu tuṇiviṟkē yāṉ māttiri kūṟiṉēṉ; avaṟṟiṉatu māttiriyi uṇarka e-ṟu.
itaṉi iraṇṭu cūttiramākkiyum urippa.
itu ‘piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal’ eṉṉum uttikku iṉam, eṉṉi? untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟiṉik kūṟupaṭutti māttiri kūṟik kōṭalum, mūlatāram mutalākak kāṟṟeḻumāṟu kūṟalum, vētattiṟku uḷaveṉṟu ivvāciriyar kūṟi ammatampaṟṟi ivar koḷvatoru payaṉ iṉṟeṉṟa liṉ. untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟu muṉṉart talikkaṭ ceṉṟu, piṉṉar miṭaṟṟilē vantu, piṉṉar neñcilē niṟṟali ‘uṟaḻccivārattu’ eṉṟār. ‘akat teḻuvaḷi’ yeṉavē mūlātāra meṉpatu peṟṟām.
iṉcāriyiyi attuccāriyiyoṭum kūṭṭuka. ēkāran tēṟṟam.
‘meyteri vaḷi’yeṉavē, poruḷ teriyā muṟkum vīḷiyum muyaṟciyāṉāmeṉiṉum poruḷ teriyāmiyiṉ avi kaṭiyap paṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, collap piṟantu coṟku uṟuppām ōciyi ivar eḻutteṉṟu vēṇṭuvareṉa uṇarka.
‘niliyum vaḷiyum muyaṟciyum mūṉṟum
iyala naṭappa teḻutteṉap paṭumē’
eṉṟārākaliṉ.	(20)
piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

4
puṇariyal
īṟum mutalum meyyum uyirum
103.	mūṉṟutali yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ
	iraṇṭutali yiṭṭa mutalā kirupaḵtu
	aṟunāṉ kīṟṟoṭu neṟiniṉ ṟiyalum
	ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum
	meyyē uyireṉṟu āyī riyala
eṉpatu cūttiram.
moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikaḷip potuvakiyāṟ puṇarkkum muṟimi uṇarttiṉamiyiṟ ‘puṇariyal’ eṉpatu ivvōttiṟkup peyarāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu muṟimi yeṉṟatu mēṟceyki yōttukkaḷuṭ puṇartaṟku uriyavāka īṇṭuk kūṟiya karuvikaḷi.
iccūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikku mutalā meḻuttum moḻikku īṟāmeḻuttum ittaṉi yeṉṟalum, ellā moḻikkum īṟum mutalum meyyum uyirumallatu illiyeṉṟu variyaṟuttalum, īṟum mutalumāka eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu uḷavō eṉṟu aiyuṟṟārkku eḻuttu muppattu mūṉṟumē aṅṅaṉam īṟum mutalumāy niṟpaveṉṟu aiyamaṟuttalum nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mutal iraṇṭu taliyiṭṭa irupaḵtu īṟu aṟu nāṉkākum mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟeḻuttiṉoṭu - moḻikku mutalā meḻuttu iraṇṭi muṭiyilē yiṭṭa irupaḵtum moḻikku īṟā meḻuttu irupattu nāṉkumākiṉṟa mūṉṟi muṭiyilē yiṭṭa muppatākiya eḻuttukkaḷōṭē, neṟiniṉṟu iyalum ellā moḻikkum - vaḻakkuneṟikkaṇiṉṟu naṭakkum mūvaki moḻikkum, meyyē uyireṉṟu āyīriyala iṟutiyum mutalum - meyyum uyirumeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷē īṟum mutalum āvaṉa e-ṟu.
irupattiraṇṭu mutalāvaṉa: paṉṉīruyirum oṉpatu uyirmeyyum moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramumām. irupattunāṉku īṟāvaṉa: paṉṉīruyirum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramumām.
meyyi muṟkūṟiṉār nālvakip puṇarcciyum meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻā veṉṟaṟku.
e-ṭu:	maram eṉa meymmutalum meyyīṟum, ili eṉa uyirmutalum uyirīṟum, āl eṉa uyirmutalum meyyīṟum, viḷa eṉa meymutalum uyirīṟumām.
moḻiyākkam, iyalpum vikāramumeṉa iraṇṭām. uyir tāmē niṉṟu mutalum īṟumātal iyalpu. avi meyyoṭu kūṭi niṉṟu aṅṅaṉamātal vikāram.	(1)
moḻikku īṟām mey puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟṟal
104.	avaṟṟuḷ
	meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal.
itu muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ taṉimey mutalāvāṉ ceṉṟataṉi vilakkaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya meyyum uyirumeṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ, meyyīṟu ellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal - mey moḻikku īṟāyavi yellām puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkum e-ṟu.
eṉavē, moḻikku mutalāyiṉavi yellām puḷḷiyiḻantu uyirēṟi niṟku meṉṟārāyiṟṟu. iṉṉum īṟṟumey puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟku meṉṟataṉāṉē, uyirmutal moḻi tammēl vantāl avi uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttu niṟku meṉpatūuṅ kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu.
ivviti muṟkūṟiyataṉṟō eṉiṉ, āṇṭut taṉi mey patiṉeṭṭum puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟum, avitām uyirēṟuṅkāṟ puḷḷi yiḻantu niṟkumeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉār; īṇṭu meymutal meyyīṟeṉap poruḷurikka vēṇṭiṉamiyiṉ moḻimutaṉ meykaḷum puḷḷi peṟumō veṉṟu aiyuṟṟa aiyam akaṟṟak kūṟiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka. maram eṉa meyyīṟu puḷḷi peṟṟu niṉṟatu, aritu eṉa vantuḻi, maramaritu eṉṟu uyir ēṟi muṭintavāṟu kāṇka.	(2)
īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaramum meypōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkum eṉal
105.	kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa.
itu muṉṉarp ‘puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyirtaṉit tiyalātu’ (eḻut. 138) eṉṟu meykku eytuvikkiṉṟa karuviyi etiratu pōṟṟi, uyirkkum eytuvikkiṉṟa karuvic cūttiram.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa - īṟṟuk kuṟṟiya lukaramum puḷḷiyīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭic cēṟal, puḷḷi peṟāmiyiṉ. aṅṅaṉam uyirēṟuṅkāṟ kuṟṟukaraṅ keṭṭuppōka niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl uyirēṟiṟṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. nākaritu eṉpuḻi muṉṉark kuṟṟukara vōciyum piṉṉar uyirōciyum peṟṟu avviraṇṭuṅ kūṭi niṉṟallatu apporuḷuṇarttalākāmiyiṉ, iḵtu uyiroṭuṅ kūṭi niṟkumeṉṟār.	(3)
uyirmey īṟu uyirīṟāyē aṭaṅkum eṉal
106.	uyirmey yīṟum uyirīṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
itu ‘meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala’ (eḻut. 103) eṉṟa uyirkkaṇ nikaḻvatōr aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; uyirmey yeṉpatōr īṟu uṇṭēṉum atu puṇarkkappaṭātu, atuvum uyirāyē aṭaṅku meṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) uyir meyyīṟum - uyirmey moḻiyiṉatu īṟṟiṉkaṇ niṉṟatum, uyirīṟṟiyaṟṟē - uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpi yuṭittu e-ṟu.
ummiyāṉ iṭiniṉṟa uyirmeyyum uyir iyalpiyuṭittu eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. ummi eccavummi. īṟṟiṉum iṭiyiṉum niṉṟaṉa uyiruḷ aṭaṅkumeṉavē mutal niṉṟaṉa meyyuḷ aṭaṅku meṉṟār. itaṉāṉē mēl viḷa eṉṟāṟpōlum uyirmeykaḷellām akara īṟeṉṟu puṇarkkumāṟu uṇarka. varaku: itaṉi mēl uyirttoṭar moḻi yeṉpa. muṉṉar ‘meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu’ (eḻut. 18) eṉṟatu ōreḻuttiṟ keṉṟu uṇarka.
ittuṇiyum moḻimarapiṉ oḻipu kūṟiṟṟu.	(4)
niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum
107.	uyiriṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
	uyiriṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyumeṉṟu
	ivveṉa aṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kāli
	niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉṟu
	āyī riyala puṇarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu mēṟkūṟum puṇarccikaḷellām irumoḻip puṇarcci yallatu illi eṉpatūum, aḵtu eḻuttuvakiyāṉ nāṉkāmeṉpa tūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyiriṟu colmuṉ uyir varuvaḻiyum - uyir taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉṉar uyir mutalākiya moḻivarum iṭamum, uyiriṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum - uyir taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉṉar mey mutalākiya moḻi varum iṭamum, meyyiṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - mey taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉṉar uyir mutalākiya moḻi varum iṭamum, meyyiṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum - mey taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉṉar meymutalākiya moḻi varum iṭamum, eṉṟu puṇarnilic cuṭṭu - eṉṟu collappaṭṭa oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu kūṭum nilimiyākiya karuttiṉkaṇ, ivveṉa aṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kāli - avaṟṟi ittaṉi yeṉa variyaṟiyi ellārum aṟiya yāṅ kūṟuṅ kālattu, niṟutta collē kuṟittuvarukiḷavi yeṉṟu āyīriyala - muṉṉar niṟuttappaṭṭa collum ataṉi muṭittalik kuṟittu varuñ collum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya e-ṟu.
eṉavē, nāṉku vakiyāṉuṅ kūṭuṅkāl irumoḻikkaṇ allatu puṇarcciyiṉṟu eṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	āvuṇṭu, āvalitu, ālili, ālvīḻntatu eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.
viḷaviṉik kuṟittāṉ eṉṟavaḻic cāriyiyum urupum nili moḻiyāyē niṟkumeṉpatu nōkki ataṉi niṟutta colleṉṟum, muṭikkuñ collik kuṟittuvarukiḷavi yeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. itaṉāṉē nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyuṅ kūṟiṉār. muṉṉar ‘meyyē uyir’     (eḻut. 103) eṉṟatu orumoḻikku; iḵtu irumoḻikkeṉṟu uṇarka.	(5)
moḻipuṇar iyal : tiripu mūṉṟum iyalpu oṉṟum
108.	avaṟṟuḷ
	niṟutta colliṉ īṟā keḻuttoṭu
	kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyiyap
	peyaroṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	peyaroṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	toḻiloṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	toḻiloṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	mūṉṟē tiripiṭaṉ oṉṟē iyalpeṉa
	āṅkan nāṉkē moḻipuṇar iyalpē.
itu muṉṉar eḻuttu vakiyāṉ nāṉku puṇarcci eytiya iruvakic colluñ colvakiyāṉum nāṉkāku meṉpatūum, aṅṅaṉam puṇarvatu colluñ collum iṉṟi, eḻuttum eḻuttum eṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - nilimoḻi varumoḻi yeṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, niṟutta colliṉ īṟāku eḻuttoṭu kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻuttu iyiya - muṉṉar niṟuttappaṭṭa colliṉatu īṟākiṉṟa eḻuttōṭu ataṉi muṭikkak karuti varukiṉṟa colliṉatu mutaleḻuttup porunta, peyaroṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅkālum - peyarccolloṭu peyarc collik kūṭṭum iṭattum, peyaroṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālum - peyarccolloṭu toḻiṟcollik kūṭṭum iṭattum, toḻiloṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅkālum - toḻiṟ colloṭu peyarccollik kūṭṭum iṭattum, toḻiloṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅkālum - toḻiṟ colloṭu toḻiṟcollik kūṭṭum iṭattum, mūṉṟē tiripu iṭaṉ oṉṟē iyalpeṉa āṅku annāṉkē - tiriyum iṭam mūṉṟu iyalpu oṉṟu eṉṟu muntunūliṟ kūṟiya an nāṉku ilakkaṇamumē, moḻi puṇar iyalpu - īṇṭu moḻikaḷ tammuṭ kūṭum ilakkaṇam e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cāttaṉki, cāttaṉuṇṭāṉ, vantāṉ cāttaṉ, vantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ cāttaṉ eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka.
ivi nāṉku ilakkaṇattoṭuṅ kūṭap patiṉāṟām. iṭiyum uriyun tāmāka nillāmiyiṟ peyarviṉiyē kūṟiṉār, iṭic collum uriccollum puṇarkkuñ ceykip paṭṭuḻip puṇarppuc ciṟupāṉmi. peyarppeyarum oṭṭup peyarumeṉa iraṇṭu vakip paṭum peyar. teriniliviṉiyuṅ kuṟippuviṉiyumeṉa iraṇṭu vakippaṭum toḻil.
nilimoḻiyatu īṟṟeḻuttu muṉṉarp piṟantu keṭṭuppōka varumoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttup piṉpiṟantu keṭṭamiyāṉ muṟiyē piṟantu keṭuvaṉa oruṅku niṉṟu puṇarumāṟiṉmiyiṉ puṇarcciyeṉpatu oṉṟiṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ, accoṟkaḷik kūṟukiṉṟōruṅ kēṭkiṉ ṟōrum avvōciyi iṭiyaṟavupaṭāmi uḷḷattiṉ kaṇṇē uṇarvarātaliṉ avvōci kēṭiṉṟi uḷḷattiṉkaṇ nilipeṟṟup puṇarntaṉavēyām. ākavē, piṉṉark kaṇkūṭākap puṇarkkiṉṟa puṇarcciyum muṭintaṉavēyāmeṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉi, muyaṟkōṭu uṇṭeṉṟāl atu kuṟittu varukiḷaviyaṉmi yiṉ puṇarkkappaṭātu. atutāṉ iṉṟeṉṟāṟ puṇarkkappaṭumeṉṟu uṇarka.	(6)
mūṉṟu tiripum āmāṟu
109.	avitām
	meypiṟi tātal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉ(ṟu)
	ivveṉa moḻipa tiriyu māṟē.
itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu tiripum āmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avitān tiriyumāṟu - muṉṉart tiripeṉṟu kūṟiya avitān tirintu puṇarum neṟiyi, mey piṟitātal mikutal kuṉṟa leṉṟu ivveṉa moḻipa - mey vēṟupaṭutal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉṟu kūṟappaṭum immūṉṟu kūṟṟiyuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
immūṉṟum allātatu iyalpāmeṉṟu uṇarka. ivi vikaṟpikkap patiṉāṟu utāraṇamām. maṭkuṭam, malittali, maravēr ivi peyaroṭu peyar puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. maṇmali eṉpatu iyalpu. coṟkēṭṭāṉ, palākkuṟittāṉ, maranaṭṭāṉ ivi peyaroṭu toḻil puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. koṟṟaṉ vantāṉ: iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṉāṟ cāttaṉ, koṭāpporuḷ, ōṭunākam ivi toḻiloṭu peyar puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉ cāttāṉ: iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṟkoḷḷum, pāṭappōyiṉāṉ, cāñāṉṟāṉ ivi toḻiloṭu toḻil puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉ koṇṭāṉ, iḵtu iyalpu.
‘mūṉṟu tiripu’ eṉṉātu ‘iṭaṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ oru puṇarccikkaṇ mūṉṟum oruṅkēyum varappeṟu meṉṟu uṇarka. makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ: iḵtu aṅṅaṉam vantavāṟu makara īṟṟu ‘nāṭpeyarkkiḷavi’ (eḻut. 331) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ uṇarka. iraṇṭu varuvaṉavuṅ kāṇka.	(7)
nilivarumoḻikaḷ aṭiyaṭuttum nikaḻtal
110.	niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum
	aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilik kuriya.
itu nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttum varumoḻi aṭiyaṭuttum avvirumoḻiyum aṭiyaṭuttum puṇarumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) niṟuttacolluṅ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyum - nili moḻiyāka niṟuttiṉa collum ataṉik kuṟittuvaruñ collum, aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilikku uriya - tāmē puṇarātu orōvoru col aṭiyaṭuttu variṉum iraṇṭum aṭiyaṭuttu variṉum puṇar nilimikku uriya e-ṟu.
aṭiyāvaṉa, ummittokiyum irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput tokiyumām.
e-ṭu:	patiṉāyirattoṉṟu, āyirattorupaḵtu, patiṉāyirat tirupaḵtu eṉa varum.
ivvaṭikaḷ oru collēyām. vēṟṟumittokiyum uvamat tokiyum piḷantu muṭiyap paṇputtokiyum viṉittokiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmiyiṉ oru collēyām. aṉmoḻittokiyun taṉakku vēṟoru muṭipiṉmiyiṉ oru collēyām. ittokic coṟkaḷellām aṭiyāy varuṅkālattu orucollāy varumeṉṟu uṇarka. uṇṭacāttaṉ vantāṉ, uṇṭuvantāṉ cāttaṉ eṉpaṉavum oru collēyām.	(8)
marūuccoṟkaḷum puṇarcci peṟutal,
poruḷiyipu illavum nilivarumoḻiyātal
111.	maruviṉ tokuti mayaṅkiyal moḻiyum
	uriyavi uḷavē puṇarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu marūuc coṟkaḷum puṇarccipeṟu meṉpatūum, niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāyp poruḷiyipillaṉavum puṇarcci peṟṟāṟpōla niṟkumeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) maru moḻiyum - iruvakiyāki maruviya coṟkaḷum, iṉtokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyum - cevikkiṉitākac coṟṟiraḷiṭattu niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟu poruḷuṇarttātu pirintu piṉṉarc ceṉṟu oṭṭip poruḷuṇartta mayaṅkutal iyaṉṟa coṟkaḷum, puṇarnilic cuṭṭu uriyavi uḷa - puṇarum nilimik karuttiṉkaṇ uriyaṉa uḷa e-ṟu.
‘moḻiyum’ eṉpataṉi ‘maru’ eṉpataṉōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. ‘iṉ tokuti’ eṉṟār, pāveṉṉum uṟuppu nikaḻap poruḷoṭṭāmaṟ cāṉṟōr coṟkaḷic cērttaliṉ.
e-ṭu:	muṉṟil, mīkaṇ ivi ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya maru. ilakkaṇam allā maru ‘vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum’ (eḻut. 483) eṉpuḻik kāṭṭutum. iṉi,
‘irumpu tirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppiṟ
paralaval aṭiya irali teṟippa’	(akam. 4)
eṉpuḻi maruppiṉirali yeṉṟu oṭṭi iraṇṭāvataṉ tokiyāyp poruḷ tantu ‘puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ ṉuyirtaṉit tiyalātu’ (eḻut. 138) eṉṟu uyirēṟi muṭintu mayaṅkiniṉṟatu. āyiṉ maruppiṟpara leṉṟu mey piṟitāy oṭṭi niṉṟavā ṟeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, maruppiṉi yuṭiya paraleṉa vēṟṟumittokip poruḷ uṇarttāmiyiṉ, aḵtu acceyyuṭku iṉṉōci nikaḻtaṟkup pakarattiṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa ṉakaram ṟakaramāyt tirintu niṉṟa tuṇiyēyāyp puṇarccip payaṉiṉṟi niṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam puṇarcci yeytiṉāṟpōla, māṭṭilakkaṇattiṉ kaṇṇum moḻi māṟṟiṉkaṇṇum niṟṟal coṟku iyalpeṉṟaṟku aṉṟē āciriyar ‘iṉtokuti’ yeṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka.
‘karuṅkā lōmik kāṇpiṉ peruñciṉi’ (akam. 3) eṉpuḻi ōmicciṉi yeṉṟu oṭṭi ōmiyiṉatu ciṉi yeṉap poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu, iṉṉōci tarutaṟkuk kakaravoṟṟu mikkuk kāṇpiṉ eṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟatu.
‘teyva mālvarit tirumuṉi yaruḷāl’ (cilap. 3 : 1) eṉpuḻit teyva vari yeṉṟu oṭṭit teyvat taṉmiyuṭiya variyeṉap poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu, iṉṉōci tarutaṟku māleṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟ pōlak kuṟintu niṉṟatu. mūṉṟu tiripum vantavāṟu kāṇka.
iṉi eccattiṉkaṇṇum,
‘poṉṉōṭip pukaraṇinutal
tuṉṉaruntiṟal kamaḻkaṭāat
teyiṟupaṭiyāka eyiṟkataviṭāak
kayiṟupiṇik koṇṭa kaviḻmaṇi maruṅkiṟ
peruṅki yāṉiirum piṭarttali yiruntu
maruntil kūṟṟat taruntoḻil cāyā’	(puṟam. 3)
eṉa māṭṭāy oṭṭiniṉṟatu, kayiṟu piṇikkoṇṭa eṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu, iṉṉōci peṟutaṟku. piṟa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ ivvāṟum piṟavāṟum puṇarcciyilvaḻip puṇarcci peṟṟāṟpōla niṟpaṉa ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ituvē ottākak koḷka.	(9)
mikkup puṇarum puṇarcciyiṉ iruvaki
112.	vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyum
	vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarmoḻi niliyum
	eḻuttē cāriyi āyiru paṇpiṉ
	oḻukkal valiya puṇaruṅ kāli.
itu mūvakit tiripiṉuḷ mikkuppuṇarum puṇarcci iruvakiya eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) puṇaruṅkāli - nālvakip puṇarcciyuḷ mikka puṇarcci puṇaruṅ kālattu, vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyum - vēṟṟumip poruṇmiyiṉik kuṟitta puṇarmoḻiyiṉatu taṉmi yum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarmoḻi niliyum - vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattup puṇarum moḻiyiṉatu taṉmiyum, eḻuttē cāriyi āyiru paṇpiṉ oḻukkal valiya - eḻuttu mikutaluñ cāriyi mikutalu mākiya avviraṇṭu kuṇattiṉāṉuñ cellutalit tamakku valiyāka vuṭiya e-ṟu.
eṉavē, ēṉip puṇarccikaḷukku ittuṇi vēṟupāṭu iṉṟeṉa uṇarka.
e-ṭu:	viḷaṅkōṭu: iḵtu eḻuttup peṟṟatu. makaviṉki: itu cāriyi peṟṟatu. iṉi alvaḻikkaṇ viḷakkuṟitu: iḵtu eḻuttup peṟṟatu. paṉiyiṉkuṟi: itu cāriyi peṟṟatu. itaṟkup paṉiyum kuṟintatum eṉpatu poruḷām. iḵtu aḷavuppeyar.
‘oḻukkal valiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ikkūṟiya iraṇṭum eḻuttuñ cāriyiyum uṭaṉ peṟutaluṅ koḷka. avaṟṟukkōṭeṉpatu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iraṇṭum peṟṟatu. kalattukkuṟiyeṉpatu al vaḻikkaṇ iraṇṭum peṟṟatu. itaṟkuk kalamuṅ kuṟintatum eṉpatu poruḷām. iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ ivviraṇṭum uṭaṉpeṟutaliṉṟu. alvaḻi muṟkūṟātatu vēṟṟumi yallātatu alvaḻi yeṉa vēṇṭutaliṉ. eḻuttuppēṟu yāppuṭimiyāṉum eḻuttiṉāṟ cāriyi yātalāṉum eḻuttu muṟkūṟiṉār. vēṟṟumi mēlic cūttirattē kūṟukiṉṟār.
alvaḻiyāvaṉa, avvurupukaḷ tokkum virintum nillātu puṇarvaṉa. avi eḻuvāyvēṟṟumi āṟu payaṉiliyoṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, viḷivēṟṟumi taṉ poruḷoṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, muṟṟu peyaroṭum viṉiyoṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, peyarecca mum viṉiyeccamum peyaroṭum viṉiyoṭum puṇarnta puṇarcci yum, uvamattokiyum, ummittokiyum, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokiyum, iṭiccollum uriccollum peyaroṭum viṉiyoṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, aṉmoḻittoki poru ḷoṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, paṇputtokiyum viṉittokiyum virintu niṉṟavaḻip puṇarnta puṇarcciyumeṉa uṇarka.	(10)
vēṟṟumiyurupiṉ peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum
113.	aioṭu kuiṉ atukaṇ eṉṉum
	avvā ṟeṉpa vēṟṟumi yurupē.
itu mēl vēṟṟumi yeṉappaṭṭa avaṟṟiṉ peyarum muṟiyun tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumiyurupu - muṟkūṟiya vēṟṟumic coṟkaḷi, ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṉum avvāṟeṉpa - ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭum avvāṟu urupumeṉṟu colvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
mēṟ collatikārattu eḻuvāyiyum viḷiyiyum kūṭṭi vēṟṟumi eṭṭeṉpārāleṉiṉ, ai mutaliya vēṟṟumi yāṟun tokkum virintum perumpāṉmiyum pulappaṭṭu niṉṟu peyarp poruḷic ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyaṉavāka vēṟupāṭu ceytu puṇarcci yeytuvikku meṉṟaṟku īṇṭu ‘āṟu’ eṉṟār. āṇṭu eḻuvāyum viḷiyuñ ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tammi vēṟupaṭuttup poruḷ māttiram uṇarttiniṉṟum viḷiyāy etirmuka mākki niṉṟum iṅṅaṉañ ciṟupāṉmiyāyp pulappaṭa nillā vēṟupāṭu uṭiyavēṉum, aviyum oruvāṟṟāṉ vēṟṟumiyāyiṉa veṉṟaṟku āṇṭu ‘eṭṭu’ eṉṟāreṉa uṇarka.	(11)
oṟṟu iṭimikkup puṇarum vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ
114.	valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ(ku)
	olvaḻi oṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.
itu nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum urupiyali nōkkiya toru karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku valleḻuttu aṭiyāy niṉṟa nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum, olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭi mikutal vēṇṭum - poruntiyavaḻi valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyi ṉum iṭikkaṇ mikkup puṇartali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
variyātu oṟṟeṉavē, valloṟṟum melloṟṟum peṟṟām.
e-ṭu:	maṇikku maṇikkaṇ, tīkku tīkkaṇ, maṉikku maṉikkaṇ eṉavum, vēykku vēykkaṇ, ūrkku ūrkkaṇ, pūḻkku pūḻkkaṇ eṉavum, uyirīṟu mūṉṟiṉum puḷḷiyīṟu mūṉṟiṉum perumpāṉmi valloṟṟu mikku varum. taṅkaṇ naṅkaṇ nuṅkaṇ eṅkaṇ eṉa melloṟṟu mikkatu. ivaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻi makarak kēṭu urupiyaliṟ kūṟupa. āṅkaṇ īṅkaṇ ūṅkaṇ eṉpaṉa cuṭṭeḻuttu nīṇṭu niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟukkēṭu kūṟutaṟku oṟṟiṉṟu.
iṉi, nāṉkaṉurupiṟku melloṟṟu mikāteṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉi, ‘olvaḻi’ eṉpataṉāṉ ēḻāmurupiṉkaṇ nampikaṇ eṉa ikara īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, naṅkikaṇ eṉa aikāra īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, tāykaṇ eṉa yakara īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, aracarkaṇ eṉa rakara īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, oṟṟu mikāmi koḷka.
iṉi, meypiṟitātali muṉṉē kūṟātu mikutali muṟkūṟiya ataṉāṉē, poṟku poṟkaṇ, vēṟku vēṟkaṇ, vāṭku vāṭkaṇ eṉat tirintu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. itaṉāṉē, avaṉkaṇ avaḷkaṇ eṉa uyartiṇip peyarkkaṇ ēḻaṉurupu iyalpāy varutaluṅ koḷka. ivaṟṟiṟkuk kuṉṟiya puṇarcci varumēṉuṅ koḷka.
koṟṟikku koṟṟikaṇ, kōtikku kōtikaṇ eṉa viravuppeyarkkum itaṉāṉē koḷka.	(12)
āṟaṉurupiṉ akaram keṭumiṭaṉ
115.	āṟaṉ urupiṉ akarak kiḷavi
	īṟā kakaramuṉik keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu āṟāvataṟkut tokimarapi nōkkiyatoru karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉurupiṉ akarakkiḷavi - atuveṉṉum āṟaṉuru piṉkaṇ niṉṟa akaramākiya eḻuttu, īṟāku akaramuṉik keṭutal vēṇṭum - neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻikaṭku ‘īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum’ (eḻut. 161) eṉa vitittataṉāṉ uḷatākiya akarattiṉ muṉṉart tāṉ keṭutali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tamatu, namatu, ematu, numatu, taṉatu, eṉatu, niṉatu eṉavarum.
itu nilimoḻikku ōr akaram peṟumeṉa vitiyātu urupu akaram ēṟi muṭiyumeṉa vitittāl varuṅ kuṟṟam uṇṭō veṉiṉ, ‘niṉava kūṟuva leṉava kēṇmati’ (puṟam. 35) eṉṟāṟpōla āṟāvataṟku uriya akara urupiṉ muṉṉarum ōr akara eḻuttuppēṟu nili moḻikkaṇ varutaluḷatākak karutiṉārātaliṉ, āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉkaṉurupiṟkum potuvāka nilimoḻikkaṇ akarappēṟu vitittu, atuveṉṉum, orumiyurupu vantāl āṇṭup peṟṟu niṉṟa akarattiṉ muṉṉar atu veṉpataṉkaṇ akaraṅ keṭuka veṉṟu īṇṭuk kūṟiṉā rātaliṉ ataṟkuk kuṟṟam uṇṭeṉṟu uṇarka.	(13)
peyariṉpiṉ vēṟṟumi niṟṟal
116.	vēṟṟumi vaḻiya peyarpuṇar niliyē.
itu vēṟṟumi peyarkkaṇ niṟkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi peyarvaḻiya - vēṟṟumikaḷ peyariṉ piṉṉiṭattaṉavām, puṇarnili - avaṟṟoṭu puṇarum nilimikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cāttaṉi, cāttaṉoṭu, cāttaṟku, cāttaṉiṉ, cāttaṉatu, cāttaṉkaṇ eṉa varum.
maṟṟu itu ‘kūṟiyamuṟiyiṉ’ (col. 69) eṉṉum vēṟṟumi yōttiṟ cūttirattāṟ peṟutumeṉiṉ, peyaroṭu peyarip puṇarttal mutaliya nālvakip puṇarcciyiṉiyum vēṟṟumi alvaḻi eṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkutaliṉ, toḻiṟpiṉṉum urupu varumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakkutaṟku īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāreṉka. āyiṉ ivvilakkutal viṉiyiyal mutaṟ cūttirattāṟ peṟutumeṉiṉ, atu mutaṉiliyik kūṟiṟṟeṉpatu āṇṭu uṇarka.	(14)
peyarniliccuṭṭu iraṇṭu eṉal
117.	uyartiṇip peyarē aḵṟiṇip peyareṉ(ṟu)
	āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa peyarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu muṟkūṟiya peyarkaṭkup peyarum muṟiyun tokiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu nilippeyar - poruḷi oruvar karututaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa nilimiyiyuṭiya peyarkaḷi, uyartiṇip peyarē aḵṟiṇip peyareṉṟu āyiraṇṭeṉpa - uyartiṇip poruḷi oruvaṉ karututaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa peyarum, aḵṟiṇip poruḷi oruvaṉ karututaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa peyarum eṉṉum avviraṇṭeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
peyariyaluḷ, avaṉ ivaṉ uvaṉ eṉpaṉa mutalāka uyartiṇip peyarum, atu itu utu eṉpaṉa mutalāka aḵṟiṇip peyarum āmāṟu avaṟṟiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār, īṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutal māttiriyē kūṟiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉik koṟṟaṉ koṟṟi eṉṟāṟpōlum viravup peyaruṅ, koṟṟaṉkuṟiyaṉ koṟṟikuṟiyaḷ koṟṟaṉ kuḷampu koṟṟi kuṟitu eṉap piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟāṉ tiṇiteritaliṉ, irutiṇip peyariṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum. koṟṟaṉcevi koṟṟicevi eṉpaṉavum piṉṉar varukiṉṟa viṉikaḷāṉ tiṇi viḷaṅki aṭaṅkumāṟu uṇarka.
iṉi, ‘aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē’ (eḻut. 155) eṉṟāṟ pōlap piṟāṇṭum ōtutalpaṟṟi ‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, viravup peyar kōṭalum oṉṟu.	(15)
avaṟṟiṉpiṉ cāriyi varumāṟu
118.	avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ cāriyi varumē.
itu cāriyi varum iṭaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ - accollappaṭṭa iru vakip peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉākiya iṭattē, cāriyi varum - cāriyic coṟkaḷ varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭūuviṉki, makaṭūuviṉki, palavaṟṟukkōṭu eṉap puṇariyaṉiliyiṭip poruṇilikku utavātu vantaṉa.
cāriyi eṉṟataṉ poruḷ, vēṟāki niṉṟa irumoḻiyun tammiṟ cārtaṟ poruṭṭu iyintu niṉṟatu eṉṟavāṟu.	(16)
cāriyikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum
119.	avitām
	iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē
	oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē
	aṉṉeṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉa eṉpa cāriyi moḻiyē.
iḵtu accāriyikaṭkup peyarum muṟiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - muṉṉarc cāriyi yeṉappaṭṭa avitām, iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē aṉṉeṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭa aṉṉa eṉpa - iṉṉum vaṟṟum attum ammum oṉṉum āṉum akkum ikkum aṉṉeṉṉuñ colloṭu kūṭa oṉpatākiya attaṉmiyuṭiyaṉavum, piṟavuñ cāriyi moḻi eṉpa - avi yoḻintaṉavuñ cāriyic collāmeṉpar āciriyar e-ṟu.
piṟavāvaṉa: tam, nam, num, um, ñāṉṟu, keḻu, ē, ai eṉpaṉavām. ivaṟṟuḷ ñāṉṟu oḻintaṉa eṭuttōtuvar āciriyar. ‘eṭutta naṟaviṉ kuliyalaṅ kāntaḷ’ (kali. 40) itu viṉittoki; cāriyi aṉṟu.
‘iṉ’ cāriyi vaḻakkuppayiṟciyum palakāl eṭuttōtappaṭutalum potuvakiyāṉ ōtiyavaḻit tāṉē cēṟalumākiya ciṟappu nōkki muṉ vittār. ‘vaṟṟum’ ‘attum’ iṉ pōla mutal tiriyumākalāṉuñ ceyki yoppumiyāṉum ataṉ piṉ vittār. ‘am’ īṟu tiriyu mātaliṉ tiripu paṟṟi ataṉpiṉ vittār. ‘oṉ’ īṟu tiriyumēṉum vaḻakkuppayiṟci yiṉṟi nāṉkā murupiṉkaṇ tiritaliṉ ataṉpiṉ vittār. ‘āṉ’ poruṭ puṇarccikkum urupu puṇarccikkum varu meṉṟu ataṉ piṉ vittār. ‘akku’ īṟu tiriyumēṉum urupu puṇarccik kaṇ vārāmiyiṉ ataṉpiṉ vittār. ‘ikku’ mutal tiriyumēṉuñ ciṟupāṉmipaṟṟi ataṉpiṉ vittār. ‘aṉ’ iṉ pōlac ciṟattaliṟ piṉ vittār.
āṉurupiṟkum āṉcāriyikkum, iṉṉurupiṟkum iṉcāriyik kum vēṟṟumi yāteṉiṉ, avi cāriyiyāṉa iṭattu yātāṉum ōr urupēṟṟu muṭiyum; urupāyiṉa iṭattu vēṟōr urupiṉi ēlāveṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉi makattukki eṉpuḻit takaravoṟṟun takara vukaramum varu meṉṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ, iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉeṉṟāl attu eṉavē vēṇṭutaliṉ āṇṭum attuniṉṟē keṭṭateṉṟu kōṭum. akku ikku eṉpaṉavum pirittuk kūṭṭak kiṭakkum. tāḻakkōleṉa akkup peṟṟu niṟṟalāṉum, āṭikku eṉpuḻik kukaram nāṉkaṉurupākāmi yāṉum ivi cāriyi yāmāṟu uṇarka.	(17)
iṉcāriyi mutal keṭṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉ
120.	avaṟṟuḷ
	iṉṉiṉ ikaram āviṉ iṟuti
	muṉṉark keṭutal urittu mākum.
itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ iṉcāriyi mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya cāriyikaḷuḷ, iṉṉiṉ ikaram-iṉcāriyiyatu ikaram, āviṉ iṟuti muṉṉar - ā eṉṉum ōreḻut torumoḻi muṉṉar, keṭutal urittumākum-keṭṭu muṭiyavum peṟum e-ṟu.
‘urittumākum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ keṭātu muṭiyavum peṟum eṉṟavāṟu. iḵtu oppakkūṟal eṉṉum utti.
e-ṭu:	āṉi āviṉi, āṉoṭu āviṉoṭu, āṟku āviṟku, āṉiṉ āviṉiṉ, āṉatu āviṉatu, āṉkaṇ āviṉkaṇ eṉa varum.
iṉi, ‘muṉṉar’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, māviṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. māṉi māviṉi, māṉoṭu māviṉoṭu, māṟku māviṟku eṉa oṭṭuka. 
iṉi, āṉkōṭu āviṉkōṭu, māṉkōṭu māviṉkōṭu eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻiyuṅ koḷka.	(18)
accāriyi īṟu tiriyumiṭaṉ
121.	aḷavākum moḻimutal niliiya uyirmici
	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉākiya nilittē.
iḵtu av viṉ cāriyi īṟu tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavākum moḻi - aḷavup peyarāyp piṉṉiṟkum moḻikku, mutal niliiya uyirmici ṉaḵkāṉ - muṉṉar niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟu niṉṟa kuṟṟukarattiṉmēl vanta iṉcāriyiyatu ṉakaram, ṟaḵkāṉākiya nilittu - ṟakaramāyt tiriyum nilimiyi yuṭittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟip patteṉa niṟutti ‘niṟiyumaḷavum’ (eḻut. 436) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ iṉcāriyi koṭuttuk, ‘kuṟṟiya lukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē’ (eḻut. 433) eṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, vēṇṭuñ ceyki ceytu ‘muṟṟaviṉ varūum’ (eḻut. 433) eṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟiraṭṭittu muṭikka.
‘niliiya’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, piṟavaḻiyum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakara mātal koḷka. patiṟṟeḻuttu, patiṟṟaṭukku, oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu, patiṟṟoṉṟu, patiṟṟiraṇṭu, patiṟṟoṉpatu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka. accūttirattiṟ ‘kuṟiyātākum’ (eḻut. 436) eṉṟataṉāṟ poruṭ peyarkkum eṇṇup peyarkkum iṉ koṭukka.	(19)
vaṟṟuccāriyi mutal tiriyumāṟu
122.	vaḵkāṉ meykeṭac cuṭṭumutal aimmuṉ
	aḵkāṉ niṟṟal ākiya paṇpē.
itu vaṟṟu mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭumutal aimmuṉ - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuc coṉmuṉ vaṟṟu varuṅkāli, vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa aḵkāṉ niṟṟalākiya paṇpu - av vaṟṟuc cāriyiyiṉatu vakaramākiya oṟṟukkeṭa āṇṭu ēṟiya akaram niṟṟal ataṟku uḷatākiya kuṇam e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aviyaṟṟi, iviyaṟṟi, uviyaṟṟi eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟi, avi ivi uvi eṉa niṟuttic ‘cuṭṭu mutalākiya aiye ṉiṟuti’ (eḻut. 177) eṉṟataṉāṉ vaṟṟum urupuṅ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñ ceyki ceyka. ivvāṟē ellā urupiṟkum oṭṭuka. aviyaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟa vaḻiyuṅ koḷka.
‘ākiya paṇpu’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, evaṉeṉpatu paṭuttalōciyāṟ peyarāyavaḻi evaṉ eṉa niṟutti, vaṟṟum urupuṅ koṭuttu, vaṟṟumici yoṟṟeṉṟu ṉakaraṅ keṭuttu, akaravuyir muṉṉar vaṟṟiṉ vakaraṅ keṭumeṉak keṭuttu, evaṟṟi evaṟṟoṭu eṉa muṭikka.	(20)
cāriyikaḷatu ṉakara īṟu tiriyumiṭaṉ
123.	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ nāṉka ṉurupiṟku.
iḵtu iṉ oṉ āṉ aṉṉeṉṉum ṉakara īṟu nāṉkun tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ṉaḵkāṉ nāṉka ṉurupiṟku ṟaḵkāṉ - ṉakāra īṟṟu nāṉku cāriyiyiṉ ṉakaramum nāṉkāmurupiṟku ṟakāramāyt tiriyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: viḷaviṟku, kōoṟku, orupāṟku, ataṟku eṉa varum.
itaṉi ‘aḷavāku moḻimutal’ (eḻut. 121) eṉpataṉ piṉ viyātu īṇṭu vittatu, ṉakara īṟukaḷellām uṭaṉ tiriyumeṉṟaṟku. āṇṭu vippiṉ iṉ cāriyiyē tiriyumeṉpatu paṭum.
‘oṉṟu mutalākap pattūrntu varūu, mellā eṇṇum’ (eḻut. 199) eṉpataṉāṉ, orupāṟku eṉpataṉi muṭikka.	(21)
āṉ cāriyi poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum īṟutirital
124.	āṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉō raṟṟē
	nāḷmuṉ varūum vaṉmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟkē.
iḵtu āṉiṉ īṟu poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ tiriyumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷmuṉ varūum vaṉmutal toḻiṟku - nāṭpeyar muṉṉar varum valleḻutti mutalāka uṭiya toḻiṟcoṟku iṭiyē varum, āṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉō raṟṟu - āṉ cāriyiyiṉ ṉakaramum nāṉka ṉurupiṉkaṇ varum āṉcāriyi pōla ṟakaramāyt tiriyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘nāḷmuṟ ṟōṉṟun toḻiṉilik kiḷavikku’ (eḻut. 247) eṉṟataṉāṉ āṉ cāriyi koṭuttuc ceyki ceyka. iṉi, ummiyi iṟantatu taḻīiyatākki, nāḷallavaṟṟumuṉ varum vaṉmutaṟṟoḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉōṭu okkumeṉap poruḷurittup, paṉiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ, vaḷiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉa iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ṟakaramātal koḷka.
iṉi, ñāpakattāṉ toḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaran tiriyumeṉavē, peyarkkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaran tiritalun tiriyāmiyuṅ koḷka. kuṟumpiṟkoṟṟaṉ, paṟampiṟpāri eṉat tirintu vantaṉa. kurukiṉkāl, eruttiṉpuṟam eṉat tiriyātu vantaṉa.	(22)
attuc cāriyi mutal keṭumiṭaṉ
125.	attiṉ akaram akaramuṉi illi.
iḵtu attu mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) attiṉ akaram - attuc cāriyiyiṉ akaram, akaramuṉi illi - akara īṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉar illiyām e-ṟu.
‘attavaṇ variṉum varinili yiṉṟē’ (eḻut. 219) eṉpataṉāṉ, makappeyar attuppeṟṟu niṉṟatu, makattukkiyeṉa akaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟatu. viḷavattukkaṇ eṉpuḻik keṭātu niṟṟal ‘attēvaṟṟē’ (eḻut. 133) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘teṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē’ eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟuka.	(23)
ikkuccāriyi mutal keṭṭumuṭiyum iṭaṉ
126.	ikkiṉ ikaram ikaramuṉi yaṟṟē.
iḵtu ikku mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ikkiṉ ikaram - ikkuc cāriyiyiṉatu ikaram. ikaramuṉi aṟṟu - ikara īṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉar muṟkūṟiya attup pōlak keṭum e-ṟu.
‘tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ’ (eḻut. 248) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟa ikku, āṭikkuk koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ikaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟatu. iḵtu iṭapporuṭṭu.	(24)
aivaruvaḻiyum atu keṭutal
127.	aiyiṉ muṉṉarum avviyal niliyum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aiyiṉ muṉṉarum avviyal niliyum - ikkiṉ ikaram ikara īṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉaraṉṟi aikāra īṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉarum mēṟkūṟiya keṭutaliyalpilē niṟkum e-ṟu.
‘tiṅkaḷu nāḷu muntukiḷan taṉṉa’ (eḻut. 286) eṉpataṉāṟ, cittirikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻip peṟṟa ikku aikārattiṉ muṉṉark keṭṭavāṟu kāṇka.	(25)
akkuccāriyi akaram oḻiya ēṉiya keṭṭumuṭiyumāṟu
128.	eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
	akkiṉ iṟutimeym miciyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu.
iḵtu akku mutal oḻiya ēṉiya keṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eppeyar muṉṉarum - evvakippaṭṭa peyarc coṉ muṉṉarum, valleḻuttu varuvaḻi - valleḻuttu varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu - iṭi niṉṟa akkuc cāriyiyiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram muṭiyat tōṉṟātu, meymmiciyoṭuṅ keṭum - akkuṟṟukaram ēṟi niṉṟa valloṟṟut taṉakkumēl niṉṟa valloṟṟoṭuṅ keṭum e-ṟu.
‘oṟṟunili tiriyā takkoṭu varūum’ (eḻut. 418) eṉpataṉāṉ, akkuppeṟṟa kuṉṟakkūki maṉṟappeṇṇi eṉpaṉavum, ‘vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ ēṉi yiraṇṭum’ (eḻut. 329) eṉpataṉāṉ, akkuppeṟṟa īmakkuṭam kammakkuṭam eṉpaṉavum, ‘tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyum’ (eḻut. 385) eṉpataṉāṉ, akkup peṟṟa tamiḻak kūttu eṉpatuvum, akku īṟum īṟṟayalum keṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam varutaliṉ ‘eppeyar’ eṉṟār.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉpataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivviti koḷka. tamiḻanūl, tamiḻayāḻ, tamiḻavariyar eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, tamakkēṟṟa iyipu valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭittuk koḷka. aṉṟik kēṭōtiya kakaravoṟṟu niṟkumeṉiṉ, cakaran takaram pakaram vantavaṟṟiṟkuk kakara voṟṟākāmi uṇarka.	(26)
ka ca ta varuvaḻi ammuccāriyiyatu iṟuti ṅa ña na-vākat tiriyumāṟu
129.	ammiṉ iṟuti kacatak kālit
	taṉmey tirintu ṅañana ākum.
iḵtu am īṟu tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ammiṉ iṟuti - ammuc cāriyiyiṉ iṟutiyākiya makaravoṟṟu, kacatak kāli - kacatakkaḷ varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, taṉmey tirintu ṅañana ākum - taṉ vaṭivu tirintu ṅañanakkaḷām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl eṉavarum.
itu ‘puḷimarak kiḷavikku’ (eḻut. 244) eṉpataṉāṉ ammup peṟṟatu. ‘kacatakkālit tiriyum’ eṉavē pakarattiṉkaṇ tiripiṉṟāyiṟṟu. ‘meytirintu’ eṉṉātu ‘taṉmey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ammiṉ makaramēyaṉṟit tam nam num um eṉṉuñ cāriyi makaramun tirital koḷka. ellār taṅkiyum, ellā naṅkiyum, ellīr nuṅkiyum, ‘vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum’ eṉa varum. (tuṟikēḻūraṉ, vaḷaṅkēḻūraṉ eṉak keḻuveṉ ṉuñ cāriyiyatu ukarakkēṭum, ekara nīṭciyuñ ceyyuṇmuṭipu eṉṟu koḷka.)	(27)
meṉkaṇam iṭikkaṇam varuvaḻi, avviṟuti keṭṭumuṭital
130.	meṉmiyum iṭimiyum varūum kāli
	iṉmi vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu ammīṟu orucār iyalpukaṇattu muṉṉark keṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) meṉmiyum iṭimiyum varūuṅkāli - meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, iṉmi vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar - ammuc cāriyi iṟuti makara miṉṟi muṭitali vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiyañeri, nuṉi, muri, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉa varum.
uriyiṟkōṭal eṉpataṉāṟ, puḷiyavili eṉa uyir varuvaḻi ammiṉ īṟu keṭutalum, puḷiyili eṉa ammu muḻuvatuṅ keṭutaluṅ koḷka. puḷiyili eṉṟatu ‘oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku’ (eḻut. 132) aṉṟu eṉpatūumām. meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamun tammuḷokkumēṉum ammu muḻuvatuṅ keṭṭu varutalum uṭimiyiṉ uyiri eṭuttōtārāyiṉar. puḷiṅkāy eṉpatu marūu muṭipu.	(28)
iṉcāriyi muḻutum keṭṭumuṭiyumiṭaṉ
131.	iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku
	iṉṉeṉ cāriyi iṉmi vēṇṭum.
iḵtu iṉcāriyi aintāmurupiṉkaṇ muḻuvatuṅ keṭum eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku - iṉṉeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku, iṉṉeṉ cāriyi iṉmi vēṇṭum - iṉ eṉṉuñ cāriyi tāṉ iṉṟi muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaviṉ, palāviṉ, kaṭuviṉ, taḻūviṉ, cēviṉ, vuviṉ eṉa varum. ivaṟṟiṟku vīḻpaḻam eṉavum, nīṅkiṉāṉ eṉavuṅ koṭuttu muṭivu uṇarka. ūriṉīṅkiṉāṉ eṉa ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
iṉi, ‘avaṟṟuḷ iṉṉiṉ ikaram’ (eḻut. 120) eṉṟataṉ piṉ itaṉi viyāta muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, iṉcāriyi keṭātu vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇuñ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum niṟṟal koḷka. pāmpiṉiṟ kaṭitu tēḷ, ‘kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala vutavi’, (aka. 86) ‘akaṭucērpu porunti yaḷaviṉiṉ tiriyātu’ (malipaṭu. 33) eṉa varum. iṉi, iṉmiyum vēṇṭu meṉṉum ummi tokku niṉṟatākki ataṉāṉ ivi kōṭalum oṉṟu.	(29)
cāriyikaḷ puṇarmoḻiyuḷ iṭiniṉṟu iyalutal,
oṭu urupiṭattuc cāriyi varutalum vārāmiyum
132.	peyarun toḻilum pirintoruṅ kicippa
	vēṟṟumi urupu nilipeṟu vaḻiyun
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum
	oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇic
	coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ vaḻivantu viḷaṅkātu
	iṭiniṉ ṟiyaluñ cāriyi iyaṟki
	uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ okkum.
itumuṟkūṟiya cāriyikaḷellām puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷē varumeṉpatūum, ammoḻitām ivi yeṉpatūum, avi vārāta moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉpatūum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
