(i-ḷ.) peyarum toḻilum - peyarc collun toḻiṟ collum, pirintu icippa oruṅku icippa - peyarun toḻilumāyp pirinticippa, peyarum peyarumāyk kūṭi yicippa, vēṟṟumi yurupu nilipeṟu vaḻiyum - vēṟṟumi ceyyum urupukaḷ tokātu nilipeṟṟa iṭattum, tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum - avvēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ tōṟṟutal vēṇṭātu tokka iṭattum, oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi - tām poruntutaṟkēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkoṭu porunti, coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ - cāriyi peṟum puṇarmoḻikaḷip pirittuk kāṇumiṭattu, cāriyi iyaṟki vaḻivantu viḷaṅkātu iṭi niṉṟu iyalum - accāriyiyiṉatu taṉmi accoṟkaḷiṉ piṉṉē vantu viḷaṅkātu naṭuvē niṉṟu naṭakkum, uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭu vayiṉ okkum - accāriyi uṇṭātalum illiyātalum oṭuvurupi ṉiṭattu ottuvarum e-ṟu.
oṭuviṟku okkum eṉavē, ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟku ovvāvāyiṉa.
e-ṭu:	viḷaviṉik kuṟittāṉ, kūḻiṟkuk kuṟṟēval ceyyum. ivi pirinticittu urupu nilipeṟṟaṉa.
‘aṉṉeṉ cāriyi’ (eḻut. 194) eṉpataṉoṭu ‘kuṟṟiyalukarat tiṟuti’ (eḻut. 195) eṉpataṉic cēra vittataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyi varutal koḷka. ivviraṇṭurupuñ cāriyi niṟpap perumpāṉmiyun tokā eṉa uṇarka.
viḷaviṉik kuṟittavaṉ, kaṭicūttirattiṟkup poṉ - ivi oruṅkicippa urupu nili peṟṟaṉa.
vāṉattiṉ vaḻukki, vāṉattu vaḻukki eṉac cāriyi peṟṟup pirinticittu aintāmurupu nilipeṟṟum, nilipeṟātum vantaṉa. vāṉattiṉ vaḻukkal, vāṉattu vaḻukkal eṉac cāriyi peṟṟu oruṅku icittu aintām urupunili peṟṟum nilipeṟātum vantaṉa. vāṉattu vaḻukki, vāṉattu vaḻukkal ivi ‘melleḻut tuṟaḻum’ (eḻut. 312) eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉpataṉāṉ attuk koṭuttu makaraṅ keṭukka oruṅkicittaṉa.
viḷaviṉatu kōṭu, viḷaviṉ kōṭu eṉa oruṅkicittuc cāriyi peṟṟavaḻi āṟaṉurupu tokātun tokkum niṉṟatu. itaṟkup pirin ticittaliṉṟu. marattukkaṭ kaṭṭiṉāṉ, marattuk kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉap pirinticittavaḻiyum, marattukkaṭ kuraṅku, marattuk kuraṅku eṉa oruṅ kicittavaḻiyuñ cāriyi niṉṟavaḻi ēḻaṉurupu tokātun tokkum niṉṟatu. ‘kiḷip peyarellām’ (eḻut. 307) eṉṟataṉuṭ ‘koḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ṇakāram ṭakāramāyiṟṟu.
‘nilāveṉ kiḷavi yattoṭu civaṇum’ (eḻut. 228) eṉa vititta attu, nilāakkatir nilāamuṟṟam peṟātāyiṟṟu, aḵtu ‘oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku’ aṉmiyiṉ. nilāttuk koṇṭāṉ, nilāttuk koṇṭavaṉ eṉpaṉa urupu tokkuḻi iruvaḻiyum peṟṟaṉa.
ellār tammiyum eṉac cāriyi īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum varutaliṉ iṭiniṟṟal perumpāṉmi eṉṟaṟku ‘iyalum’ eṉṟār.
pūviṉoṭu virinta kūntal, pūvoṭu virinta kūntal eṉa uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ ottaṉa.
iṉi ‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṭu urupiṉkaṭ peṟṟum peṟāmi yum varutalaṉṟip, peṟṟē varutaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	(30)
attum vaṟṟum varuvaḻi, nilivarumoḻikaḷiṉ tiripu
133.	attē vaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl
	oṟṟumey keṭutal teṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē
	avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu attu vaṟṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻiyatu oṟṟuk kēṭum, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ oṟṟuppēṟumākiya ceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) attēvaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl oṟṟu - attum vaṟṟumākiya avviraṇṭu cāriyimēl niṉṟa oṟṟu, mey keṭutal teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟu - taṉ vaṭivu keṭutal teḷiyappaṭṭatu; avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē - avviru cāriyi muṉṉum varum valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalattukkuṟi, avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum.
‘attiṭi varūuṅ kalameṉ aḷavē’ (eḻut. 168) ‘cuṭṭu mutal vakaram aiyu meyyum’ (eḻut. 183) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ attum vaṟṟum peṟṟuvarum makara vakara īṟukaṭku īṟṟu valleḻuttuviti iṉmiyiṉ, ‘avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu miku’meṉṟu cāriyi valleḻuttu vitittār. valleḻuttu iṉṟit tirintu muṭivaṉa ṇakāramum, ṉakāramum, lakāramum, ḷakāramumām. makara īṟṟiṟku attum, vakara īṟṟiṟku vaṟṟum varumeṉpatu avvac cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ peṟṟām.
‘vaṟṟē attē’ eṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉ muṉṉar attiṉmici yoṟṟuk keṭātu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	viṇṇattuk koṭkum, veyilattuc ceṉṟāṉ, iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, attiṉ akaram akara muṉṉarēyaṉṟip piṟa uyir muṉṉaruṅ keṭum orōviṭatteṉṟu koḷka. aṇṇāttēri, tiṭṭāttukkuḷam eṉa ākārattiṉ muṉṉarum varum attiṉ akaraṅ keṭṭatu. ivaṟṟi akara īṟākkiyum muṭippa.
iṉit ‘teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, attiṉ akaran teṟṟeṉak keṭātu niṟkum iṭamuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	atavattukkaṇ, viḷavattukkaṇ eṉa varum.	(31)
eḻuttuccāriyikaḷ : karam, kāram, kāṉ
134.	kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
	nērat tōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyi.
itu moḻiccāriyi viṭṭu, eḻuttukkaṭku varuñ cāriyikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi - kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu porunti, eḻuttiṉ cāriyi nērat tōṉṟum - eḻuttiṉkaṇ varuñ cāriyiyātaṟku ellā āciriyarāṉum uṭampaṭat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
‘kāramum karamum’ aṭuttuc colliyavaḻi iṉiticittalāṉum, vaḻakkuppayiṟci yuṭimiyāṉum, kāram vaṭaveḻuttiṟkum uriyatāta lāṉuñ cērak kūṟiṉār. ‘kāṉ’ attaṉmi yiṉmiyiṉāṟ piṉ vittār.
‘nērattōṉṟum’ eṉavē nērattōṉṟātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa. avi, āṉam ēṉam ōṉam eṉka. ivi citinta vaḻakkēṉuṅ kaṭiya lākāvāyiṉa.	(32)
karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttup peṟāmi
135.	avaṟṟuḷ
	karamuṅ kāṉum neṭṭeḻut tilavē.
aḵtu avaṟṟuṭ cila cāriyi cilaveḻuttoṭu vārāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ, karamuṅ kāṉum neṭṭeḻut tila - karamuṅ kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṟku varutalila e -ṟu.
eṉavē, neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuk kāram varumāyiṟṟu. ākāram īkāram eṉa oṭṭuka. aikāram aukāra meṉac cūttiraṅkaḷuḷ varumāṟu kāṇka.(33)
kuṟṟeḻuttu immūṉṟum peṟutal
136.	varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟuṅ kuṟṟeḻut tuṭiya.
iḵtu aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; eṉṉi? neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuc cila cāriyi vilakkiṉāṟ pōlak kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum vilakkaṟpāṭu uṇṭō veṉa aiyam nikaḻtaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟum - varalāṟṟu muṟimiyiyuṭiya mūṉṟu cāriyiyum, kuṟṟeḻuttuṭiya - kuṟṟeḻuttup peṟṟu varutaliyuṭiya e-ṟu.
akāram akaram maḵkāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. ‘vakāra miciyum’ (eḻut. 330) ‘akara ikaram’ (eḻut. 54) ‘vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa’ (eḻut. 122) eṉavum piṟavuñ cūttiraṅkaḷuṭ kāṇka. iḵkāṉ oḵkāṉ eṉpaṉa peruvaḻakkalla.
‘varaṉmuṟi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḵkāṉ eṉpuḻi āytam peṟutal koḷka. itu ‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarāytap puḷḷi’ (eḻut. 38) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟātāyiṟṟu, moḻiyāy nillāmiyiṉ.	(34)
aiyum auvum, kāṉum peṟutal
137.	aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum.
iḵtu ‘avaṟṟuṭ karamuṅ kāṉum’ eṉpataṟkoru puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum - neṭṭeḻut tukkaḷuḷ aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ muṉ vilakkappaṭṭa kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aikāṉ, aukāṉ eṉa varum.
ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu, kārattik karutaliṉ.	(35)
uṭalmēl uyirvan toṉṟutal
138.	puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyirtaṉit tiyalātu
	meyyoṭuñ civaṇum avviyal keṭuttē.
itu puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vanta kālattup puṇarum muṟimi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalātu - puḷḷi yīṟṟuc coṉmuṉṉar vanta uyirmutaṉmoḻiyiṉ uyir taṉittu naṭavātu, meyyoṭuñ civaṇum - appuḷḷiyoṭum kūṭum, avviyal keṭuttu - tāṉ taṉittu niṉṟa avviyalpiṉik keṭuttu e-ṟu.
eṉavē, ‘nīroṭu kūṭiya pālpōla’ niṉṟateṉṟu oṟṟumi kūṟiṉār, īṇṭu. itaṉāṉē uyirmeyyeṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
e-ṭu:	pālaritu, pālāḻi, ālili, poruḷīṭṭam, vāṉulaku, vāṉūṭu, vēleṟintāṉ, vēlēṟṟāṉ, poruḷiyam, poruḷoṉṟu, nāṇōṭiṟṟu, colluviyam eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ iyalpallāta puḷḷi muṉṉar uyirvantālum ivviti koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ataṉi ataṉoṭu nāṭuri eṉa varum. ivaṟṟic ‘cuṭṭumuta lukara maṉṉoṭu’ (eḻut. 176), ‘urivaru kāli nāḻik kiḷavi’ (eḻut. 240) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭikka.
‘puḷḷiyīṟṟu muṉṉum’ eṉa ummiyi māṟṟi eccavummi yākkik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉarum eṉa avviti koḷka. eṉavē, ‘kuṟṟiyalukaramu maṟṟu’ (eḻut. 105) eṉṟataṉoṭum poruntiṟṟām.
e-ṭu:	nākaritu, varakaritu eṉavarum.	(36)
puṇarcciyuḷ uyirnīṅkiyavaḻi uyirmeyyatu nili
139.	meyuyir nīṅkiṉ taṉṉuru vākum.
iḵtu uyirmey, puṇarccikkaṇ uyir nīṅkiyavaḻip paṭuvatoru viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mey uyir nīṅkiṉ - mey taṉṉoṭu kūṭi niṉṟa uyir puṇarcciyiṭattup pirintu vēṟu niṉṟatāyiṉ, taṉ uruvākum - tāṉ muṉṉarp peṟṟu niṉṟa puḷḷivaṭivu peṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: 	āl ili, ataṉ ai eṉa varum.
uyir eṉṉa vaṭiviṟṟeṉṟu āciriyar kūṟāmiyiṉ, uyirkkaṇ ārāycci yiṉṟu.
iṉi, ekara okaraṅkaḷip puḷḷiyāṉ vēṟṟumi ceytaliṉ, toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉa vaṭivuṭiya eṉṟu kōṭalumām. puṇarcci yuḷ uyirmeyyiṉip pirippārātaliṉ, itu kūṟākkāṟ ‘kuṉṟak kūṟalām’ eṉṟu uṇarka.	(37)
uṭampaṭumeyt tōṟṟam
140.	ellā moḻikkum uyirvaru vaḻiyē
	uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal variyār.
iḵtu uyirīṟum uyirmutaṉmoḻiyum puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻvatoru karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā moḻikkum - nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyumāyp puṇarum evvaki moḻikkum, uyir varuvaḻi - uyir mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvu koḷal variyār - uṭampaṭu meyyiṉatu vaṭivi uyirīṟu kōṭali nīkkār, koḷvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
avi yakaramum vakaramum eṉpatu mutaṉūl paṟṟik kōṭum;
‘uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāram
uyirmutaṉ moḻivarūuṅ kāli yāṉa’
eṉavum,
‘iṟutiyum mutalum uyirnili variṉē
uṟumeṉa moḻipa uṭampaṭu meyyē’
eṉavuṅ kūṟiṉārākaliṉ. uyirkaḷuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra īṟu, yakara uṭampaṭumey koḷḷum. ēkāram, yakāramum vakāramuṅ koḷḷum. allaṉavellām vakara uṭampaṭumeyyē koḷḷumeṉṟu uṇarka.
e-ṭu:	kiḷiyaḻakitu, ‘kurīiyōppuvāḷ’ (kuṟun. 72), ‘variyā makaḷir’ (aiṅkuṟu. 255) eṉavum; viḷavaḻakitu, palāvaḻakitu, pūvaḻakitu, kōvaḻakitu, kuvaṭintatu eṉavum oṭṭuka. ‘ēē yivaḷorutti pēṭiyō veṉṟār’ (cīvaka. 612) ‘ēvāṭal kāṇka’ eṉa ēkārattiṟku iraṇṭum vantaṉa.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, vikārappaṭṭa moḻikkaṇ ṇum uṭampaṭumey koḷka.
e-ṭu:	maravaṭi, āyirutiṇi eṉavarum.
‘variyār’ eṉṟataṉāṉ uṭampaṭumey kōṭal orutaliyaṉṟu.
e-ṭu:	kiḷi aritu, mūṅkā illi eṉavum varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ‘viṇvattuk koṭkum’ eṉac ciṟupāṉmi puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉum varum. celvuḻi uṇpuḻi eṉpaṉa viṉit toki eṉa maṟukka.	(38)
eḻuttu orutaṉmittāṉa puṇarmoḻi ōcivēṟṟumiyāṉ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal
141.	eḻuttō raṉṉa poruḷteri puṇarcci
	iciyiṉ tirital niliiya paṇpē.
iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavāmeṉa eytātatu eytuvik kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eḻuttōraṉṉa poruḷ teri puṇarcci - eḻuttu oru taṉmittāṉa poruḷ viḷaṅka niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ, iciyiṉ tirital niliiya paṇpu - eṭuttal paṭuttal nalita leṉkiṉṟa ōci vēṟṟumiyāṟ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal nilipeṟṟa kuṇam e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi, cemparutti, kuṟumparampu, nākaṉṟēvaṉpōttu, tāmarikkaṇiyār, kuṉṟēṟāmā eṉa iciyiṉ tirintaṉa.	(39)
appuṇarmoḻikaḷ kuṟippāṉ poruḷ pulappaṭuttal
142.	avitām
	muṉṉap poruḷa puṇarcci vāyiṉ
	iṉṉa eṉṉum eḻuttukkaṭaṉ ilavē.
itu mēlataṟkoru puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām - palaporuṭkup potuveṉṟa puṇar moḻikaḷtām, muṉṉapporuḷa - kuṟippāṉ uṇarum poruṇmi yiṉiyuṭiya, puṇarccivāyiṉ iṉṉa veṉṉum eḻuttukkaṭaṉ ila - puṇarcciyiṭattu ittaṉmiyaveṉṉum eḻuttu muṟimiyi uṭiyavalla e-ṟu.
cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉpuḻip poṉṉārāycci yuḷvaḻip poṉṉeṉavuñ, cempārāycci yuḷvaḻic cempeṉavuṅ kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatu. ‘iciyiṉ tirital’ eṉṟatu oliyeḻuttiṟkeṉavum, ‘eḻuttuk kaṭaṉila’ eṉṟatu varivaṭiviṟkeṉavuṅ koḷka.	(40)
puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

5
tokimarapu
ka ca ta pa - varuvaḻi, nilimoḻi ṅa ña na ma - mikutal
143.	kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟ ṟōṉṟum
	melleḻut tiyaṟki colliya muṟiyāṉ
	ṅañanama veṉṉum oṟṟā kummē
	aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayi ṉāṉa
eṉpatu cūttiram.
uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum mēli akattōttiṉuḷ muṭikkumvaḻi īṟukaṭōṟum virittu muṭippaṉavaṟṟi īṇṭu orōvoru cūttirat tāṉ tokuttu muṭipu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, ivvōttut ‘tokimarapu’ eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēl mūvaki moḻiyum nālvakiyāṟ puṇarvuḻi, mūṉṟu tiripum ōriyalpum eyti, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi yeṉa irupakutiyavāki, eḻuttuñ cāriyiyum mikkup puṇarumāṟu ituveṉṟu uṇartti, avitām virinta cūttirap poruḷavaṉṟiyun tokkup puṇarumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ, ivvōttup puṇariyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
ittaliccūttiram uyirmayaṅkiyaliyum, puḷḷimayaṅkiyaliyum nōkkiyatoru varumoḻik karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟ ṟōṉṟum iyaṟki melleḻuttu - uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum muṉṉar niṟpak kacatapakkaḷi mutalākavuṭiya moḻikaḷ vantāl avaṟṟiṟku mēlē tōṉṟi niṟkum iyalpākiya melleḻuttukkaḷ, colliya muṟiyāṉ ṅañanama eṉṉum oṟṟākum - neṭuṅkaṇakkiṟ poruntak kūṟiya muṟiyāṉē kacatapakkaḷukku ṅañanama veṉṉum oṟṟukkaḷ niraṉiṟi vakiyāṉām: aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayiṉāṉa - attaṉmittākiya muṟimiyiṉi yuṭiya moḻika ḷiṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
itu ‘marappeyarkkiḷavi’ (eḻut. 217) eṉpataṉāṉ melleḻuttup peṟṟatu. maraṅ kuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyumāṟu ‘alvaḻiyellām’ (eḻut. 314) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟutumēṉum, īṇṭut ‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nilimoḻikkaṇ tōṉṟi niṉṟa oṟṟuttirital koḷka.
‘aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻi’ yaṉmiyiṉ, viḷakkuṟumi viḷak kuṟittāṉ eṉpuḻi melleḻuttup peṟāvāyiṉa; ivi ēḻāvatum iraṇṭāvatun tiritaliṉ. iṅṅaṉam eḻuttup peṟuvaṉavun tirivaṉavumellām varumoḻiyēpaṟṟi varumeṉṟu uṇarka.	(1)
meṉkaṇam iṭikkaṇam uyirkkaṇam varuvaḻi,
iruvaḻiyum iyalpātal
144.	ñanama yavaveṉum mutalāku moḻiyum
	uyirmuta lākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi yaṉittum ellā vaḻiyum
	niṉṟa colmuṉ iyalpā kummē.
itu muṟkūṟiya nālvakip puṇarcciyuḷ iyalpu puṇaruṅkāl, ikkūṟiya patiṉēḻeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy vanta iṭattu, irupattu nāṉkīṟṟiṉ muṉṉarum vēṟṟumiyilum alvaḻiyilum varumoḻi iyalpāy muṭikaveṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ñanamayava eṉum mutalāku moḻiyum - ñanamayava eṉṟu collappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷ mutalāy niṟkuñ coṟkaḷum, uyir mutalākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa  -  uyireḻuttu mutalāy niṉṟa coṟkaḷun tammiṟkūṭa, aṉṟiyaṉittum - appatiṉēḻākiya varumoḻikaḷum, ellāvaḻiyum - vēṟṟumiyum alvaḻiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, niṉṟa coṉmuṉ - irupattunāṉku īṟṟavāy niṉṟa peyarccoṉmuṉṉar, iyalpākum - tiripiṉṟi iyalpu puṇarcciyāy niṟkum e-ṟu.
uyirīṟṟiṉkaṇ ekara okaram oḻintaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	viḷa palā kiḷi kurī kaṭu pū cē ki cō ku eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntiyatu eṉa meymmutaṉmoḻi varuvittu, poruḷ tarutaṟku ēṟpaṉa aṟintu kūṭṭuka.
cō eṉpatu araṇ. ataṟkuc cōñoḷkiṟṟu eṉak koḷka. ku veṉpataṟkuk keḷañekiḻntatu, nīṭiṟṟu eṉka. iṉi ivaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar uyirmutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl aḻakitu āyiṟṟu illi īṇṭiṟṟu uṇṭu ūṟiṟṟu eḻuntatu ēyntatu aitu oṉṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auviyattatu eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuṭ cōvukku, iṭintatu īṇṭiyatu uḷḷatu ūṟiṟṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟoṭu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiyum oṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumikkaṇ viḷa mutaliyavaṟṟi niṟutti, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi aḻaku ākkam iḷami īṭṭam uyarvu ūṟṟam eḻucci ēṟṟam aiyam oḻivu ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka. ēlātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṟkūṟiyavāṟupōla ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka.
iṉip puḷḷiyīṟṟu ṇakāramum ṉakāramum mēṟkūṟupa. ēṉiya īṇṭuk kūṟutum.
e-ṭu:	uriñ verin eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻntatu nīṭiṟṟu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum, ñekiḻcci nīṭṭippu aṭivu ākkam eṉavum varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. maram vēy vēr yāḻ eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntiyatu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi aṭivu ākkam eṉavum varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
ivaṟṟuḷ, makara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṭ keṭutal ‘tuvara’ (eḻut. 310) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. alvaḻikkaṭkeṭutal ‘alvaḻi yellām’ (eḻut. 314) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. nilimoḻit tiripu īṇṭuk koḷḷāmi uṇarka. yakara īṟu yakarattiṉ muṉṉar iraṇṭiṭattuṅ keṭutal īṇṭu ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. vēl tev kōḷ eṉa niṟutti, ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu iruvaḻiyum oṭṭuka. ṇakāra lakāra ḷakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar nakaram varumoḻiyāka vantuḻi annakaran tiritaliṉ attirinta utāraṇaṅkaḷ īṇṭuk koḷḷaṟka. ivaṟṟuḷ tirintu varuvaṉavuḷa; avi eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum ēṉiyōttuk kaḷuḷ muṭikiṉṟavāṟṟāṉ uṇarka.
iṉi, ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ uyirkkaṇamāyiṉ oṟṟiraṭṭiyum uṭampaṭumey peṟṟum uyirēṟiyum muṭiyuṅ karuvittiripukaḷ tiripeṉappaṭā, ivviyalpiṉkaṇṇa eṉṟuṇarka. varaku ñāṉṟatu, varaku ñāṟci eṉak kuṟṟukarattiṉkaṇṇum ivvāṟē koḷka.
irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumikkum alvaḻikkum akattōttiṉuḷ nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivaṉavaṟṟi oru cūttirattāṟ ṟokuttu muṭittār. mēlum ivvāṟē kūṟupa. ivviyalpu varumoḻi nōkkik kūṟiyateṉṟu uṇarka. ivviyalpu puṇarcci meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu, uyirkkaṇ nikaḻāmiyiṉ mey muṟkūṟiṉār.
	(2)
toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟiṉkaṇ melleḻuttu uṟaḻntum muṭital
145.	avaṟṟuḷ
	melleḻut tiyaṟki uṟaḻiṉum variyār
	colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟuti yāṉa.
itu muṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku ammuṭipu vilakkip piṟitu viti eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu kaṇattiṉuḷ, melleḻut tiyaṟki uṟaḻiṉum variyār - melleḻuttu iyalpātalēyaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭiyiṉum nīkkār; colliya toṭar moḻi iṟutiyāṉa - collappaṭṭa toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟukkaṇ e-ṟu.
ummi etirmaṟi. eṉavē, uṟaḻāmi valiyuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	katirñeri katirññeri nuṉimuri eṉavum, itaḻñeri itaḻññeri nuṉimuri eṉavum varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttorumoḻikaḷuḷḷuñ cila uṟaḻccipeṟṟu muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pūñeri pūññeri nuṉimuri, kāyñeri kāyññeri nuṉi muri eṉa varum.
‘colliya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ōreḻuttorumoḻikaḷuṭ cila mikku muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kiññerittār, nīṭṭiṉār maṟittār eṉa varum.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, īreḻuttorumoḻikkaṇ meyññāṉam nūl maṟantōr eṉavarum. ivaṟṟi nalintu kūṟap piṟattaliṉ iyal peṉpārum uḷar. pūñāṟṟiṉār eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa mikātaṉa.	(3)
ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar yāvum ñāvum
oruviṉi vanta taṉmi ottal
146.	ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
	viṉiyō raṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, yakara ñakara mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu nikaḻvatoru taṉmi kūṟukiṉṟatu. ituvum puṇariyaloḻipāyk karuvip pāṟpaṭum.
(i-ḷ.) ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum - ṇakāra ṉakārameṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉarvanta yāvum ñāvum mutalākiya viṉiccoṟkaḷ, viṉiyōraṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - oru viṉi vanta taṉmiyi okkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘maṇyātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē’ ‘maṇñātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē’ eṉavum, ‘poṉyātta tārap puravi parikkumē’ ‘poṉñātta tārap puravi parikkumē’ eṉavum varum.
viṉikkaṇ eṉavē, maṇyāmi maṇñāmi eṉap peyarkkaṇ vārāvāyiṉa. ‘ñā’ muṟkūṟātu ‘yā’ muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ ñāc ceṉṟavaḻi yāc cellātu, yāc ceṉṟavaḻi ñāc cellumeṉṟu koḷka. maṇñāṉṟatu eṉṟavaḻi maṇyāṉṟatu eṉṟu vārāmi uṇarka.	(4)
ṇakara ṉakara īṟukaḷ alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpātal
147.	moḻimuta lākum ellā eḻuttum
	varuvaḻi niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻit tiripiṭaṉ ilavē.
itu, ṇakāra īṟum, ṉakāra īṟum alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) moḻi mutalākum ellā veḻuttum varuvaḻi - moḻikku mutalāmeṉappaṭṭa irupattiraṇṭeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum - muṉṉark kūṟiniṉṟa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum, vēṟṟumi yalvaḻit tiripiṭaṉ ilavē - vēṟṟumi yallāta iṭattut tiriyumiṭam ila e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟuttik, kaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntiyatu aṭintatu āyiṟṟu illi īṇṭiṟṟu uṇṭu ūṭṭiṟṟu evviṭattatu ēṟiṟṟu aitu oḻukiṟṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auviyatu eṉa oṭṭuka.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ṇakārattiṟkuc ciṟupāṉmi tiripum uṇṭeṉṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	cāṭkōl eṉa varum. itaṟkuc cāṇākiya kōleṉka.
ivi ‘niṉṟacoṉmuṉ iyalpākum’ (eḻut. 144) eṉṟavaḻi aṭaṅkāvāyiṉa, atu varumoḻipaṟṟit tiriyāmi kūṟiyatākaliṉ. itu nilimoḻipaṟṟit tiriyāmi kūṟiyatu.	(5)
avvīṟukaḷ vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum
iyalpukaṇam varuvaḻi iyalpātal
148.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum valleḻut talvaḻi
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki āvayi ṉāṉa.
itu muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ vēṟṟumikkaṇ tiripu eyti niṉṟavaṟṟi, īṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu alvaḻit tiriyāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āvayiṉāṉa - alvaḻikkaṇ aṅṅaṉan tiriyātu niṉṟa avvoṟṟukkaḷ, vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcci yiṭattum, valleḻut talvaḻi mēṟ kūṟiyaṟki - valleḻuttallāta iṭattu mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipām e-ṟu.
eṉavē, valleḻuttu vantuḻit tiriyu meṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi nuntiyatu aḻaku ākkam iṉmi eṉa ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka.
ituvuñ ceykic cūttiram. mēl nāṉku cūttirattāṟ kūṟiyaṉa valleḻuttu vantuḻit tiriyumāṟu tattam īṟṟuṭ kūṟupa.	(6)
la ṉa - puḷḷi muṉ, ta nakkaḷ ṟa ṉa - ātal
149.	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉart
	tana eṉavariṟ ṟaṉavā kummē.
itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyali (368, 369ām nūṟpā) nōkkiyatoru varumoḻikkaruvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) la ṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar - lakāra ṉakāra meṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ta na eṉavariṉ - takāramum nakāramum mutaleṉṟu collumpaṭiyākac cilacoṟkaḷvariṉ, ṟaṉavākum - niraṉiṟiyāṉē avi ṟakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷākat tiriyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaḵṟītu kaṉṉaṉṟu, poṉṟītu poṉṉaṉṟu eṉa varum.
nilimoḻittiripu tattam īṟṟuṭ kūṟupa. (369)	(7)
ṇa ḷa - puḷḷi muṉ, avi ṭa ṇa eṉat tirital
150.	ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇakāra ḷakāra meṉṟu collap paṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar atikārattāṉ takāra nakāraṅkaḷ varumeṉiṉ, ṭaṇa veṉat tōṉṟum - avi niraṉiṟiyāṉē ṭakāra ṇakāraṅkaḷāyt tirintu tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇṭītu, maṇṇaṉṟu, muḵṭītu muṇṇaṉṟu eṉa varum. nilimoḻit tiripu tattam īṟṟuṭ kūṟupa (399).	(8)
muṉṉili ēvalorumiviṉi
vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
151.	uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum
	iyalpā kunavum uṟaḻā kunavumeṉ(ṟu)
	āyī riyala valleḻuttu variṉē.
itu muṉṉili viṉiccol vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum - uyirīṟāy vanta muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷum, puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum - puḷḷiyīṟāy vanta muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷum, valleḻuttu variṉ - valleḻuttu mutalākiya moḻivariṉ, iyalpākunavum uṟaḻākunavu meṉṟu āyīriyala - iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavumeṉa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉi yuṭiya e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eṟikoṟṟā koṇākoṟṟā uṇkoṟṟā tiṉkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivi iyalpu. naṭakoṟṟā naṭakkoṟṟā, īrkoṟṟā īrkkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci.
‘īṟu’ eṉṟu ōtiṉamiyiṉ viṉiccollē koḷka. ivi muṉṉiṉṟāṉ toḻiluṇarttuvaṉavum, avaṉit toḻiṟ paṭuttuvaṉavumeṉa iruvakiya. i ai āy mutaliyaṉa toḻiluṇarttuvaṉa. naṭa, vā, uṇ, tiṉ mutaliyaṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟun toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉa. nilkoṟṟā niṟkoṟṟā eṉat tirintu uṟaḻntaṉavum, ‘uṟaḻā kunavum’ eṉṉum potuvakiyāṉ muṭikka. ‘iyalpu muṟaḻvumeṉ ṟiraṇṭiyalpiṉa’ eṉṉātu ‘ākunavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tukkoṟṟā nokkoṟṟā ñeḷḷā nākā māṭā vaṭukā eṉa ōreḻuttorumoḻi muṉṉili viṉic col mikkē muṭital koḷka.	(9)
mēli muṭipuṟkup poruntāta muṉṉili
(ēvalorumi) viṉi yīṟukaḷ
152.	auveṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum
	ñanamava eṉṉum puḷḷi yiṟutiyuṅ
	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟutiyum uḷappaṭa
	muṟṟat tōṉṟā muṉṉili moḻikkē.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu; muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cila ākātaṉa vaṟṟi varintu uṇarttaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) auveṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum - auveṉa varukiṉṟa uyirīṟṟuc collum, ñanamava eṉṉum puḷḷiyiṟutiyum - ñanamava eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum, kuṟṟiya lukarattu iṟutiyum - kuṟṟiyalukaratti iṟutiyilēyuṭiya collum, muṉṉili moḻikku uḷappaṭa muṟṟat tōṉṟā - muṉṉar muṉṉili moḻikkup poruntak kūṟiya iyalpum uṟaḻcciyumākiya muṭipiṟku muṟṟat tōṉṟā e-ṟu.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ nilimoḻi ukaram peṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kuvukoṟṟā kuvukkoṟṟā, vuvukoṟṟā vuvukkoṟṟā, uriñukoṟṟā, uriñukkoṟṟā, porunu koṟṟā porunukkoṟṟā, tirumukoṟṟā tirumukkoṟṟā, tevvukoṟṟā tevvukkoṟṟā eṉa vum kūṭṭukoṟṟā kūṭṭukkoṟṟā eṉavum varum.	(10)
uyartiṇippeyar iruvaḻiyum iyalpātal
153.	uyirī ṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti uyartiṇip peyarum
	ellā vaḻiyum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu uyartiṇippeyar vaṉkaṇam, meṉkaṇam, iṭik kaṇam, uyirkkaṇameṉṉum nāṉku kaṇattiṉum iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarum - uyirīṟāy vanta uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum, puḷḷi yiṟuti uyartiṇip peyarum - puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉiyuṭiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum, ellā vaḻiyum - nāṉku kaṇattu alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, iyalpeṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e -ṟu.
vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷi ‘ña na ma ya va’ (eḻut. 144) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭippārum uḷar. atu poruntātu; ivvāciriyar uyartiṇip peyarum viravup peyarum eṭuttōtiyē muṭippārātaliṉ.
e-ṭu:	nampi avaṉ eṉavum, naṅki avaḷ eṉavum niṟutti, alvaḻikkaṭ kuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ eṉavum; kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ eṉavum; ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉavum; ñāṉṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ eṉavum; yāvaṉ valiyaṉ eṉavum; yāvaḷ valiyaḷ eṉavum; aṭintāṉ āyiṉāṉ auviyattāṉ eṉavum; aḻakiyaḷ āṭiṉāḷ auviyattāḷ eṉavum oṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumikkaṇ ki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum; ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum; yāppu vaṉmi eṉavum; aḻaku --- auviyam eṉavum ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. oruvēṉ eṉat taṉmip peyarkkaṇṇuṅ kuṟiyēṉ ciṟiyēṉ tīyēṉ periyēṉ eṉavum, ki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum oṭṭuka.
nī muṉṉili viravuppeyarātaliṉ īṇṭik kākātu.
iṉi ‘uyirīṟu puḷḷiyiṟuti’ eṉṟa mikiyāṉē, uyartiṇip peyar tirintu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kapilaparaṇar, iṟivaneṭuvēṭṭuvar, maruttuva māṇikkar eṉa ṉakara īṟu keṭṭu iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. ācīvakappaḷḷi, nikkantakkōṭṭam eṉa ivi avvīṟu keṭṭu oṟṟu mikku muṭintaṉa. īḻavakkatti, vāṇikatteru, aracakkaṉṉi, kōlikakkaruvi eṉa ivi orumi yīṟum paṉmi yīṟuṅ keṭṭu mikku muṭintaṉa. kumarakōṭṭam kumarak kōṭṭam, piramakōṭṭam piramakkōṭṭam eṉa ivi īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntaṉa. vaṇṇārappeṇṭir: iḵtu īṟukeṭṭu mikku muṭintatu. palcaṅkattār, palcāṉṟōr, pallaracar eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa rakaravīṟum ataṉ muṉṉiṉṟa akaramuṅ keṭṭup piṟa ceykikaḷum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
iṉi, ‘ellā vaḻiyum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇi viṉiccol iyalpāyun tirintum muṭivaṉa ellāṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	uṇku uṇṭu varutu cēṟu uṇpal uṇṭēṉ uṇpēṉ eṉṉun taṉmiviṉikaḷik koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu oṭṭuka. uṇṭīr cāṉṟīr pārppīr eṉa muṉṉilikkaṇṇum, uṇpa uṇṭār - cāṉṟōr pārppār eṉap paṭarkkikkaṇṇum oṭṭuka. ivi iyalpu.
uṇṭaṉeñcāṉṟēm uṇṭēnām eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa tirintu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(11)
ikara īṟṟu uyartiṇippeyar tirintumuṭiyumāṟu
154.	avaṟṟuḷ
	ikara iṟupeyar tiripiṭa ṉuṭittē.
iḵtu uyartiṇip peyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ ikara iṟupeyar - muṟkūṟiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷuḷ ikara īṟṟuppeyar, tiripiṭaṉuṭittu - iruvaḻiyun tirintu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eṭṭippū, kāvitippū, nampippēṟu eṉa ivvuyartiṇip peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumikkaṇ mikku muṭintaṉa.
eṭṭi, kāviti eṉpaṉa tēyavaḻakkākiya ciṟappup peyar. eṭṭi maram aṉṟu. aḵtu ‘eṭṭi kumara ṉiruntōṉ ṟaṉṉi’ (maṇi. 4 : 58) eṉṟataṉāṉ uṇarka. ivi eṭṭiyatupū eṭṭikkuppū eṉa viriyum.
iṉi, nampikkollaṉ nampiccāṉṟāṉ nampittuṇi nampip piḷḷi eṉavum, ceṭṭikkūttaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum alvaḻikkaṇ uyartiṇippeyar mikku muṭintaṉa.
‘iṭaṉuṭittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ikara īṟallātaṉavum īṟu tiriyātu niṉṟu valleḻuttup peṟutal koḷka. naṅkippeṇ naṅkiccāṉi eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ ciṟupāṉmi aikāra īṟu mikkaṉa.
ivvīṟ ṟaḵṟiṇip peyar mikku muṭital uyir mayaṅkiyaluṭ kūṟupa.	(12)
viravuppeyaruḷ cila iyalpāy muṭital
155.	aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar iyalpumā ruḷavē.
itu viravuppeyaruḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar - uyartiṇip peyarōṭu aḵṟiṇi viraviya viravuppeyar, iyalpumāruḷa - iyalpāy muṭivaṉa vum uḷa, ummiyāṉ iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉavum uḷa e-ṟu.
uyartiṇip peyarōṭu aḵṟiṇi ceṉṟu viraviṟṟeṉṟatu eṉṉi? collatikārattu ‘irutiṇic coṟkumō raṉṉa vurimiyiṉ’ (col. 174) eṉṟu cūttirañ ceyvārāl eṉiṉ, atuvum poruntumāṟu kūṟutum. cāttaṉ cātti, muṭavaṉ muṭatti eṉa varum viravup peyarkkaṇ uyartiṇikku uriyavāka ōtiya āṇpālum peṇpālum uṇartti niṉṟa īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē aḵṟiṇi yāṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttiṉa eṉṟal vēṇṭum; eṉṉi? aḵṟiṇikku orumippālum paṉmippālum uṇarttum īṟaṉṟi āṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ uḷavāka āciriyar ōtāmiyiṉ. aṅṅaṉam uyartiṇi irupālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ niṉṟē aḵṟiṇiyāṇ pāliyum peṇpāliyum uṇarttutaliṉ, aḵṟiṇi uyartiṇi yoṭu ceṉṟu viraviṟṟeṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ uṇmit taṉmit tōṟṟaṅ kūṟuvāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār. ivvāṟē viḷimarapiṉkaṭ ‘kiḷanta viṟuti yaḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar’ (col. 152) eṉpuḻiyum āciriyar uyartiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viraviya viravuppeya reṉa āṇṭum uṇmit taṉmit tōṟṟaṅ kūṟuvār. māṇākkaṉ iṉitu uṇartaṟku ivvāṟu viravup peyariṉatu uṇmit taṉmit tōṟṟam iraṇṭu atikārattuṅ kūṟi, avviravuppeyar vaḻakkiṉkaṇ irutiṇip poruḷum uṇartti irutiṇic collāy niṟṟaṟkum otta urimiyavāmeṉap pulappaṭa niṟkumāṟu kāṭṭiṉāreṉṟu  uṇarka.
iṉi, avi alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy niṟkumāṟu:- cāttaṉ koṟṟaṉ, cātti koṟṟi eṉa niṟuttik, kuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ, kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ eṉavum; ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ yāvaṉ valiyaṉ, ñāṉṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ yāvaḷ valiyaḷ eṉavum; aṭintāṉ auvittāṉ, aṭintāḷ auvittāḷ eṉavum, nāṉku kaṇattōṭum oṭṭi uṇarka.
iṉi vēṟṟumikkaṇ ki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi aḻaku auviyam eṉavum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ ṉakāram niṟpat takāra nakāram vantuḻit tiriyum utāraṇam īṇṭuk koḷḷaṟka.
iṉic cāttaṉkuṟitu, cāttikuṟitu eṉa aḵṟiṇi muṭipēṟ paṉavuṅ koḷka. ivaṟṟoṭu viṉiccol talippeyya ivi irutiṇikkum uriyavām. (āṇṭu nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivataṉi īṇṭut tokuttār. iḵtu uyartiṇikkum okkum.)
ummiyāṉ, iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa ṉakāra īṟṟuṭ kāṭṭutum.	 (13)
mūṉṟām vēṟṟumit tokiyuḷ iyalpum uṟaḻvum
156.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
	valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟiyāṉ
	tammi ṉākiya toḻiṟcol muṉvariṉ
	meymmi yākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	ammuṟi yiraṇṭum uriyavi uḷavē
	vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.
itu mēl uyirīṟṟiṟkum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumikkaṭ kūṟum muṭipu peṟātu niṟkum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tammiṉākiya toḻiṟ col - mūṉṟāvataṟku uriya viṉi mutaṟ poruḷāṉuḷavākiya toḻiṟcol, puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉum uyiriṟu kiḷavi muṉṉum variṉ - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉarum uyirīṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉarum varumāyiṉ, meymmiyākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum ammuṟiyiraṇṭum uriyavi uḷa - avaṟṟuḷ iyalpāka lum uṟaḻattōṉṟalumākiya am muṟi yiraṇṭum peṟutaṟku uriya uḷavātalāṉ, vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṟ colliya muṟiyāṉ vēṇṭum valleḻuttu mikuti - uyirmayaṅkiyaluḷḷum puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷḷum vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkuc colliya muṟiyāṉ virumpum valleḻuttu mikutiyi, pōṟṟal - īṇṭuk koḷḷaṟka e-ṟu.
‘meymmi’ paṭṭāṅkātaliṉ iyalpām.
e-ṭu:	nāy puli eṉa niṟuttik, kōṭpaṭṭāṉ cārappaṭṭāṉ tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa varuvittu iyalpāya vāṟu kāṇka. cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭpaṭṭāṉ vaḷikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārappaṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivi uṟaḻntaṉa.
‘puḷḷiyiṟuti uyiriṟukiḷavi’ eṉṟataṉāṟ pēeykōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēeykkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉa ekarappēṟum uṟaḻccikkuk koṭukka.
‘ammuṟi yiraṇṭum uriyavi uḷavē’ eṉṟataṉāṟ pāmpu kōṭpaṭṭāṉ pāppukkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nili moḻi yoṟṟut tiritaluṅ koḷka. ivvīṟukaḷ nāykkāl tērkkāl kiḷikkāl eṉa āṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikumāṟu kāṇka.	(14)
iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tiripukaḷāvaṉa
157.	melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalum
	uyirmika varuvaḻi uyirkeṭa varutalum
	cāriyi uḷvaḻic cāriyi keṭutalum
	cāriyi uḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu niliyalum
	cāriyi yiyaṟki yuṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum
	aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyark kavviyal niliyalum
	meypiṟi tākiṭat tiyaṟki yātalum
	aṉṉa piṟavun taṉṉiyal maruṅkiṉ
	meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarin ticikkum
	aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit tiripu tokuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum - ‘marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē’ (eḻut. 217) eṉṟataṉāṉ viḷaṅkuṟittāṉeṉa melleḻuttu mikumiṭattu viḷakkuṟittāṉeṉa valleḻuttut tōṉṟutalum,
valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum - ‘makara viṟuti’ (eḻut. 310) eṉpataṉāṉ marakkuṟittāṉ eṉa valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu maraṅkuṟittāṉ eṉa melleḻuttut tōṉṟutalum,
iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalum - ‘tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟki yākum’ (eḻut. 358) eṉṟavaḻit tāykoli eṉa iyalpāy varumiṭattut tāykkoli eṉa mikuti tōṉṟutalum,
uyirmika varuvaḻi uyirkeṭa varutalum - ‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum’ (eḻut. 226) eṉavuṅ, ‘kuṟṟeḻut timparum’ (eḻut. 267) eṉavum, ‘ēye ṉiṟutikku’ (eḻut. 277) eṉavuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāṉ uyirmikku varumiṭattup palākkuṟittāṉ kaḻukkoṇarntāṉ ēkkaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa uyir keṭa varutalum,
cāriyi uḷvaḻic cāriyikeṭutalum - ‘vaṇṭum peṇṭum’ (eḻut. 420) eṉpataṉāṟ cāriyippēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu vaṇṭu koṇarntāṉ eṉac cāriyi keṭṭu niṟṟalum,
cāriyi uḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu niliyalum - ‘vaṇṭum peṇṭum’ eṉpataṉāṟ cāriyippēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu vaṇṭiṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉat taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum (itaṟku valleḻuttuppēṟu īṟṟu vakiyāṟ koḷka),
cāriyi iyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum - ‘puḷimarak kiḷavikku’ (eḻut. 244) eṉavum, ‘paṉiyumariyum’ (eḻut. 283) eṉavum, ‘pūlvē leṉṟā’ (eḻut. 375) eṉavum, peṟṟa cāriyi peṟātu iyalpāy niṉṟu puḷikuṟittāṉ puḷikkuṟittāṉ, paṉitaṭintāṉ, paṉittaṭintāṉ, pūlkuṟittāṉ pūṟkuṟittāṉ eṉa mikkun tirintum uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟutalum,
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum - ‘uyirīṟākiya uyar tiṇip peyarum’ (eḻut. 153) eṉpataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpāy varumeṉṟavi nampiyik koṇarntāṉ naṅkiyik koṇarntāṉ eṉṟavaḻi iraṇṭaṉurupu tokātē valloṟṟu mikku niṟṟalum,
‘oḻiyātu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, makaṟpeṟṟāṉ makaṭpeṟṟāṉ eṉavum, āṭūuvaṟicol (col. 2) ‘maḻavarōṭṭiya’ (akam. 1) ‘avaṟkaṇ ṭemmuḷ’ (akam. 48) eṉavum oḻintum varumeṉṟu koḷka.
aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyarkku avviyal niliyalum - uyartiṇi yōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravum peyarkkuk koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉeṉa urupu tokātē niṟṟalum,
‘avviyal niliyalum’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, makappeṟṟēṉeṉa viravuppeyarkkaṇṇun tokutal koḷka. urupiyaluḷ, ‘tēruṅkāli’ (eḻut. 202) eṉṟa ilēcāṉ itaṟkum muṉṉiyataṟkum valleḻuttup pēṟuṅ koḷka.
meypiṟi tākiṭattu iyaṟki yātalum - puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ ṇakāra ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttiyiyiṉ meypiṟitāmeṉṟa iṭattu meypiṟitākātu, maṇkoṇarntāṉ poṉkoṇarntāṉ eṉa iyaṟkiyāy varutalum,
aṉṉa piṟavum - avipōlvaṉa piṟavum,
avi ‘eṟkaṇṭu peyaruṅkāli yāḻaniṉ kaṟkeḻu ciṟukuṭi’  (akanā. 318) eṉavum, ‘nappuṇar villā nayaṉillōr naṭpu’ (naṟ. 165) eṉavum varuvaḻi, eṟkaṇṭu nappuṇarvu eṉṉun toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum uyar tiṇip peyarkaḷ melleḻuttup peṟutaṟku uriyaṉa valleḻuttup peṟutal koḷka. iṉṉun tiṉipiḷantāṉ mayirkuṟittāṉ taṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṟuttāṉ pukaḻntāṉ eṉa varum.
taṉ iyal maruṅkiṉ - taṉṉiyē nōkkit tiripu naṭakkumiṭattu, meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷ varintu icikkum - poruḷpeṟa eṭut tōtappaṭṭu ēṉi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyatu potu muṭipiṉit tāṉ nīkki vēṟu muṭipiṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum, aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripeṉa moḻipa - iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyatu vēṟupaṭṭa puṇarcci eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
‘meypeṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉē cāriyiyuḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu niliyātu ceyyuṭkaṇ varuvaṉavum, piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇ uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. ‘maṟaṅkaṭinta varuṅkaṟpiṉ’ eṉavum, ‘cilcolliṟ palkūntal’ (puṟam. 166) eṉavum, ‘āyiru tiṇiyiṉicikkumaṉ’ (col. 1) eṉavum, piṟāṇṭum perumpāṉmiyum varumeṉṟu koḷka. mikoṇarntāṉ mik koṇarntāṉ, vilkōl viṟkōḷ eṉa uṟaḻntum varum.
iṉi, ivvāṟu tiriyātu akattōttiṟ kūṟiya potumuṭipē tamakku muṭipāka varuvaṉavum koḷka. avi kaṭukkuṟittāṉ, ceppukkoṇarntāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa. ‘tammiṉākiya toḻiṟcoṉ muṉvariṉ’ (eḻut. 156) eṉṟa atikārattāṉ, viṉivantuḻiyē iṅṅaṉam perumpāṉmi tirivateṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉit ‘taṉṉiṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ ēḻāvataṟkum viṉi yoṭu muṭivuḻit tirital koḷka. atu ‘varipāy varuṭi’ (malipaṭu. 503), ‘pulampuk kaṉaṉē pullaṇaṟ kāḷi’ (puṟam. 258) eṉṟāṟpōl varum.	(15)
ikara aikāra īṟṟuppeyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu
158.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi iai yeṉṉum
	īṟṟuppeyark kiḷavi mūvaki niliya
	avitām
	iyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum
	uṟaḻā kunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu ikara īṟṟup peyarkkum aikāra īṟṟup peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi - vēṟṟumi yallā iṭattu, i ai eṉṉum īṟṟuppeyarkkiḷavi mūvaki niliya - i ai eṉṉum īṟṟi yuṭiya peyarccoṟkaḷ mūvakiyākiya muṭipu niliyiyuṭiya; avitām - ammuṭipukaḷtām, iyalpākunavum - iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum, valleḻuttu mikunavum - valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavum - uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum, eṉmaṉār pulavar - eṉa iviyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	parutti kuṟitu, kāri kuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa ivi iyalpu. mācittiṅkaḷ, cittirittiṅkaḷ, alikkoṟṟaṉ, pulikkoṟṟaṉ, kāvikkaṇ, kuvaḷikkaṇ eṉa ivi mikuti. kiḷikuṟitu kiḷikkuṟitu, tiṉikuṟitu tiṉikkuṟitu eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci.
‘peyark kiḷavi mūvaki niliya’ eṉavē peyarallāta iraṇṭīṟṟu viṉiccollum iṭiccollum uriccollum iyalpum mikutiyumākiya iruvaki niliyavām. ollikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatu aikāra īṟṟu viṉiccoṉ mikuti. ‘iṉi yaṇi’ (eḻut. 236) yeṉpataṉkaṇ ikarīṟṟu viṉiyeccam eṭuttōtupa. ivaṟṟiṟku iyalpu vantuḻik kāṇka. ‘ceṉmati pāka’ iḵtu ikara īṟṟu iṭiccolliyalpu. mikuti vantuḻik kāṇka. ‘tillic collē’ (col. 253) iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu iṭiccoṉmikuti. iyalpu vantuḻik kāṇka. ‘kaṭikā’ iḵtu ikara īṟṟu uriccolliyalpu. mikuti vantuḻik kāṇka. paṇittōḷ iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu uriccoṉ mikuti. iyalpu vantuḻik kāṇka.	(16)
ikara aikāra īṟṟu iṭiccol muṭipu
159.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya ikara iṟutiyum
	ekaramutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟutiyum
	cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
	yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
	valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻā kunavuñ
	colliyal maruṅkiṉ uḷaveṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷuṇartti niṉṟa ikara aikāra īṟṟu iṭiccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) colliyal maruṅkiṉ - ikara aikāraṅkaṭku muṉṉark kūṟiya mūvaki ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ iyalpi nīkkic collumiṭattu, cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya avvikara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, ekara viṉāviṉ mutal ikara iṟutiyum - ekaramākiya viṉāviṉi mutalākavuṭiya avvikara īṟṟu iṭic collum, cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum - cuṭṭākiya uṟup peḻuttu nīṇṭa avvikāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum - yāveṉ viṉāviṉi mutaṟkaṇuṭiya avvikāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, valleḻuttu mikunavum - val leḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavum - uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum, uḷaveṉa moḻipa - uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, itōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, utōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, etōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; āṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ, īṇṭik koṇṭāṉ ūṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ, yāṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum ivi mikkaṉa. atōḷi avviṭameṉṉum poruṭṭu. avvaḻikoṇṭāṉ avvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ, ivvaḻikoṇṭāṉ ivvaḻik koṇṭāṉ, uvvaḻikoṇṭāṉ uvvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ, evvaḻikoṇṭāṉ  evvaḻik koṇṭāṉ eṉa uṟaḻntaṉa.
cuṭṭucciṉi nīṇṭataṟkum yā viṉāviṟkum varum aikāra īṟṟukku utāraṇam akkālattu āyiṭikoṇṭāṉ āyiṭik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum vaḻaṅkiṟṟuppōlum.
iṉi āṅkavikoṇṭāṉ āṅkavikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉa kāṭṭuvārum uḷar. avi tiripuṭiyaṉavām.
‘colliyal’ eṉṟataṉālē piṟa aikāra īṟu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka. aṉṟik kūttar, paṇṭic cāṉṟōreṉavum, orutiṅkaḷikkuḻavi, orunāḷikkuḻavi eṉavum varum.	(17)
neṭilmuṉṉarum taṉikkuṟilmuṉṉarum
nilimoḻiyīṟṟumey tirital
160.	neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟumey keṭutalum
	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuruvu iraṭṭalum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.
itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatoru nilimoḻik karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu mey keṭutalum - neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu keṭutalum, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉ uruvu iraṭṭalum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa oṟṟuttaṉ vaṭivu iraṭṭittalum, aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiyal eṉpa - aṟiyumpaṭi vanta aṭippāṭṭiya leṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
iṅṅaṉam neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭuvaṉa: ṇakāramum, ṉakāramum, makāramum, lakāramum, ḷakāramum eṉa aivakiyavām.
e -ṭu:	kōṇimirntatu, tāṉallaṉ, tānallar, vēṉaṉṟu, tōṇaṉṟu eṉa nakaram varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭṭatu. kōṟītu, vēṟītu eṉat takaram varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ lakāravoṟṟuk keṭṭatu. ēṉiya vantuḻik kāṇka.
ivaṟṟi ‘laṉaveṉa varūum’ (eḻut. 149) ‘ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ’ (eḻut. 150) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭittuk keṭumāṟu kāṇka.
oṟṟiraṭṭuvaṉa, ñakāra nakāra rakāra ḻakāram oḻintaṉa. kaṇṇaḻakitu, poṉṉakal, tammāṭi, collaḻakitu, eḷḷaḻakitu, neyyakal, tevvalaṉ eṉak kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru iraṭṭiṉa.
mēlic cūttirattu nāṉkaṉurupu piṟkūṟiyataṉāṉ, oṟṟiraṭṭutal uyir mutaṉmoḻikkaṇṇateṉṟu uṇarka.
kuṟiyatu piṟkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ tammi emmi niṉṉi eṉa neṭiyaṉa kuṟuki niṉṟavaḻiyuṅ kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāy iraṭṭutalum, viraṉaṉṟu kuṟaṇimirntatu eṉak kuṟiliṇiyiṉ muṉṉar vanta oṟṟukkeṭutalum, varāṟītu naṉṟu eṉak kuṟiṉeṭiṟkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟukkeṭutalum, ‘atukoṟōḻi’ (kuṟun. 5) eṉavuṅ kuriciṟīyaṉ eṉavun toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟu niṉṟa oṟṟuk keṭutalum, iṭiccollōṭu oṭṭuppaṭṭu niṟṟalum, kāṟṟītu eṉavum viraṟṟītu eṉavum oṟṟu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.
iṉi ‘aṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ tēṉṟītu nāṇṭītu, tēṉaṉmi nāṇaṉmi eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa keṭāmi niṟṟaluṅ keṭutalum takara nakaraṅkaḷ vantuḻi yeṉpatūuṅ koḷka.
‘neṟiyiyal’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, cuṭṭiṉmuṉ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi avvaṭi avvāṭi eṉa, iṭi vakaravoṟṟu ilvaḻiyum iraṭṭutal koḷka.	(18)
mēlik kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭāta iṭaṉ,
avvīṟu akaram peṟutal
161.	āṟaṉ urupiṉum nāṉkaṉ urupiṉum
	kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lilli
	īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum
	neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa.
iḵtu urupiyali nōkkiyatoru nilimoḻikkaruvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻimuṉ āṉa - neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttuk kuṟuki muṭiyum aṟuvakippaṭṭa moḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta, āṟaṉurupiṉum nāṉkaṉurupiṉum - āṟām vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum nāṉkām vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum, kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭalilli - muṉṉar nilimoḻikku iraṭṭumeṉṟa kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭi varutalilli; īṟākupuḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum - nilimoḻi yīṟṟukkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟukkaḷ akarampeṟṟu niṟkum e-ṟu.
urupiyalil, ‘nīyeṉorupeyar’ (eḻut. 179) eṉavum, ‘tāmnā meṉṉum’ (eḻut. 188) eṉavum, ‘tāṉyāṉeṉṉum’ (eḻut. 192) eṉavuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāṟ kuṟuki oṟṟiraṭṭit tammi nammi emmi taṉṉi niṉṉi eṉṉi eṉa varuvaṉa, itaṉāṉē tamatu namatu ematu taṉatu niṉatu eṉatu eṉavum; tamakku namakku emakku taṉakku niṉakku eṉakku eṉavum oṟṟiraṭṭātu akaram peṟṟuvantaṉa. nāṉkāvataṟku oṟṟumikutal ‘valleḻuttu mutaliya’ (eḻut. 114) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. āṟaṉurupākiya akaram ēṟi muṭiyāmikkuk kāraṇam ‘āṟaṉurupi ṉakarakkiḷavi’           (eḻut. 115) eṉpuḻik kūṟiṉām. oṟṟiraṭṭāmiyum akarappēṟum, iraṇṭaṟkum otta viti yeṉṟu uṇarka.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ neṭumutal kuṟukāta tam nam num eṉa varuñ cāriyikaṭkum ivviru vitiyuṅ koḷka. ellār tamakkum, ellā namakkum, ellīr numakkum, ellār tamatum, ellā namatum, ellīr numatum eṉa varum.	(19)
‘num’ iṟutiyum aṟṟātal
162.	nummeṉ iṟutiyum annili tiriyātu.
itu neṭumutal kuṟukāta nummeṉkiṉṟatum avviti peṟumeṉ kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nummeṉ iṟutiyum - neṭumutal kuṟukā nummeṉṉum makaravīṟum, annili tiriyātu - muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭāmiyum īṟākupuḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyalum eytum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	numatu numakku eṉa varum.	(20)
yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi, nilimoḻi yīṟu ukaram peṟāmi
163.	ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
	yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟki.
itu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatoru nilimoḻic ceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti - ukarap pēṟṟoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ, yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟki - yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu avvukaram peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
avvīṟukaḷāvaṉa, puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ ukaram peṟumeṉṟu vitikkum pala īṟukaḷumeṉṟu koḷka. uriñ yāṉā aṉantā ātā ilakā īntā uḻuntā ūrā eyiṉā ēṟā aiyā oḻukkā ōtā auviyā eṉavum, uriñyātu aḻaku eṉavum oṭṭuka. ēṉip puḷḷikaḷōṭum ēṟpaṉa aṟintu oṭṭuka.
‘ñakāriyoṟṟiya’ (eḻut. 296) eṉpataṉāṉum, ‘ñanamava viyiyiṉum’ (eḻut. 297) eṉpataṉāṉum yakaramum uyirum vantāl ukaram peṟātu iyalpāmeṉpatu peṟutaliṉ īṇṭu vilakkal vēṇṭā veṉiṉ, eṭuttōttilvaḻiyatē uyttuṇarcci yeṉṟu koḷka.
itu mutalāka alvaḻi kūṟukiṉṟār.	(21)
aḷavu niṟi eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu
164.	uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yāki
	aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇuñ cuṭṭi
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa ellāc collum
	tattaṅ kiḷavi tammakap paṭṭa
	mutti varūuṅ kālan tōṉṟiṉ
	otta teṉpa ēyeṉ cāriyi.
iḵtu aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇumākiya peyarkaḷ tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki - uyirum puḷḷiyun tamakku īṟāy, aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇuñ cuṭṭi uḷaveṉappaṭṭa ellāc collum - aḷaviyum niṟiyiyum eṇṇiyuṅ karuti varuvaṉa uḷaveṉṟu āciriyar kūṟappaṭṭa ellāc coṟkaḷum, tattaṅ kiḷavi tammakappaṭṭa - tattamakku iṉamākiya coṟkaḷāyt tammiṟ kuṟinta coṟkaḷ, mutti varūuṅ kālan tōṉṟiṉ - tam muṉṉē varuṅ kālan tōṉṟumāyiṉ, ēyeṉ cāriyi ottatu eṉpa - tām ēyeṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭital poruntiṟṟeṉpar āciriyar e-ṟu.
‘munti’ mutti yeṉa vikāram.
e-ṭu:	nāḻiyēyāḻākku, uḻakkēyāḻākku, kalaṉēpatakku eṉa aḷavuppeyar ēkāram peṟṟut tammuṉṉart tammiṟ kuṟintaṉa vantaṉa. toṭiyēkaḵcu, kaḻañcēkuṉṟi, koḷḷēyiyavi eṉa niṟippeyar ēkāram peṟṟut tammuṉṉart tammiṟ kuṟintaṉa vantaṉa. oṉṟēkāl, kālēkāṇi, kāṇiyēmuntiriki eṉa eṇṇuppeyar ēkāram peṟṟut tammuṉṉart tammiṟ kuṟintaṉa vantaṉa.
atikārampaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu muṟkūṟātataṉāṉē kuṟuṇi nāṉāḻi, ainnāḻiyuḻakku eṉa ēkāramiṉṟi varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka.	(22)
‘ari’ varuvaḻi nilimoḻi cāriyi peṟāmi
165.	ariyeṉa varūum pālvari kiḷavikkup
	puriva taṉṟāṟ cāriyi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ariyeṉa varūum pālvari kiḷavikku - ammūvakic coṉ muṉṉar varum ari yeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa poruṭkūṟṟi uṇaraniṉṟa colliṟku, cāriyiyiyaṟki purivataṉṟu - ēyeṉ cāriyi peṟuntaṉmi poruntuvataṉṟu e-ṟu.
āl - aci.
e-ṭu:	uḻakkari, ceviṭṭari, mūvuḻakkari eṉavum; kaḵcari, kaḻañcari, toṭiyari, koḷḷari eṉavum; oṉṟari, pattari eṉavum ivi ēyeṉ cāriyi peṟāvāy vantaṉa.
‘purivataṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṟ kalavari yeṉpataṉi oṟṟuk keṭuttuc ceyki ceytu muṭikka. itaṉāṉē ceviṭṭari yeṉpuḻi ṭakaravoṟṟu mikutaluṅ koḷka.
‘oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku’ (eḻut. 132) aṉmiyiṟ cāriyi peṟāvāyiṉa eṉṟālōveṉiṉ, avi peṟṟum peṟātum varuvaṉavaṟ ṟiṟkuk kūṟiyatākalāṉum, itu ‘tammakap paṭṭa’ (eḻut. 164) eṉa varintōtiṉamiyāṉum vilakkal vēṇṭiṟṟu.	(23)
‘kuṟi’ varuvaḻi vēṟṟumimuṭipu peṟutal
166.	kuṟiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kāli
	niṟiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu; ēyeṉ cāriyi vilakki vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉoṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kāli - kuṟiyeṉṉuñ col aḷavuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmuṉ varuṅkālattiṟku, vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki - vēṟṟumip puṇarcci muṭipiṟku urittākak kūṟuntaṉmi, niṟiyat tōṉṟum - nirampat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	urikkuṟi kalakkuṟi eṉavum, toṭikkuṟi koṭkuṟi eṉavum, kāṇikkuṟi kāṟkuṟi eṉavum varum.
urinelluṅ kuṟinellum eṉka.
‘vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki’ eṉavē ivi vēṟṟumi yallavāyiṉa. eṉavē, urikkuṟi yeṉpataṟku uriyum uḻakkumeṉap poruḷāyiṟṟu. ēṉiyavum aṉṉa. ‘muṉvaru kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉē kalappayaṟu, kalappāku eṉṟāṟpōlap poruṭpeyaroṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum ivvēṟṟumi muṭipu eytuvikka. pāku eṉṟatu pākkiṉi.
‘niṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉē urikkūṟu, toṭikkūṟu, kāṇikkūṟu eṉak kūṟeṉṟataṟkum immuṭipu eytuvikka.	(24)
kuṟṟukara īṟu ‘kuṟi’ varuvaḻi iṉcāriyi peṟutal
167.	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṉṉē cāriyi.
itu vēṟṟumi muṭipu vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukarattuc cāriyi - kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu aḷavup peyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟiyeṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumvaḻi varuñ cāriyi, iṉṉē - iṉ cāriyiyēyām e-ṟu.
‘kuṟṟiyalukarattu’ itaṟku attu vitittu muṭikka. ‘kuṟṟiya lukarak kiṉṉē’ eṉpatum pāṭam.
e-ṭu:	uḻakkiṉkuṟi āḻākkiṉkuṟi eṉavum, kaḻañciṉkuṟi kaḵciṉkuṟi eṉavum, oṉṟiṉkuṟi pattiṉkuṟi eṉavum varum.
itaṟku uḻakkuṅkuṟiyum eṉpatu poruḷ. itu vēṟṟumik kaṇṇāyiṉ uḻakkiṟkuṟiyeṉa niṟkum.	(25)
‘kalam’ attup peṟutal
168.	attiṭi varūuṅ kalameṉ aḷavē.
ituvum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu, vēṟṟumi vitivilakki attu vakuttaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) kalameṉ aḷavē - kalameṉṉum aḷavuppeyar kuṟi yoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, attu iṭi varūum - attuc cāriyi iṭi vantu puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalattuk kuṟi eṉa varum.
itaṉi ‘attē vaṟṟē’ (eḻut. 33) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. itaṟku kalamuṅ kuṟiyum eṉpatu poruḷ.
cāriyi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉē, muṉ iṉcāriyi kalam peṟṟavaḻi valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(26)
‘paṉi’yum ‘kā’vum ‘iṉ’ peṟutal
169.	paṉiyeṉ aḷavuṅ kāveṉ niṟiyum
	niṉiyuṅ kāli iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
ituvum atu, vēṟṟumiviti vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) paṉiyeṉ aḷavuṅ kāveṉ niṟiyum - paṉiyeṉṉum aḷavuppeyarum kāveṉṉum niṟippeyaruṅ kuṟiyeṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, niṉiyuṅkāli iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - ārāyuṅkālattu iṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉiyiṉkuṟi, kāviṉkuṟi eṉa varum.
iviyum ummittoki.
‘niṉiyuṅkāli’ eṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumikku uriya vitiyeyti valleḻuttup peṟutaluñ ciṟupāṉmi koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paṉikkuṟi, kākkuṟi eṉa varum.
ittuṇiyum alvaḻi muṭipu. ivaṟṟi ‘vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi iai’ (eḻut. 158) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟātu vēṟōtiṉār, ivi aḷavum, niṟiyum, eṇṇumātaliṉ.	(27)
aḷavu niṟip peyarkaḷukku moḻimutalām eḻuttukkaḷ
170.	aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻimuta lāki
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa oṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē
	avitām
	kacatapa eṉṟā namava eṉṟā
	akara ukaramō ṭaviyeṉa moḻipa.
itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ, aḷaviṟkum niṟikkum moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu iṉiya eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻi mutalāki uḷaveṉap paṭṭa oṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē - aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum moḻikku mutalāyuḷḷaṉaveṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa oṉpateḻuttukkaḷ; avitām kacatapa eṉṟā namava eṉṟā akaravukaramōṭu aviyeṉa moḻipa-avveḻuttukkaḷtām kacatapakkaḷum namavakkaḷum akara ukaramumākiya aviyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku: ivi aḷavu. kaḻañcu, cīrakam, toṭi, palam, niṟi, mā, vari, anti: ivi niṟi. niṟikku ukara mutaṟpeyar vantuḻik kāṇka.
iṉi, ‘uḷaveṉappaṭṭa’ eṉṟataṉāṉē uḷaveṉappaṭātaṉavum uḷa. avi, immi ōraṭi iṭā eṉa variyaṟi kūṟātaṉavuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, tēyavaḻakkāy oruñār, orutuvali eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka.
iṅṅaṉam variyaṟi kūṟiṉār, akattōttiṉuḷ muṭipu kūṟiyavaḻi atikārattāṉ vaṉkaṇattiṉmēṟ cellātu oḻinta kaṇattiṉuñ cellumeṉṟaṟku. eṇṇuppeyar variyaṟi yiṉmiyiṟ kūṟārāyiṉār.	(28)
ivvōttup puṟaṉaṭi
171.	īṟiyal maruṅkiṉ iviyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
	kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellām
	meyttalip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
	ottavi yuriya puṇarmoḻi niliyē.
iḵtu ivvōttiṟkup puṟaṉaṭi; eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcā ṉum muṭiyātaṉavaṟṟiṟku ituvē ōttākaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) īṟu iyal maruṅkiṉ- uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya coṟkaḷ varumoḻiyoṭu kūṭi naṭakkumiṭattu, ivaṟṟu iyalpu  iviyeṉak kūṟiya kiḷavip pallāṟellām - im moḻikaḷiṉ muṭipu iviyeṉak kūṟi muṭikkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉuṭiya avvāṟṟāṉ muṭiyātuniṉṟa palavaki muṭipukaḷellām, meyt talippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi-uṇmiyit talippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu kūṭi, puṇarmoḻinili ottavi uriya - puṇarum moḻikaḷiṉ nilimikkaṭ poruntiṉavi uriyavām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	naṭañeḷḷā eṉa uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷavi meṉkaṇattōṭu iyalpāy muṭintatu. maṇṇukoṟṟā maṇṇukkoṟṟā, maṉṉukoṟṟā maṉṉukkoṟṟā, uḷḷukoṟṟā uḷḷukkoṟṟā, kollukoṟṟā kollukkoṟṟā eṉpaṉa puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉilikkiḷavi ukaram peṟṟum uṟaḻntum muṭintaṉa.
uriñuñeḷḷā : iḵtu ‘auveṉa varūum’ (eḻut. 152) eṉpataṉ oḻipu. patakkanāṉāḻi, patakkamunnāḻi eṉa ivi ēyeṉcāriyi peṟātu akkup peṟṟu ataṉ iṟuti meymmiciyoṭuṅ keṭṭup puṇarntaṉa. vāṭṭāṉi tōṟṟaṇṭi eṉpaṉa takaram vantuḻit tirintu neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟuk keṭātu niṉṟaṉa. cīrakari eṉpataṉic cīrakam ari yeṉa niṟuttik kakara voṟṟiṉ mēlēṟiṉa akarattiyum makaravoṟṟi yuṅ keṭuttu ari yeṉpataṉ akaratti yēṟṟi muṭikka. itu niṟip peyar. oru māvari yeṉpataṉi oru mā ari eṉa niṟutti, varumoḻi akaraṅ keṭuttu orumāriyeṉa muṭikka. kalavari yeṉpataṉik kalari eṉa muṭikka. akara makaraṅ keṭuttu ‘nākaṇi’ (cīvaka. 287) yeṉap piṟavum varuvaṉavellām ic cūttirattāṉ muṭikka.	(29)
yāvar, yātu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ marūu muṭipu
172.	palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum
	peyariṭi vakaraṅ keṭutalum ēṉi
	oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉāviṭi
	oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum iraṇṭum
	maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ tiriyumaṉ payiṉṟē.
itu marūuccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāvareṉṉum peyariṭi vakaraṅ keṭutalum - palari yaṟiyum avar mutaliya colliṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāvareṉṉum peyar iṭiyil vakaraṅ keṭutalum, ēṉi oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉā iṭi oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum - oḻinta oṉṟaṉi aṟiyum atu mutaliya colliṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāteṉṉum viṉāccol iṭiyilē uyiroṭu poruntiya vakaram varutalum, iraṇṭum - ākiya avi yiraṇṭum, maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ maṉ payiṉṟu tiriyum - marumuṭipiṉ pakutiyiṭattu mikavum payiṉṟu tiriyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avar yār eṉavum, atu yāvatu eṉavum varum.
avar yāvareṉpatu vakaraṅ keṭṭu avar yāreṉa niṉṟavaḻi, ‘yāa reṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi’ (col. 210) eṉṟa viṉiyiyaluṭ kūṟum viṉikkuṟippuc collām piṟaveṉiṉ, ākātu; avvakaraṅ keṭṭā lum īṇṭu yāvareṉṉum peyart taṉmiyāyē niṟṟaliṉ. atu peṟṟavā ṟeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, īṇṭup palaraṟi coṉmuṉ vanta yāvareṉpataṉ vakaram keṭumeṉavē, ēṉi avaṉ avaḷ eṉṉum irupāl muṉṉum yāvareṉpatu vārāteṉṟum atu tirintu maruvāy niṟku meṉṟuṅ kūṟutalāṉum, ‘yāvareṉṉum peyariṭi’ eṉpataṉāṉum peṟṟām. itaṉāṉē avaṉyāvar, avaḷyāvar eṉṟāṟ pālvaḻuvā meṉpatu peṟṟām. itaṉi ‘yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāvareṉṉu, māvayiṉ mūṉṟōṭu’ (col. 162) eṉap peyarāka ōtiyavāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. aṉṟiyum yāreṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi muppāṟkum uritteṉṟu yāreṉṉum viṉā viṉikkuṟippiṉi avaṉyār, avaḷyār, avaryār eṉa muppāṟkum oppa urimi kūṟutalāṉum atu vēṟeṉpatu peṟṟām. atu viṉiyiyaluḷ ōtiṉamiyāṉum ‘viṉāviṟkēṟṟal’ (col. 66) eṉap payaṉiliyāka ōtiṉamiyāṉum vēṟāyiṟṟu.
iṉi ‘yāryārk kaṇṭē yuvappar’ eṉap palaraṟi col muṉṉaraṉṟi iyalpāka vanta yāreṉpatu yāṇṭu aṭaṅkumeṉiṉ, atuvum yāri yārikkaṇṭeṉa urupu viritaliṉ yāvari eṉṉum vakaraṅkeṭṭa peyarēyām. aṅṅaṉam nilimoḻi varumoḻiyāy niṟṟal ‘payiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. itaṉāṉē, ‘yāvatu naṉṟeṉa vuṇarār māṭṭum’ (kuṟun. 78) eṉa ēṉi oṉṟaṟicollum nilimoḻiyāy niṟṟal koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, yāravar, yāvatatu eṉa ivviru collum nilimoḻiyāy varutal koḷka.	(30)
tokimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.

6
urupiyal
iṉṉa īṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉar vēṟṟumiyurupu
iṉcāriyi peṟum eṉal
173.	aā uū ēau eṉṉum
	appā lāṟaṉ nilimoḻi muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ kiṉṉē cāriyi
eṉpatu cūttiram.
urupukaḷoṭu peyar puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, ivvōttu ‘urupiyal’ eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēl tokuttup puṇarttataṉi īṇṭu virittup puṇarkkiṉṟārākaliṉ, ivvōttut tokimarapōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu.
iccūttiram akara mutaliya īṟṟāṉ varum āṟu īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ niṉṟu iṉ peṟṟu urupiṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa varum puṇarcci mēṟkūṟupa.
(i-ḷ.) a ā u ū ē au eṉṉum appāl āṟaṉ nilimoḻi muṉṉar - a ā u ū ē au eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa akkūṟṟu āṟaṉiyum īṟākavuṭiya nilimoḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa, vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku iṉṉē cāriyi - vēṟṟumiyurupukaṭku iṭiyē varuñ cāriyi iṉ cāriyiyē e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaviṉi viḷaviṉoṭu viḷaviṟku viḷaviṉatu viḷaviṉkaṇ eṉavum, palāviṉi palāviṉoṭu palāviṟku palāviṉatu palāviṉkaṇ eṉavum, kaṭuviṉi kaṭuviṉoṭu kaṭuviṟku kaṭuviṉatu kaṭuviṉkaṇ eṉavum, taḻūuviṉi taḻūuviṉoṭu taḻūuviṟku taḻūuviṉatu taḻūuviṉkaṇ eṉavum, cēviṉi cēviṉoṭu cēviṟku cēviṉatu cēviṉkaṇ eṉavum, vuviṉi vuviṉoṭu vuviṟku vuviṉatu vuviṉkaṇ eṉavum varum. ivvāṟē ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
‘iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ(ku)
iṉṉeṉ cāriyi yiṉmi vēṇṭum’	(eḻut. 131)
eṉavē, ēṉiya iṉ peṟumeṉṟaliṉ, ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) eṉpataṉāṉ iyalpeṉṟatu vilakkiṟṟām.	(1)
urupupuṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟuc cāriyi peṟum peyarkaḷ
174.	pallavi nutaliya akara iṟupeyar
	vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal ecca miṉṟē.
iḵtu iṉcāriyi vilakki vaṟṟu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) pallavi nutaliya peyariṟu akaram-paṉmip poruḷik karutiṉa peyarkaḷiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa akaram, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal eccamiṉṟu-vaṟṟuc cāriyiyoṭu poruntutali oḻitalilli e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pallavaṟṟi pallavaṟṟoṭu, palavaṟṟi palavaṟṟoṭu, cillavaṟṟi cillavaṟṟoṭu, cilavaṟṟi cilavaṟṟoṭu, uḷḷavaṟṟi uḷḷavaṟṟoṭu, illavaṟṟi illavaṟ ṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘eccamiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, mēl iṉ peṟṟaṉa piṟa cāriyiyum peṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	nilātti, tulātti, makatti eṉa varum.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, pallavi nutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ mūṉṟāmurupu vaṟṟup peṟṟē muṭital koḷka.	(2)
‘yā’ viṉāppeyarum vaṟṟup peṟutal
175. yāveṉ viṉāvum āyiyal tiriyātu.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) yāveṉ viṉāvum - yāveṉṟu collappaṭum ākāra īṟṟu viṉāppeyarum, āyiyal tiriyātu - muṟkūṟiya vaṟṟup pēṟṟiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāvaṟṟi yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	(3)
cuṭṭumutal ukaram aṉcāriyi peṟṟumuṭital
176.	cuṭṭumuta lukaram aṉṉoṭu civaṇi
	oṭṭiya meyyoḻit tukaraṅ keṭumē.
iḵtu ukara īṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal ukaram aṉṉoṭu civaṇi - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara īṟṟuccol aṉ cāriyiyoṭu porunti, oṭṭiya mey oḻittu ukaraṅ keṭum - tāṉ poruntiya meyyi niṟutti ukaraṅ keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ataṉi ataṉoṭu, itaṉi itaṉoṭu, utaṉi utaṉoṭu eṉa varum.
atiṉi atiṉoṭu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa marūu muṭippuḻi muṭintaṉa.
‘oṭṭiya’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, cuṭṭumutal ukaramaṉṟip piṟa ukaramum uyir varuvaḻik keṭuvaṉa koḷka. avi katavu, kaḷavu, kaṉavu eṉa niṟutti, aḻakitu illi eṉa varuvittu ukaraṅ keṭuttu muṭikka.
ivaṟṟi vakara īṟākki ukaram peṟṟaṉaveṉṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ, vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇuñ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payiṉṟu varum vakara īṟukaḷi oḻittu āciriyar ‘vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu’ (eḻut. 81) eṉṟāṟ pōla varintōtal kuṉṟak kūṟalām. ātaliṉ, avi ukara īṟeṉṟē koḷka. avi, celavu varavu taravu uṇavu kaṉavu eṉa vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum, ‘puṉka ṇuṭittāṟ puṇarvu’ (kuṟaḷ. 1152), ‘pāṭaṟiyā tāṉi yiravu,’ ‘kaṇṇārak kāṇak katavu’ (muttoḷ. 42) eṉac ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payiṉṟu varumāṟu uṇarka.	(4)
cuṭṭumutal aikāram vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭital
177.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya aiyeṉ iṟuti
	vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittē.
iḵtu aikāra īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya aiye ṉiṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalāka vuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuccol, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittu - vaṟṟuc cāriyiyoṭu porunti niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
ummiyāṉ vaṟṟoṭu cila urupiṉkaṇ iṉ cāriyi peṟṟu niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aviyaṟṟi aviyaṟṟoṭu, iviyaṟṟi iviyaṟ ṟoṭu, uviyaṟṟi uviyaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. iṅṅaṉam aikāram niṟka, vaṟṟu vantuḻi ‘vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa’ (eḻut. 122) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka.
iṉi, ummiyāṉ aviyaṟṟiṟku, aviyaṟṟiṉkaṇ eṉa nāṉkāvatum ēḻāvatum, vaṟṟum iṉṉum peṟṟu vantavāṟu kāṇka.
iṉi, ‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṟ ‘pallavi nutaliya akara’ (eḻut. 174) īṟṟiṟkum ivviraṇṭu urupiṉkaṇ vaṟṟum iṉṉum koṭuttup palavaṟṟiṟku, palavaṟṟiṉkaṇ eṉa muṭikka. itumēl varuvaṉa vaṟṟiṟkum okkum.	(5)
‘yāvi’ vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyumāṟu
178.	yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
	āyiyal tiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar
	āvayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum - yāveṉṉum viṉāviṉiyuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuc collum, āyiyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar-muṟkūṟiya cuṭṭu mutal aikāram pōl vaṟṟuppeṟum avviyalpiṉ tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; āvayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭuṅ keṭum-avvīṟṟiṭattu vakaram aikārattoṭu kūṭak keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāvaṟṟi, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
vakaram vaṟṟumici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuvataṉik kēṭu ōtiya mikiyāṉē, piṟa aikāramum vaṟṟuppeṟutal koḷka. kariyavaṟṟi kariyavaṟṟoṭu, neṭiyavaṟṟi neṭiyavaṟṟoṭu, kuṟiyavaṟṟi kuṟiyavaṟṟoṭu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivi karumi neṭumi kuṟumi eṉṉum paṇpuppeyaraṉṟik kariyavi neṭiyavi kuṟiyavi eṉap paṇpukoḷ peyarāy niṟṟaliṉ, vakara aikāram keṭuttu vaṟṟuccāriyi koṭuttu muṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi ‘aimpālaṟiyum paṇpu tokumoḻi’ (eḻut. 482) aṉmi uṇarka.	(6)
nī ‘niṉ’ āki urupēṟṟal
179.	nīyeṉ orupeyar neṭumutal kuṟukum
	āvayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟā kummē.
iḵtu īkāra īṟu iṉṉavāṟu puṇarumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ oru peyar neṭu mutal kuṟukum - nīyeṉṉum oru peyar taṉmēl niṉṟa neṭiyatākiya īkāraṅ kuṟuki ikaramām; āvayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟākum - avviṭattu varum ṉakaram oṟṟāy niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	niṉṉi niṉṉoṭu niṉakku eṉac ceykiyaṟintu oṭṭuka.
niṉakku eṉpataṟku, ‘āṟa ṉurupiṉum nāṉka ṉurupiṉum’ (eḻut. 161) ‘valleḻuttu mutaliya’ (eḻut. 114) eṉpaṉa koṇarntu muṭikka. niṉatu eṉpataṟku, ‘āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi’ (eḻut. 115) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. niṉ eṉpatu nī eṉpataṉ vēṟoru peyarō eṉak karututali vilakkutaṟku ‘oru peyar’ eṉṟār. ‘peyar kuṟukum’ eṉṉātu ‘mutal kuṟukum’ eṉṟatu, appeyariṉ eḻuttiṉkaṇṇatu kuṟukkameṉṟaṟku. ‘neṭumutal’ eṉavē nakaraṅkuṟukutali vilakkiṟṟu. uyirmey yoṟṟumi paṟṟi neṭiyatu mutalāyiṟṟu.
‘uṭimiyu miṉmiyu moṭuvayi ṉokkum’ (eḻut. 123) eṉṟataṉi nōkki oṭuviṭattuc cāriyi peṟṟē vanta atikāratti māṟṟutaṟkuc cāriyip pēṟṟiṭi eḻuttup pēṟu kūṟiṉār.	(7)
ōkāra īṟu oṉcāriyi peṟutal
180.	ōkāra iṟutik(ku) oṉṉē cāriyi.
iḵtu ōkāra īṟu iṉṉavāṟu puṇarumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ōkāra iṟutikku oṉṉē cāriyi-ōkāra īṟṟiṟku iṭivaruñ cāriyi oṉcāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōoṉi, kōoṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘oṉṉē’ eṉṟa ēkārattāṟ perumpāṉmiyāka varuñ cāriyi oṉṉē; ciṟupāṉmi iṉ cāriyi varumeṉṟu koḷka. ‘oṉṟātu niṉṟa kōviṉi yaṭarkka vantu’ (cīvaka. 316) eṉavum, kōviṉi kōviṉoṭu, cōviṉi cōviṉoṭu, ōviṉi ōviṉoṭu eṉavum varum.
ō eṉpatu matakunīr tāṅkum palaki.	(8)
a ā īṟṟu marappeyar attup peṟumiṭaṉ
181.	aā eṉṉum marappeyark kiḷavikku
	attoṭuñ civaṇum ēḻa ṉurupē.
iḵtu akara ākāra īṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) a ā eṉṉum marappeyark kiḷavikku - aā veṉṟu collappaṭum maratti uṇarttukiṉṟa peyarākiya colliṟku, ēḻaṉurupu attoṭuñ civaṇum - ēḻāmurupu iṉṉōṭaṉṟi attōṭum poruntum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷavattukkaṇ, palāvattukkaṇ eṉavarum.
‘valleḻuttu mutaliya’ (eḻut. 114) eṉpataṉāṉ valleḻuttuk koṭuttut ‘teṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē’ (eḻut. 133) eṉpataṉāṉ ‘attiṉakaram akaramuṉi’k (eḻut. 125) keṭāmic ceyki ceytu muṭikka.	(9)
ña na - īṟukaḷ iṉcāriyi peṟutal
182.	ñaneṉ puḷḷikku iṉṉē cāriyi.
itu puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ ñakara īṟum nakara īṟum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ñana eṉ puḷḷikku iṉṉē cāriyi - ñanaveṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟukaṭku varuñcāriyi iṉ cāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñiṉi uriñiṉoṭu, poruniṉi poruniṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(10)
cuṭṭumutal vakarīṟu vaṟṟup peṟutal
183.	cuṭṭumutal vakaram aiyum meyyuṅ
	keṭṭa iṟuti yiyaltiri piṉṟē.
itu nāṉku moḻikku īṟām vakara īṟṟuṭ cuṭṭumutal vakara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal vakaram-av iv uv eṉṉuñ cuṭṭeḻut tiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vakara īṟṟuc col, aiyum meyyuṅ keṭṭa iṟuti yiyal tiripiṉṟu-aikāramum aikārattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyuṅ keṭṭu vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭinta ‘yāvi’ (eḻut. 178) eṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuc colliyalpiṉ tiripiṉṟi vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avaṟṟi avaṟṟoṭu, ivaṟṟi ivaṟṟoṭu, uvaṟṟi uvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(11)
‘tev’ iṉcāriyi peṟutal
184.	ēṉi vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu eytātatu eytuvittatu; peyarkkē yaṉṟi uriccol vakarattiṟku muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - oḻinta uriccol vakaram iṉcāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tevviṉi, tevviṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
iḵtu uriccollāyiṉum paṭuttalōciyāṟ peyarāyiṟṟu. 	(12)
makara īṟṟup peyar attup peṟutal
185.	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ attē cāriyi.
itu makara īṟu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ attē cāriyi - makaramākiya puḷḷiyīṟṟuc coṉmuṉ varuñ cāriyi attuc cāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maratti marattoṭu, nukatti nukattoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘attē vaṟṟē’ (eḻut. 133) ‘atti ṉakaram’ (eḻut. 125) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭikka.	(13)
avaṟṟuṭ cila iṉcāriyi peṟutalum uḷa eṉal
186.	iṉṉiṭi varūum moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
itu makara īṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṉiṭi varūum moḻiyumā ruḷa - makara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷuḷ attē yaṉṟi iṉcāriyi iṭiyē vantu muṭiyuñ coṟkaḷum uḷa e-ṟu.
ār: aci.
e-ṭu:	urumiṉi urumiṉoṭu, tirumiṉi tirumiṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(14)
‘num’ īṟu cāriyi peṟāmi
187.	nummeṉ iṟuti iyaṟki yākum.
itu makara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nummeṉ iṟuti iyaṟki yākum - nummeṉṉum makara īṟu mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāka muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nummi, nummoṭu, numakku, nummiṉ, numatu, nuṅkaṇ eṉavarum.
numakku numatu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku ‘āṟa ṉurupiṉum nāṉka ṉurupiṉum’ (eḻut. 161) ‘nummeṉ iṟutiyu mannili’ (eḻut. 162) ‘valleḻuttu mutaliya’ (eḻut. 114) ‘āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi’ (eḻut. 115) eṉpaṉa koṇarntu muṭikka.
nuṅkaṇ eṉpataṟku mēlic cūttirattu ‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ makara voṟṟuk keṭuttu, ‘valleḻuttu mutaliya’ (eḻut. 114) eṉpataṉāṉ melloṟṟuk koṭukka. ‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟār cāriyi peṟāmi karuti.(15)
tām nām yām - muṟiyē tam nam em - ātal
188.	tāmnām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum
	yāmeṉ iṟutiyum ataṉō raṉṉa
	āey yākum yāmeṉ iṟuti
	āvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum
	ēṉi yiraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum.
itu makara īṟṟiṉ muṟkūṟiya muṭipu ovvātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tām nām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum yāmeṉ iṟutiyum ataṉōraṉṉa - tām nām eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum makara īṟum yām eṉṉum makara īṟum num eṉṉum makara īṟu pōla attum iṉṉum peṟātu muṭitaliyuṭiya; yāmeṉ iṟuti ā e ākum - yā meṉṉum makara īṟṟuc colliṉ ākāram ekāramām; āvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu niṉṟa yakaramākiya mey keṭutali virumpum āciriyaṉ; ēṉi iraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum - oḻinta tām nām eṉṉum iraṇṭum neṭiyavākiya mutal kuṟukit tam nam eṉa niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tammi tammoṭu, nammi nammoṭu, emmi emmoṭu eṉa āṟu urupōṭum oṭṭuka. āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉka ṉurupiṟkuṅ karuvi yaṟintu muṭikka.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṟ kaṇvariṉ piṟavayiṉ meyyum keṭukka. taṅkaṇ naṅkaṇ eṅkaṇ eṉa ēḻaṉurupiṉkaṇ makaraṅ keṭuttu ‘valleḻuttu mutaliya’ (eḻut. 114) eṉpataṉāṉ melleḻuttuk koṭukka.	(16)
‘ellām’ vaṟṟup peṟṟu (urupiṉ piṉṉar)
‘um’moṭu iṟutal
189.	ellā meṉṉum iṟuti muṉṉar
	vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum
	ummi niliyum iṟuti yāṉa.
itu makara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā meṉṉum iṟuti muṉṉar vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum - ellā meṉṉum makarīṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉar attum iṉṉum iṉṟi vaṟṟeṉṉuñ cāriyi muṭiyat tōṉṟi muṭiyum; umminiliyum iṟutiyāṉa - āṇṭu ummeṉṉuñ cāriyi iṟutikkaṇ nilipeṟum e-ṟu.
makaram vaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉak keṭukka.
e-ṭu:	ellāvaṟṟiyum, ellāvaṟṟiṉum, ellāvaṟṟukkaṇṇum eṉavarum.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉi muṟṟukarattiṟku ummiṉ ukaraṅ keṭuttuk koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellāvaṟṟoṭum, ellāvaṟṟukkum ellāvaṟṟatum eṉa varum. muṟṟukaramātaliṉ ēṟi muṭiyātu.	(17)
iccol uyartiṇiyāyiṉ muṭiyumāṟu
190.	uyartiṇi yāyiṉ nammiṭi varumē.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇiyāyiṉ - ellāmeṉa niṉṟa makara īṟṟu viravup peyar uyartiṇip peyarāmeṉiṉ, nam iṭivarum - nam meṉṉuñ cāriyi iṭi niṉṟu puṇarum e-ṟu.
makara īṟṟiṉi mēl vaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉak keṭutta atikārat tāṟ keṭukka.
e-ṭu:	ellā nammiyum, ellā nammiṉum, ellā naṅkaṇum eṉa ukaram peṟṟum; ellā nammoṭum, ellā namakkum, ellā namatum eṉa ukaraṅ keṭṭum makaram niṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟku nam ellāriyum nam ellāroṭum eṉpatu poruḷāka oṭṭuka. itaṟku nammu vakuttatē vēṟupāṭu. ‘īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niṟṟal’ (eḻut. 161) nāṉkāvataṟkum āṟāvataṟkuṅ koḷka.	(18)
ellārum ellīrum eṉpaṉa muṭiyumāṟu
191.	ellāru meṉṉum paṭarkki yiṟutiyum
	ellīru meṉṉum muṉṉili yiṟutiyum
	oṟṟum ukaramuṅ keṭumeṉa moḻipa
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakarap puḷḷi
	ummi niliyum iṟuti yāṉa
	tammiṭi varūum paṭarkki mēṉa
	nummiṭi varūum muṉṉili moḻikkē.
itu makarīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ellāru meṉṉum paṭarkki yiṟutiyum - ellāru meṉṉum makara īṟṟu uyartiṇip paṭarkkip peyarum, ellīru meṉṉum muṉṉili yiṟutiyum - ellīru meṉṉum makara īṟṟu uyartiṇi muṉṉilippeyarum, oṟṟum ukaramuṅkeṭumeṉa moḻipa - makara voṟṟum ataṉmuṉṉiṉṟa ukaramuṅkeṭṭu muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar; rakarap puḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - avvukaram ēṟi niṉṟa rakara oṟṟukkeṭātu niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ; iṟutiyāṉa ummi niliyum-avviru moḻiyiṉiṟutikkaṇṇum ummeṉṉuñ cāriyi nilipeṟum; paṭarkki mēṉa tam iṭi varūum - paṭarkkic col liṭattut tammuccāriyi iṭivarum; muṉṉili moḻikku num iṭi varūum - muṉṉilic coṟku nummuc cāriyi iṭi varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ellārtammiyum, ellār tammiṉum, ellār taṅkaṇum eṉa ukaram peṟṟum, ellārtammoṭum, ellārtamakkum, ellār tamatum eṉa ukaraṅ keṭṭum, makaram niṟkum. ellīrnummiyum, ellīr nummiṉum, ellīrnuṅkaṇum eṉa ukaram peṟṟum, ellīrnummoṭum, ellīrnumakkum, ellīrnumatum eṉa ukaraṅ keṭṭum makaram niṟkum.
muṉṉar ‘mey’ (eḻut. 188) eṉṟa ilēcāṟ koṇṭa makarakkēṭu ivaṟṟiṟkum mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuṅ koḷka.
paṭarkkip peyar muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉē, rakara īṟṟup paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilippeyarum, makarīṟṟut taṉmip peyarum, tam num nam eṉṉuñ cāriyi iṭiyē peṟṟu iṟuti ummuccāriyiyum peṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka. kariyārtammiyum cāṉṟārtammiyum eṉavum, kariyīr nummiyum cāṉṟīrnummiyum eṉavum, kariyēmnammiyum iruvēm nammiyum eṉavum ellāvurupoṭuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
‘ukaramum oṟṟum’ eṉṉātataṉāṉ, ikkāṭṭiyavaṟṟiṟkellām mūṉṟu urupiṉkaṇṇum ummiṉ ukaraṅkeṭutal koḷka.
‘niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakarap puḷḷi’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, tammup peṟāmi varumaviyum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘ellārkku mellā nikaḻpavi’ (kuṟaḷ. 582) eṉavarum.(19)
tāṉ yāṉ - muṟiyē taṉ eṉ - ātal
192.	tāṉyāṉ eṉṉum āyī riṟutiyum
	mēṉmup peyaroṭum vēṟupā ṭilavē.
itu ṉakara īṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉ yāṉ eṉṉum āyī riṟutiyum - tāṉ yāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu ṉakara īṟum, mēl muppeyaroṭum vēṟupāṭu ilavē-mēl makara īṟṟuṭ kūṟiya mūṉṟu peyaroṭum vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tāṉeṉpatu kuṟukiyum yāṉeṉpataṉkaṇ ākāram ekaramāy yakara voṟṟuk keṭṭum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	taṉṉi eṉṉi eṉa ellāvurupōṭum oṭṭuka; ceyki yaṟintu. oṟṟiraṭṭutal ‘neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar’ (eḻut. 160) eṉpataṉuḷ ilēcāṟ koḷka.	(20)
aḻaṉ puḻaṉ - eṉpaṉa muṭiyumāṟu
193.	aḻaṉē puḻaṉē āyiru moḻikkum
	attum iṉṉum uḻaṟat tōṉṟal
	otta teṉpa uṇaru mōrē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aḻaṉē puḻaṉē āyiru moḻikkum - aḻaṉ puḻaṉ ākiya avviru moḻikkum, attum iṉṉum uṟaḻattōṉṟal otta teṉpa - attuc cāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum māṟi varat tōṉṟutalip poruntiṟ ṟeṉpar, uṇarumōr - aṟivōr e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aḻatti aḻaṉiṉi, puḻatti puḻaṉiṉi eṉac ceyki yaṟintu ellāvurupiṉōṭum oṭṭuka. ṉakaratti attiṉ mici oṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu ‘atti ṉakaram’ (eḻut. 125) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka.
‘tōṉṟal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, evaṉ eṉa niṟutti vaṟṟuk koṭuttu vēṇṭuñceyki ceytu evaṟṟi, evaṟṟoṭu eṉa muṭittataṉi ellāvurupiṉōṭum oṭṭuka. eṟṟi eṉpuḻi nilimoḻi vakaram itaṉāṟ keṭukka.
iṉi ‘ottatu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ekiṉ eṉa niṟutti, attum iṉṉuṅ koṭuttuc ceyki ceytu ekiṉatti, ekiṉiṉi eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘attu’ iṉitu icittaliṉ muṟkūṟiṉār.	(21)
‘ēḻ’ ‘aṉ’ peṟṟu muṭital
194.	aṉṉeṉ cāriyi ēḻa ṉiṟuti
	muṉṉart tōṉṟum iyaṟkit teṉpa.
iḵtu ēḻeṉṉum eṇṇup peyar aṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇarka eṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṉeṉ cāriyi ēḻaṉiṟuti muṉṉart tōṉṟum iyaṟkit teṉpa - aṉṉeṉṉuñ cāriyi ēḻeṉṉum eṇṇup peyariṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟum iyalpiṉi yuṭitteṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēḻaṉi, ēḻaṟku, ēḻaṉiṉ eṉka. ēṉi urupukaḷōṭuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
cāriyi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṟ, piṟavum aṉpeṟuvaṉa koḷka. pūḻaṉi, yāḻaṉi eṉa ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
mēl varukiṉṟa iṉcāriyiyic cēravittamiyāṉ, ivi yellām iṉcāriyi peṟṟu varutaluṅ koḷka. ēḻiṉi, pūḻiṉi, yāḻiṉi eṉa varum.	(22)
kuṟṟukara īṟu ‘iṉ’ peṟutal
195.	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟuti muṉṉar
	muṟṟat tōṉṟum iṉṉeṉ cāriyi.
itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟuti muṉṉar - kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar, muṟṟattōṉṟum iṉṉeṉ cāriyi - muṭiyat tōṉṟum iṉṉeṉṉuñ cāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nākiṉi nākiṉoṭu, varakiṉi varakiṉoṭu eṉavarum. ēṉiyavaṟṟoṭuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉē piṟa cāriyi peṟuvaṉavuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vaḻakkattāṟ pāṭṭārāyntāṉ eṉavum, kariyataṉi eṉavum varum.	(23)
īreḻuttorumoḻik kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu ṭaṟavoṟṟu iraṭṭutal
196.	neṭṭeḻut timpar oṟṟumikat tōṉṟum
	appāl moḻika ḷalvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu akkuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku iṉavoṟṟu mikumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttimpar oṟṟu mikattōṉṟum - neṭṭeḻut tiṉ piṉṉar varukiṉṟa kuṟṟukaraṅkaṭku iṉavoṟṟu mikattōṉṟā niṟkum; appāl moḻikaḷ alvaḻi āṉa - oṟṟu mikattōṉṟāta kacatapakkaḷ īṟākiya moḻikaḷ allāta iṭattu e-ṟu.
eṉavē, ṭakāra ṟakāraṅkaḷ īṟāṉa colliṭit tōṉṟumāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṭṭi yāṭṭoṭu yāṭṭukku yāṭṭiṉ yāṭṭatu yāṭṭukkaṇ eṉavum, yāṟṟi cōṟṟi eṉavum iṉa voṟṟu mikkaṉa. ivi appāl moḻikaḷ allaṉa.
nāku, kācu, pōtu, kāpu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa appāl moḻikaḷ; avi iṉavoṟṟu mikāvāyiṉa.	(24)
āṇṭuc cāriyip pēṟiṉmi
197.	avitām
	iyaṟkiya vākuñ ceyaṟkiya eṉpa.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avitām iyaṟkiya ākuñ ceyaṟkiya eṉpa - aṅṅaṉam iṉavoṟṟu mikuvaṉatām iṉcāriyi peṟātu iyalpāka muṭiyuñ ceytiyiyuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muṉṉark kāṭṭiyaṉavē koḷka.
‘ceyaṟkiya’ eṉṟa mikiyāṉē, uyirttoṭar moḻikaḷil ēṟpaṉavaṟṟiṟkum oṟṟu mikattōṉṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	muyiṟṟi muyiṟṟoṭu muyiṟṟukku muyiṟṟiṉ muyiṟṟatu muyiṟṟukkaṇ eṉa varum.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, yāṭṭiṉi muyiṟṟiṉi eṉa vilakkiya iṉpeṟutaluṅ koḷka.	(25)
kuṟṟukarīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar aṉcāriyi peṟutal
198.	eṇṇiṉ iṟuti aṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇiṉ iṟuti aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉatu kuṟṟukara īṟu aṉcāriyiyoṭu poruntum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉṟaṉi iraṇṭaṉi eṉa ellā eṇṇiṉiyum, ellā urupiṉoṭuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
muṉṉarc ‘ceyaṟkiya’ eṉṟa ilēcāṉē, oṉṟiṉi iraṇṭiṉi eṉa iṉ cāriyiyuṅ koṭukka.	(26)
orupaḵtu mutalāya peyarkaḷ
orupāṉ mutalākat tirintu muṭital
199.	oṉṟumuta lākap pattūrntu varūum
	ellā eṇṇuñ colluṅ kāli
	āṉiṭi variṉum māṉa milli
	aḵteṉ kiḷavi āvayiṟ keṭumē
	uytal vēṇṭum paḵkāṉ meyyē.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭu iṟutiyāka niṉṟa kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar ēḻiṉōṭum, patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar vantu puṇarntu oṉṟāy niṉṟa coṟkaḷ cāriyi peṟṟut tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalākap pattu ūrntu varūum ellā eṇṇum - oṉṟumutalāka eṭṭīṟāka niṉṟa eṇkaḷiṉ mēlē patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar ēṟi varukiṉṟa orupatu mutalāṉa ellā eṇkaḷiyum, colluṅkāli - muṭipu kūṟuṅ kālattu, āṉ iṭivariṉum māṉa milli - muṟkūṟiya aṉ cāriyiyē yaṉṟi āṉ cāriyi iṭiyē variṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli; āvayiṉ aḵteṉ kiḷavi keṭum - av āṉ peṟṟuḻip paḵteṉṉum eṇṇiṭattu aḵteṉṉuñ coṟ keṭṭup pōm; paḵkāṉ mey uytal vēṇṭum - avvakarattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa pakaramākiya oṟṟuk keṭātu niṟṟali āciriyaṉ virumpum e-ṟu.
‘niṉṟa pattaṉoṟṟukkeṭa vāytam, vantiṭi niliyum’ (eḻut. 437) eṉpataṉāṉ āytam peṟṟatu.
e-ṭu:	orupaḵtu irupaḵtu muppaḵtu nāṟpaḵtu aimpaḵtu aṟupaḵtu eṇpaḵtu eṉak kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ vitikkumāṟē niṟutti, aḵteṉpataṉik keṭuttup pakaravoṟṟi niṟutti, āṉcāriyi koṭuttu, orupāṉi irupāṉi eṉa ellā eṇṇoṭum ellā urupiṉiyuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka. ummi etirmaṟiyātaliṉ orupaḵtaṉi irupaḵtaṉi eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
‘colluṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pattūr kiḷaviyē yaṉṟi ‘oṉpāṉ mutaṉili’ (eḻut. 463) ‘oṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭi’ (eḻut. 475) eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum pakarattuḷ akaram pirittu aḵteṉpatu keṭuttu āṉ koṭukka.	(27)
‘yātu’, āytam iṭi vanta cuṭṭumutal
ukarīṟṟup peyarkaḷ - muṭiyumāṟu
200.	yāteṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
	āyta iṟutiyum aṉṉoṭu civaṇum
	āytaṅ keṭutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu eṇṇup peyarallāta kuṟṟukara īṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) yātu eṉ iṟutiyum - yāteṉa varuṅ kuṟṟukara īṟum, cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - aṉcāriyi yoṭu poruntum; āvayiṉ āṉa āytaṅ keṭutal - avviṭattu vanta āytaṅ keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yātaṉi yātaṉoṭu eṉavum, ataṉi ataṉoṭu, itaṉi itaṉoṭu, utaṉi utaṉoṭu eṉavum varum.	(28)
ticippeyar ēḻaṉurupoṭu muṭiyumāṟu
201.	ēḻa ṉurupiṟkut ticippeyar muṉṉarc
	cāriyik kiḷavi iyaṟkiyu mākum
	āvayi ṉiṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvuṅ kuṟṟukara īṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku ēḻaṉurupoṭu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ticip peyar muṉṉar ēḻaṉurupiṟku - ticip peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta kaṇṇeṉṉum urupiṟku muṭipu kūṟuṅkāl, cāriyik kiḷavi iyaṟkiyumākum - muṉkūṟiya iṉ cāriyiyākiya col niṉṟu muṭitalē yaṉṟi nillātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum; āvayiṉ iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭum - aṅṅaṉam iyalpāyavaḻit ticippeyariṟutik kuṟṟukaran taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyoṭuṅ keṭum e-ṟu.
e ṭu:	vaṭakkiṉkaṇ kiḻakkiṉkaṇ teṟkiṉkaṇ mēṟkiṉkaṇ eṉavum, vaṭakkaṇ kiḻakkaṇ teṟkaṇ mēṟkaṇ eṉavum varum. iṉpeṟuvaḻi ukaraṅ keṭāteṉṟu uṇarka.
‘āvayiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṟ kīḻcār kīḻpuṭi, mēlcār mēlpuṭi, teṉcār teṉpuṭi, vaṭacār vaṭapuṭi eṉac cāriyiyiṉṟip pala vikārappaṭṭu niṟpaṉavuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, kīḻikkuḷam mēlikkuḷam kīḻiccēri mēliccēri eṉa aikāram peṟutaluṅ koḷka.	(29)
ivvōttup puṟaṉaṭi
202.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
	colliya alla ēṉiya vellām
	tēruṅ kāli urupoṭu civaṇic
	cāriyi niliyuṅ kaṭappā ṭilavē.
iḵtu ivvōttiṉuḷ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟiṟ kellām, ituvē ōttāyatōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) colliya alla puḷḷiyiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum - muṟkūṟiya puḷḷiyīṟum uyirīṟum allāta puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collum, ēṉiyavumellām - muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷ tammu ḷoḻintu niṉṟaṉavumellām, tēruṅ kāli urupoṭu civaṇic cāriyi niliyuṅ kaṭappāṭu ila - ārāyuṅ kālattu urupukaḷoṭu poruntic cāriyi niṉṟu muṭiyum muṟimiyi uṭiyavalla, niṉṟum nillātum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘ēṉiyavum’ eṉa ummi virikka. kūṟāta puḷḷi yīṟukaḷ aintu. avi ṇakara, yakara, rakara, lakara, ḷakaraṅkaḷām. maṇṇiṉi maṇṇi, vēyiṉi vēyi, nāriṉi nāri, kalliṉi kalli, muḷḷiṉi muḷḷi eṉa varum. uyirīṟṟuḷ oḻintatu ikaram oṉṟumēyātaliṉ, ataṉip piṟkūṟiṉār. kiḷiyiṉi kiḷiyi eṉa varum.
iṉit tāṉ yāṉ aḻaṉ puḻaṉ eṉṉum ṉakara īṟṟiṉum, ēḻeṉṉum ḻakara īṟṟiṉum oḻintaṉa, poṉṉiṉi poṉṉi, tāḻiṉi tāḻi eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉa piṟavumām. iṉi, īkāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa tīyiṉi tīyi, īyiṉi īyi, vīyiṉi vīyi eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa piṟavumām. aikāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa, tiṉiyiṉi tiṉiyi, kaḻiyiṉi kaḻiyi eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa piṟavumām. ēṉi īṟukaḷilum varuvaṉa uṇarntu koḷka.
mēlē peyarīṟṟuc ceykiyellān tattam īṟṟiṉkaṇ muṭippārātaliṉ avi īṇṭuk kūṟal vēṇṭā.
iṉit ‘tēruṅkāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, urupukaḷ nilimoḻiyāka niṉṟu tamporuḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi vēṟupaṭum urupīṟṟuc ceyki yellām īṇṭu muṭittuk koḷka.
e-ṭu:	nampiyik koṇarntāṉ, maṇṇiṉik koṇarntāṉ, koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉa mūvakip poruḷoṭuṅ kūṭi niṉṟa urupiṟku oṟṟukkoṭukka. maliyoṭu poru tatu, mattikiyāṟ puṭittāṉ, cāttaṟkuk koṭuttāṉ, ūrkkuc ceṉṟāṉ, kākkiyiṟ karitu, kākkiyatu pali, maṭiyuṭ paḻukkāy, taṭāviṉuṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉa urupu kāraṇamākap poruḷoṭu puṇarum vaḻi iyalpāyum īṟutirintum oṟṟumikkum vantaṉa koḷka.
iṉik, kaṇ kāl puṟam mutaliyaṉa peyarāyum urupāyum niṟku mātaliṉ, avi urupākak koḷḷumvaḻi vēṟupaṭuñ ceykika ḷellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka.
iḵtu urupiyalātaliṉ ‘urupoṭu civaṇi’ eṉa vēṇṭā, ammikiyāṉē urupupuṇarccikkaṭ ceṉṟa cāriyikaḷellām īṟṟup potu muṭipu uḷvaḻip poruṭpuṇarccikkuṅ koḷka. viḷaviṉkōṭu, kiḷiyiṉkāl eṉa ellā īṟṟiṉuṅ koḷka. nampiyi, koṟṟaṉi eṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟuñ cāriyi peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum īṇṭē koḷka.	(30)
urupiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

7
uyirmayaṅkiyal
akara īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi
alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
203.	akara iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ
	tattam otta oṟṟiṭi mikumē
eṉpatu cūttiram.
uyirīṟu niṉṟu vaṉkaṇattoṭuñ ciṟupāṉmi ēṉik kaṇaṅkaḷoṭum mayaṅkip puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, ivvōttu ‘uyirmayaṅkiyal’ eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēṟ peyarōṭu urupu puṇarumāṟu kūṟip peyarvaruvaḻi urupu tokku niṉṟa poruṭ puṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ urupiyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
iccūttiram, akara īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) akara iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar - akaramākiya iṟutiyi yuṭiya peyarccoṉ muṉṉar, vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ - vēṟṟumiyallāta viṭattuk kacatapa mutaṉmoḻikaḷ varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟumāyiṉ, tattam otta oṟṟu iṭi mikum - tattamakkup poruntiya ak kacatapakkaḷākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷakkuṟitu, nuṇakkuṟitu, atakkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa oṭṭuka.
ivi aḵṟiṇi iyaṟpeyarākiya eḻuvāy viṉikkuṟippup paṇpākiya payaṉiliyoṭu muṭintaṉa.
‘otta’ eṉṟamiyātu ‘tattam otta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akara īṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikkum, melleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum muṭipum, akarantaṉṉi uṇara niṉṟavaḻi vaṉkaṇattoṭu mikku muṭiyum muṭipum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	taṭakki tavakkoṇṭāṉ ‘vayakkaḷiṟu’ (puṟam. 100) ‘vayappuli’ (akam. 22) kuḻakkaṉṟu eṉavum, taṭañcevi ‘kamañcūl’ (muruku. 7)  eṉavum, akkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum varum.
iṉi, iṭiccol valloṟṟup peṟṟu varuvaṉa uḷavēl avaṟṟiyum ivvilēciṉāṉ muṭittuk koḷka.	(1)
akara īṟṟuḷ valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa
204.	viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum
	eṉaveṉ eccamum cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum
	āṅka eṉṉum uriyacik kiḷaviyum
	ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum iṭiccollum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - viṉiyi oḻipākavuṭiya akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum, uvamak kiḷaviyum - uvamavurupāy niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, eṉaveṉ eccamum - eṉaveṉṉum vāypāṭ ṭāṉ niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum - cuṭṭākiya akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, āṅka eṉṉum uriyacik kiḷaviyum - āṅka veṉṉum akara īṟṟu uriyaci yiṭiccollum, ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum - muṉṉark kūṟiya valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṇa tāva cāva eṉa niṟuttik, koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭikka.
ivviṉiyeccam oḻintaṉa ellām iṭiccol leṉṟu uṇarka. pulipōlak koṉṟāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tāviṉāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, akkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum, āṅkak koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ, ‘āṅkak kuyilu mayiluṅ kāṭṭik, kēḷvaṉi viṭuttup pōki yōḷē’ eṉavum varum.
‘uvamam’ viṉiyecca viṉikkuṟippēṉum, oṉṟaṉoṭu poruvappaṭutal nōkki uvamaviyaliṉkaṇ āciriyar vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟiṉār.
‘eṉaveṉṉum eccamum’, irucolliyum iyivikkiṉṟa nilimiyāṉ iṭiccollōttiṉuḷ vēṟōtiṉār.
‘āṅka’ eṉpatu, ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantatallāmi ‘āṅka veṉṉu muriyaci’ eṉṟataṉāṉum ‘āṅka vuriyaci’ (co. 279) eṉṉum iṭiyiyaṟ cūttirattāṉum uṇarka.
ivi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyum muṭipu ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) eṉpuḻik kūṟiyatēyām. avi, tāva pulipōla koḷḷeṉa āṅka eṉa niṟutti, ñanamayava mutaliya moḻi ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu puṇarttu iyalpāmāṟu oṭṭik koḷka. cuṭṭu mēṟkūṟupa.	(2)
akaraccuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar meṉkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu
205.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ
	oṭṭiya oṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.
itu ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ meṉkaṇam iyalpākum eṉa muṟkūṟiyataṉi vilakki mikku muṭika eṉṟaliṉ,  eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ - akaraccuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamakkaḷ mutalākiya moḻivariṉ, oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭi mikutal vēṇṭum - tattamakkup poruntiṉa oṟṟu iṭimikutali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aññāṇ, annūl, ammaṇi eṉa varum.
‘oṭṭiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ aññeḷintatu, annaṉṟu, ammāṇṭatu eṉa akaran taṉṉi yuṇara niṉṟavaḻiyum mikutal koḷka.	(3)
ataṉ muṉṉar iṭikkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu
206.	yavamuṉ variṉē vakaram oṟṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) yava muṉ variṉ - yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻi akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉē variṉ, vakaram oṟṟum - iṭikkaṇ vakaram oṟṟām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avyāḻ, avvaḷi eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, akaran taṉṉi yuṇara niṉṟa vaḻiyum vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḷintatu eṉa varum.	(4)
uyirkkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu
207.	uyirmuṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir muṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu - uyirkaḷ akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉ variṉum muṟkūṟiya vakaram mikku varum iyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	a eṉa niṉṟa cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar aṭi mutaliyaṉa varuvittu vakaram oṟṟit taṉṉuru iraṭṭi uyirēṟṟi, avvaṭi avvāṭi avvili avvīyam avvural avvūrti avveḻu avvēṇi avviyam avvoḻukkam avvōṭi avvuviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar’ (eḻut. 160) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘neṟiyiyal’ eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṭṭutal kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, atu nilimoḻit toḻileṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, akaran taṉṉi uṇara niṉṟa vaḻiyum vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḻakitu eṉa varum. ‘tiriyātu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēṟ cuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi vakarakkēṭu koḷka.	(5)
ceyyuṭkaṇ akaraccuṭṭu nīṭal
208.	nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittē.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkic ceyyuṭku āvatoru viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - akaraccuṭṭu nīṭavarutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘āyiru tiṇiyi ṉicikkumaṉa collē’ (col. 1), ‘āyirupāṟcol (col. 3) eṉa varum.
itu, varumoḻi variyātu kūṟaliṉ vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇam ellāvaṟṟoṭuñ ceṉṟatu. avaṟṟiṟku utāraṇam vantavaḻik kāṇka. innīṭci irumoḻip puṇarccikkaṇ varutaliṉ ‘nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭal’ (col. 403) ākāmi uṇarka.	(6)
‘cāva’, vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
209.	cāva eṉṉuñ ceyaveṉ eccattu
	iṟuti vakaraṅ keṭutalum urittē.
itumēl ‘viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi’ eṉṟa eccattiṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) cāva eṉṉuñ ceyaveṉ eccattu iṟuti vakaram - cāva veṉṟu collappaṭuñ ceyaveṉeccattu iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa vakaramum, keṭutalum urittu - keṭṭu niṟṟalum keṭātu niṟṟalum uriya e-ṟu.
e -ṭu:	kōṭṭiṭic cākkuttiṉāṉ eṉa varum. cīṟiṉāṉ takarttāṉ puṭittāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. keṭātatu muṉṉar muṭittām.
itaṉi ‘viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi’ (eḻut. 204) eṉṟataṉpiṉ viyātata ṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattum innilimoḻikkēṭu koḷka. cāñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ yāttāṉ vīḻntāṉ aṭintāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka.(7)
akara īṟṟuḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉa
210.	aṉṉa veṉṉum uvamak kiḷaviyum
	aṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinilik kiḷaviyum
	ceymmaṉa veṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum
	ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum
	ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum
	ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	amma eṉṉum uripporuṭ kiḷaviyum
	palavaṟ ṟiṟutip peyarkkoṭi uḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi aṉittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu akara īṟṟuḷ orucār peyarkkum viṉikkum iṭikkum muṉṉeytiyatu vilakkiyum, eytātatu eytuvittum ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṉa eṉṉum uvamak kiḷaviyum - aṉṉa eṉṟu collap paṭum uvamavurupākiya akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, aṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinilik kiḷaviyum - aṇiyārik karutiṉa viḷiyākiya nilimi yiṉiyuṭiya akara īṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarccollum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum - ceymmaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭun toḻiṟcol poruḷ taruṅkāl um īṟṟāṉ iṟuñ collum, ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum - oruvarāṉ ēvaṟṟoḻiṉmi karutik kūṟappaṭṭa ēvaṟ poruṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭittali uṇarttum akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum, ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyta eṉṟu collappaṭum peyareccamākiya akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum, ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyyiya eṉṟu collap paṭum viṉiyeccamākiya akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum, amma eṉṉum uripporuṭ kiḷaviyum - amma eṉṟu collappaṭum etirmukamākkiya akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, palavaṟṟu iṟutip peyarkkoṭi uḷappaṭa - paṉmip poruḷi uṇarttum akara īṟṟup peyarkaḷ aintaṉiyum muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟoṭu kūṭṭik koṭuttal uḷḷiṭṭu, aṉṟi aṉittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa - av veṭṭuc collum iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	poṉṉaṉṉa kutiri, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi eṉa, itu ‘viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum uvamak kiḷaviyum’ (eḻut. 204) eṉpataṉāṉ mikku muṭitali vilakkiṟṟu.
ūra kēḷ, cel, tā, pō eṉa ‘uyirīṟākiya vuyartiṇi’ (eḻut. 153) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ iyalpāy muṭivatu īṇṭu ṉakaraṅ keṭṭu akara īṟāy viḷiyēṟṟu muṭintamiyiṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
