uṇmaṉa kutiri cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku uṇṇu meṉa virittum, yāṉuṇmaṉa nīyuṇmaṉa avaṉuṇmaṉa eṉa niṟuttik, kūḻ cōṟu tēṉ pāl eṉa varuvittum muṭikka. ivaṟṟiṟkum avvāṟē virittuk koḷka. iṅṅaṉañ ceyyumeṉpataṉ eccap poruṭṭākiya maṉave ṉiṟuticcol akkālam vaḻaṅkiyatātaliṉ āciriyar ataṉiyum vēṟāka eṭuttōtiṉār.
yāṉum niṉṉōṭuṭaṉ varuka, avaṉ celka, avaḷ celka, avar celka eṉa niṟuttik, kāṭṭiṉkaṇ ceruviṉkaṇ tāṉikkaṇ pōriṉkaṇ eṉa varuvittu muṭikka. ivi ēvaṟ poruṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭittaṉa.
‘ēval kaṇṇiya’ eṉavē ēval kaṇṇātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa; avi nī celka atu celka avi celka eṉa niṟutti, muṟkūṟiya kāṭu mutaliya vaṟṟi varuvittu muṭikka. ivi ēvaṟ poruṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭiyātaṉa. aḵṟiṇi ēvaṟ poruṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭiyāmi, viṉiyiyaluḷ (col. 228) viyaṅkōṭkaṇṇē poruḷiyaluñ ceyyuḷiyalum paṟṟik kūṟutum.
‘maṉavum’ ‘viyaṅkōḷum’ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
uṇṭa kutiri cennāy takar paṉṟi - ituvum atu. itaṟku uriya uṇṇāta kutiri yeṉṉum etirmaṟiyum, nalla kutiri yeṉṉum kuṟippuṅ koḷka.
uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ; itu muṉṉar viṉiyeccam valleḻuttup peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
amma koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉpatu iṭiccollāta liṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu. itu kēḷāy koṟṟaṉē eṉa etirmuka mākkiyavāṟu kāṇka.
pallakutiri, palakutiri, cillakutiri, cilakutiri, uḷḷakutiri, illa kutiri, cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉa oṭṭuka. ikkālattup palla cilla eṉpaṉa vaḻaṅkā. ituvum viḷakkuṟitu eṉṟāṟ pōlap palakkutiriyeṉa valleḻuttu eytiyataṉi vilakkiṟṟu.
viḷinilik kiḷaviyākiya peyar muṟkūṟātataṉāṉē, ceyyumeṉ pataṉ maṟiyākiya ceyyāta veṉpataṟkum ivviyalpu muṭipu koḷka. atu vārāta koṟṟaṉ eṉa varum. ivviyalpu muṭipiṟkuc ceymmaṉa ciṟattaliṉ, viyaṅkōṭku muṉ vittār.
‘ēval kaṇṇiya’ eṉpataṉāṉ ēval kaṇṇātatum uḷateṉṟu kūṟi ‘maṉṉiya peruma nī’ (puṟam. 91) eṉa utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭukaveṉiṉ, atu poruntātu; kūṟukiṉṟāṉ avaṉ nilipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟē karutik kūṟutaliṉ atuvum ēval kaṇṇiṟṟēyām.
ellāvaṟṟiṉuñ ciṟanta ‘palavaṟṟiṟuti’ muṟkūṟukaveṉiṉ, atu vaḻakkiṟkuñ ceyyuṭkum vēṟu vēṟu muṭipuṭit teṉṟaṟkuñ, ceyyuṇ muṭipu ivviyalpupōṟ ciṟappiṉṟeṉṟaṟkum, akara īṟṟuḷ muṭipu kūṟātu niṉṟa muṟṟuviṉiyum viṉikkuṟippum ivviyalpu muṭipu peṟumeṉṟaṟkum piṉ vittār.
uṇṭaṉa kutiri itu muṟṟuviṉi. kariyaṉa kutiri itu muṟṟu viṉikkuṟippu.
iḵtu iyalpukaṇattu muṭipu. ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) eṉpuḻip poruntuvaṉa vellāṅ koḷka.	(8)
‘vāḻiya’ iṟuti yakaram keṭutalum uṇmi
211.	vāḻiya eṉṉuñ ceyaveṉ kiḷavi
	iṟuti yakaraṅ keṭutalum urittē.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) vāḻiya eṉṉuñ ceya eṉ kiḷavi - vāḻuṅkālam neṭuṅ kālamākuka eṉṉum poruḷit tarum vāḻiya veṉṟu collappaṭuñ ceyaveṉeccakkiḷavi, iṟuti yakaraṅ keṭutalum urittu - iṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa yakaravoṟṟuṅ keṭṭu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
‘keṭutalum’ eṉavē, keṭātu muṭitalē perumpāṉmi eṉṟavāṟu.
e-ṭu:	vāḻi koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉa varum.
vāḻiya eṉpatē perumpāṉmi. vāḻiya yāṉ nī avaṉ avaḷ avar atu avi eṉa itu mūṉṟiṭattuñ cēṟaliṉ ‘uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum’ (eḻut. 151) eṉpuḻi muṉṉili yiyalpām eṉṟa taṉkaṇ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. itu kuṟippu viyaṅkōḷ.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ’ iḵtu iyalpukaṇattuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vāḻi ñeḷḷā eṉavarum.
itu vāḻttappaṭum poruḷ vāḻavēṇṭumeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉa ṉākak kūṟutaliṉ ēval kaṇṇiṟṟēyām.
allākkāl ‘vāḻttiyal vakiyē nāṟpākku murittē’ (cey. 109) eṉpataṟkum, vāḻttiyalākac cāṉṟōr kūṟiya ceyyuḷkaḷukkum payaṉiṉṟāmeṉṟu uṇarka.	(9)
‘amma’ iṟuti akaram nīṭalum uṇmi
212.	uripporuṭ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum variyār.
iḵtu amma veṉpataṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) uripporuṭ kiḷavi - etirmukamākkum poruḷi yuṭiya ammaveṉṉuñ col, nīṭṭamum variyār - akaramāki niṟṟalēyaṉṟi ākāramāy nīṇṭu muṭitaliyum nīkkār e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ammā koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉa varum.
ummiyāṉ, nīḷāmiyē perumpāṉmiyām. variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ in nīṭci iyalpu kaṇattuṅ koḷka. ammāñeḷḷā, nākā, māṭā, vaṭukā, ātā eṉa oṭṭuka.	(10)
‘palāañ cilāam’ nilivarumoḻikaḷ
213.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi uḷavē
	ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa.
itu muṟkūṟiya palavaṟṟiṟutikkaṇ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟu iṟuti nīṭum moḻi uḷa - palavaṟṟi uṇarttum aivakic colliṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭu muṭiyum moḻikaḷuñ cila uḷa; ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi āṉa - yāṇṭuḷaveṉiṟ ceyyu ḷātalik karutiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarccippaṭuñ ceyyuṇmuṭi puṭiya moḻikaḷiṉkaṇ e-ṟu.
‘uṭittu’ eṉṉātu ‘uḷa’ eṉṟa paṉmiyāṉ, varumoḻikkaṇ cila eṉpatu vantu nīṭu meṉṟu koḷka.
‘ceyyuḷāṉa’ eṉṉātu ‘ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻiyāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pala eṉpataṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭuḻi nilimoḻi akarap pēṟum varumoḻi ñakaramākiya melleḻuttup pēṟum, varumoḻi yiṟuti nīṇṭavaḻi akarappēṟum makaramākiya melleḻuttup pēṟuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘palāañ cilāa meṉmaṉār pulavar’.  itaṉ coṉ ṉili pala cila eṉṉuñ cevveṇ.	(11)
pala, cila - tammuṉ tām vantu muṭiyumāṟu
214.	toṭaral iṟuti tammuṉ tāmvariṉ
	lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākalu murittē.
itu pala cila eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku iyalpēyaṉṟit tiripum uṇṭeṉa, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) toṭar al iṟuti - toṭarmoḻi yallāta īreḻut torumoḻiyākiya pala cila eṉṉum akara īṟṟuc col, tammuṉ tāmvariṉ - tammuṉṉē tām varumāyiṉ, lakaram ṟakara voṟṟu ākalum urittu - tam īṟṟiṉ niṉṟa lakara voṟṟu ṟakaravoṟṟākat tirintu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
ummiyāṉ tiriyāmiyum uritteṉṟār.
e-ṭu:	paṟpala koṇṭār, ciṟcila vitti eṉa varum.
akara īṟṟuc cuṭṭallāta kuṟṟeḻuttu ōreḻuttoru moḻiyātal iṉmiyiṉ ‘toṭaraliṟuti’ yeṉavē īreḻuttoru moḻiyē uṇarttiṟṟu. ‘taṉmuṉ’ eṉṉātu ‘tammuṉ’ eṉṟa paṉmiyāṟ pala cila eṉa niṉṟa iraṇṭun taḻuvappaṭṭaṉa.
‘tammuṉvariṉ’ eṉṉātu ‘tām’ eṉṟataṉāṟ palaviṉmuṉ pala varutaluñ cilaviṉ muṉ cila varutaluṅ koḷka. lakaram ṟakara voṟṟā meṉa oṟṟiṟkut tiripu kūṟi akaraṅ keṭutal kūṟiṟṟilareṉiṉ, atu vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittaleṉṉum uttipeṟa vittateṉṟu uṇarka. itaṉi ñāpakameṉpārum uḷar.
aruttāpattiyāṉ ‘tammuṉ tāmvariṉ’ lakaram ṟakara oṟṟām. eṉavē, tammuṉ piṟavariṉ lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟākātu akaram keṭumeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	palkaṭal cēṉi tāṉi paṟi eṉavum, ‘palyāṉi’       (puṟam. 63) palvēḷvi eṉavum, cilkāṭu cēṉi tāṉi paṟi eṉavum, cilyāṉi cilvēḷvi eṉavum varum.
‘urittu’ eṉṟatu akara īṟṟorumi paṟṟi.	(12)
āṇṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu tōṉṟal
215.	valleḻut tiyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟum.
itu muṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṟkum uḷḷatoru muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu iyaṟki - muṟkūṟiya pala cila veṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum akara īṟṟup potuvitiyiṟ kūṟiya valleḻuttumikum iyalpu, uṟaḻattōṉṟum - mikutalum mikāmiyumāy uṟaḻntuvarat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	palappala palapala, cilaccila cilacila eṉa varum.
īṇṭun ‘tammuṉ tām’ varutal koḷka.
‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaraṅ keṭa lakaram tirintun tiriyātum uṟaḻntu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paṟpala palpala, ciṟcila cilcila eṉavarum.
‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaraṅ keṭa lakaram melleḻuttum āytamumākat tirintu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘paṉmīṉ vēṭṭattu’ (kuṟun. 123), paṉmalar, paḵṟāli, paḵṟāḻici, ciṉṉūl, ciḵṟāḻici eṉa varum.
ivi muṉṉart ‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟu eṭuttōtiya mikiyāṉ akaraṅ keṭa niṉṟa lakaravoṟṟiṉ muṭipākaliṉ ‘takaram varuvaḻi āytam’ (eḻut. 369) ‘melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ’ (eḻu. 367) eṉpavaṟṟāṉ muṭiyā.		(13)
akara īṟu, vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
216.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu akara īṟṟiṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟi, vaṉkaṇattoṭu vēṟṟumi tokku niṉṟa muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō  raṟṟu - akara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum muṟkūṟiya alvaḻiyōṭu oru taṉmittāyk, kacatapa mutaṉ moḻi vantuḻit tattam otta voṟṟu iṭimikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	iruviḷakkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ eṉa varum. iruviḷakkuṟumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉak kuṇa vēṟṟumik kaṇṇuṅ koḷka.
‘iruviḷa’ eṉpatu ōli; vēṇāṭṭakattu ōrūr; karuvūriṉakattu oru cēriyu meṉpa. iruviḷaviṟ koṟṟaṉ eṉa virikka.	(14)
ivvīṟṟu marappeyar melleḻuttu mikutal
217.	marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu akara īṟṟu marap peyarkku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) marap peyarkkiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum - akara īṟṟu marap peyarāya col vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ataṅkōṭu viḷaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
itu ‘kacatapa mutaliya’ (eḻut. 143) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum.	(15)
‘maka’ iṉcāriyi peṟutal
218.	makappeyark kiḷavikku iṉṉē cāriyi.
iḵtu akara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) makappeyark kiḷavikku iṉṉē cāriyi - akara īṟṟu maka eṉṉum peyarccolliṟku vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ varuñcāriyi iṉ cāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	makaviṉki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
cāriyippēṟu variyātu kūṟiyavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇuñ cellumeṉpatu āciriyarkkuk karuttākaliṉ, makaviṉ ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.
mēl ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyi peṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(16)
aḵtu attup peṟutalum uḷatātal
219.	attavaṇ variṉum varinili iṉṟē.
iḵtu īṟṟu valleḻuttōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṇ - muṟkūṟiya makaviṭattu, attu variṉum varinili iṉṟu  - iṉṉēyaṉṟi attuc cāriyiyum īṟṟu valleḻuttum vantu muṭiyiṉum nīkkum nilimiyiṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	makattuk ki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, makappālyāṭu eṉa valleḻuttuppēṟum, makaviṉki eṉa mēl iṉcāriyi peṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum, viḷaviṉkōṭu eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvuṅ koḷka. ‘nili’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ, makampālyāṭu eṉa melleḻuttup pēṟuṅ koḷka.	(17)
palavaṟṟiṟuti vaṟṟup peṟutal
220.	palavaṟ ṟiṟuti urupiyal niliyum.
iḵtu atikārattāṉ iyipu valleḻuttoṭu vaṟṟum vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) palavaṟṟiṟuti - palla pala cila uḷḷa illa eṉṉum palavaṟṟi yuṇarttum akara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ iṟuti, urupiyal niliyum - urupiyaṟkaṇ vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarntāṟ pōla urupiṉatu poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum vaṟṟup peṟṟup puṇarum e-ṟu.
iyipu valleḻuttu atikārattāṟ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pallavaṟṟukkōṭu palavaṟṟukkōṭu cilavaṟṟukkōṭu uḷḷavaṟṟukkōṭu illavaṟṟukkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa oṭṭuka.
urupu virintuḻi niṟkumāṟu pōlaṉṟi, avvurupu tokku ataṉ poruḷ niṉṟu puṇaruṅkāl vēṟupāṭu uṭimiyiṉ, avvēṟu pāṭukaḷ īṇṭu ōtiṉār ittuṇiyumeṉṟu uṇarka.	(18)
ākāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ (mikku) muṭiyumāṟu
221.	ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ākāra īṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyatu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻit tattam otta oṟṟu iṭimikum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mūṅkākkaṭitu, tārākkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(19)
‘ceyyā’ vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccamum aṟṟātal
222.	ceyyā eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	avviyal tiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu viṉiccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyā eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - ceyyā veṉṉum viṉiyeccamākiya collum ummiyāṟ peyarecca maṟiyākiya collum, avviyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar - valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum avviyalpu tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṇṇākkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, uṇṇākkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum varum.	(20)
ummittokikkaṇ nilimoḻi ākāra īṟu muṭiyumāṟu
223.	ummi eñciya irupeyart tokimoḻi
	meymmi yāka akaram mikumē.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ummittoki muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ummi eñciya irupeyart tokimoḻi - ummi tokku niṉṟa irupeyarākiya tokic coṟkaḷ; meymmiyāka akaram mikum - meyyāka nilimoḻi yīṟṟakaram mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uvāappatiṉāṉku, irāappakal eṉa varum.
‘meymmiyāka’ eṉpataṉāṉ, valleḻuttuk koṭukka. iḵtu eḻuvāyum payaṉiliyumaṉṟi ummit tokiyātaliṉ māṭṭēṟṟāṉ valleḻuttup peṟātāyiṟṟu.
‘ummi tokka’ eṉṉātu ‘eñciya’ eṉṟa vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumi yāṉ, arāappāmpu eṉap paṇputtokikkum, irāakkoṭitu eṉa eḻuvāy muṭipiṟkum, irāakkākki eṉap peyarecca maṟikkum akarappēṟu koḷka.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum akarappēṟu koḷka. iṟāavaḻutuṇaṅkāy eṉavarum. iḵtu ummit toki. arāakkuṭṭi eṉpatu paṇputtokiyum vēṟṟumit tokiyumām. uvāap paṭṭiṉi eṉpatu vēṟṟumit toki.	(21)
ākāra īṟṟuḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉa
224.	āvum māvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum
	yāveṉ viṉāvum palavaṟ ṟiṟutiyum
	ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyāvum
	taṉtoḻil urikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyō(ṭu)
	aṉṟi aṉittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkalum eytātatu eytuvittalum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āvum - āveṉṉum peyarum, māvum - māveṉṉum peyarum, viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum - viḷittaliyuṭiya peyarākiya uyartiṇic collum, yāveṉ viṉāvum - yāveṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, palavaṟṟu iṟutiyum - paṉmip poruḷi uṇarttum ākāra īṟṟup peyarecca maṟiyākiya muṟṟuviṉiccollum, ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyāvum - muṉṉili yēval viṉiyik karutivarum etirmukamākkuñ col liṉic cērnta miyāveṉṉum ākāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, taṉ toḻil urikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyōṭu - taṉatu toḻiliṉic collum ākāra īṟṟut taṉmiyākiya viṉāc colloṭu kūṭa, aṉṟi yaṉittum - avveḻuvakiyākiya collum, iyalpeṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyu meṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ākuṟitu mākuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu; kuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa oṭṭuka.
ivi ākāra īṟṟup peyarākaliṉ, mikku muṭivaṉa mikāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
ūrākēḷ cel tā pō eṉa iḵtu iyalpāmeṉṟa uyartiṇip paṭarkkip peyar tirintu muṉṉiliyāy viḷiyēṟṟaliṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
yā kuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa ituvum peyarākaliṉ, eytiya iyipu valleḻuttu vilakkiyatām.
uṇṇā kutirikaḷ cennāykaḷ takarkaḷ paṉṟikaḷ eṉa iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu; ‘ceyyā’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattup (eḻut. 222) peṟṟa valleḻuttiṉi vilakkaliṉ.
kēṇmiyā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum, uṇkā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum, ivviṭic coṟkaḷ muṭiyāmiyiṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
uṇkā eṉpatu, yāṉuṇpēṉō eṉṉum poruṭṭu. iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇāyiṉ ‘ña na ma ya va’ (eḻut. 144) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭipey tum.	(22)
ākāra īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
225.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu ākāra īṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum - ākāra īṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇṇē yaṉṟi vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu - akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tārā mūṅkā vaṅkā eṉa niṟuttik, kāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varuvittu valleḻuttuk koṭuttu oṭṭuka.	(23)
ivvīṟṟuḷ akara eḻuttuppēṟu eyti muṭivaṉa
226.	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟum akarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu avvīṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu; akaramum valleḻuttoṭu peṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ākāra īṟṟiṟkum, ōreḻuttu moḻikkum - ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya ākāra īṟṟiṟkum, akarakkiḷavi aṟiyat tōṉṟum - nilimoḻikkaṇ akaramākiya eḻuttu viḷaṅkat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	palāakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum kāakkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘ōreḻuttorumoḻi’ akarampeṟutal ciṟupāṉmiyeṉṟaṟku ataṉip piṟkūṟiṉār. itu nilimoḻic ceykiyātaliṟ palāa vili palāanār eṉa iyalpukaṇattuṅ koḷka.
‘aṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ avvakaram īriṭattum poruntiṉavaḻik koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, aṇṇāattēri tiṭṭāattukkuḷam eṉa attuk koṭuttum, uvāattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum ñāṉṟuṅ koṭuttum, uvāattāṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum āṉuṅ koṭuttum, iṭāviṉuṭ (iṟikūṭi) koṇṭāṉ eṉa iṉṉum ēḻuṉurupuṅ koṭuttuñ ceyki ceytu muṭikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, mūṅkāviṉkāl mūṅkāviṉṟali eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipuvalleḻuttuk kēṭuṅ koḷka.	(24)
‘irā’ vēṟṟumikkaṇ akaram peṟāmi
227.	irāveṉ kiḷavikku akaram illi.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟup peyarkku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) irāveṉ kiḷavikku  - irāveṉṉum ākāra īṟṟuc col liṟku, akaram illi - muṟkūṟiya akaram peṟutaliṉṟi valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	irākkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
irāakkākki irāakkūttu eṉap peyarecca maṟipporuḷ tārātu, irāviṭattukkākki irāviṭattukkūttu eṉa vēṟṟumi karutiyavaḻi, irākkākki irākkūttu eṉa akaram peṟāteṉṟu uṇarka.	(25)
‘nilā’ attuppeṟṟu muṭital
228.	nilāveṉ kiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu akaram vilakki atikāra valleḻuttiṉōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nilāveṉ kiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum - nilāveṉṉuñ col attuccāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nilāattukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
nilimoḻittoḻil nilimoḻittoḻili vilakkumātaliṉ, attu vakuppa akaram vīḻntatu. itaṟku ēḻaṉurupu virikka. nilāakkatir eṉpatu ‘vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum’ (eḻut. 225) eṉpataṉāṉ iyipu valleḻuttup peṟṟatu. nilāamuṟṟam eṉpatu ‘oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku’ aṉmiyiṉ attup peṟātāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, nilāattu ñāṉṟāṉ eṉa iyalpu kaṇattuk kaṇṇum ēṟpaṉa koḷka.	(26)
yā, piṭā, taḷā - ivi mūṉṟum muṭiyumāṟu
229.	yāmarak kiḷaviyum piṭāvun taḷāvum
	āmup peyarum melleḻuttu mikumē.
itu varumoḻi valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) yāmarak kiḷaviyum - yāveṉṉum maratti uṇara niṉṟa collum, piṭāvum- piṭāveṉṉuñ collum, taḷāvum - taḷāveṉ ṉuñ collum, ām muppeyarum melleḻuttu mikum - ākiya mūṉṟu peyarum valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāaṅkōṭu, piṭāaṅkōṭu, taḷāaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
varumoḻit toḻilākiya melleḻuttu vakuppavē, valleḻuttu vilakkiṟṟām. itaṟku vilakkāmiyiṉ akaram peṟṟatu.(27)
ivi valleḻuttuppēṟum eytutal
230.	valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉa milli.
iḵtu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmaṟ kāttatu, akarattoṭu melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttum peṟumeṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illi - muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarkkum melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāakkōṭu, piṭāakkōṭu, taḷāakkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
‘māṉamilli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, immūṉṟaṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. yāviṉ kōṭu, piṭāviṉkōṭu, taḷāviṉkōṭu eṉa varum. cāriyi peṟavē akaram vīḻntatu. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, yāattukkōṭu, piṭāattuk kōṭu, taḷāattukkōṭu eṉa attup peṟutaluṅ koḷka. akaramum valleḻuttum peṟutaliṉ, ‘yāmarak kiḷavi’ (eḻut. 229) eṉpataṉik ‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum’ (eḻut. 226) eṉpataṉpiṉ viyātavataṉāṉ, irāviṟkoṇṭāṉ nilāviṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(28)
māmarak kiḷaviyum āvum māvum muṭiyumāṟu
231.	māmarak kiḷaviyum āvum māvum
	āmup peyarum avaṟṟō raṉṉa
	akaram valleḻut taviyavaṇ niliyā
	ṉakaram oṟṟum āvum māvum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki eytātatu eytuvittatu. im mūṉṟum valleḻuttup peṟā eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. māmarattukku akaramum ṅañanama oṟṟum, ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟku ṉakara oṟṟum eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) māmarak kiḷaviyum āvummāvum ām muppeyarum avaṟṟōraṉṉa - māmaramākiya collum āveṉṉuñ collum māveṉṉuñ collumākiya mūṉṟu peyarum yāmaram mutaliya mūṉṟōṭum oru taṉmiyavāy melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum, āvum māvum akaram avaṇ niliyā ṉakaram oṟṟum - avaṟṟuḷ āvum māvum puṇarcciyiṭattu akaram nilipeṟāvāy ṉakara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum, eṉavē aruttāpattiyāṉ māmarattiṟku akaram nilipeṟṟu ṅañanama oṟṟum peṟumāyiṟṟu, avi valleḻuttu avaṇ niliyā - ammūṉṟu peyarum muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttup puṇarcciyiṭattu nilipeṟāvāy varum e-ṟu.
‘avaṇ niliyā’ eṉṟataṉi iraṇṭiṭattuṅ kūṭṭuka.
e-ṭu:	māaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū, āṉkōṭu māṉkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
‘māmarak kiḷaviyum āvum māvum avaṟṟōraṉṉa’ eṉṟu ñāpakamākak kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, māṅkōṭeṉa akaramiṉṟiyum varum.
iṉi, ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṟ kāyāṅkōṭu nuṇāṅkōṭu āṇaṅ kōṭu eṉṟāṟ pōlap piṟavum melleḻuttup peṟutalum, aṅkāk koṇṭāṉ iṅkākkoṇṭāṉ uṅkākkoṇṭāṉ eṅkāk koṇṭāṉ eṉa ivaṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇara niṉṟu iṭiccoṟkaḷ valleḻuttup peṟutalum, āviṉkōṭu māviṉkōṭu eṉac ciṟupāṉmi iṉ peṟutalum, peṟṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻvuṅ koḷka.
māṭṭēṟṟāṉ mūṉṟu peyarum valleḻuttup peṟātu melleḻuttup peṟṟavāṟum, māmaram akaram peṟṟavāṟum ic cūttirattiṉ kaṇṇaḻivāṉ uṇarka.	(29)
mēli vitiyāṉ muṭinta ‘āṉ’ akaramum peṟutal
232.	āṉoṟ ṟakaramoṭum niliyiṭaṉ uṭittē.
iḵtu avaṟṟuḷ āṉ eṉṟataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉoṟṟu - āveṉṉuñ coṉ muṉṉarp peṟṟuniṉṟa ṉakaravoṟṟu, akaramoṭum niliyiṭaṉ uṭittu - akarattoṭu kūṭi niṟkum iṭaṉum uṭittu e-ṟu.
‘iṭaṉuṭittu’ eṉṟavataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇattatu immuṭipeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘āṉaney teḷittu nāṉa nīvi’, ‘āṉamaṇi kaṟaṅkum kāṉat tāṅkaṇ’ eṉa varum.
‘akaramoṭum’ eṉṟa ummiyāṉ, akaramiṉṟi varutalē perumpāṉmi. āṉey teḷittu, āṉmaṇi, āṉvāl eṉa varum.(30)
‘āṉ’ muṉ pakara īkāram muṭiyumāṟu
233.	āṉmuṉ varūum īkāra pakaran
	tāṉmikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalum urittē.
iḵtu āṉ eṉpataṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṉmuṉ varūum īkāra pakaram - āṉeṉṉuñ coṉ muṉṉar varumoḻiyāy varukiṉṟa īkārattoṭu kūṭiya pakaramākiya moḻi, tāṉ mikat tōṉṟi - ap pakaramākiya tāṉ mikku niṟpa nilimoḻi ṉakarattiṟkuk kēṭu tōṉṟi, kuṟukalum urittu - īkāram ikaramākak kuṟuki niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āppi eṉa varum.
ummi etirmaṟi yākalāṉ, āṉpī eṉṟumām.	(31)
ivvīṟṟuṭ cila ceyyuṭkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
234.	kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉikeṭa ukaram
	aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittē.
iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉikeṭa  -  kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ākārattiṉatu iraṇṭu māttiriyiṉ oru māttiri keṭṭu aḵtu akaramāy niṟpa, ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - āṇṭu ukaram pulappaṭa varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘iṟavuppuṟat taṉṉa piṇarppaṭu taṭavumutaṟ
	 cuṟavukkōṭ ṭaṉṉa muḷḷilit tāḻi’	(naṟ. 19)
	‘puṟavuppuṟat taṉṉa puṉkā yukāy’	(kuṟun. 264).
eṉa varum. ‘ukaram’ vakuppavē nilimoḻi akaraṅ keṭṭatu. atikāra valleḻuttu vilakkāmiyiṉ niṉṟu muṭintatu.
iṉi, nilimoḻittoḻil variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattiṟkum ivviti eytiṟṟākaliṉ, āṇṭuvarum ukaram pulappaṭa vārāmiyum uṇarka. cuṟavuyarkoṭi, ‘aravuyarkoṭi’, muḻavuṟaḻtōḷ eṉa ivi kuṟiyataṉiṟutic ciṉikeṭṭu, varumoḻi uyir mutaṉ moḻiyāy varutaliṉ vakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu, ukaram peṟātu muṭintaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭā murupu virikka; mūṉṟāvatumām.	(32)
ikara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ vali mikal
235.	ikara iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu ikara īṟṟup peyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu tokimarapiṟ kūṟi īṇṭu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ikara iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar - ikara īṟṟup peyarccoṉ muṉṉar atikārattāṟ kacatapa mutaṉ moḻi vantuḻi, vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci yāyiṉ tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kiḷikkāl ciṟaku tali puṟam eṉa varum. puli nari eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum avi.
iṉi, kiḷikuṟumi kiḷikkuṟumi eṉak kuṇampaṟṟi vanta uṟaḻcci muṭipu mēl ‘valleḻuttu mikiṉum’ (eḻut. 246) eṉṉum cūttirattu ‘olvaḻi yaṟital’ eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.	(33)
ivvīṟṟu iṭiccoṟkaḷum, viṉiyeccamum aṉṉavātal
236.	iṉiaṇi eṉṉuṅ kāliyum iṭaṉum
	viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum cuṭṭu maṉṉa.
iḵtu eytātatu eytuvittatu, ivvīṟṟu iṭiccoṟkum viṉiccoṟkum muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) iṉiaṇi eṉṉuṅ kāliyum iṭaṉum - iṉiyeṉṟum aṇiyeṉṟuñ collappaṭukiṉṟa kālattiyum iṭattiyum uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccollum, viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - ivvīṟṟu viṉiyeccamākiya collum, cuṭṭum  -  ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭākiya iṭic collum, aṉṉa - muṟkūṟiyavāṟē valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	iṉikkoṇṭāṉ aṇikkoṇṭāṉ tēṭik koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, ik koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum varum.
ivviṭic col mūṉṟum ippoḻutu koṇṭāṉ, aṇiya iṭattē koṇṭāṉ, ivviṭattuk koṟṟaṉ eṉa urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vanta vēṟṟumiyātaliṉ vēṟōti muṭittār.	(34)
‘iṉṟi’yatu ikaram ukaramāyt tiriyum ceyyuḷmuṭipu
237.	iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉiyeñ ciṟuti
	niṉṟa ikaram ukara mātal
	toṉṟiyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viṉiyeccattuḷ oṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu iṟuti niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal - iṉṟiyeṉṟu collappaṭum viṉiyeccak kuṟippiṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ikaram ukaramākat tirintu muṭital, toṉṟiyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - paḻaka naṭanta kūṟṟiyuṭiya ceyyuḷuḷ urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘uppiṉṟu puṟki yuṇkamā koṟkiyōṉē’ eṉa varum.
‘niṉṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, viṉiyeccattiṟku muṉ eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka. ‘toṉṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aṉṟi eṉpatūuñ ceyyuḷil immuṭipu eytutal koḷka. ‘iṭaṉaṉṟu tuṟattal valli yōrē’, ‘vāḷaṉṟu piṭiyā vaṉkaṇāṭavar’, ‘nāḷaṉṟupōki’ (puṟam. 124) eṉa varum. muṟṟiyalikaran tirintu kuṟṟiyalukaramāy niṉṟatu.	(35)
ikaraccuṭṭiṉ iyalpu akaraccuṭṭu pōṟal
238.	cuṭṭi ṉiyaṟki muṟkiḷan taṟṟē.
iḵtu ikara īṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyar iyalpu kaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟki - ikara īṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ iyalpu, muṉ kiḷantaṟṟu - muṉ akara īṟṟuc cuṭṭiṟkuk kūṟiya taṉmittām e-ṟu.
eṉṟatu, ‘cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ’ (eḻut. 205 - 208) eṉpatu mutaliya nāṉku cūttirattāṉuṅ kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi; avi meṉkaṇattu melleḻuttu mikutalum, iṭikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇat tum vakaram peṟutaluñ, ceyyuṭ kaṇ vakaraṅ keṭṭuc cuṭṭu nīṭalumām.
e-ṭu:	iññāṇ nūl maṇi eṉavum, ivyāḻ ivvaṭṭu eṉavum, ivvaṭi ivvāṭi ivvili ivvīyam ivvural ivvūrti ivveḻu ivvēṇi ivviyam ivvoṭu ivvōkkam ivvuviyam eṉavum, ‘īvayiṉāṉa’ eṉavum varum.
‘ītukāṇ ṭōṉṟumeñ ciṟunal lūrē’ eṉṟatum ‘kaḷvaṉō vallaṉ kaṇavaṉeṉ kāṟcilampu, koḷḷum vilipporuṭṭāṟ koṉṟārē yītoṉṟu’ (cilap. ūrcūḻvari) eṉṟatum ituveṉṉuñ cuṭṭumutal ukara īṟātaliṉ, atu ceyyuḷakattatu; puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭiyumeṉa uṇarka.	(36)
‘tūṇi’ patakkoṭu muṭital
239.	patakkumuṉ variṉē tūṇik kiḷavi
	mutaṟkiḷan teṭutta vēṟṟumi yiyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷuḷ aḷavup peyaruḷ oṉṟaṟkut tokimarapiṉuḷ eytiya ēyeṉ cāriyi vilakki vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tūṇik kiḷavi muṉ patakku variṉ - tūṇiyākiya aḷavup peyariṉ muṉṉarp patakku eṉṉum aḷavuppeyar varumāyiṉ, mutaṟ kiḷantu eṭutta vēṟṟumi iyaṟṟu - muṉpu vitanteṭutta vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tūṇippatakku eṉa varum. iḵtu ummit toki.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ aṭiyoṭu vantuḻiyum ivviti koḷka. irutūṇip patakku muttūṇip patakku eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘kiḷanteṭutta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ tūṇikkoḷ cāmi tōri pāḷitam eṉap poruṭpeyar muṉ vantuḻiyum, irutūṇik koḷ eṉa atutāṉ aṭiyaṭuttuḻiyun, tūṇittūṇi toṭittoṭi kāṇikkāṇi pūṇip pūṇi eṉat taṉ muṉṉart tāṉ vantuḻiyum ivviti koḷka.  iṉṉum itaṉāṉē taṉ muṉṉart tāṉ vantuḻiyum atutāṉ aṭi yaṭuttu vantuḻiyum ikkuc cāriyi peṟutaluṅkoḷka. tūṇikkut tūṇi irutūṇikkut tūṇi eṉavarum. ivaṟṟuṭ paṇput tokiyum uḷa.(37)
‘nāḻi’ muṉ ‘uri’ vantu muṭiyumāṟu
240.	urivaru kāli nāḻik kiḷavi
	iṟuti ikaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	ṭakara moṟṟum āvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) urivarukāli - nāḻi muṉṉar uri varumoḻiyāy varuṅ kālattu, nāḻik kiḷavi - annāḻi eṉṉuñ col, iṟuti ikaram mey yoṭuṅ keṭum - taṉ iṟutiyiṉiṉṟa ikaran tāṉēṟiya meyyoṭuṅ keṭum, āvayiṉāṉ ṭakaram oṟṟum - avviṭattu ṭakaram oṟṟāy varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāṭuri eṉa varum. itaṉāṉ ēkāram vilakkuṇṭatu.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉ, irunāṭuri munnāṭuri eṉavum oṭṭuka.
‘iṟuti yikaram’ eṉa muṉṉum ōr ikaram uḷḷatu pōlak kūṟiyavata ṉāṉ, īṇṭi nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum nili moḻikaḷāy niṉṟu piṟaporuṭpeyaroṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭitaluṅ koḷka. nāḻikkāyam urikkāyam, cukku tōri pāḷitam eṉavarum.	(38)
‘paṉi’ attum iṉṉum peṟṟumuṭital
241.	paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumik(ku)
	attum iṉṉum cāriyi yākum.
iḵtu ikara īṟṟu vēṟṟumiyuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyi peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉiyeṉa varūuṅ kāla vēṟṟumikku - paṉiyeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa nōyaṉṟik kālatti uṇara niṉṟa vēṟṟumi muṭipuṭiya peyarkku, attum iṉṉum cāriyi ākum - attum iṉṉum cāriyiyāka varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉiyattuk koṇṭāṉ, paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘vēṟṟumi’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉpeṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(39)
‘vaḷi’ eṉum peyarum aṟṟātal
242.	vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum
	avviyal niliyal cevvi teṉpa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūtak kiḷaviyum - vaḷiyeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa iṭakkarallāta aimperum pūtaṅkaḷiṉ oṉṟi uṇara niṉṟa collum, avviyal niliyal cevvi teṉpa - muṉṉark kūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟum avviyalpiṉkaṇ niṟṟal cevviteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vaḷiyattukkoṇṭāṉ, vaḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘cevvitu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉ peṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(40)
‘uti’ marappeyar meli mikal
243.	utimarak kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.
itu marappeyariṉ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) utimarak kiḷavi - utitta leṉṉun toḻilaṉṟi uti eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇara niṉṟa col, melleḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	utiṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
ammuccāriyi vitikkiṉṟa puḷimarattiṉi itaṉpiṉ vittamiyāṉ, utiyaṅkōṭu eṉa itaṟkum ammuppeṟutal koḷka. iḵtu ikkālattu otiyeṉa maruviṟṟu.	(41)
‘puḷi’ marappeyar ammuccāriyi peṟutal
244.	puḷimarak kiḷavikku ammē cāriyi.
itu valleḻuttu vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷimarak kiḷavikku ammē cāriyi - cuviyaṉṟip puḷiyeṉṉum marattiṉi uṇara niṉṟa colliṟku ammeṉṉuñ cāriyi varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
cāriyip pēṟṟiṭi muṉṉarc cūttirattu eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ammuppeṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(42)
cuvippuḷi melleḻuttu mikku muṭital
245.	ēṉip puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉip puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikum - am marap peyaraṉṟic cuvippuḷi uṇara niṉṟa peyar valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiṅkūḻ cāṟu tayir pāḷitam eṉa varum.
pāḷitam - pāṟcōṟu. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka.	(43)
appeyar valimikkum muṭital
246.	valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉa milli
	olvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamilli - cuvippuḷi melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟamilli, olvaḻi aṟital vaḻakkattu āṉa - poruntum iṭam aṟika vaḻakkiṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷikkūḻ cāṟu tayir pāḷitam eṉa varum.
‘olvaḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, puḷiccāṟu pōla ēṉiya vaḻakkup payiṟci ilaveṉṟu koḷka.
‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟukkaṇ eṭuttōttum ilēcumiṉṟi varuvaṉa ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ēṟkumāṟu ceykiyaṟintu muṭittukkoḷka. avi, iṉṉiṉik koṇṭāṉ aṇṇaṇik koṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉa aṭiyaṭuttaliṉ ‘iṉiyaṇi’ (eḻut. 236) eṉṟavaḻi muṭiyāvāy valleḻuttup peṟṟaṉa.
kappitanti ceṉṉitanti eṉpaṉa, ‘aḵṟiṇi viravup peyar’ (eḻut. 155) eṉpataṉāṉ iyalpeytātu, īṇṭu varumoḻit takara akaraṅ keṭṭuk kappinti ceṉṉinti eṉa muṭintaṉa.
kūtāḷi kaṇaviri eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku ammuk koṭuttu ikaraṅ keṭuttuk kūtāḷaṅkōṭu kaṇaviraṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa muṭikka. ‘kūtaḷa naṟumpū’ eṉak kuṟintum varum.
iṉi, ivi makara īṟāyum vaḻaṅkum. atu ‘veṇkū tāḷattut taṇpūṅ kōtiyar’ (paṭṭi. 85) eṉa attup peṟṟu makaraṅ keṭṭum, ‘kaṇavira māli yiṭūuk kaḻintaṉṉa’ (akam. 31) eṉa makaraṅ keṭṭum, kaṇaviraṅkōṭu eṉa melleḻuttup peṟṟum niṟkum.
kaṭṭi eṉa niṟutti, iṭi akal eṉat tantu ṭakarattiṉ ikaraṅ keṭuttuk kaṭṭiṭi kaṭṭakal eṉa muṭikka. paruttikkuc ceṉṟāṉeṉa iyipu valleḻuttum ikkuṅ koṭuttu muṭikka. tuḷiyattuk koṇṭāṉ tuḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum iṉṉum koṭuttu muṭikka. ‘puḷiṅkāy vēṭkit taṉṟu’ (aiṅkuṟu. 51) eṉavum, puḷimpaḻam eṉavum ammuppeṟātu melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitaluṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttuk keṭuttuk kiḷiyiṉ kāl, puḷiyiṉ kōṭu, utiyiṉ kōṭu eṉa muṭikka.	(44)
ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyar āṉ cāriyi peṟutal
247.	nāḷmuṉ tōṉṟun toḻilnilik kiḷavikku
	āṉiṭi varutal aiya miṉṟē.
iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttu vilakki, āṉ cāriyi vitikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷ muṉ tōṉṟun toḻil nilik kiḷavikku  - ikara īṟṟu nāṭpeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟun toḻiṟcoṟku, āṉ iṭi varutal aiyamiṉṟu - āṉ cāriyi iṭi vantu muṭital aiyamiṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ, cōtiyāṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘aiyamiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. itaṟkuk kaṇṇeṉ urupu virikka.	(45)
tiṅkaṭpeyar ikkuc cāriyi peṟutal
248.	tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ ikkē cāriyi.
iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ikku vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ ikkē cāriyi - tiṅkaḷi uṇara niṉṟa ikara īṟṟup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart toḻiṉilik kiḷavi variṉ varuñ cāriyi ikkuc cāriyiyām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭikkukkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ  tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa iyipu valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭikka. itaṟkum kaṇṇeṉ urupu virikka.	(46)
īkāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
249.	īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu īkāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - īkāra īṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	īkkaṭitu, tīkkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	(47)
ivvīṟṟuppeyaruḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉa
250.	nīeṉ peyarum iṭakkarp peyarum
	mīeṉa marīiya iṭamvari kiḷaviyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkalum, eytātatu eytuvittaluṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ peyarum iṭakkarppeyarum - nī yeṉṉum peyarum iṭakkarp peyarākiya īkāra īṟṟup peyarum, mī eṉa marī iya iṭamvari kiḷaviyum - mī eṉṟu colla maruvāy vaḻaṅkiṉa ōriṭatti varintu uṇarttuñ collum, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākum - puṇaru miṭattu muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nī kuṟiyi ciṟiyi tīyi periyi eṉavum, pīkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum iviyiṟṟukkup potuvāṉ eytiya valleḻuttu vilakkuṇṭaṉa. mīkaṇ cevi tali puṟam; iḵtu ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya maruvātaliṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
nī eṉpatu ‘aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyaruḷ’ (eḻut. 155) aṭaṅkātō veṉiṉ, mēl niṉki yeṉat tirintu muṭitaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. mīkaṇ eṉpatu mēliṭattuk kaṇṇeṉa vēṟṟumi eṉiṉum, iyalpu paṟṟi uṭaṉ kūṟiṉār.	(48)
avaṟṟuḷ, ‘mī’ valleḻuttu mikutalum peṟutal
251.	iṭamvari kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum
	uṭaṉili moḻiyum uḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu valleḻuttu mikuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu ikantu paṭāmaṟ kāttatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭam vari kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum - iṭatti varintu uṇarttum mīyeṉṉuñ colliṉ muṉṉar iyalpāy muṭitalē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum, uṭaṉili moḻiyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa - tammil ōciyiyintu niṟṟaliyuṭiya moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mīkkōḷ, mīppal eṉa varum.
‘uṭaṉili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ mīṅkuḻi mīntōl eṉa melleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka.	(49)
ivvīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
252.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu īkāra īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu - īkāra īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻipōla valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	īkkāl ciṟaku tali puṟam, tīkkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉa varum.	(50)
‘nī’ niṉ ātal
253.	nīeṉ orupeyar urupiyal niliyum
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu; valleḻuttu vilakki ṉakaravoṟṟē peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) nī eṉ oru peyar urupiyal niliyum - nīyeṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi urupu puṇarccikkaṇ neṭumutal kuṟuki ṉakaram oṟṟi niṉṟāṟpōla īṇṭup poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭiyum, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākum - avvāṟu muṭipuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu mikātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	niṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
iḵtu īkāra īṟu ikara īṟāy, ikara īṟu ṉakara īṟāy niṉṟuḻiyum ‘nīyeṉorupeyar’ eṉṟaliṉ tirintataṉ tiripatuvēyāyiṟṟu. ‘iyaṟkiyākum’ eṉavē nilimoḻittoḻil atikāra valleḻutti vilakkātāyiṟṟu.	(51)
ukara īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
254.	ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ukara īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu - ukara īṟṟup peyar al vaḻikkaṇ akara īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avval leḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṭukkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.	(52)
ukaraccuṭṭum vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi valimikutal
255.	cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu ukara īṟṟuc cuṭṭu vaṉkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟṟu ākum - ukara īṟṟuc cuṭ ṭiṉ muṉṉum valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ukkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉa varum.	(53)
piṟakaṇam variṉ, akaraccuṭṭiṉ iyalpiṟṟātal
256.	ēṉavi variṉē mēlnili iyala.
iḵtu ukara īṟṟuc cuṭṭu oḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷoṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉavi variṉ - ukara īṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉmuṉ valleḻut tallāta meṉkaṇam mutaliya mūṉṟum variṉ, mēlnili iyala - akara īṟṟuc cuṭṭu muṭintāṟpōla ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ oṟṟumikkum yavavariṉum uyirvariṉum vakaram oṟṟiyuñ ceyyuḷil nīṇṭum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uññāṇ nūl maṇi eṉavum, uvyāḻ uvvaṭṭu eṉavum, uvvaṭi uvvāṭi eṉavum, ūvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum.	(54)
cuṭṭumutal ukaram iyalpāka muṭital
257.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti iyalpā kummē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki iyalpu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara īṟṟup peyar, iyalpākum - muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atu kuṟitu, itu kuṟitu, utu kuṟitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum.
muṟkūṟiyavi cuṭṭumāttiri, ivi cuṭṭuppeyarāka uṇarka.(55)
ataṉmuṉṉar ‘aṉṟu’ varuvaḻiyum,
‘ai’ varuvaḻiyum ceyyuḷmuṭipu
258.	aṉṟuvaru kāli āvā kutalum
	aivaru kāli meyvarintu keṭutalum
	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ uritteṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuc cuṭṭumutaṟpeyarkku oru ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aṉṟu varukāli ā ākutalum - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa cuṭṭumutal ukara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar aṉṟeṉṉum viṉikkuṟippuc col varuṅkālattu attakaravoṟṟiṉmēl ēṟi niṉṟa ukaram ākāramāyt tirintu muṭitalum, aivarukāli meyvarintu keṭutalum - ataṉ muṉṉar aiyeṉṉuñ cāriyi varuṅkālattu attakaravoṟṟu niṟka ataṉ mēl ēṟiya ukaraṅ keṭutalum, ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ uritteṉamoḻipa - ceyyuṭkaṇ uritteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atāaṉṟamma, itāaṉṟamma, utāaṉṟamma, ‘atā aṉṟeṉpa veṇpā yāppē’ (cey. 82) eṉavum, ati maṟṟamma, itimaṟṟamma, utimaṟṟamma eṉavum varum.
‘moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟi muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ataṉṟu itaṉṟu utaṉṟu eṉa ukaraṅ keṭṭut takaravoṟṟu niṟṟal koḷka.	(56)
ukara īṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumimuṭipu
259.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - ukara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu - akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṭukkāy cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kaṭukkaṭumi eṉavum varum.	(57)
eru, ceru - eṉpavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu
260.	eruvum ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇit
	tiripiṭa ṉuṭiya teriyuṅ kāli
	ammiṉ makaram ceruvayiṉ keṭumē
	tammoṟṟu mikūum valleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vitiyum, oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉoṭu cāriyi vitiyum, cāriyi peṟātavaḻi valleḻuttu melleḻuttup pēṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eruvum ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇi - eruveṉṉuñ collum ceruveṉṉuñ collum ammuccāriyiyoṭu porunti, tiripu iṭaṉuṭiya teriyuṅ kāli - atikāra valleḻuttup peṟāmal tiriyum iṭaṉuṭiya ārāyuṅkālattu; ammiṉ makarañ ceruvayiṟ keṭum - āṇṭu ammuccāriyiyiṉatu īṟṟiṉ makarañ ceruveṉṉuñ colliṭattuk keṭṭu muṭiyum; valleḻuttu mikūum - āṇṭu ceruviṉkaṇ valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; iyaṟkit tam oṟṟu mikūum - ammup peṟātavaḻi iraṇṭaṟkun tamakku iyaṟkiyākiya valloṟṟum melloṟṟum mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eruveṉa niṟuttik, kuḻi cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉat tantu, ammukkoṭuttu ‘ammiṉiṟuti kacatakkāli’ (eḻut. 129) eṉpataṉāṉ, eruvaṅkuḻi cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉa muṭikka. ceruveṉa niṟuttik kaḷam cēṉi tāṉi paṟi eṉat tantu iṭi ammukkoṭuttu makaraṅ keṭuttu valleḻuttuk koṭuttuc ceruvakkaḷam cēṉitāṉi paṟiyeṉa muṭikka.
iṉi ammup peṟātavaḻi erukkuḻi eruṅkuḻi eṉa valleḻuttum melleḻuttuṅ koṭuttu muṭikka. iṉic ceruviṟku ēṟpuḻik kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉcerukkaḷameṉa valleḻuttē koṭuttu muṭikka.
‘teriyuṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, eruviṉkuṟumi ceruviṉ kaṭumi eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻtalum, eruvañāṟci ceruvañāṟci eṉa iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ ammup peṟutalum koḷka. makaram, ‘meṉmiyu miṭimiyum’ (eḻut. 130) eṉpataṉāṟ keṭukka.
‘tammoṟṟu mikūum’ eṉa uṭampoṭu puṇarttuc cūttirañ ceytaliṉ, ukaram nīṭavarutaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	varūum, tarūum, paṭūum eṉa varum.	(58)
ḻakara ukara īṟṟuccol muṭiyumāṟu
261.	ḻakara ukaram nīṭiṭaṉ uṭittē
	ukaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu; valleḻuttiṉōṭu ukaram peṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) ḻakara ukaram nīṭiṭaṉ uṭittu - ukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷuḷ ḻakarattoṭu kūṭiya ukara īṟṟuccol nīṇṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu; āvayiṉāṉ ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram vantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eḻūukkatavu ciṟi tāṉi paṭi eṉavarum.
‘nīṭiṭaṉuṭittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nīḷātum ukaram peṟātum varumāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	kuḻuttōṟṟam eṉa varum.
iṉṉum itaṉāṟ paḻukkāy eṉa alvaḻikkaṇṇum iv vitiyiṉṟi varutal koḷka.
‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, perumpāṉmi ceyyuṭkaṇ nīṇṭu ukaram peṟṟu,
‘eḻūuttāṅkiya katavumalittavar
kuḻūukkaḷiṟṟuk kuṟumpuṭittaliṉ’	(puṟam. 97)
eṉavum,
‘paḻūuppal laṉṉa paruvukirp pāvaṭi’	(kuṟun. 180)
eṉavum varutal koḷka.	(59)
‘oṭu’ marappeyar melleḻuttu mikutal
262.	oṭumarak kiḷavi utimara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu avvīṟṟu marappeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṭu marak kiḷavi - oṭuveṉṉum marattiṉi uṇara niṉṟa col, utimara iyaṟṟu - utiyeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy mel leḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṭuṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
‘maram’ eṉṟār, oṭu veṉṉum nōyi nīkkutaṟku. muṉṉar utimarattiṉ piṉṉar ammup peṟukiṉṟa puḷimaram vitta iyipāṉ, itaṟkum ammup pēṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	oṭuvaṅkōṭu eṉavarum.	(60)
cuṭṭumutal ukaram (vēṟṟumikkaṇ) muṭiyumāṟu
263.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal niliyum
	oṟṟiṭi mikāa valleḻut tiyaṟki.
itu cuṭṭup peyarkku valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti urupiyal niliyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum urupu puṇarcciyiṟ kūṟiya iyalpilē niṉṟu aṉcāriyi peṟṟu ukaraṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum, valleḻuttu iyaṟki oṟṟu iṭi mikāa-valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikā e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ataṉkōṭu, itaṉkōṭu, utaṉkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
‘oṟṟiṭi mikā’ eṉavē, cāriyi vakuppa valleḻuttu vīḻā veṉpatu peṟṟām. ‘valleḻut tiyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ukara īṟṟuḷ eruvuñceruvum oḻittu ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. kaṭuviṉkuṟi, oṭuviṉkuṟi, eḻuviṉpuṟam, koḻuviṉ kūrmi eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ‘utukkāṇ’ (akam. 4) eṉṟa vaḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum koḷka.	(61)
ūkāra īṟṟuppeyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
264.	ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟē.
itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē ūkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu - ūkāra īṟṟup peyar alvaḻikkaṇ ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaḻūukkaṭitu, koṇmūkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum.	(62)
ivvīṟṟu viṉiyeccamum ēvalorumiyum aṉṉavātal
265.	viṉiyeñcu kiḷavikkum muṉṉili moḻikkum
	niṉiyuṅ kāli avvaki variyār.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viṉiyeccattiṟku mikku muṭiyum eṉṟaliṉ, eytātatu eytuvittatūum, muṉṉili viṉikku iyalpum uṟaḻpum (151) māṟṟutaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiyatūum nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉiyeñcu kiḷavikkum - ūkāra īṟṟu viṉiyecca mākiya coṟkum, muṉṉili moḻikkum - muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkum, niṉiyuṅkāli avvaki variyār - ārāyuṅ kālattu avval leḻuttu mikku muṭiyuṅ kūṟṟiṉi nīkkār e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṇṇūk koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, kitūkkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum varum.
‘niṉiyuṅkāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarkkum alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭikka. āṭūuk kuṟiyaṉ makaṭūuk kuṟiyaḷ eṉa varum. uyartiṇippeyar eṭuttōtiyē muṭippārātaliṉ ammuṭipu peṟāmiyiṉ, īṟṟup potuvitiyāṉ muṭiyātu ilēcāṉ muṭittām.	(63)
ivvīṟṟu vēṟṟumimuṭipu
266.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu ūkāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu - ūkāra īṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻi pōla valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaḻūuk kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi, koṇmūk kuḻām celavu tōṟṟam paṟivu eṉa varum.	(64)
ukara eḻuttuppēṟu tōṉṟumiṭaṉ
267.	kuṟṟeḻut timparum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum ukarak kiḷavi.
iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ukaram vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟeḻuttu imparum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉ vanta ūkāra īṟṟu moḻikkum, ōreḻuttu moḻikkum - ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya ūkāra īṟṟu moḻikkum, ukarakkiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - ukaramākiya eḻuttu niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uṭūukkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉavum, tūuk kuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉavum varum.
‘niṟṟal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, uyartiṇip peyarkkum valleḻuttum ukaramum koṭukka. āṭūukki makaṭūukki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum. ivi tokimarapiṉuḷ iyalpātal eytiyavaṟṟi (153) īṇṭu iruvaḻikkaṇṇum muṭittār; īṟṟup potu oppumi kaṇṭu.	(65)
‘pū’ mēliviti peṟāmi
268.	pūeṉ orupeyar āyiyal piṉṟē
	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē.
iḵtu ūkāra īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum iyipu valleḻuttum vilakkip, perumpāṉmi melleḻuttuñ ciṟupāṉmi valleḻut tum peṟumeṉa (eytiyatu vilakki)p piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) pūveṉ oru peyar a iyalpu iṉṟu - pūveṉṉum ūkāra īṟṟiyuṭiya oru peyar mēṟkūṟiya ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum avviyal iṉmiyi uṭittu, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - avviṭattu melleḻuttu mikku muṭitalē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pūṅkoṭi cōli tāmam pantu eṉavum, pūkkoṭi ceyki tāmam pantu eṉavum varum.
‘pū’ veṉpatu, poliveṉṉum viṉikkuṟippi uṇarttātu niṟṟaṟku ‘oru peyar’ eṉṟār.	(66)
‘ū’ eṉum peyar ṉakara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭital
269.	ūeṉ orupeyar āvoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu, ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki ṉakaram vitittaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) ūveṉ oru peyar - ūveṉat taciyi uṇartti niṉṟa ōreḻuttorumoḻi, āvoṭu civaṇum - ākāra īṟṟil āveṉṉuñ col valleḻuttup peṟātu ṉakara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭintāṟpōla ṉakara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ūveṉa niṟutti ṉakara oṟṟak koṭuttu, ūṉ kuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉa muṭikka.
ū eṉpatu taciyi uṇartti niṉṟa vaḻakku, āciriyar nūl ceyta kālattu vaḻakku. aṉṟit tēya vaḻakkēṉum uṇarka.	(67)
appeyar ṉakara oṟṟōṭu akkum peṟutal
270.	akkeṉ cāriyi peṟutalum urittē
	takkavaḻi aṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) akkeṉ cāriyi peṟutalum urittu - atikārattāṉ niṉṟa ū veṉṉum peyar muṟkūṟiya ṉakarattōṭu akkeṉṉuñ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭitalum urittu, vaḻakkattāṉa takkavaḻi aṟital - ammuṭipu vaḻakkiṭattut takka iṭam aṟika e-ṟu.
‘takkavaḻi aṟital’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, cāriyi peṟṟuḻi ṉakaram vilakkuṇṇātu niṟṟalum, muṉ māṭṭēṟṟāl vilakkuṇṭa valleḻuttuk keṭātu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ūṉakkuṟi ceyki talipuṟam eṉa varum.
‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ūkāra īṟṟuc colliṟku urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttuk keṭukka.
e-ṭu:	koṇmūviṉ kuḻām, uṭūuviṉṟali, ūviṉkuṟi eṉa varum.	(68)
āṭūu makaṭūup peyarkaḷ iṉ cāriyi peṟalumām eṉal
271.	āṭūu makaṭūu āyiru peyarkkum
	iṉiṭi variṉum māṉa milli.
itu ‘kuṟṟeḻuttimparum’ (eḻut. ) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘niṟṟal’ eṉṟa ilēcāṉ eytiya valleḻuttēyaṉṟic cāriyiyum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappu viti uṇarttiyatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṭūu makaṭūu āyiru peyarkkum - āṭūu makaṭūuvākiya uyar tiṇippeyar iraṇṭaṟkum, iṉiṭi variṉum māṉam illi - muṉṉeytiya valleḻuttē yaṉṟi iṉ cāriyi iṭiyē variṉum kuṟṟamilli e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭūuviṉki, makaṭūuviṉki, cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
‘māṉamilli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉpeṟṟuḻi mēl eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(69)
ekaramum okaramum īṟāka varumiṭaṉ
272.	ekara okaram peyarkkī ṟākā
	muṉṉili moḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar
	tēṟṟamum ciṟappum alvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu ekara okaram īṟām iṭam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tēṟṟamum ciṟappum alvaḻi āṉa - teḷivup poruḷuñ ciṟappup poruḷum allāta vēṟṟumip poruṇmiyiṭattu aḷapeṭuttuk kūṟutaliṉ uḷavākiya, ekara okaram peyarkku īṟākā - ekara okaraṅkaḷ peyarkku īṟāy vārā, viṉikku īṟāy varum, muṉṉili moḻipa eṉmaṉār pulavar - avitān taṉmiyiṉum paṭarkkiyiṉum vārā, muṉṉilic colliṭattaṉavāmeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
eṉavē, teḷivup poruḷiṉuñ ciṟappup poruḷiṉum muṟiyē vantu peyarkku īṟām iṭiccollākiya ekara okaram mūṉṟiṭattiṟkum uriyavāmeṉṟu poruḷāyiṟṟu. eṉa, iṅṅaṉam aruttāpattiyāṟ koṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam mēlic cūttirattāṟ kūṟupa.
e-ṭu:	ēekkoṟṟā, ōokkoṟṟā, cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa varum.
ivi, eṉakku oru karumampaṇi eṉavum, iṅṅaṉañ ceykiṉṟa taṉi oḻi eṉavum muṉṉiliyēvaṟ poruḷavāy vantaṉa. itaṟku valleḻuttup peṟumāṟu mēlē kūṟupa.	(70)
tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ okaramum vali mikappeṟāmi
273.	tēṟṟa ekaramum ciṟappiṉ ovvum
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā.
iḵtu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmaṟ kāttu eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) tēṟṟa ekaramuñ ciṟappiṉ ovvum mēṟkūṟu iyaṟki - muṉṉar aruttāpattiyāṟ peyarkkaṇ varumeṉṟa tēṟṟap poruṇmiyiṉ ekaramuñ ciṟappupporuṇmiyiṉ okaramum mūṉṟiṭattum varumeṉṟa ilakkaṇattaṉavām; valleḻuttu mikā - avi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyā; eṉavē muṉṉilikkaṇ varumeṉṟa ekara okaraṅkaḷ valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉēekoṇṭēṉ, nīyēekoṇṭāy, avaṉēe koṇṭāṉ eṉavum; yāṉōokoṭiyēṉ, nīyōo koṭiyi, avaṉōokoṭiyaṉ eṉavum peyarkkaṇ īṟāy iyalpāy vantavāṟu kāṇka.
itu muṉṉar eytiya ilakkaṇam ikavāmaṟkāttār. muṉniṉṟa cūttirattiṉ muṉṉilikkum valleḻuttu mikuttu eytātatu eytuvit tār. iccūttirattiṟku aḷapeṭuttal ‘teḷiviṉēyum’ (col. 261) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ koḷka. eṉavē, muṭivu peṟṟuḻi iṅṅaṉam iṭiccollum eṭuttōtip puṇarppa reṉpatūum peṟṟām.	(71)
ēkāra īṟṟuppeyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
274.	ēkāra iṟuti ūkāra iyaṟṟē.
itu niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē ēkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēkāra iṟuti - ēkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻik kaṇ, ūkāra iyaṟṟu - ūkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēkkaṭitu, cēkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.	(72)
iv(vīṟṟu) iṭiccoṟkaḷ iyalpāy muṭital
275.	māṟukōḷ eccamum viṉāvum eṇṇum
	kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu iṭiccoṟkaḷ iyalpāyp puṇarkaveṉa eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) māṟukōḷ eccamum - māṟukōṭali yuṭiya eccap poruṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, viṉāvum - viṉāp poruṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, eṇṇum - eṇṇup poruṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra vīṟṟu iṭic collum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākum - muṟkūṟiya valleḻut tup peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉēkoṇṭēṉ ceṉṟēṉ tantēṉ pōyiṉēṉ eṉpuḻi, yāṉkoṇṭilēṉeṉa māṟukoṇṭa oḻivupaṭa niṉṟatu. nīyē koṇṭāy ceṉṟāy tantāy pōyiṉāy eṉavum, nilaṉē nīrē tīyē vaḷiyē, koṟṟaṉē cāttaṉē eṉavum varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, pirinili ēkāramum īṟṟaci ēkāramum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. avaruḷ ivaṉē koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ‘kaḻiyē, ciṟukura ṉeytaloṭu pāṭō vātē; kaṭalē, pāṭeḻun tolikkum’ (akam. 150) eṉavum varum.	(73)
ivvīṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
276.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - ēkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu - ūkāra īṟṟu alvaḻi pōla valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēkkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum, vēkkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavum varum.
vēkkuṭam - vētaliyuṭiya kuṭameṉa viriyum.	(74)
ē eṉṉum iṟuti ekaram peṟumāṟu
277.	ēyeṉ iṟutikku ekaram varumē.
itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ekaram vitittaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēyeṉ iṟutikku ekaram varum - avvēṟṟumikkaṇ ēyeṉṉum iṟutikku ekaram varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēekkoṭṭil cāli tuḷi puḻi eṉa varum.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum varumeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ēeñekiḻcci nērmi eṉa varum.
‘uriyiṟ kōṭalāṉ’ ekaram ēṟpuḻik koḷka.	(75)
cē eṉum marappeyar meli mikutal
278.	cēeṉ marappeyar oṭumara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.
(i-ḷ.) cē eṉ marappeyar - peṟṟamaṉṟic cēveṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa peyar, oṭumara iyaṟṟu - oṭumaram pōla melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cēṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.	(76)
appeyar peṟṟamāyiṉ iṉcāriyi peṟutal
279.	peṟṟam āyiṉ muṟṟiṉ vēṇṭum.
iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttu vilakki iṉ vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) peṟṟam āyiṉ - muṟkūṟiya cēveṉpatu peṟṟattiṉi uṇarttiya poḻutāyiṉ, muṟṟa iṉ vēṇṭum - muṭiya iṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyavēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cēviṉkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, muṟkūṟiya cēveṉṉum marappeyarkkum ēveṉpataṟkum urupiṟku eytiya cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻtal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	cēviṉkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, ēviṉ kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum varum.
cāriyippēṟu varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattum iṉpeṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	cēviṉalam maṇi vāl, cēviṉimil, cēviṉaṭi, cēviṉāṭṭam eṉa varum.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉ peṟātu varutaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ceyyuṭkaṇ ‘teṉṟaṟku vīṇikkuc cēmaṇikkuk kōkilattiṟku, aṉṟiṟku’ eṉa varum.	(77)
aikāra īṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
280.	aikāra iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. tokimarapiṉuḷ ‘vēṟṟumiyalvaḻi iai yeṉṉum’ (eḻut. 158) eṉpataṉkaṇ alvaḻi muṭittār.
(i-ḷ.) aikāra iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar - aikāra īṟṟup peyarccoṉ muṉṉar atikārattāṟ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi, vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum - vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci yāyiṉ tamakkup poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉikkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
‘vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum yāṉiyik koṇarntāṉeṉa valleḻuttu mikutal koḷka.	(78)
cuṭṭu mutal ‘ai’ īṟu vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭital
281.	cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal niliyum.
itu valleḻuttiṉoṭu vaṟṟu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal iṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuppeyar, urupiyal niliyum - urupu puṇarcciyiṟ kūṟiya iyalpu pōlap poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aviyaṟṟukkōṭu, iviyaṟṟukkōṭu, uviyaṟṟuk kōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
itaṉi ‘vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa’ (eḻut. 122) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka.(79)
vici, ñemi, nami - immarappeyarkaḷ meli mikutal
282.	vicimarak kiḷaviyum ñemiyum namiyum
	āmup peyarum cēmara iyala.
itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) vicimarak kiḷaviyum - vicittaṟṟoḻilaṉṟi vici yeṉṉum maratti uṇaraniṉṟa collum, ñemiyum - ñemi yeṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa collum, namiyum - nami eṉṉum marattiṉi uṇara niṉṟa collum āmup peyarum - ākiya ammūṉṟu peyarum, cēmara iyala - valleḻuttu mikātu cēmaram pōla mel leḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viciṅkōṭu, ñemiṅkōṭu, namiṅkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
ivi, ‘kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttu’ (eḻut. 143) eṉṟu uṇarka.	(80)
paṉi, ari, āviri - ippeyarkaḷ muṭiyumāṟu
283.	paṉiyum ariyum āvirik kiḷaviyum
	niṉiyuṅ kāli ammoṭu civaṇum
	aiyeṉ iṟuti arivarintu keṭumē
	meyavaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttu vilakki ammu vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉiyum ariyum āvirik kiḷaviyum - paṉiyeṉṉum peyarum ariyeṉṉum peyarum āviriyeṉṉum peyarum, niṉiyuṅkāli ammoṭu civaṇum - ārāyuṅ kālattu valleḻuttu mikātu ammuc cāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum; aiyeṉ iṟuti ari varintu keṭum - avviṭattu ai yeṉṉum īṟu ariyeṉṉuñ colli nīkki ēṉi iraṇṭaṟkuṅkeṭum; mey avaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meykeṭātu accol liṭattē niṟka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉi āviri eṉa niṟutti, ammu varuvittu aikāraṅ keṭuttu oṟṟiṉmēlē ammiṉ akaramēṟṟip, paṉaṅkāy āviraṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum. ariyeṉa niṟutti ammuk koṭuttu aikāraṅ keṭātu, ariyaṅ kōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa muṭikka.
valleḻuttuk kēṭu mēlē ‘kaṭiniliyiṉṟu’ (eḻut. 285) eṉṟata ṉāṟ kūṟutum. ‘niṉiyuṅkāli’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ, tūtuṇi vaḻutuṇi tilli ōli tāḻi eṉa niṟutti, ammukkoṭuttu aikāraṅkeṭuttut tūtuṇaṅkāy vaḻutuṇaṅkāy tillaṅkāy  ōlampōḻ tāḻaṅkāy eṉa muṭikka.	(81)
paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar ‘aṭṭu’p puṇarumāṟu
284.	paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭuvaru kāli
	niliyiṉ ṟākum aiyeṉ uyirē
	ākāram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
itu nilimoḻic ceyki nōkki eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭuvaru kāli - muṟkūṟiya vāṟaṉṟip paṉiyeṉṉum coṉmuṉṉar aṭṭeṉṉuñ col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, niliyiṉṟu ākum aiyeṉ uyir - niṟṟalilliyākum aiyeṉṉum uyir; āvayiṉāṉ ākāram varutal - avviṭattu ākāram vantu ammeymmēlēṟi muṭika e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉāaṭṭu eṉa varum. itaṟku mūṉṟāvatum āṟāvatum viriyum.
‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ōrānayam viccāvāti eṉṉum vēṟṟumi muṭipum, kēṭṭāmūlam pāṟāṅkal eṉṉum alvaḻi muṭipuṅ koḷka. ivaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻikaḷi maṟuttalum oṉṟu.	(82)
‘koṭi’ vantu puṇarumāṟu
285.	koṭimuṉ variṉē aiyavaṇ niṟpak
	kaṭinili yiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti.
itumēl aikāraṅ keṭuttu ammuppeṟuka eṉṟār; īṇṭu atu keṭātu niṟka valleḻuttup peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu ikantu paṭāmaṟ kāttatu. ammu vilakki valleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatumām.
(i-ḷ.) paṉimuṉ koṭi variṉ - paṉiyeṉṉuñ coṉ muṉṉark koṭiyeṉṉuñ col variṉ, ai avaṇ niṟpa - kēṭu ōtiya aikāram āṇṭuk keṭātu niṟpa, valleḻuttu mikuti kaṭiniliyiṉṟu - valleḻuttu mikku muṭital nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉikkoṭi eṉa varum. itaṟku iraṇṭāvatum mūṉṟāvatum viriyum.
‘kaṭiniliyiṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aikāra īṟṟuppeyarkaḷellām eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum ammuccāriyiyum piṟacāriyiyum peṟṟuḻi atikāra valleḻuttuk keṭuttuk koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, urupiṟkucceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. paṉiyiṉ kuṟi ariyiṉ kōṭu āviriyiṉ kōṭu viciyiṉ kōṭu ñemiyiṉ kōṭu namiyiṉ kōṭu eṉavum, tūtuṇiyiṉ kāy vaḻutuṇiyiṉ kāy uḻiyiṉ kōṭu vaḻiyiṉ kōṭu eṉavum varum.
paṉitiraḷ, paṉittiraḷ, eṉṉum uṟaḻccimuṭipu toki marapiṉuṭ puṟaṉaṭiyāṟ koḷka; alvaḻiyu mātaliṉ. aṉṟi, īṇṭu ‘avaṇ’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷvārum uḷar.	(83)
ivvīṟṟut tiṅkaṭpeyarum nāṭpeyarum muṭiyumāṟu
286.	tiṅkaḷum nāḷum muntukiḷan taṉṉa.
iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ikkuc cāriyiyum, valleḻuttu vilakki āṉ cāriyiyum vakuttaliṉ, eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappuvitiyum, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituvitiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tiṅkaḷum nāḷum - aikāra īṟṟut tiṅkaḷi uṇara niṉṟa peyarum nāḷi uṇaraniṉṟa peyarum, muntu kiḷantaṉṉa - ikara īṟṟut tiṅkaḷum nāḷumpōla ikkum āṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cittirikkuk koṇṭāṉ, kēṭṭiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
cittiri nāḷāyiṉ āṉ cāriyi koṭukka. valleḻuttukkēṭu muṉṉark ‘kaṭiniliyiṉṟu’ (eḻut. 285) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
tiṅkaḷ muṟkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, uḻiṅkōṭu amiṅkōṭu uṭiṅkōṭu eṉa melleḻuttuk koṭuttum, kaliṅ kōṭu kalikkōṭu eṉa uṟaḻcci eytuvittum, kariyavaṟṟukkōṭu kuṟiyavaṟṟukkōṭu neṭiyavaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa aikāra īṟṟup paṇpukoḷ peyarkku vaṟṟukkoṭuttu aikāraṅ keṭuttu vaṟṟumici yoṟṟeṉṟu oṟṟuk keṭuttum, aviyattuk koṇṭāṉ aviyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum iṉṉum koṭuttum, paṉiyiṉmāṇpu kēṭṭiyi ṉāṭṭiṉāṉeṉa iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉ cāriyi koṭuttum muṭikka. aikāra īṟu iṉcāriyi peṟutal toki marapiṉuṭ kūṟāmiyiṉ īṇṭuk koṇṭām.	(84)
‘maḻi’ attum iṉṉum peṟṟumuṭital
287.	maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiyal niliyum.
itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu vitiyum, iyipu valleḻuttu vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituvitiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi - maḻiyeṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuc col, vaḷiyiyal niliyum - vaḷiyeṉṉuñ col attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maḻiyattukkoṇṭāṉ, maḻiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
īṇṭu iṉpeṟṟuḻi valleḻuttukkēṭu ‘kaṭiniliyiṉṟu’   (eḻut. 285) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. cāriyippēṟu varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇuṅ koḷka. maḻiyattu ñāṉṟāṉ, maḻiyiṉ ñāṉṟāṉ niṟuttiṉāṉ māṭṭiṉāṉ vantāṉ aṭintāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka.	(85)
‘vēṭki’ muṉṉar ‘avā’p puṇarum ceyyuḷmuṭipu
288.	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ vēṭki eṉṉum
	aiyeṉ iṟuti avāmuṉ variṉē
	meyyoṭuṅ keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar
	ṭakāram ṇakāra mātal vēṇṭum.
itu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ vēṭki eṉṉum aiyeṉ iṟuti - ceyyuḷiṭattu vēṭkiyeṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuc col, avā muṉvariṉ - avā veṉṉuñ coṟku muṉṉar variṉ, meyyoṭuṅ keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - avvikāram tāṉ ūrnta meyyoṭuṅkūṭak keṭumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu niṉṟa ṭakāra oṟṟu ṇakāra oṟṟāyt tirital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘vēṇavā naliya veyya vuyirā’ (naṟ. 61) eṉa varum.
vēṭkiyāvatu poruḷkaḷmēl tōṉṟum paṟṟuḷḷam. avāvāvatu, apporuḷkaḷip peṟavēṇṭumeṉṟu mēṉmēl nikaḻum āci, eṉavē, vēṭkiyā luṇṭākiya avāveṉa mūṉṟaṉurupu virintatu. itaṉi vēṭkiyum avāvumeṉa alvaḻi yeṉpārum uḷar. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟuvār pāṟaṅkal eṉpataṉi ammuk koṭuttu īṇṭu muṭippar.	(86)
ōkāra īṟṟup peyaratu alvaḻi muṭipu
289.	ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ōkāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟu - ōkāra īṟṟup peyarccol ēkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōkkaṭitu, cōkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.	(87)
ivvīṟṟu iṭiccol iyalpāy muṭital
290.	māṟukōḷ eccamum viṉāvum aiyamum
	kūṟiya valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu iṭiccol muṭipu kūṟaliṉ, eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) māṟukōḷ eccamum - māṟukōṭali uṭiya eccap poruṇmiyi oḻipākavuṭiya ōkāramum, viṉāvum - viṉāp poruṇmiyiyuṭiya ōkāramum, aiyamum - aiyap poruṇmiyi yuṭiya ōkāramum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākum - muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉō koṇṭēṉ eṉavum, nīyō koṇṭāy eṉavum, pattōpatiṉoṉṟō, puṟṟōputalō eṉavum varum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘yāṉō tēṟē ṉavarpoy vaḻaṅkalarē’ (kuṟun. 21) eṉap piriniliyum, naṉṟō tītō kaṇṭatu eṉat terinili yum, ōokoṇṭāṉ eṉac ciṟappum, ‘kuṉṟuṟaḻnta kaḷiṟeṉkō koyyuḷiya māveṉkō’ (puṟam. 387) eṉa eṇṇuniliyum val leḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. itaṉāṉē īṟṟaci varumēṉum uṇarka.	(88)
291.	oḻintataṉ niliyum moḻintavaṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oḻintataṉ niliyum moḻintavaṟṟu iyaṟṟu - oḻiyici ōkārattiṉatu niliyum muṟkūṟiya ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	koḷalō koṇṭāṉ, celalō ceṉṟāṉ, taralō tantāṉ, pōtalō pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ōci vēṟṟumiyāṉ orucol tōṉṟap poruḷ tantu niṟkum.	(89)
ōkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
292.	vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē
	okaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu ōkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu - ōkāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum avvēkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu ottu valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; āvayiṉāṉ okaram varutal - avviṭattu okaram varuka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōokkaṭumi, kōokkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉa varum.	(90)
‘kō’ ‘il’loṭu puṇarumvaḻi iyalpātal
293.	illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu; eṉṉi? muṉṉar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi okaram peṟuka eṉa varintu kūṟātum, nilimoḻit toḻil variyātuṅ kūṟaliṉ nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇuñ cēṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkiyākum - ōkāra īṟṟuk kōveṉṉum moḻiyiṉi illeṉṉum varumoḻiyoṭu colliṉ okaram mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōvil eṉa varum.
kōveṉṟatu uyartiṇip peyaraṉṟō veṉiṉ, kōvanta teṉṟu aḵṟiṇiyāy muṭitaliṉ aḵṟiṇippāṟpaṭṭateṉka.	(91)
ivvīṟṟuppeyar oṉcāriyi peṟṟum muṭital
294.	urupiyal niliyum moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	āvayiṉ valleḻut tiyaṟki yākum.
itu valleḻuttu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) urupiyal niliyum moḻiyumāruḷa - ōkāra īṟṟuc cila poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ urupu puṇarcciyatu iyalpilē niṉṟu oṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākum - avviṭattu valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōoṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
cāriyippēṟu varumoḻi valleḻutti vilakkāmi itaṉāṉum peṟṟām.	(92)
aukāra īṟu iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu
295.	aukāra iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
	alvaḻi yāṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	valleḻuttu mikutal varinili yiṉṟē
	avviru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal
	cevvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē.
iḵtu aukāra īṟu iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aukāra iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar - aukāram iṟutiyākiya peyarccoṉmuṉṉar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉ, alvaḻiyāṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum - al vaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum, valleḻuttu mikutal varinili yiṉṟu - valleḻuttu mikkumuṭital nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu, avviru vīṟṟum ukaram varutal cevvi teṉpa ciṟanticiṉōr - avviru kūṟṟu muṭipiṉkaṇṇum nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaram vantu muṭital cevvi teṉṟu colluvar ciṟantōr e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kuvukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum varum.
‘cevvitu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattum ukaram peṟutal koḷka. kuvuñemirntatu ñemircci eṉavum, vuvuvalitu valimi eṉavum varum.
‘nili’ yeṉṟataṉāṟ, kuviṉ kaṭumi eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, aikāramum ikaramum vēṟṟumikkaṇ urupu tokiyāyuḻi iyalpātaluṅ koḷka.	(93)
uyir mayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

8
puḷḷimayaṅkiyal
ñakāra īṟu iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇartal
296.	ñakāri oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar
	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	valleḻut tiyiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē
	ukaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa
eṉpatu cūttiram.
niṟutta muṟiyāṉē uyirīṟu puṇarttup puḷḷiyīṟu vaṉkaṇat toṭuñ ciṟupāṉmi ēṉik kaṇattoṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟaliṉ ivvōttup ‘puḷḷimayaṅkiyal’ eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
iccūttiram ñakāra īṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭu iruvaḻiyum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ñakāri oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar - ñakāram īṟṟiṉkaṇ oṉṟāka niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyariṉ muṉṉar, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - alvaḻiyic collumiṭattum vēṟṟumiyic collumiṭattum, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ avveḻuttumikum-valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉ avvalleḻuttu varumoḻikkaṇ mikku muṭiyum, āvayiṉāṉ ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram varuka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, uriñukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum varum.	(1)
ña na ma va varuvaḻiyum ivvīṟu ukaram peṟutal
297.	ñanamava iyiyiṉum ukaram niliyum.
iḵtu avvīṟu meṉkaṇattoṭum iṭikkaṇattu vakarat toṭum muṭiyumeṉa eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) ñanamava iyiyiṉum ukaram niliyum - aññakara īṟu vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ñanamava mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉum nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum. e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñuñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum.
yakarattōṭum uyiroṭum puṇarumāṟu tokimarapiṉuḷ ‘ukara moṭu puṇarum’ (eḻut. 163) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār.	(2)
nakāra īṟum ukaram peṟṟu muṭital
298.	nakara iṟutiyum ataṉō raṟṟē.
itu nakara īṟu muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟi eytātatu eytuvikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nakara iṟutiyum - nakara īṟṟuppeyarum muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅka ḷoṭu puṇarumvaḻi, ataṉōraṟṟu - aññakara īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avveḻuttu mikku ukaram peṟṟum ñanamava vantuḻi ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	porunukkaṭitu verinukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum varum.
muṭipu oppumi nōkki nakarīṟu īṇṭup puṇarttār. īṇṭu vēṟṟumi yoḻittu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka.	(3)
ivvīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ akaram peṟṟumuṭital
299.	vēṟṟumikku ukkeṭa akaram niliyum.
itu nilimoḻi ukaram vilakki akaram vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumikku - annakara īṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikku, ukkeṭa akaram niliyum - mēleytiya ukaraṅ keṭa akarattoṭu nilipeṟṟup puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	porunakkaṭumi verinakkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉa varum.
‘akaraniliyum’ eṉṉātu ‘ukaraṅkeṭa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi iyipuvalleḻuttu vīḻvuñ ciṟupāṉmi ukarappēṟuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	veriniṉkuṟi poruniṉkuṟi uriñiṉkuṟi eṉavum, ‘uyaval yāṉi verinuc ceṉṟaṉṉa’ (akam. 168) eṉavum varum. yāṉiyiṉatu mutukiṉmēṟ ceṉṟaṉṉa eṉa virikka.
porun eṉpatu oru cātippeyarum, porunutal eṉṉum viṉippeyarumām.	(4)
‘verin’ nakaram keṭṭu melimikku muṭital
300.	verineṉ iṟuti muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi
	varumiṭaṉ uṭittē melleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu annakara īṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) verin eṉ iṟuti - verin eṉṟu collappaṭum nakara īṟṟumoḻi, muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi - taṉīṟṟu nakaram muṉpeṟṟa ukarattōṭu eñcāmik keṭṭa iṭattu, melleḻuttu iyaṟki varum iṭaṉ uṭittu - melleḻuttup peṟum iyalpu vantu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	veriṅkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉa varum.
melleḻuttu varumoḻi nōkki vantatu. ‘veyilveri niṟutta payilitaḻp pacuṅkuṭi’ (akam. 37) eṉpatil nakara ikaramē iṭṭeḻutupa.	(5)
accol valimikku muṭitalum uṭimi
301.	āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittē.
iḵtu ataṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) āvayiṉ - avverin eṉṉuñ col avvāṟu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟa iṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu - mel leḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	verikkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉa varum.	(6)
ṇakāra īṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
302.	ṇakāra iṟuti valleḻut tiyipiṉ
	ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭkē.
itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē ṇakāra īṟu vēṟṟumip poruṭkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇakāra iṟuti - ṇakāra īṟṟuppeyar, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyiyiṉ, ṭakāramākum vēṟṟumip poruṭku - ṭakāramākat tirintu muṭiyum vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ e-ṟu. 
e-ṭu:	maṭkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa varum. maṇki puṇki eṉpaṉa iraṇṭāvataṉ tiripiṉ muṭintaṉa.
kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉpaṉa mēl muṭittum.	(7)
‘āṇ’, ‘peṇ’ iyalpāy muṭiyumāṟu
303.	āṇum peṇṇum aḵṟiṇi iyaṟki.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇum peṇṇum - āṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum peṇṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum, aḵṟiṇi iyaṟki - toki marapiṉuḷ ‘moḻi mutalākum’ (eḻut. 147) eṉpataṉkaṇ aḵṟiṇip peyar muṭinta iyalpupōlat tāmum vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṇki peṇki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
itu tokimarapiṉuḷ ‘aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar’ (eḻut. 155) eṉpataṉuḷ muṭinta iyalpaṉṟōveṉiṉ, ivi āṇṭu muṭintaṉa pōlat tattam marapiṉ viṉiyāṟ pālaṟiyap paṭuvaṉa aṉṟi iru tiṇikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇiyāy muṭitaliṉ, aḵṟiṇip peyaratu iyalpōṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭittāreṉka. āṇkaṭitu peṇkaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻi muṭipu ‘moḻi mutalākum’ (eḻut. 147) eṉpataṉkaṇ varumoḻi muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉ muṭikka.	(8)
‘āṇ’ marappeyar ammuccāriyi peṟutal
304.	āṇmarak kiḷavi arimara iyaṟṟē.
itu tiripu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇmarak kiḷavi - āṇpāli uṇarttātu āṇeṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa col, ari mara iyaṟṟu - ariyeṉṉum maram ammuppeṟṟa iyalpiṟṟāyt tāṉum ammup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṇaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ’ iyalpukaṇattuṅ koḷka. āṇa nār ili eṉa varum.
viravuppeyaraṉṟeṉṟaṟku ‘maram’ eṉṟār.	(9)
‘viṇ’ eṉum pūtakkiḷaviyatu ceyyuḷ muṭipu
305.	viṇṇeṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayiṉ
	uṇmiyum urittē atteṉ cāriyi
	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toḻilvaru kāli.
itu ceyyuḷuḷ tiripu vilakkic cāriyi vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṇ eṉa varūuṅ kāyap peyarvayiṉ - viṇṇeṉṟu collavarukiṟa ākāyatti uṇara niṉṟa peyarkkaṇ, attu eṉ cāriyi uṇmiyum urittu - atteṉṉuñ cāriyi uṇṭātalum urittu illiyātalum urittu; ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toḻil varukāli - ceyyuḷiṭattut toḻiṟcol varuṅkālattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘viṇṇattuk koṭkum vaṇṇat tamarar’ ‘viṇṇattuk koṭkum viricela lūrtiyōy’ eṉavum, ‘viṇkuttu nīḷ vari veṟpa kaḷipavō’ (nālaṭi. 226) eṉavum varum.
viṇṇeṉṉum kuṟippiṉi nīkkutaṟkuk ‘kāyam’ eṉṟār.
viṇvattuk koṭkum eṉa uṭampaṭumey puṇarntu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. atikāra valleḻuttiṉmiyiṟ cāriyi valleḻuttuk koṭukka.	(10)
ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar ukaram peṟṟu muṭital
306.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki ukaramum valleḻuttum vitikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyar ellām toḻiṟ peyar iyala - ṇakāra īṟṟu mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeya rellām alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyaratu iyalpiṉavāy vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi valleḻuttum ukaramum peṟṟum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇṇukkaṭitu paṇṇukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, maṇṇukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāl toḻiṟpeyarallaṉavum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum iṉcāriyi peṟṟum puṇarvaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	veṇṇukkari, ‘tāḻpeyal kaṉikural kaṭuppap paṇṇup peyarttu’ (maturik. 560) eṇṇuppāṟu, veṇṇiṉkari eṉa varum.	(11)
ivvīṟṟuk kiḷippeyar iyalpāy muṭital
307.	kiḷippeya rellāṅ koḷattiri pilavē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkut tiripu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷip peyarellām - ṇakāra īṟṟuḷ ōriṉatti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarellām, koḷat tiripu ila - tiripuṭiya veṉṟu karutumpaṭiyākat tiritalilavāy iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	umaṇ eṉa niṟuttik kuṭi cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉat tantu muṭikka.
iṉi, ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa cāriyi peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka. maṇṇappattam eṇṇanōli eṉavum, kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉavuṅ koḷka.
‘koḷa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭap poruṇmi uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷ tirintu muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
aṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ iṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ uṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, āṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ īṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ ūṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ yāṅkaṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, avaṭkoṇṭāṉ ivaṭkoṇṭāṉ uvaṭkoṇṭāṉ evaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum oṭṭuka.	(12)
‘eṇ’ eṉum uṇavuppeyaratu alvaḻimuṭipu
308.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi eṇṇeṉ uṇavuppeyar
	vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.
iḵtu avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟu alvaḻiyuḷ vēṟṟumi muṭipu pōlat tirintu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi alvaḻi - vēṟṟumiyallāta iṭattu, eṇṇeṉ uṇavuppeyar - variyaṟip poruṇmi uṇarttātu eṇṇeṉṟu collappaṭum uṇaviṉi uṇarttum peyar, vēṟṟumi iyaṟki niliyalum urittu - vēṟṟumiyatu tirintu muṭiyum iyalpiṉ niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.
ummiyāṉ, tokimarapiṉuḷ ‘moḻimutalākum’ (eḻut. 147) eṉṟataṉāṟ kūṟiya iyalpu perumpāṉmiyāyiṟṟu. aḵtu eṇkaṭitu eṉa varum.	(13)
‘muraṇ’ eṉum toḻiṟpeyar iyalpāy muṭital
309.	muraṇeṉ toḻiṟpeyar mutaliyal niliyum.
iḵtu, ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟkut toḻiṟ peyarkku eytiya ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki ivvīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipum vēṟṟumimuṭipum eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) muraṇ eṉ toḻiṟpeyar - māṟupāṭu uṇarttum muraṇeṉṉun toḻiṟpeyar, mutaliyal niliyum - toki marapiṟ kūṟiya alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyātu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum (147) īṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muraṇkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, muraṭkaṭumi cēṉi tāṉi paṟi eṉavum, muraṇñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum.
itaṉit ‘toḻiṟpeyarellām’ (eḻut. 306) eṉṟataṉ piṉ viyāta muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, muraṇkaṭumi muraṭkaṭumi, araṇkaṭumi araṭkaṭumi eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuṅ koḷka.	(14)
makara īṟṟuppeyaratu vēṟṟumimuṭipu
310.	makara iṟuti vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ
	tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikumē.
itu muṟiyāṉē makara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṭ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) makara iṟuti vēṟṟumi āyiṉ - makara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ, tuvarak keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum - annilimoḻi makaram muṟṟak keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	marakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. ‘muṇṭakakkōti’ eṉavum varum.
‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum uyartiṇip peyarkkaṇṇum viravuppeyarkkaṇṇum makarakkēṭu koḷka. marañāṇ maranūl ivaṟṟiṟku nāṉkaṉurupu virikka. maramaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉa oṭṭuka. naṅki eṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, taṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum varum. īṇṭu makarakkēṭē koḷka; muṭipu mēṟkūṟupa (eḻut. 320).	(15)
ivvīṟṟayal akaram nīṭalum nīṭāmiyum uḷavām iṭaṉ
311.	akaram ākāram varūuṅ kāli
	īṟṟumici akaram nīṭalu murittē.
iḵtu avvīṟṟu muṭipu vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) akara ākāram varūum kāli - akara mutaṉmoḻiyum ākāra mutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, īṟṟu mici akaram nīṭalum urittu - makara oṟṟiṉ mēl niṉṟa akaram nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu nīṭāmiyum urittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maram kuḷam eṉa niṟutti, makaraṅkeṭuttu, aṭi āmpal eṉat tantu, rakara ḷakaraṅkaḷiṉ niṉṟa akaram ākāramākki, marāaṭi kuḷāampal eṉa muṭikka.
mēṟ ‘celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa’ (eḻut. 312) eṉpataṉāṟ, kuḷāampal eṉpuḻi āmpal eṉpataṉ ākāratti akaramākkuka. ummiyāṉ, maravaṭi kuḷavāmpal eṉa nīṭāmiyuṅ koḷka. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ivvīṟṟup piṟavum vēṟupaṭa muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kōṇākōṇam kōṇāvaṭṭam eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟiṟku uḷḷeṉṉum urupu virikka. kōṇākōṇattiṟku valleḻuttukkēṭu mēlic cūttirattu ilēcāṟ koḷka.	(16)
ivvīṟu keṭṭu melleḻuttu uṟaḻntuvaralumām eṉal
312.	melleḻut tuṟaḻum moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	celvaḻi aṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa.
itu makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikutaloṭu melleḻuttum uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumāruḷa - makara īṟṟuḷ valleḻuttiṉoṭu melleḻuttup peṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻi kaḷum uḷa, vaḻakkattu āṉa celvaḻi aṟital  -  vaḻakkattiṉ kaṇ vaḻaṅkum iṭam aṟika e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kuḷaṅkari kuḷakkari cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟuṭ kuḷaṅkari kuḷakkaripōla allaṉa otta uṟaḻcciyāy vaḻaṅkā veṉṟaṟkuc ‘celvaḻiyaṟital’ eṉṟār. ‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuḷattukkoṇṭāṉ īḻattucceṉṟāṉ kuṭattuvāy pilattuvāy eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa, makaraṅ keṭṭu attuppeṟṟaṉa. (ivi ‘attē vaṟṟē’ (eḻut. 133) eṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟuk keṭāvāyiṉa, aḵtu alvaḻikkuk kūṟutaliṉ.) maḻakaḷiṟu eṉpatu ‘maḻavuṅ kuḻavu miḷamip poruḷa’ (col. 312) eṉṟa uriccol. atu makara īṟaṉṟu. caṇpakaṅkōṭu eṉpatu vaḻakkiṭattuc cellātu. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, makaraṅkeṭātu niṟpaṉavuṅ koḷka. ‘pulampuk kaṉaṉē’ (puṟam. 258) ‘kalampeṟu kaṇṇuḷa rokkaṟ ṟaliva’ (malipaṭu. 50) eṉa varum.	(17)
illam eṉum marappeyar meli mikal
313.	illa marappeyar vicimara iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.
(i-ḷ.) illa marappeyar - pukku uṟiyum illaṉṟi illa meṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa col, vici mara iyaṟṟu - vici yeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	illaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattu ‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ (eḻut. 312) eṉṟataṉāṉ, makarakkēṭu koḷka.	(18)
ivvīṟṟuppeyaratu alvaḻi muṭipu
314.	alvaḻi yellām melleḻut tākum.
itu makaram alvaḻikkaṇ tirika eṉa muṟkūṟāmiyiṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi yellām melleḻut tākum - makara īṟu alvaḻikka ṇellām melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maraṅkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. maram peritu eṉpuḻit tiripiṉṟeṉpatu ‘āṇikūṟa’leṉṉum utti.
iṉi ‘ellām eṉṟataṉāṉ, alvaḻikkaṇ makara īṟu piṟavāṟṟāṉ muṭivaṉavellām muṭikka. vaṭṭattaṭukku, caturap palaki, āytap puḷḷi, vēḻakkarumpu, kalakkoḷ, cukku, tōri, payaṟu; nīlakkaṇ eṉṉum paṇput tokikkaṇ makaraṅ keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭintaṉa. āyuta vulakki, ‘akara mutala’ (kuṟaḷ. 1): ivi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaraṅ keṭṭu muṭintaṉa. ellāruṅ kuṟiyar nāṅkuṟiyēm: ivi uyartiṇip peyar makaran tirintu melleḻuttāy muṭintaṉa. kolluṅ koṟṟaṉ, uṇṇuñ cāttaṉ, ‘kavaḷa māntu malikeḻu nāṭaṉ’, poru māraṉ, tāvupari, paṟakkunāri, ōṭunākam, āṭupōr, varukālam, kollum yāṉi, pāṭumpāṇaṉ eṉa ivi makaran tirintum keṭṭum nili peṟṟum vanta peyareccam.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum makaraṅ keṭutaluṅ keṭāmiyuṅ koḷka. marañāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉavum, maramyātu valitu aṭintatu eṉavum varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ‘pavaḷavāyeṉa’ (cīvaka. 141) uvamattum, nilanīreṉa eṇṇiṭattum kēṭu koḷka.	(19)
‘akam’ muṉṉark ‘ki’ muṭiyumāṟu
315.	akameṉ kiḷavikkuk kimuṉ variṉē
	mutaṉili yoḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum
	varinili yiṉṟē āciri yarkka
	melleḻuttu mikuta lāva yiṉāṉa.
itu makara īṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ immoḻi immuṭipu eytuka eṉṟaliṉ, eytātateytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) akam eṉ kiḷavikkuk ki muṉvariṉ - akameṉṉuñ colliṟkuk kiyeṉṉuñ colmuṉṉē varumāyiṉ, mutaṉili oḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum - muṉṉiṉṟa akaraṅ keṭātu niṟpa ataṉ muṉ niṉṟa kakaramum makaravoṟṟuṅ keṭṭu muṭitalum keṭātu niṉṟu muṭitalum, vari nili iṉṟē yāciriyarkka - nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu āciriyarkku, āvayiṉāṉ melleḻuttu mikutal - avi keṭṭavaḻi melleḻuttu mikku muṭika e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aṅki eṉa varum.
akaṅki eṉak keṭātu muṭintavaḻi ‘alvaḻi ellām’ (eḻut. 314) eṉṟataṉāṉ, makaran tirintu muṭital koḷka. itu paṇputtoki. atikārattāṉum poruṇōkkāṉum vēṟṟumit tokiyaṉmi uṇarka.	(20)
‘ilam’ muṉṉarp ‘paṭu’viṉ ceyyuḷmuṭipu
316.	ilameṉ kiḷavikkup paṭuvaru kāli
	niliyalu murittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.
iḵtu ilameṉpatu muṟṟuviṉic collākātu kuṟippākiya uriccollāy niṟkuṅkāl alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ilam eṉ kiḷavikku - illāmi eṉṉum kuṟippākiya uriccoṟku, paṭu varu kāli - uṇṭātaleṉṉum poruḷpaṭum viṉik kuṟippu varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ceyyuḷāṉa niliyalum urittu -  ceyyuḷiṭattu makaram, kēṭum tiripumiṉṟi niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
eṉavē, ummiyāṟ piṟacol varuṅkālattuk kēṭun tiripum peṟṟu niṟṟalum uritteṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘ilampaṭu pulava rēṟṟaki niṟiya’ (malipaṭu. 576) eṉa varum.
itaṟku illāmi uṇṭākiṉṟa pulavareṉap poruḷ kūṟuka. ‘ilampāṭu nāṇut tarum’ (cilap. 9 : 71) eṉkiṉṟatō veṉiṉ, illāmi uṇṭātal nāṇuttarumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuka. itaṉi neṟpāṭu periteṉṟāṟpōlak koḷka. itu poruḷilameṉa muṟṟuviṉic collāmāṟum uṇarka. ilaniṉṟateṉak keṭṭavāṟum, ilaṅkeṭa viyantāṉ ilañciṟitāka ilantīteṉṟu eṉak kacatakkaḷ varumvaḻit tirintavāṟuṅ kāṇka.
‘ellām’ (eḻut. 314) eṉṟataṉāṉ ilam varuvatu pōlum, ilam yāriṭattu eṉa vakara yakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉark keṭātu niṟṟal koḷka.
itaṉi ilattāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉa vēṟṟumi yeṉṟā rāl uriyāciriyar eṉiṉ, paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉpatu peyareccamāta liṟ paṟṟaveṉṉun toḻil tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟa mutaṉilic collic cūttirattu āciriyar eṭuttōtiṟṟilarātalāṉum, paṭuveṉpatu tāṉum pulavareṉṉum peyaroṭu muṭiyuṅkāl kālam kāṭṭum īṟukaḷ iraṇṭum tokka mutaṉilic collāy niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉi eṭuttōti ṉārātalāṉum, āciriyarkku aṅṅaṉam kūṟutal karuttaṉmi uṇarka. aṉṟiyum paṟṟappaṭṭa eṉpuḻi iraṇṭu mutaṉili kūṭi oṉṟāy niṉṟu paṟṟutalic ceyyappaṭṭa eṉap poruḷ tārāmiyāṉum, alvaḻi yatikāramātalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmi uṇarka.	(21)
‘āyiram’ otta eṇ varuvaḻi attuppēṟu
317.	attoṭu civaṇum āyirat tiṟuti
	otta eṇṇu muṉvaru kāli.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu eṇṇup peyaruḷ oṉṟaṟkut toki marapiṉuḷ ‘uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yāki’ (eḻut. 164) eṉpataṉāṉ eytiya ēyeṉ cāriyi vilakki attu vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyirattu iṟuti - āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyariṉ iṟuti makaram, otta eṇṇu muṉvarukāli - taṉakku akappaṭum moḻiyāyp poruntiṉa eṇṇuppeyar taṉ muṉ varum kālattu, attoṭu civaṇum - tokimarapiṟ kūṟiya ēyeṉ cāriyi oḻittu attuc cāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āyirattoṉṟu, āyirattoṉpatu eṉa oṉṟu mutal oṉpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka.
makaratti attiṉmici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu ‘attiṉakara makaramuṉi yilli’ (eḻut. 125) eṉṟu muṭikka. āyirattorupatu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum oṭṭuka.
nilimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ, āyirattukkuṟi kūṟu mutal eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, āyirappatteṉpuḻi makaraṅkeṭuttu valloṟṟu mikuttu muṭikka.	(22)
‘āyiram’ aṭiyoṭu puṇariṉum aṟṟātal
318.	aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum ataṉō raṟṟē.
iḵtu ‘aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilik kuriya’ (eḻut. 110) eṉṟamiyiṉ, avveṇṇup peyari aṭiyaṭuttu muṭikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum - avvāyira meṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṭiyaṭutta moḻiyoṭu variṉum, ataṉō raṟṟu - muṟkūṟiyataṉōṭu orutaṉmittāy attup peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṉāyirattoṉṟu iraṇṭu, irupatiṉāyirattoṉṟu, āṟāyirattoṉṟu, nūṟāyirattoṉṟu, munnūṟāyirat toṉṟu, ainnūṟāyirattoṉṟu eṉa oṭṭuka.
muṉṉar ilēciṉāṉ muṭintavaṟṟiyum aṭiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka. patiṉāyirattukkuṟi kūṟu mutal eṉavum, nūṟāyirappattu eṉavum varum.	(23)
aḷavum niṟiyum varuvaḻi āyiram muṭiyumāṟu
319.	aḷavum niṟiyum vēṟṟumi yiyala.
iḵtu avveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟip peyarum vantāl muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ, eytātateytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum - atikārattāṉ āyirantāṉē niṉṟuḻiyum aṭiyaṭuttu niṉṟuḻiyum aḷavup peyarum niṟip peyarum vantāl, vēṟṟumi iyala - makara īṟṟu vēṟṟumiyōṭu ottu valleḻuttu vantuḻi makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkum, iyalpukaṇam vantuḻi ‘tuvara’ (eḻut. 310) eṉṉum ilēcāṉ eytiya makaraṅkeṭṭum puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āyiram patiṉāyiram nūṟāyiram eṉa niṟuttik, kalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi akal uḻakku eṉavum, kaḵcu kaḻañcu toṭi tulām palam eṉavun tantu oṭṭuka.
‘vēṟṟumi yiyala’ eṉavē, tām vēṟṟumiyallavāyiṉa.	(24)
ivvīṟṟup paṭarkki muṉṉilip peyarkaḷum,
tām-nām-yām-eṉpaṉavum muṭiyumāṟu
320.	paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilip peyarum
	toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum peyarnilik kiḷaviyum
	vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum
	melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu uyartiṇippeyarum viravuppeyarum urupiyaluḷ muṭintavāṟē īṇṭup poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭikaveṉa eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilip peyarum - ellārumeṉṉum paṭarkkip peyarum ellīrumeṉṉum muṉṉilip peyarum, toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum peyarnilik kiḷaviyum - kiḷit toṭarccip poruḷavāy neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyun tām nām yāmeṉṉum peyarākiya nilimiyuṭiya collum, vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ urupu puṇarccikkuk kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum, āvayiṉāṉ melleḻuttu mikutal - mēl neṭu mutal kuṟukum moḻikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ellārum eṉpataṉi makara oṟṟum ukaramuṅ keṭuttu, rakarap puḷḷiyi niṟuttik, ki cevi tali puṟam eṉattantu, iṭiyilē tammuccāriyiyum iṟutiyilē ummuccāri yiyum koṭuttu, ellārtaṅkiyum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa muṭikka.
itaṟku, ‘ammiṉiṟuti’ (eḻut. 129) eṉṉum cūttirattuḷ ‘taṉmey’ eṉṟataṉāṟ ‘piṟa cāriyikkaṇ makara oṟṟut tirintu ṅañanavākum’ eṉac ceyki ceytu muṭikka.
ellīrum eṉpataṟku, iṭiyilē nummuc cāriyiyum iṟutiyilē ummuccāriyiyum koṭuttu, muṟkūṟiya ceykikaḷellāñ ceytu, ellīr nuṅkiyum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa muṭikka.
tām, nām eṉpaṉavaṟṟi ‘ēṉi yiraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum’ (eḻut. 188) eṉak kuṟukki, ‘makaraviṟuti’ (eḻut. 310) eṉpataṉkaṇ ‘tuvara’ eṉpataṉāṉ makaraṅkeṭuttut, taṅki naṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa muṭikka.
yām eṉpataṉi ākāratti ekaramākki yakara oṟṟik keṭuttu ‘makaraviṟuti’ (eḻut. 310) eṉpataṉkaṇ ‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ makaraṅ keṭuttu, eṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa muṭikka. toṭakkaṅ kuṟukuvaṉa vaṟṟiṟku iccūttirattāṉ melleḻuttu mikukka.
