‘urupiyal niliyum’ eṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumiyātal peṟā niṟkavum, piṉṉum ‘vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ’ eṉṟa mikiyāṉē, paṭarkkip peyarum muṉṉilip peyarum iyalpu kaṇattu ñakāramum nakāramum vantuḻit tammuc cāriyiyum, nummuc cāriyiyum peṟutalum, ‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, makaraṅkeṭṭu ummup peṟutalum oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellār taññāṇum, ellīr nuññāṇum nūlum eṉa varum.
iṉit toṭakkaṅ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum avviraṇṭu ilēcāṉum makaraṅkeṭutalum oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	taññāṇ, naññāṇ, eññāṇ nūl eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ‘āvayiṉāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, ellārtam  ellīrnum eṉa niṉṟavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar, ēṉi maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉpaṉa vantuḻi makaraṅ keṭāmiyum ummuppeṟutaluṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, toṭakkaṅ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkun tammaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉa makaraṅ keṭāmiyuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, tamakāṇam namakāṇam emakāṇam numakāṇam eṉa urupīṟṟuc ceykikaḷuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, num eṉpataṟku makaratti melloṟṟākki nuṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varutalum, nuññāṇ eṉa oṟṟiraṭṭutalum, numvali eṉa makaram keṭātu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ellārkiyum ellīrkiyum eṉat tammum nummum peṟātu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.	(25)
appeyarkaḷ alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
321.	allatu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟki yākum.
itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkiyākum - ammūṉṟu peyarum alvaḻiyic collumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
īṇṭu ‘iyaṟki’ yeṉṟatu cāriyi peṟāmi nōkki. ivaṟṟiṉ īṟutirital ‘alvaḻi yellām’ (eḻut. 314) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellāruṅkuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, ellīruṅ kuṟiyīr ciṟiyīr tīyīr periyīr eṉavum, tāṅkuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, tāṅkuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉavum, nāṅkuṟiyam ciṟiyam tīyam periyam eṉavum, yāṅkuṟiyēm ciṟiyēm tīyēm periyēm eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, ivaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar ñakāra nakāram vantāl avi avvoṟṟāyt tirital koḷka. ellāruñ ñāṉṟār nīṇṭār, ellīruñ ñāṉṟīr nīṇṭīr eṉavum, tāññāṉṟār nīṇṭār eṉavum, nāññāṉṟām nīṇṭām eṉavum, yāñ ñāṉṟēm nīṇṭēm eṉavum varum.
iṉi, ellārum vantār yāttār aṭintār, ellīrum vantīr yāttīr aṭintīr eṉavum, tām vantār yāttār aṭintār eṉavum, nām varutum yāttum aṭitum eṉavum, yām varuvēm yāppēm aṭivēm eṉavum ēṉikkaṇaṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar makarantiriyātu niṟṟal, uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarum’ (eḻut. 153) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum.	(26)
‘ellām’ iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu
322.	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	ellā meṉumpeyar urupiyal niliyum
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻic cāriyi niliyātu.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum urupiyaloṭu māṭṭeṟintu eytātateytu vittatu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum - alvaḻikkaṭ colliṉum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṭ colliṉum, ellāmeṉum peyar urupiyal niliyum - ellāmeṉṉum viravup peyar urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpilē niṉṟu muṭiyum, vēṟṟumi yalvaḻic cāriyi niliyātu - appeyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyallāta iṭattu vaṟṟuc cāriyi nillātāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘urupiyal niliyum’ eṉṟa māṭṭēṟu, alvaḻikkaṇ ummup peṟṟu niṟṟalum, poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟu niṟṟalum uṇarttiṟṟu.
ummuccāriyi oṉṟumē peṟṟu muṭikiṉṟa alvaḻiyiṉiyum, vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟu muṭikiṉṟa vēṟṟumiyōṭu uṭaṉōti, atuvum vaṟṟup peṟumāṟu pōla māṭṭeṟinta mikiyāṉē, vaṉkaṇattu alvaḻikkaṇ nilimoḻi makarakkēṭum, varumoḻi valleḻuttup pēṟum, īṟṟummip pēṟum, meṉkaṇattu makaraṅ keṭṭu ummup peṟṟum peṟātum varutalum, ēṉikkaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu ummup peṟṟum, makaraṅ keṭātu ummup peṟātum varutaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellākkuṟiyavum ciṟiyavum tīyavum periyavum eṉavum, ellā ñāṇum nūlum maṇiyum eṉavum, ellā ñāṉṟaṉa nīṇṭaṉa māṇṭaṉa eṉavum, ellā yāḻum vaṭṭum aṭiyum eṉavum, ellām vāṭiṉa āṭiṉa eṉavum varum. iṉi, vēṟṟumikkaṇ ellāvaṟṟukkōṭum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa ivi vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku makaram vaṟṟiṉ mici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭukka.
iṉi, meṉkaṇattukkaṇ ellāvaṟṟu ñāṇum nūlum maṇiyum eṉavum, ēṉikkaṇattukkaṇ ellāvaṟṟiyāppum vaḻiyum aṭiyum eṉavum varum. ēṉik kaṇamum vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟaṉa.	(27)
appeyar alvaḻikkaṇ meliyum mikappeṟutal
323.	melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉa milli.
iḵtu orucār valleḻuttēyaṉṟi melleḻuttum vitittaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉa milli - avvellā meṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ mēl ilēciṉāṟ kūṟiya valleḻuttēyaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli e-ṟu.
eṉavē, valleḻuttu mikutalē perumpāṉmiyāyiṟṟu. muṟ kūṟiya ceykimēlē itu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, makarak kēṭum ummuppēṟuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellāṅ kuṟiyavum ciṟiyavum tīyavum periyavum eṉa varum.
‘māṉamilli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku iṟuti ummup peṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu ummup peṟṟu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellāk kollarum cāṉṟārum taccarum pārppārum kuṟiyarum ciṟiyarum tīyarum periyarum eṉavum, ellā ñāṉṟārum nāykarum maṇiyakārarum vaṇikarum aracarum eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, uyartiṇikkaṇ ellāṅkuṟiyarum ciṟiyarum tīyarum periyarum eṉa makaraṅkeṭṭu melleḻuttu mikku ummup peṟutalum, ellāṅ kuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, kuṟiyīr kuṟiyam eṉavum ummup peṟātu varutaluṅkoḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iṭikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇattum makaraṅ keṭātu ummiṉṟi varutaluṅ koḷka. ellām vantēm aṭintēm eṉa varum.	(28)
appeyar uyartiṇikkaṇ vēṟṟumiyil muṭiyumāṟu
324.	uyartiṇi yāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum.
iḵtu ellā meṉpataṟku uyartiṇi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇiyāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum - ellā meṉpatu uyartiṇiyāy niṟkumāyiṉ urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iṭikkaṇ nammum iṟutikkaṇ ummum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
urupiyalil ‘ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar - vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi’ (eḻut. 189) vakuttataṉāṉ, vaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉṟu makaraṅ keṭutta atikārattāṉ ‘uyartiṇi yāyi ṉammiṭi varum’ (eḻut. 190) eṉa nammiṉ muṉṉum makaraṅ keṭuttār; ataṉōṭu īṇṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ. atu koṇṭu īṇṭum makaraṅ keṭukka. ‘ammiṉiṟuti’ (eḻut. 129) eṉpuḻit ‘taṉmey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ nammuccāriyiyiṉatu makaran tirital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellā naṅkiyum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa oṭṭuka.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟaliṉ, ellā naññāṟciyum nīṭciyum eṉa ēṟpaṉavaṟṟoṭu muṭipu aṟintu oṭṭuka.	(29)
‘num’ īṟukeṭa meli mikutal
325.	numme ṉorupeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.
itu num eṉpataṉ makara īṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.
(i-ḷ.) nummeṉ oru peyar melleḻuttu mikum - nummeṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa viravuppeyar poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nuṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
‘makaraviṟuti’ (eḻut. 310) eṉpataṉkaṇ ‘tuvara’ eṉpataṉāṉ, makaraṅkeṭukka. ‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, uṅki eṉa varuvatūuṅ koḷka.
‘tuvara’ (eḻut. 310) eṉṟataṉāṉ, ñakara nakaraṅkaḷ vantuḻi makaram keṭutalum, ‘oru peyar’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟu mikutaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	nuññāṇ nūl eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ‘oru peyar’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nummaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉa makaraṅ keṭāmiyum koḷka.	(30)
ataṉ alvaḻi muṭipu
326.	allataṉ maruṅkiṟ colluṅ kāli
	ukkeṭa niṉṟa meyvayiṉ īvara
	iyiṭi nilii īṟukeṭa rakaram
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntē
	appāṉ moḻivayiṉ iyaṟki yākum.
itu nummeṉpataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) allataṉ maruṅkiṟ colluṅkāli - nummeṉpataṉi alvaḻikkaṇ kūṟumiṭattu, u keṭa niṉṟa meyvayiṉ ī vara - nakara vukarattuḷ ukaraṅkeṭṭup pōka oḻintu niṉṟa nakara voṟṟiṭattē īkāram vantu niṟpa, i iṭinilii īṟukeṭa  -  ōr ikaram iṭiyilē vantu nilipeṟṟu makaramākiya īṟu keṭṭuppōka, puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntu rakaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum - āṇṭup puḷḷi peṟṟu oru rakaram vantu niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ, appāl moḻivayiṉ - ak kūṟṟiṉiyuṭiya nilimoḻiyiṭattu, iyaṟki yākum - varuñcol iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nīyir kuṟiyīr ciṟiyīr tīyīr periyīr eṉa varum.
‘colluṅkāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, nīyir ñāṉṟīr; nīṇṭīr, māṇṭīr, yāttīr, vāṭiṉīr, aṭintīr eṉa ēṉikkaṇattilum oṭṭuka.	(31)
ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar
piṟa īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla muṭital
327.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
itu vēṟṟumikkaṇ makaramkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkum alvaḻikkaṇ melleḻuttāyt tirintum varumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakki ñakara īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla niṟkumeṉap piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyarellām - makara īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarel lām, toḻiṟpeyar iyala - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar pōla alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, iyalpukaṇattu ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cemmuk kaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, cemmukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum. ‘tummuc ceṟuppa’ (kuṟaḷ. 1318) eṉpatum atu. ivi ‘kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru viraṭṭal’ (eḻut. 160) eṉpataṉāṉ iraṭṭiṉa.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ukaram peṟātu nāṭṭaṅkaṭitu āṭṭaṅ kaṭitu eṉa melleḻuttāy alvaḻikkaṇ tiritalum, nāṭṭakkaṭumi āṭṭakkaṭumi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikutaluṅ koḷka.		(32)
īm kam urum - immūṉṟum attoḻiṟpeyar aṉiyavātal
328.	īmuṅ kammum urumeṉ kiḷaviyum
	āmup peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa.
itu makara īṟṟup poruṭpeyaruṭ cila, avvīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarōṭu ottu muṭika eṉa eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) īmuṅ kammum urumeṉ kiḷaviyum a muppeyarum - īmeṉṉuñ colluṅ kammeṉṉuñ collum urumeṉṉuñ collumākiya ammūṉṟu peyarum, avaṟṟōraṉṉa - muṟkūṟiya toḻiṟ peyarōṭu oru taṉmiyavāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, iyalpukaṇattu ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
īm eṉpatu cuṭukāṭu; kam eṉpatu toḻil.
e-ṭu:	īmukkaṭitu, kammuk kaṭitu, urumukkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, īmukkaṭumi, kammukkaṭumi, urumukkaṭumi, ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum oṭṭuka.
‘kiḷavi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum alvaḻikkaṇṇum uyir varuvaḻi ukaram peṟātu, īmaṭivu, īmaṭintatu eṉa niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. ‘taṉṉiṉamuṭittal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, ammu tammu nammu eṉac cāriyik kaṇṇum ukaram varutal koḷka.	(33)
īmum kammum akkuppeṟṟu muṭiyumāṟu
329.	vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ ēṉi yiraṇṭum
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭum akkeṉ cāriyi.
itu mēl muṭipu kūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṟku vēṟṟumikkaṇ vēṟoru muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ - vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarcci yāyiṉ, ēṉi iraṇṭum tōṟṟam vēṇṭum akkeṉ cāriyi - iṟutiyiṉ urumoḻinta iraṇṭum akkeṉṉuñ cāriyi tōṉṟi muṭitali vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	īmakkuṭam kammakkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavum, ñāṟci neruppu māṭci viṟaku eṉavum oṭṭuka.
‘akku’ vakuppavē, nilimoḻit toḻilākiya ukaraṅ keṭṭu, muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu vilakkappaṭāmiyiṉ niṉṟu muṭintatu.
vaṉkaṇattiṟku muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattiṟ kūṟiyatu kuṇavēṟṟumik keṉṟum, īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu poruṭpuṇarccik keṉṟuṅ koḷka.	(34)
puṇarmoḻikkaṇ vakāram varuvaḻi makarīṟu kuṟukutal
330.	vakāra miciyum makaraṅ kuṟukum.
itu muṉṉar ‘ariyaḷavu kuṟukal’ (eḻut. 13) eṉavum ‘ṉakāri muṉṉar’ (eḻut. 52) eṉavum kūṟiya makaram irumoḻikkaṇṇum kuṟuku meṉa, ataṉ īṟṟakattu eytātatu eytuvikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vakāramiciyum makāraṅ kuṟukum - makāram oru moḻik kaṇṇēyaṉṟi vakārattiṉ mēluṅ kuṟukum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nilam valitu, varum vaṇṇakkaṉ eṉa varum.	(35)
ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
331.	nāṭpeyark kiḷavi mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa
	attum āṉmici varinili yiṉṟē
	oṟṟumey keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkku vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṭpeyarkkiḷavi mēṟ kiḷantaṉṉa - makara īṟṟu nāṭpeyar ikara īṟṟu nāṭpeyarpōla āṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum, attu āṉmiciyum varinili iṉṟu - attuc cāriyi āṉcāriyi mēlum piṟa cāriyi mēlum varutal nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu, oṟṟu meykeṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - āṇṭu nilimoḻi makara oṟṟuk keṭuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
ummiyi ‘āṉmiciyum’ eṉa māṟuka.
e-ṭu:	makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ōṇattāṟ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉka. ēṉi nāḷkaḷōṭum oṭṭuka.
makara oṟṟukkeṭuttu ‘atti ṉakara makaramuṉi yilli’ (eḻut. 125) eṉa akaraṅkeṭuttuk, ‘kuṟṟiya lukaramu maṟṟeṉa moḻipa’ (eḻut. 105) eṉa āṉēṟṟi, āṉi ṉakaramum, (eḻut. 124) eṉṟataṉāṉ ṟakaramākki muṭikka.
makattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ñāṉṟeṉuñ cāriyimēl attu vantatu.
variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, immuṭipu nāṉku kaṇattuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	makattāṉ ñāṟṟiṉāṉ niṟuttiṉāṉ māyntāṉ, vantāṉ, aṭintāṉ eṉa varum.	(36)
ṉakāra īṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
332.	ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
	ṟakāra mākum vēṟṟumip poruṭkē.
itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē ṉakāra iṟuti vēṟṟumikkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṟakāra mākum - ṉakāra īṟṟup peyar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantu iyiyiṉ ṉakāram ṟakāramākum, vēṟṟumip poruṭku - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	poṟkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa varum 	(37)
‘maṉ’ mutaliya ēḻum aṉṉa iyalpiṉavātal
333.	maṉṉuñ ciṉṉum āṉum īṉum
	piṉṉum muṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum
	aṉṉa iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu avvīṟṟu acinili iṭiccoṟkaḷum, ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭap poruḷ uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷum, viṉiyeccamum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) maṉṉum ciṉṉum āṉum īṉum piṉṉum muṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - maṉṉeṉṉuñ collum ciṉṉeṉṉuñ collum āṉeṉṉuñ collum īṉeṉṉumcollum piṉṉeṉ ṉuñ collum muṉeṉṉum collum viṉiyeccamākiya collum, aṉṉa iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar - muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉi yuṭiyavāy ṉakaram ṟakaramāy muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘atumaṟ koṇkaṉ ṟērē’ ‘kāppum pūṇṭiciṟ kaṭiyum pōkali’ (akam. 7) eṉavum, āṟkoṇṭāṉ īṟkoṇ ṭāṉ piṟkoṇṭāṉ muṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, variṟ koḷḷum cellum tarum pōm eṉavum varum.
peyarāntaṉmiyavākiya āṉ īṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟi muṟ kūṟātataṉāṉ, āṉ koṇṭāṉ īṉ koṇṭāṉ eṉat tiriyāmiyuṅ koḷka. piṉ muṉ eṉpaṉa, peyarum urupum viṉiyeccamumāy niṟṟaliṉ peyar īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār. ēṉiya, urupiyaluḷḷum viṉi yeñcu kiḷavi yeṉpataṉkaṇṇum muṭiyum. appeyari muṟkūṟāta taṉāṟ, piṉkoṇṭāṉ muṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉat tiriyāmiyuṅ koḷka.
‘iyala’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ūṉ eṉṉuñ cuṭṭu ūṉ koṇṭāṉeṉa iyalpāy muṭital koḷka.	(38)
avvayiṉ mutaliya mūṉṟum ‘evvayiṉu’m aṉṉa muṭipiṉavātal
334.	cuṭṭumutal vayiṉum ekaramutal vayiṉum
	appaṇpu niliyum iyaṟkiya eṉpa.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇarttum iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal vayiṉum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vayiṉeṉṉuñ collum, ekaramutal vayiṉum - ekaramākiya mutal viṉāviṉiyuṭiya vayiṉeṉṉuñ collum, appaṇpu niliyum iyaṟki eṉpa - mēl ṉakaram ṟakaramā meṉṟa taṉmi nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum iyalpiyuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟuvār āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ, ivvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ, uvvayiṟ koṇṭāṉ, evvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘iyaṟkiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka.	(39)
‘kuyiṉ’  iyalpātal
335.	kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟki yākum.
itu ṉakaran tiriyātu iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkiyākum - kuyiṉeṉṉuñ col tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kuyiṉkuḻām celavu tōṟṟam paṟivu eṉa varum.
kuyiṉ eṉpatu mēkam. aḵtu aḵṟiṇip peyar. toki marapiṉuḷ uyartiṇip peyarum viravuppeyarum iyalpāka veṉṟār. kuyiṉ viṉiyumām.
‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, kāṉkōḻi kōṉkuṇam vāṉkari eṉa varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka.	(40)
‘ekiṉ’ marappeyar ammup peṟutal
336.	ekiṉmara māyiṉ āṇmara iyaṟṟē.
itu tiripu vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) ekiṉ maramāyiṉ - ekiṉeṉpatu puḷḷaṉṟi marap peya rāyiṉ, āṇmara iyaṟṟu - āṇmarattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy ammuppeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ekiṉaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.	(41)
ēṉi ekiṉ muṭiyumāṟu
337.	ēṉi ekiṉē yakaram varumē
	valleḻut tiyaṟki mikutal vēṇṭum.
ituvum atu, tiripu vilakki akaram vitittaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi ekiṉē akaram varum - maramallāta ekiṉ nili moḻikkaṇ akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum, valleḻuttiyaṟki mikutal vēṇṭum - āṇṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttiyalpu mikku muṭitali vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ekiṉakkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattōṭu itaṉi oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ, iyalpu kaṇattum akarappēṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ekiṉañāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi yāppu aṭivu eṉa varum.
‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, akarattoṭu melleḻuttup pēṟuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ekiṉaṅkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
iṉic ciṟupāṉmi ekiṉcēval ekiṉaccēval peṭi eṉpaṉa āṟaṉurupu virivuḻi īṇṭi ilēcāṉ muṭikka. paṇpu karutiya vaḻi ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka.	(42)
ivvīṟṟuk kiḷippeyar iyalpāy muṭital
338.	kiḷippeya rellāṅ kiḷippeya riyala.
itu ṉakaran tiritali vilakki iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷip peyarellām - ṉakara īṟṟuk kiḷippeya rellām, kiḷippeyar iyala - ṇakara īṟṟuk kiḷippeyar pōlat tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eyiṉkuṭi cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa varum.
eyiṉ vantatu eṉṟu aḵṟiṇikkum eytutaliṉ, toki marapiṉuḷ muṭiyātāyiṟṟu, āṇṭu uyartiṇikkē kūṟutaliṉ.
iṉi ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, eyiṉakkaṉṉi piḷḷi eṉa akkum valleḻuttum peṟutalum, eyiṉa vāḻvu eṉa valleḻuttup peṟāmiyum koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, pārppaṉak kaṉṉi kumari cēri piḷḷi eṉa ākāram kuṟukki akkum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttum, pārppaṉa vāḻkki eṉa valleḻuttuk koṭātum muṭikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum veḷḷāḷaṉeṉa niṉṟataṉi aṉkeṭuttup pirittu ḷakāra voṟṟiṉi ṇakāra voṟṟākki, veḷḷāṇ kumari piḷḷi māntar vāḻkki oḻukkam eṉa muṭikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, mutaleḻutti nīṭṭi ḷakāra voṟṟiṉik keṭuttu vēḷāṇeṉa muṭikka. itaṉāṉē, poruna vāḻkkiyum muṭikka. vēṭṭuvak kumari eṉpatu marūu vaḻakku.	(43)
‘mīṉ’ iṟuti valleḻuttōṭu uṟaḻtal
339.	mīṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻut tuṟaḻvē.
ituvum atu, taṉ tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) mīṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu uṟaḻvu - mīṉeṉṉuñ col tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘mīṉkaṇ’ (cīvaka.54) mīṟkaṇ, mīṉciṉi mīṟciṉi, mīṉṟali mīṟṟali, mīṉpuṟam mīṟpuṟam eṉa varum.	(44)
‘tēṉ’ muṭiyumāṟu
340.	tēṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻut tiyiyiṉ
	mēṉili ottalum valleḻuttu mikutalum
	āmuṟi yiraṇṭum urimiyu muṭittē
	valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi iṟuti illi.
ituvum atu, mēlataṉoṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - tēṉeṉṉuñ col valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉ, mēlnili ottalum - mīṉeṉpataṟkuk kūṟiya tiripuṟaḻcci nili ottu muṭitalum, valleḻuttu mikutalum - varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalumākiya, āmuṟi iraṇṭum urimiyum uṭittu - ammuṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya iraṇṭaṉiyum urittātaliyum uṭittu, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi iṟutiyilli - valleḻuttu mikku varumiṭattu iṟutiyil niṉṟa ṉakaraṅ keṭum e-ṟu.
‘urimiyum’ eṉṉum ummi, ‘melleḻuttu mikiṉum’ (eḻut. 341) eṉa mēlvarukiṉṟataṉi nōkkiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	tēṉkuṭam tēṟkuṭam, cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa mēṉili ottaṉa. tēkkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa ṉakaraṅ keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkaṉa.	(45)
appeyar ṉakaram keṭṭu melimikavum peṟutal
341.	melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉa milli.
ituvum atu; uṟaḻcciyum valleḻuttum aṉṟi melleḻuttum vitittaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamilli - muṟkūṟiya tēṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu vantāl avvalleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi mel leḻuttu mikiṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli e-ṟu.
ṉakarakkēṭu atikārattāṟ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	tēṅkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavarum.	(46)
appeyar meṉkaṇam varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
342.	melleḻut tiyiyiṉ iṟutiyō ṭuṟaḻum.
itu toki marapiṉuḷ ‘vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum valleḻuttal vaḻi’ (eḻut. 148) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiya iyalpi vilakki uṟaḻumeṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ - attēṉeṉ kiḷavi mel leḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyiyiṉ, iṟutiyōṭu uṟaḻum - nili moḻi yiṟutiyiṉ ṉakara voṟṟuk keṭutaluṅ keṭāmiyumākiya uṟaḻcci yāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tēṉñeri tēñeri, tēṉuṉi tēnuṉi, tēṉmoḻi tēmoḻi eṉa varum.
mēl ‘āmuṟi’ (eḻut.. 340) eṉṟataṉāṉ, tēñeri tēññeri, tēnuṉi, tēnnuṉi, tēmoḻi tēmmoḻi eṉa ṉakaraṅ keṭṭut tattam melleḻuttu mikkum mikātum muṭintaṉavuṅ koḷka.
iṉi mēl ‘māṉamilli’ (eḻut. 341) eṉṟataṉāṉ, īṟu keṭṭu melleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavuṅkoṇṭu tēñeri tēnuṉi tēmoḻi eṉpaṉa kāṭṭiṉ avi muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅku meṉka.	(47)
appeyar ‘iṟāl’ varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
343.	iṟāal tōṟṟam iyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu attēṉeṉpataṟku uyirkkaṇattu orumoḻi muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṟāal tōṟṟam - tēṉeṉṉuñ col iṟāleṉṉum varumoḻiyatu tōṟṟattukkaṇ, iyaṟkiyākum - nilimoḻiyiṉ ṉakaraṅ keṭātē niṉṟu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.	(48)
e-ṭu:	tēṉiṟāl eṉa varum.
atu ‘tēttiṟāl’ eṉavum muṭital
344.	oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalu murittē.
ituvum ataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalum urittu - attēṉeṉpatu iṟāloṭu puṇarumiṭattup piṟitumoru takarattoṭu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
‘valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟutiyilli’ (eḻut. 340) eṉṟataṉāṉ, nilimoḻi īṟu keṭukka. ‘takarammikum’ eṉṉātu ‘oṟṟumiku takaram’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, īroṟṟākkuka.
e-ṭu:	tēttiṟāl eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattōṭu itaṉi yoṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṟ, piṟa varumoḻikkaṇṇum immuṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	tēttaṭi, tēttī eṉa varum.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, tēṉaṭi, tēṉī eṉṉum iyalpuṅ koḷka.	(49)
miṉ mutaliya nāṟcollum iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu
345.	miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum kaṉṉum
	annāṟ collum toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyum vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintum varuka eṉa eytuvitta muṭipi vilakkit toḻiṟpeyariyalpā meṉap piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum kaṉṉum annāṟ collum - miṉṉeṉṉuñ collum piṉṉeṉṉuñ collum paṉṉeṉṉuñ collum kaṉṉeṉṉuñ collumākiya annāṉku collum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala - alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉuṅ ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarpōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇat tum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘miṉṉuccey viḷakkattu’ (kali. 41: 6) ‘piṉṉup piṇi yaviḻnta’  eṉavum, paṉṉukkaṭitu kaṉṉukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, miṉṉukkaṭumi piṉṉuk kaṭumi paṉṉukkaṭumi kaṉṉuk kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum.
‘toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala’ eṉṟu ōtātu kiḷan tōtiṉār, ivi toḻiṉilikkaṇṇaṉṟi vēṟu tam poruḷuṇara niṉṟavaḻiyum immuṭipu eytu meṉṟaṟku.
‘miṉ’ eṉpatu miṉṉutaṟṟoḻilum, ‘miṉṉunimirn taṉṉa’ (puṟam. 57) eṉa miṉṉeṉappaṭuvatoru poruḷum uṇarttum. ēṉiya vum aṉṉa.	(50)
avaṟṟuḷ ‘kaṉ’ vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
346.	vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ ēṉi yekiṉoṭu
	tōṟṟam okkum kaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi.
itu nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaram vilakki akaram vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ ēṉi ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam okkum - vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ oḻinta maramallāta ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam ottu akaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum, kaṉ eṉ kiḷavi - kaṉṉeṉṉuñcol e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṉṉakkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉa varum. kaṉṉakkaṭumi eṉak kuṇa vēṟṟumi yuñ ciṟupāṉmi koḷka.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu akaramum melleḻuttum ēṉikkaṇattu akaramuṅ koḷka.
kaṉṉaṅkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, kaṉṉañāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum varum.
kaṉṉaṅkaṭumi eṉak kuṇa vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ivviti koḷka. ‘poṉṉakar varippiṟ kaṉṉan tūkki’ (aiṅkuṟu. 247) eṉpatō veṉiṉ, atu makara īṟṟup poruṭ peyar.	(51)
ivvīṟṟu iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart ‘tanti’ puṇarumāṟu
347.	iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tanti muṟivariṉ
	mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa akaram niliyum
	meyyoḻit taṉkeṭum avviyaṟ peyarē.
iḵtu ‘aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē’ (eḻut. 155) eṉṟataṟku īṇṭut tiripu kūṟaliṉ, eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) iyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tanti muṟivariṉ - ivvīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ iyaṟpeyariṉ muṉṉart tantiyeṉṉum muṟippeyar varumoḻiyāy varumāyiṉ, mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa akaram niliyum - attanti yeṉpataṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa takaravoṟṟukkeṭa ataṉmēlēṟi niṉṟa akaraṅkeṭātu niṟkum, avviyaṟpeyar meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭum - annilimoḻiyākiya iyaṟpeyar aṉṉeṉṉuñ colliṉ akaram ēṟiniṉṟa meyyi oḻittu avvaṉtāṉ keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cāttanti, koṟṟanti eṉa varum.
‘mutaṟkaṇmey’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ, cāttaṉṟanti koṟṟaṉṟanti eṉṉum iyalpu muṭipuṅ koḷka.	(52)
‘ātaṉ’, ‘pūtaṉ’ muṉṉar ammuṟippeyar puṇarumāṟu
348.	ātaṉum pūtaṉum kūṟiya iyalpoṭu
	peyaroṟ ṟakaram tuvarak keṭumē.
itu mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ātaṉum pūtaṉum - muṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyaruḷ ātaṉum pūtaṉum eṉṉum iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tanti yeṉṉum muṟippeyaroṭu muṭiyuṅkāl, kūṟiya iyalpoṭu - muṟ kūṟiya nilimoḻi aṉ keṭutalum varumoḻit takara voṟṟuk keṭutalumākiya ceykikaḷuṭaṉē, peya roṟṟu akaran tuvarakkeṭum - nilimoḻip peyariṉ aṉkeṭa niṉṟa takaravoṟṟum varumoḻiyiṉ takara voṟṟukkeṭa niṉṟa akaramum muṟṟak keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ānti, pūnti eṉa varum.
‘iyalpu’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, peyaroṟṟum akaramum keṭātē niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ātanti pūtanti eṉa varum.
iṉit ‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḻāṉ puḻāṉ eṉa niṟuttit tanti eṉa varuvittu, nilimoḻi ṉakaramum varumoḻit takaramum akaramuṅ keṭuttu, aḻānti puḻānti eṉa muṭikka.	(53)
iyaṟpeyar aṭiyaṭuttuḻi iyalpāy muṭital
349.	ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi iyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi - avviyaṟ peyar paṇpu aṭuttu varumvaḻi, iyaṟkiyākum - muṟkūṟiya iruvakic ceykiyum tavirttu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	peruñcāttaṉṟanti, peruṅkoṟṟaṉṟanti eṉa varum.
koṟṟaṅkoṟṟaṉṟanti, cāttaṅkoṟṟaṉṟanti eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa paṇpaṉṟi aṭi aṭuttaṉavātaliṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka.	(54)
iyaṟpeyar muṉṉar makaṉākiya muṟippeyar varuvaḻi muṭipu
350.	appeyar meyyoḻit taṉkeṭu vaḻiyum
	niṟṟalu murittē ameṉ cāriyi
	makkaḷ muṟitokūum maruṅki ṉāṉa.
itu mēlataṟku vēṟoru varumoḻikkaṇ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) appeyar makkaḷ āṉa muṟi tokūum maruṅkiṉum - avviyaṟpeyar muṉṉart tantiyaṉṟi makaṉākiya muṟip peyar vantu tokumiṭattiṉum, meyyoḻittu aṉ keṭuvaḻi ammeṉ cāriyi niṟṟalum urittu - avviyaṟpeyariṉ tāṉ ēṟiya mey niṟka aṉ keṭṭu ammuccāriyi vantu niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
‘āṉa’ eṉpataṉi makkaḷōṭum, ‘um’miyi maruṅkiṉoṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. ‘muṟi tokūu maruṅkiṉ’ eṉṟatu, iṉṉāṟku makaṉeṉṉum muṟippeyarāyc cērumiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
e-ṭu:	koṟṟaṅkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṅkoṟṟaṉ eṉa nilimoḻi aṉ keṭṭu ammu vantatu. ivaṟṟiṟku, atuveṉurupu viriyātu ataṉ uṭimip poruḷ virikka. itu muṟippeyar.
iṉi ummiyāṟ, koṟṟaṅkuṭi cāttaṅkuṭi eṉap piṟa peyar tokkaṉavuṅ koḷka. ‘meyyoḻittu’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, koṟṟamaṅkalam cāttamaṅkalam eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ ammiṉ makaraṅ keṭutalum, vēṭṭa maṅkalam, vēṭṭaṉkuṭi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ nilimoḻiyoṟṟu iraṭṭu taluṅ koḷka.	(55)
iyaṟpeyar cila ‘tanti’yum
makaṉmuṟiyum varuvaḻi iyalpātal
351.	tāṉum pēṉum kōṉum eṉṉum
	āmuṟi yiyaṟpeyar tiripiṭa ṉilavē.
itu mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉum pēṉum kōṉum eṉṉum a muṟi iyaṟpeyar - avviyaṟpeyaruḷ tāṉum pēṉum kōṉumeṉṉum ammuṟiyiṉi yuṭiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ, tantiyoṭum makkaḷ muṟimiyoṭum puṇarum vaḻi, tiripiṭaṉila - muṟkūṟiya tiripukaḷiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tāṉṟanti pēṉṟanti kōṉṟanti eṉavum, tāṉkoṟ ṟaṉ pēṉkoṟṟaṉ, kōṉkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum varum.
pēṉ kōṉ eṉpaṉa muṟkālattu vaḻakku. ivi tokimarapiṉuḷ ‘aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar’ (eḻut. 155) eṉpuḻi iyalpāyiṉavēṉum īṇṭu ivvīṟṟiṟkut tiripu kūṟutaliṉ, ataṉi vilakki iyalpāmeṉpa tūuṅ kūṟiṉār.	(56)
tāṉ, yāṉ eṉumivi taṉ, eṉ eṉat tirital
352.	tāṉyā ṉeṉumpeyar urupiyal niliyum.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu, toki marapiṉuḷ ‘aḵṟiṇi viravup peyar’ (eḻut. 155) eṉpataṉāṉē iyalpāy niṉṟa tāṉ eṉpataṉiyum, ‘uyirīṟākiya’ (eḻut. 153) eṉpataṉāṉ iyalpāy niṉṟa yāṉ eṉpataṉiyum avviyalpu vilakki urupiyaloṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) tāṉ yāṉ eṉum peyar - tāṉ eṉṉum viravup peyarum yāṉ eṉṉum uyartiṇip peyarum, urupiyal niliyum - urupiyaliṟ kūṟiya iyalpilē nilipeṟṟut tāṉ eṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukit taṉ eṉṟum, yāṉ eṉpatu ākāram ekaramāy yakaraṅ keṭṭu eṉ eṉṟum muṭiyum e-ṟu. 
e-ṭu:	taṉki, eṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇun taṉñāṇ eṉñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉa varum.	(57)
alvaḻikkaṇ avi iyalpāy muṭital
353.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kuṟukalun tiritalun
	tōṟṟa milli eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi alvaḻi - muṟkūṟiya tāṉ yāṉ eṉpaṉa vēṟṟumip puṇarcci yallātaviṭattu, kuṟukalun tiritalun tōṟṟa milli eṉmaṉār pulavar - tāṉ eṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukutalum yāṉ eṉpatu avvāṟu tiritalun tōṟṟamiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tāṉkuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ valiyaṉ eṉavum; yāṉ kuṟiyēṉ ciṟiyēṉ tīyēṉ periyēṉ ñāṉṟēṉ nīṇṭēṉ māṇṭēṉ valiyēṉ eṉavum varum.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumikkaṇ avvāṟaṉṟi ṉakaram tiritaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	taṟpukaḻ taṟpāṭi, eṟpukaḻ eṟpāṭi eṉa varum.	(58)
‘aḻaṉ’ vēṟṟumi muṭipu
354.	aḻaṉeṉ iṟutikeṭa valleḻuttu mikumē.
itu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṉakaran tiriyātu keṭuka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḻaṉ eṉ iṟutikeṭa - aḻaṉeṉṉuñ col taṉ īṟṟu ṉakaraṅ keṭa, valleḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aḻakkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa varum.
aḻakkuṭa meṉpatu piṇakkuṭatti.	(59)
‘muṉ’, ‘il’loṭu puṇarvaḻi ilakkaṇamarūumuṭipu
355.	muṉṉeṉ kiḷavi muṉṉart tōṉṟum
	illeṉ kiḷavimici ṟakara moṟṟal
	tolliyal maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapē.
itu ‘maruviṉṟokuti’ (eḻut. 111) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiya ilakkaṇa marūukkaḷiṉ oṉṟaṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉ eṉ kiḷavi muṉṉart tōṉṟum il eṉ kiḷavi mici - muṉṉeṉṉuñ colliṉ muṉṉē varum illeṉṉuñ colliṉ mēlē, ṟakaram oṟṟal - oru ṟakara voṟṟu vantu niṉṟu muṭital, tolliyal maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapu - paḻiyatākiya iyalpiṉiyuṭiya vaḻakkiṭattu maruvi vanta ilakkaṇa muṭipu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muṉṟil eṉa varum.
‘ilmuṉ’ eṉa niṟkaṟpālatu, muṉṟil eṉṟu talitaṭumāṟutaliṉ marūuvāyiṟṟu. muṉṉeṉpataṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutal ilakkaṇamēṉum, aḵtaṉṟit taṉakku iṉamāyatoru ṟakara voṟṟup peṟutaliṉ vēṟu muṭipāyiṟṟu.	(60)
‘poṉ’ ceyyuḷmuṭipu peṟumāṟu
356.	poṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yīṟukeṭa muṟiyiṉ	
	muṉṉart tōṉṟum lakāra makāram
	ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toṭariya lāṉa.
iḵtu avvīṟṟup peyaroṉṟaṟkuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) poṉ eṉ kiḷavi īṟu keṭa -  poṉ eṉṉuñ col taṉ īṟākiya ṉakaraṅ keṭāniṟka, muṉṉar lakāra makāram muṟiyil tōṉṟum - ataṉmuṉṉar lakaramum makaravoṟṟum muṟiyāṉē vantu niṟkum, ceyyuḷ maruṅkiṉ toṭariyalāṉa - aṅṅaṉam niṟpatu ceyyuḷiṭattuc coṟkaḷ tammuḷ toṭarccippaṭum iyalpiṉkaṇ e-ṟu.
‘muṟiyiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, makaram oṟṟātal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘polampaṭip polinta koycuvaṟ puravi’ (malipaṭu. 574) eṉa varum.
‘toṭariyalāṉa’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, vaṉkaṇattukkaṇṇum lakaram niṟka, makaram valleḻuttiṟkēṟṟa melleḻuttākat tirital koḷka. ‘polaṅkalañ cumanta pūṇṭāṅ kiḷamuli’ (akam. 16), ‘polañcuṭa rāḻi pūṇṭa tērē’, ‘polantārk kuṭṭuvaṉ’ (puṟam. 343) eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ‘polanaṟunteriyal’ (puṟam. 29) ‘polamala rāviri’ (kali. 138) eṉṟāṟ pōla makaraṅ keṭṭup piṟakaṇattu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.	(61)
yakara īṟṟuppeyaratu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
357.	yakara iṟuti vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ
	valleḻut tiyiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē.
itu muṟiyāṉē yakara īṟṟiṟku vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) yakara iṟuti vēṟṟumip poruḷvayiṉ - yakara īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumipporuṭ puṇarccikkaṇ, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikum - valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi vantu iyiyiṉ avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāykkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.	(62)
‘tāy’ iyalpāy muṭital
358.	tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟki yākum.
itu viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiya valleḻuttu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tāyeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkiyākum - tāyeṉṉuñ col valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tāyki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
mēlic cūttirattāṉ mikutiyuṅ kūṟutaliṉ, ‘aḵṟiṇi viravup peyaruḷ’ (155) aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu.	(63)
‘makaṉtāy’ makaṉviṉi varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
359.	makaṉviṉi kiḷappiṉ mutaṉili iyaṟṟē.
iḵtu eytātatu eytuvittatu; tāyeṉpatu aṭiyaṭuttuḻi valleḻuttu mikuka eṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) makaṉ viṉi kiḷappiṉ - tāyeṉṉuñ col taṉakku aṭiyāy muṉvanta makaṉatu viṉiyip piṉṉāka oruvaṉ kūṟumiṭattu, mutal nili iyaṟṟu - ivvīṟṟuḷ mutaṟkaṭ kūṟiya nilimiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	makaṉṟāykkalām ceru tuṟattal pakittal eṉa varum.
makaṉ tāyoṭu kalāytta kalām eṉa viriyum. ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum ēṟkum urupu virikka. viṉi, īṇṭup pakimēṟṟu.	(64)
ivvīṟṟuppeyar melleḻuttōṭu uṟaḻvaṉavum uḷavātal
360.	melleḻut tuṟaḻum moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
iḵtu eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumāruḷa - yakara īṟṟuḷ atikāra valleḻuttiṉoṭu melleḻuttu mikku uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum uḷa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vēykkuṟi vēyṅkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉavarum.(65)
ivvīṟṟatu alvaḻimuṭipu
361.	alvaḻi yellām iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu avvīṟṟu alvaḻikku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻi ellām iyalpeṉa moḻipa - yakara īṟṟu alvaḻi ellām iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāykaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, avvāykkoṇṭāṉ ivvāyk koṇṭāṉ uvvāykkoṇṭāṉ evvāykkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa muṭivaṉavum, tāyk koṇṭāṉ tūyppeytāṉ eṉṟāṟpōlum viṉiyeccamum, poyccol meyccol eyppaṉṟi eṉṟāṟpōlum paṇput tokiyum, vēykkaṭitu vēykaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻi yuṟaḻcci muṭivuṅ koḷka.	(66)
rakāra īṟṟup peyaratu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
362.	rakāra iṟuti yakāra iyaṟṟē.
itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē rakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) rakāra iṟuti - rakāra īṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ, yakāra iyaṟṟu-yakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tērkkāl celavu tali puṟam eṉa varum.
immāṭṭēṟṟiṉi, yakara īṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻi yeṉṉum iraṇṭiyum karuti māṭṭeṟintāreṉpar, alvaḻi muṭipum īṇṭuk kāṭṭuvar. yām ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṟ kāṭṭutum. itu ḻakara īṟṟiṟkum okkum.
māṭṭēṟṟāṉ uṟaḻcciyuṅ koḷka. vērkkuṟi vērṅkuṟi eṉa varum.	(67)
ār mutaliya nāṉkum meli mikappeṟutal
363.	ārum vetiruñ cārum pīrum
	melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) ārum vetiruñ cārum pīrum - āreṉṉuñ collum vetireṉṉuñ collum cāreṉṉuñ collum pīreṉṉuñ collum, melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟat tōṉṟum - melleḻuttu mikku muṭital meymmi peṟat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e- ṭu:	ārṅkōṭu, vetirṅkōṭu, cārṅkōṭu, pīrṅkoṭi, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
pīr marameṉpār pīrṅkōṭeṉpar. ‘pīrvāyp pirintanīr niṟi muṟi ceytu’ eṉṟāṟpōlac cāṉṟōr palaruñ ceyyuḷ ceytavāṟu kāṇka.
‘meypeṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘āraṅ kaṇṇi yaṭupōrc cōḻar’ (akam. 93) eṉa ār ammuppeṟutalum, ‘mārip pīrat talarcila koṇṭē’ (kuṟun. 98) eṉap pīr attup peṟutaluṅ koḷka. itaṉi atikārap puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭippārum uḷar. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, kūrṅkatirvēl īrṅkōti eṉṟāṟpōlavum, kutirṅkōṭu vilarṅ kōṭu ayirṅkōṭu tuvarṅkōṭu cilirṅkōṭu eṉṟāṟ pōlavum melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, tuvaraṅkōṭu eṉa ammup peṟutaluṅ koḷka.	(68)
‘cār’, ‘kāḻ’ varuvaḻip puṇarumāṟu
364.	cāreṉ kiḷavi kāḻvayiṉ valikkum.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) cār eṉ kiḷavi kāḻvayiṉ valikkum - cār eṉpatu kāḻ eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu valleḻuttu mikkup puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cārkkāḻ eṉa varum.
cāriṉatu vitteṉpatē poruḷ. itaṉi vayirameṉiṟ kiḷan tōtuvāreṉṟu uṇarka.	(69)
‘pīr’ ammuc cāriyiyoṭum muṭital
365.	pīreṉ kiḷavi ammoṭuñ civaṇum.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) pīr eṉ kiḷavi ammoṭuñ civaṇum - pīr eṉṉuñ col melleḻuttēyaṉṟi ammup peṟṟum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pīraṅkoṭi cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, ‘poṉ pōṟ pīramoṭu pūtta putaṉmalar’ (neṭunal. 14) eṉavum varum.(70)
ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu.
lakāra īṟṟuppeyaratu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
366.	lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra iyaṟṟē.
itu muṟiyāṉē lakāra īṟṟi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra iyaṟṟu - lakāra īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṉakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy lakaram ṟakaramāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṟkuṟi ciṟi tali puṟam, neṟkatir cōṟu tali puṟam eṉavarum.	(71)
ivvīṟu iruvaḻiyum meṉkaṇam varuvaḻit tirital
367.	melleḻut tiyiyiṉ ṉakāra mākum.
itu ṉakāramām eṉṟaliṉ ataṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṉakāra mākum - avvīṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyiyiṉ ṉakaramākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṉñeri nuṉi muri eṉa varum.
iccūttirattiṉi vēṟṟumi yiṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttukkōṭaṟkaṇ ciṅkanōkkāka vittamiyāṉ, alvaḻikkum immuṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kaṉñerintatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉa varum.	(72)
ivvīṟṟu alvaḻimuṭipu
368.	alvaḻi yellām uṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu avvīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻiyellām uṟaḻeṉa moḻipa - ivvīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷellām tantiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalkuṟitu kaṟkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. 
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, kalkuṟumi kaṟkuṟumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉak kuṇampaṟṟivanta vēṟṟumikkum uṟaḻcci koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē viṉiccollīṟu tirintaṉavum urupu tirintaṉavuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vantāṉāṟkoṟṟaṉ poruvāṉāṟpōkāṉ eṉavum attāṟkoṇṭāṉ ittāṟkoṇṭāṉ uttāṟ koṇṭāṉ ettāṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum varum.
akkāṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōlap piṟavum muṭipu uḷḷaṉa vellām itaṉāṉ muṭittuk koḷka.	(73)
lakaram āytamākavum tirintuniṟkumiṭaṉ
369.	takaram varuvaḻi āytam niliyalum
	pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulami yōrē.
itu lakaram ṟakaramāyt tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamākavum tiriyumeṉṟa liṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) takaram varuvaḻi āytam niliyalum - takaram mutalākiya moḻi vantāl lakaram ṟakaramāyt tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamākat tirintu niṟṟalum, pukar iṉṟu eṉmaṉār pulamiyōr - kuṟṟamiṉṟeṉṟu colluvār āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaḵṟītu kaṟṟītu eṉa varum.
‘pukariṉṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘neṭiyataṉiṟuti’ (eḻut. 370) eṉpataṉuḷ vēṟītu vēṟṟītu eṉṉum uṟaḻcci muṭipuṅ koḷka.	(74)
taṉineṭiloṟṟu(t tirintu uṟaḻtalēyaṉṟi)
iyalpātalum uṇmi
370.	neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpumā ruḷavē.
iḵtu ‘alvaḻiyellāmuṟaḻ’ (eḻut. 368) eṉṟataṉōṭu iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpumāruḷa - neṭṭeḻuttiṉ īṟṟu lakāra īṟu kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa lakāram pōlat tirintu uṟaḻtalē yaṉṟi iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pālkaṭitu tītu peritu eṉavarum. iyalpākātu tirintaṉa vēṟkaṭitu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa.	(75)
nel mutaliya nāṟcollum alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
371.	nelluñ celluṅ kolluñ collum
	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumi iyala.
iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ uṟaḻntu muṭika eṉṟataṉi vēṟṟumi muṭipip peṟum eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) nelluñ celluṅ kolluñ collum - nelleṉ ṉuñ collum celleṉṉuñ collum kolleṉṉuñ collum colleṉṉuñ collumākiya in nāṉku collum, allatu kiḷappi ṉum vēṟṟumi iyala - alvaḻiyic collumiṭattun tām vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉ iyalpiṉavāy lakaram ṟakaramāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
ummi ciṟappu.
e-ṭu:	neṟkāyttatu. ceṟkaṭitu, koṟkaṭitu, coṟkaṭitu; ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.	(76)
‘il’ eṉum viṉikkuṟippu muṭintu niṟkumāṟu
372.	illeṉ kiḷavi iṉmi ceppiṉ
	valleḻuttu mikutalum aiyiṭi varutalum
	iyaṟki yātalum ākāram varutalum
	koḷattaku marapiṉ ākiṭa ṉuṭittē.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viṉikkuṟippuc colluḷ oṉṟaṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) illeṉ kiḷavi iṉmi ceppiṉ - illeṉṉuñ col iruppiṭamākiya illi uṇarttātu oru poruḷiṉatu illāmiyi uṇarttum iṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum - valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum, ai iṭi varutalum - aikāram iṭiyē varutalum, iyaṟkiyātalum - iraṇṭum vārātu iyalpāy muṭitalum, ākāram varutalum - ākāram vantu muṭitalumākiya in nāṉku muṭipum, koḷattaku marapiṉ - coṟkumuṭipākak koḷattakum muṟiyāṉē, ākiṭaṉuṭittu - taṉ muṭipām iṭaṉ uṭittu e-ṟu.
‘koḷattaku marapiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi aikāram varutalum, aikāram vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum mikāmiyum, ākāram vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘il’ eṉa niṟuttik, koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ teḷivu poruḷ eṉat tantu valleḻuttum aikāramuṅ koṭuttu illik koṟṟaṉeṉa ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. iṉṉum avvāṟē niṟutti, aikāramē koṭuttu illikoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ teḷivu poruḷ eṉa valleḻuttu mikātu muṭikka.
iṉṉum ‘koḷattaku marapiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ēṉikkaṇattiṉ muṉṉum aikāramē koṭuttu illi ñāṇ nūl maṇi vāṉam āṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.
iḵtu, il eṉpatoru mutaṉili niṉṟu varumoḻiyōṭu iṅṅaṉam puṇarntateṉpatu uṇartaṟku ‘illeṉkiḷavi’ eṉṟum, ‘iyaṟkiyātalum’ eṉṟum kūṟiṉār. immuṭipu viṉiyiyaluḷ viravu viṉikkaṇ ‘iṉmiceppal’ eṉpuḻi ‘illi il’ (col. 222) eṉṟu uri kūṟiyavataṉāṉum, ‘avaṉilli’ eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa utāraṇamāka ellā āciriyaruṅ kāṭṭiyavāṟṟāṉum uṇarka.
itaṉāṉē, iṅṅaṉam puṇartta collaṉṟi ‘illi’ eṉa aikāra īṟāy niṟpatoru col iṉmiyum uṇarka. āyiṉ, iṉmi mutaliyavaṟṟiyum ivvāṟē puṇarkka eṉiṉ, avi varumoḻiyiṉṟi oru collāy niṟṟaliṉ puṇarkkārāyiṉār.
iṉi, iyalpu varumāṟu:- eṇṇilkuṇam ceyki tuṭi poruḷ eṉavum, poyyilñāṉam, ‘miyilvāṇmukam’ (kali. 7) eṉavum varum.
iṉi, ākāram varumāṟu:-illākkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ poruḷ eṉa ākāram valleḻuttup peṟṟu varum.
piṟkūṟiya iraṇṭum illeṉṉum viṉikkuṟippu mutaṉili yaṭiyākat tōṉṟiya peyarecca maṟi tokkum virintum niṉṟaṉa.
iyalpu muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ, immuṭipiṟku vēṇṭuñ ceyki ceyka. tāviṉīṭci eṉṟāṟpōl vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum vēṇṭuñ ceyki ceytu muṭikka.	(77)
‘val’ toḻiṟpeyarpōla ukaram peṟṟu muṭital
373.	valleṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.
iḵtu iruvaḻiyun tirintum uṟaḻntum varumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakkit toḻiṟpeyaroṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) val eṉ kiḷavi - valleṉṉuñ col alvaḻik kaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum, toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇat tum iṭik kaṇattu vakārattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vallukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, vallukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.	(78)
‘nāy’ ‘palaki’ varuvaḻi, ‘val’ muṭiyumāṟu
374	nāyum palakiyum varūuṅ kāli
	āvayiṉ ukaraṅ keṭutalu murittē
	ukaraṅ keṭuvaḻi akaram niliyum.
iḵtu eytiyatu oruvaḻi vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) nāyum palakiyum varūuṅ kāli - valleṉpataṉ muṉ nāyeṉṉuñ collum palakiyeṉṉuñ collum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, āvayiṉ ukaraṅ keṭutalum urittu - avviṭattu ukaram keṭātu niṟṟalēyaṉṟik keṭṭu muṭiyavum peṟum, ukaraṅkeṭuvaḻi akaram niliyum - avvukaraṅ keṭumiṭattu akaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	valla nāy, vallappalaki eṉa varum.
ummi etirmaṟiyākalāṉ ukaraṅ keṭātē niṉṟu, vallunāy valluppalaki eṉa varutaluṅ koḷka. ‘akaram niliyum’ eṉṉātu ‘ukaraṅ keṭum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ piṟa varumoḻikkaṇṇum ivvakarappēṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vallak kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉa varum.	(79)
‘pūl’ mutaliya mūṉṟum ammup peṟutal
375.	pūlvē leṉṟā āleṉ kiḷaviyōṭu
	āmup peyarkkum amiṭi varumē.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) pūl vēl eṉṟā āleṉ kiḷaviyōṭu ā muppeyarkkum - pūleṉṉuñ collum vēleṉṉuñ collum āleṉṉuñ collumākiya ammūṉṟu peyarkkum, am iṭi varum - vēṟṟumikkaṇ tiripiṉṟi ammuccāriyi iṭivantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pūlaṅkōṭu, vēlaṅkōṭu, ālaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpukaṇattum oṭṭuka.
e-ṭu:	pūlañeri, vēlañeri, ālañeri, nīḻal viṟaku eṉavarum.
‘eṉṟā’ eṉa eṇṇiṭi yiṭṭamiyāṟ, pūlāṅkōṭu pūlāṅkaḻi eṉa ākāram pūlukkukkoḷka.	(80)
ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar
piṟa īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar aṉiyavātal
376.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇun tiripum uṟaḻcciyum vilakki ñakārīṟṟut toḻiṟ peyaroṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟpeyarellām - lakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peya rellām, toḻiṟpeyar iyala - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyariṉ iyalpiṉavāy iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟumuṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pullukkaṭitu, kallukkaṭitu, vallukkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, vallukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.
ivaṟṟiṟkup pullutal, kallutal, vallutal eṉap poruḷurikka,
iṉi, ‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, toḻiṟpeyarviti eytātu piṟaviti eytuvaṉavuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kaṉṉalkaṭitu, piṉṉalkaṭitu, kaṉṉaṟkaṭumi, piṉṉaṟ kaṭumi eṉavum varum.
itaṉāṉē, meṉkaṇam vantuḻip piṉṉaṉ ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu, piṉṉaṉ ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉa oṭṭuka. iṉi, āṭal pāṭal kūṭal nīṭal mutaliyaṉavum alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyum vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintum muṭital itaṉāṟ koḷka.	(81)
‘veyil’ attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭital
377.	veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiyal niliyum.
itu tiripuvilakki attum iṉṉum vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) veyil eṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiyal niliyum - veyileṉṉuñ col maḻiyeṉṉuñ coṟpōla attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
maḻi eṉpataṉi ‘vaḷiyeṉa varūum’ (eḻut. 242) eṉpataṉuṭaṉum, vaḷiyeṉpataṉip ‘paṉiyeṉavarūum’ (eḻut. 241) eṉpataṉuṭaṉum māṭṭeṟintavāṟu kāṇka.
e-ṭu:	veyilattukkoṇṭāṉ veyiliṟkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
iḵtu, attumici yoṟṟuk keṭātu niṉṟa iṭam. iḵtu, ‘avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu’ (eḻut. 133) mikkatu, atikāra valleḻuttiṉmiyiṉ. iyalpu kaṇattuṅ koḷka, cāriyi varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ.	(82)
cuṭṭu mutal vakaram vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi muṭital
378.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya vakara iṟuti
	muṟpaṭak kiḷanta urupiyal niliyum.
itu muṟiyāṉē vakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya vakara iṟuti - vakara īṟṟup peyar nāṉkaṉuḷ cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vakara īṟṟup peyar mūṉṟum, muṟpaṭak kiḷanta urupiyal niliyum - muṟpaṭak kūṟiya urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉavāy vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭiyum e -ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avaṟṟukkōṭu, ivaṟṟukkōṭu, uvaṟṟukkōṭu, cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
‘muṟpaṭak kiḷanta’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, vaṟṟiṉōṭu iṉṉum peṟutal koḷka. avaṟṟiṉ kōṭu ivaṟṟiṉ kōṭu uvaṟṟiṉ kōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa oṭṭuka. iḵtu ēṉik kaṇattōṭum oṭṭuka.	(83)
avvakaram alvaḻikkaṇ āytamātal
379.	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi āyta mākum.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi āyta mākum - accuṭṭumutal vakaram vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ vēṟṟumi yallāta iṭattu āytamāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aḵkaṭiya iḵkaṭiya uḵkaṭiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa varum.
ivvaḻakku ikkālattu aritu.	(84)
avvakaram, meṉkaṇattoṭu muṭiyumāṟu
380.	melleḻut tiyiyiṉ avveḻut tākum.
iḵtu eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ avveḻuttākum - avvakara īṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyiyumāyiṉ avvakaravoṟṟu avvam melleḻuttāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aññāṇ iññāṇ uññāṇ, nūl maṇi eṉavarum.(85)
ēṉi iṭi uyirkkaṇaṅkaḷ varuvaḻi iyalpātal
381.	ēṉavi puṇariṉ iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
ituvum atu, avvīṟu ēṉik kaṇaṅkaḷoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉavi puṇariṉ-accuṭṭumutal vakara īṟṟōṭu iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum vantu puṇarumāyiṉ, iyalpeṉa moḻipa - avvakaran tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avyāḻ ivyāḻ uvyāḻ, vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.
īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu nilimoḻikkeṉṟum, āṇṭu ‘niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyal pākum’ (eḻut. 144) eṉṟatu varumoḻik keṉṟum uṇarka.	(86)
‘tev’ toḻiṟpeyar pōla ukaram peṟṟu muṭital
382.	ēṉi vakaran toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.
iḵtu eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi vakaram - ‘vakarakkiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu’ (eḻut. 81) eṉṟataṉuḷ oḻintu niṉṟa uriccollākiya vakaram iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tevvukkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, tevvukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.
‘uriyiṟ kōṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, temmuṉi eṉa vakaravoṟṟu makara voṟṟākat tirital koḷka.	(87)
ḻakāra īṟṟuppeyaratu vēṟṟumi muṭipu
383.	ḻakāra iṟuti rakāra iyaṟṟē.
itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē ḻakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ḻakāra iṟuti rakāra iyaṟṟu - ḻakāra īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam vantāl vēṟṟumikkaṇ rakāra īṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pūḻkkāl, ciṟaku tali puṟam eṉa varum.	(88)
‘tāḻ’, ‘kōl’ uruvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
384.	tāḻeṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ
	akkiṭi varutal urittu mākum.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, valleḻuttiṉōṭu akku vakuttaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) tāḻ eṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ - tāḻeṉṉuñ col kōleṉṉuñ colloṭu puṇarum iṭattu, akku iṭivarutalum urittākum - valleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi akkuccāriyi iṭiyē vantu niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
urittumākum eṉṟa ummiyi varutalum eṉa māṟuka.
eṉavē, akkuppeṟātu valleḻuttu mikutal valiyuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu:	tāḻakkōl, tāḻkkōl eṉa varum.
itu tāḻit tiṟakkuṅkōl eṉa viriyum.	(89)
‘tamiḻ’ akkum peṟṟu muṭital
385.	tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyum ataṉō raṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) tamiḻ eṉ kiḷaviyum - tamiḻeṉṉuñ collum, ataṉō raṟṟu - valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalēyaṉṟi akkuccāriyi peṟṟum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘ataṉōraṟṟē’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, itaṟkut tamiḻkkūtteṉa valleḻuttu mikutalē valiyuṭittu.
e-ṭu:	tamiḻakkūttu, cēri tōṭṭam paḷḷi eṉa varum.
tamiḻiyuṭiya kūttu eṉa virikka.
tamiḻavariyar eṉṟāṟpōla, valleḻuttup peṟātu akkup peṟṟaṉa, ‘uṇarakkūṟiya’ (eḻut. 405) eṉṉum puṟaṉaṭiyāṟ koḷka. tamiḻanāṭu tamiḻnāṭu eṉa ēṉik kaṇattu muṭipu, ‘eppeyar muṉṉarum’ (eḻut. 128) eṉpataṉuḷ, ‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭittām.	(90)
‘kumiḻ’ marappeyar melleḻuttup peṟṟumuṭital
386.	kumiḻeṉ kiḷavi marappeya rāyiṉ
	pīreṉ kiḷaviyō(ṭu) ōriyaṟ ṟākum.
itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttum ammum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kumiḻ eṉ kiḷavi marappeyar āyiṉ - kumiḻ eṉṉum col kumiḻttal eṉṉun toḻilaṉṟi marappeyarāyiṉ, pīr eṉ kiḷaviyōṭu ōr iyaṟṟu ākum - pīr eṉṉum collōṭu ōriyalpiṟṟāy oruvaḻi melleḻuttum oruvaḻi ammum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kumiḻṅkōṭu, kumiḻaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
‘ōriyaṟṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavaṟṟiṟkum immuṭipu koḷka. makiḻṅkōṭu, makiḻaṅkōṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(91)
‘pāḻ’ vēṟṟumiyuḷ muṭiyumāṟu
387.	pāḻeṉ kiḷavi melleḻut tuṟaḻvē.
itu valleḻuttiṉoṭu melleḻuttup peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) pāḻ eṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu uṟaḻvu - pāḻeṉṉuñ collīṟu valleḻuttiṉoṭu melleḻuttup peṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	pāḻkkiṇaṟu pāḻṅkiṇaṟu cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.
itu pāḻuṭkiṇaṟu eṉa viriyum, pāḻttakiṇaṟu eṉa viṉit toki muṭiyāmiyiṉ.	(92)
‘ēḻ’ aṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭital
388.	ēḻeṉ kiḷavi urupiyal niliyum.
iḵtu eṇṇuppeyar ivvāṟu muṭika eṉṟaliṉ, eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēḻ eṉ kiḷavi - ēḻeṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar iṟuti, urupiyal niliyum - urupu puṇarccikkaṇ kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē nilipeṟṟu aṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
aḵtu ‘aṉṉeṉ cāriyi yēḻa ṉiṟuti’ (eḻut. 194) eṉpatām.
e-ṭu:	ēḻaṉkāyam, cukku tōri payaṟu eṉa varum.
iyipu valleḻuttu ōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ vīḻkka. iḵtu ēḻaṉāṟkoṇṭa kāyam eṉa viriyum.	(93)
‘ēḻ’, aḷavu niṟi eṇṇup peyaroṭu puṇarumāṟu
389.	aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇum varuvaḻi
	neṭumutal kuṟukalum ukaram varutalum
	kaṭinili yiṉṟē āciri yarkka.
itu mēlataṟku eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇum varuvaḻi - avvēḻeṉpataṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, neṭumutal kuṟukalum ukaram varutalum kaṭiniliyiṉṟē āciriyarkka - muṉṉiṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ māttiri kuṟukutalum āṇṭu ukaram varutalum nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu āciriyarkku e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: 	eḻukalam, cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi eṉavum; eḻukaḻañcu, toṭi palam eṉavum, eḻumūṉṟu eḻunāṉku eṉavum varum.
‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇattup poruṭpeyarkkum immuṭipu koḷka. eḻukaṭal, cili tici piṟappu eṉavarum.	(94)
appeyar pattu eṉpataṉoṭu muṭiyumāṟu
390.	patteṉ kiḷavi oṟṟiṭi keṭuvaḻi
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum āytap puḷḷi.
itu mēlataṟku eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu, varumoḻi nōkki vitittaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) pattu eṉ kiḷavi oṟṟiṭi keṭuvaḻi - avvēḻaṉoṭu patteṉpatu puṇarumiṭattu appatteṉ kiḷavi iṭiyoṟṟuk keṭuvaḻi, āytappuḷḷi niṟṟalvēṇṭum - āytamākiya puḷḷi niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eḻupaḵtu eṉa varum.	(95)
‘āyiram’ varuvaḻi ‘ēḻ’ muṭiyumāṟu
391.	āyiram varuvaḻi ukaraṅ keṭumē.
itu neṭumutal kuṟuki niṉṟu ukaram peṟātu eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram varuvaḻi-ēḻeṉpataṉ muṉ āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, ukaraṅ keṭum-neṭu mutal kuṟukiniṉṟu ukarampeṟātu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eḻāyiram eṉa varum.	(96)
‘nūṟāyiram’ varuvaḻi, ‘ēḻ’ iyalpāy muṭital
392.	nūṟūrntu varūum āyirak kiḷavikkuk
	kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukka miṉṟē.
iḵtu eytiyatu muḻuvatūum vilakkiṟṟu, ukaraṅkeṭṭu ataṉ mēlē neṭumutal kuṟukātu eṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟu ūrntu varūum āyirak kiḷavikku - avvēḻeṉpatu nūṟeṉṉuñ colmēl varum āyirameṉṉuñ colliṟku, kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukkamiṉṟu - muṟkūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutaliṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēḻ nūṟāyiram eṉavarum.
‘kūṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟṟu eḻu nūṟāyiram eṉavum varum. itaṉāṉē, ēḻāyiram eṉa mēl mutaṉili kuṟukāmiyuṅ koḷka. itaṉāṉē, eḻuñāyiṟu eḻunāḷ eḻuvaki eṉa iyalpukaṇattu neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutaluṅ koḷka.	(97)
tāmari mutaliya pēreṇṇuppeyar varuvaḻiyum aṟṟātal
393.	aiam palleṉa varūu miṟuti
	alpeya reṇṇiṉum āyiyal niliyum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti - avvēḻaṉ muṉṉar aiyeṉṟum ammeṉṟum palleṉṟum varukiṉṟa iṟutikaḷiyuṭiya, alpeyar eṇṇiṉum - poruṭpeyarallāta eṇṇuppeyarākiya tāmari veḷḷam āmpal eṉpaṉa vantālum, ā iyal niliyum - neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟṟu vārātu avviyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēḻtāmari, ēḻveḷḷam, ēḻāmpal eṉa varum.	(98)
uyirkkaṇam varuvaḻiyum iyalpātal
394.	uyirmuṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu.
ituvum atu.
(i -ḷ.) uyirmuṉ variṉum - avvēḻeṉpataṉ muṉṉar aḷavup peyarum eṇṇup peyarumākiya uyirmutaṉmoḻi variṉum, ā iyal tiriyātu - neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟṟu vārātu muṭiyum iyalpiṉ tiriyātu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēḻakal, ēḻuḻakku, ēḻoṉṟu, ēḻiraṇṭu eṉa varum.	(99)
‘kīḻ’ iyalpōṭu uṟaḻtal
395.	kīḻeṉ kiḷavi uṟaḻat tōṉṟum.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟṟumikkaṇ uṟaḻcci kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kīḻ eṉ kiḷavi uṟaḻat tōṉṟum - kīḻeṉṉuñ col uṟaḻcciyāyt tōṉṟi muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘tōṉṟum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟukātu valleḻuttup peṟṟum peṟātum varumeṉṟa iraṇṭum uṟaḻcciyāy varumeṉṟu koḷka. iyipu valleḻuttu atikārattāṟ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kīḻkkuḷam kīḻkuḷam, cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa varum.	(100)
ḷakāra īṟṟuppeyaratu vēṟṟumimuṭipu
396.	ḷakāra iṟuti ṇakāra iyaṟṟē.
itu niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē ḷakāra īṟṟuccol vēṟṟumikkaṇ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ḷakāra iṟuti ṇakāra iyaṟṟu - ḷakāra īṟṟup peyar ṇakāra īṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ḷakāram ṭakāramāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muṭkuṟi, ciṟi tali puṟam eṉa varum.	(101)
meṉkaṇam varuvaḻi iruvaḻiyum ḷakaram ṇakaramātal
397.	melleḻut tiyiyiṉ ṇakāra mākum.
itu mēlataṟku meṉkaṇattu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṇakāramākum - ḷakāra īṟu melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantu iyiyiṉ ṇakaramāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muṇñeri, nuṉimaram eṉa varum.
itaṉi vēṟṟumiyiṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttukkōṭaṟkaṇ ‘ciṅkanōkkāka’ vittaliṉ, alvaḻikkum immuṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	muṇñerintatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉa varum.	(102)
ivvīṟu tiriyātum tirintum uṟaḻntuvarum alvaḻi muṭipu
398.	alvaḻi yellām uṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.
itu mēlataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) alvaḻiyellām - ḷakāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām, uṟaḻeṉa moḻipa - tiriyātum ṭakāramāyt tirintum uṟaḻntu muṭiyu meṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muḷkaṭitu muṭkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuṇavēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ivvuṟaḻcci koḷka. muḷkuṟumi muṭkuṟumi, ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum, kōḷkaṭumi kōṭkaṭumi vāḷkaṭumi vāṭkaṭumi eṉavum oṭṭuka. itaṉāṉē, atōṭkoṇṭāṉ itōṭkoṇṭāṉ utōṭ koṇṭāṉ etōṭkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉavum koḷka.	(103)
takaram varuvaḻi āytamākavum tirintu niṟkum muṭipu
399.	āyta niliyalum varinili yiṉṟē
	takaram varūuṅ kāli yāṉa.
itu mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēl ciṟappuviti vakuttatu, takaram varuvaḻi uṟaḻcciyēyaṉṟi āytamākat tirintum uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) takaram varūuṅ kāliyāṉa - takara mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi yāy varuṅkālattu, āytam niliyalum variniliyiṉṟu - ḷakāram ṭakāramākat tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamākat tirintu niṟṟalum nīkkum nilimiyiṉṟu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muḵṭītu, muṭṭītu eṉa varum.	(104)
taṉineṭiliṟuti ḷakaram iyalpātalum,
kuṟṟoṟṟu vēṟṟumimuṭipu peṟutalum
400.	neṭiyata ṉiṟuti iyalpā kunavum
	vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi vēṟṟumi niliyalum
	pōṟṟal vēṇṭum moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
itu mēlataṟku, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpu ākunavum - avvīṟṟu neṭiyataṉ iṟuti tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavaṟṟiyum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi vēṟṟumi niliyalum - vēṟṟumiyallāta viṭattu vēṟṟumiyiṉ iyalpi uṭiyaṉavāyt tirintu muṭitaliyum, pōṟṟal vēṇṭum moḻiyumāruḷa - pōṟṟutal vēṇṭuñ coṟkaḷum uḷa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōḷkaṭitu, vāḷkaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉavum, ‘puṭṭēmpap puyaṉmāṟi’ (paṭṭiṉappāli. 89) eṉavum varum.
‘pōṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, utaḷaṅkāy cetiḷ pū tōl eṉa ammup peṟutaluṅ koḷka. utaḷ eṉpatu yāṭṭiṉi uṇart tuṅkāl muṟkūṟiya muṭipukaḷ iruvaḻikkum ēṟṟavāṟē muṭikka. utaṭ kōṭu, utaḷkaṭitu, utaṇaṉṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. ‘mōttiyun takarum utaḷu mapparum’ eṉṟār marapiyalil (47).	(105)
ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar muṭipu
401.	toḻiṟpeya rellān toḻiṟpeya riyala.
iḵtu ivvīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyarkku iruvaḻiyum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) toḻiṟ peyarellām - ḷakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyarellām iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟ peyar pōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tuḷḷukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, tuḷḷukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, iruvaḻiyun toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟātu tirintun tiriyātum muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. kōḷkaṭitu kōṭkaṭitu, kōḷkaṭumi kōṭkaṭumi eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa piṟavum varum.
iṉi, vāḷkaṭitu vāṭkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, vāḷkaṭumi vāṭkaṭumi eṉavuṅ kāṭṭuka. vāḷ - kollutal.	(106)
‘iruḷ’ attum iṉṉum peṟutal
402.	iruḷeṉ kiḷavi veyiliyal niliyum.
itu tiripu vilakki attum iṉṉum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) iruḷ eṉ kiḷavi - iruḷeṉṉuñ col vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ, veyiliyal niliyum - veyileṉṉuñ coṟpōla attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ iruḷiṟkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
cāriyi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpukaṇattum oṭṭuka.
e-ṭu:	iruḷattuñāṉṟāṉ, nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ; iruḷiṉ ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(107)
‘puḷ’, ‘vaḷ’ - iraṇṭum iruvaḻiyum ukaram peṟutal
403.	puḷḷum vaḷḷun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
ituvum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu, tiripum uṟaḻvum vilakkit toḻiṟpeyarviti vakuttaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷum vaḷḷum - puḷḷeṉṉum collum vaḷḷeṉ ṉum collum iruvaḻikkaṇṇum, toḻiṟ peyar iyala - ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇat tum, iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum e- ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷḷukkaṭitu vaḷḷukkaṭitu, ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, puḷḷukkaṭumi vaḷḷuk kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.
itaṉit ‘toḻiṟ peyarellām’ (eḻut. 401) eṉpataṉpiṉ viyātataṉāṉ, iruvaḻiyum vēṟṟumittiripu eyti muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	puṭkaṭitu vaṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, puṭkaṭumi vaṭkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum, puṇ ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉavum, puṇñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum varum.
puḷḷuvalitu puḷvalitu, puḷḷuvaṉmi puḷvaṉmi eṉa vakarattiṉ muṉṉar ukaram peṟṟum peṟātum varutaliṉ, ‘niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyal pākum’ (eḻut. 144) eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭiyāmi uṇarka. itu vaḷḷiṟkum okkum.	(108)
‘makkaḷ’ īṟṟu ḷakaram ṭakaramākum iṭaṉ
404.	makka ḷeṉṉum peyarcco liṟuti
	takkavaḻi aṟintu valittalu murittē.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu, ‘uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇippeyar’ (eḻut. 153) eṉpataṉuṭ kūṟiya iyalpu vilakkit tiripu vakuttaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarccol iṟuti - makkaḷeṉṉum peyarccol iṟuti iyalpēyaṉṟi, takkavaḻi aṟintu valittalum urittu - takka iṭam aṟintu valloṟṟākat tirintu muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
‘takkavaḻi’ yeṉṟār, perumpāṉmi makkaḷ uṭampu uyir nīṅkik kiṭanta kālattiṉ aḵtu immuṭipupeṟum eṉṟaṟku.
e-ṭu:	makkaṭki, cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.
‘ikkiṭantatu makkaṭṭali’ eṉpataṉuḷ avvāṟātal koḷka. makkaḷki, cevi, tali, puṟam eṉat tiriyātu niṉṟatu, uyiruṇmi peṟṟatu. iṉic ciṟupāṉmi, makkaṭpaṇpu, makkaṭcuṭṭu eṉavum varum.		(109)
ivvōttup puṟaṉaṭi
405.	uṇarak kūṟiya puṇariyal maruṅkiṟ
	kaṇṭuceyaṟ kuriyavi kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē.
iḵtu ivvōttiṉkaṇ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām ituvē ōttākak koṇṭu, cāriyi peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuc cāriyiyum, eḻuttup peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku eḻuttuṅ koṭuttu muṭittukkoḷka eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uṇarak kūṟiya puṇariyal maruṅkiṉ - uṇarakkūṟappaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum iyalpiṭattu, kaṇṭu ceyaṟku uriyavi - mēl muṭitta muṭipaṉṟi vaḻakkiṉuṭ kaṇṭu muṭittaṟku uriyavi tōṉṟiyavaḻi, kaṇṇiṉar koḷal - avaṟṟiyuṅ karutik koṇṭu ēṟṟavāṟē muṭikka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇṇappattam eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ ṇakara īṟu akkup peṟṟatu. maṇṇaṅkaṭṭi eṉa ammuppeṟṟatu.
poṉṉappattam eṉa ṉakara īṟu akkup peṟṟatu. poṉṉaṅkaṭṭi eṉa ammup peṟṟatu. kāṉaṅkōḻi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu. maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi kāṉāṅkōḻi eṉpaṉa marūu.
vēyiṉṟali eṉa yakara īṟṟu urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttuk keṭukka.
nīrkuṟitu eṉa rakara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyiṟṟu. vērkuṟitu vērkkuṟitu itu rakara īṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci. vaṭacārkkūri mēlcārk kūri ivi valleḻuttu mikka marūu muṭipu. amparkkoṇṭāṉ imparkkoṇṭāṉ umparkkoṇṭāṉ emparkkoṇṭāṉ eṉa ivvīṟu ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉa valloṟṟuppeṟṟaṉa. takarkkuṭṭi pukarppōttu eṉpaṉa paṇputtoki karutiṟṟēl īṇṭu muṭikka; vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ muṉṉar muṭiyum.
viḻaleṉṉum lakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṟakaramākātu ṉakaramāy muṭital koḷka. viḻaṉkāṭu; ceṟu tāḷ puṟam eṉavarum. kallampāṟi, ucilaṅkōṭu, eliyālaṅkāy, puṭōlaṅkāy eṉa avvīṟu ammup peṟṟatu. kallāmpāṟi eṉpatu marūu. aḻalattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa avvīṟu attup peṟṟatu. aḻukkaṟpōr, puḻukkaṟcōṟu eṉpaṉa avvīṟṟu alvaḻittiripu.
yāḻkuṟitu eṉpatu ḻakara īṟṟu alvaḻi yiyalpu. vīḻkuṟitu vīḻkkuṟitu eṉpaṉa avvīṟṟu alvaḻiyuṟaḻcci. tāḻappāvi eṉpatu avvīṟṟu alvaḻi akkup peṟṟatu. yāḻiṉkōṭu ceyki tali puṟam eṉa avvīṟṟu urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
muṉṉāḷivāḻvu, muṉṉāḷipparicu, oru nāḷikkuḻavi, oru tiṅkaḷikkuḻavi eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ḷakāra īṟu aikāramum ataṉōṭu valleḻuttum peṟutal koḷka.
piṟavum ivvōttiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa vellām koṇarntu itaṉāṉ muṭikka. kuḷattiṉ puṟam, marattiṉpuṟam eṉa urupiṟku eytiya attōṭu iṉpeṟutaluṅkoḷka.
iṉik ‘kaṭicol lilli’ (col. 452) eṉpataṉāṉ, vaḻakkiṉkaṇ  ṇuñ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum vantu tirintu muṭiyuñ coṟkaḷum uḷa. avaṟṟik ‘kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē’ eṉpataṉāṉ, maṇṇukkup pōṉāṉ, poṉṉukku viṟṟāṉ, poruḷukkup pōṉāṉ, nellukku viṟṟāṉ, koḷḷukkuk koṇṭāṉ, patiṉēḻu eṉṟāṟpōla ‘vaḻakkiṉkaṇ’ ukaram peṟuvaṉavum; ‘viṇṇukkumēl’, ‘maṇṇukku nāppaṇ’ (tiruk. 162),
‘pallukkut tōṟṟa paṉimulli piṅkiḷikaḷ
collukkut tōṟṟiṉṉun tōṟṟilavāl-nellukku
nūṟōoonū ṟeṉpāḷ nuṭaṅkiṭikkum meṉmulikku
māṟōmā  laṉṟaḷanta maṇ’ 
eṉṟāṟpōlac ceyyuṭkaṇ ukarampeṟuvaṉavum, piṟavum muṭikka.
paṟku neṟku eṉpaṉa mutaliyaṉavuṅ koḷka. ivi urupiṉ poruḷpaṭavārātu urupiṉkaṇ vantaṉavēṉum īṇṭuk kāṭṭiṉām; āṇṭup ‘puḷḷiyiṟutiyum’ (eḻut. 202) eṉṉum urupiyaṟ cūttirattu ilēcu kōṭaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟeṉṟu karuti. iṉi, accūttirattut ‘tēruṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭittalum oṉṟu.	(110)
puḷḷimayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

9
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal
kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum
406.	īreḻut torumoḻi uyirttoṭa riṭittoṭar
	āytat toṭarmoḻi vaṉṟoṭar meṉṟoṭar
	āyiru mūṉṟē ukaraṅ kuṟukiṭaṉ.
eṉpatu cūttiram.
ivvōttuk kuṟṟiyalukarameṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa eḻuttup poruṭpeyarōṭum eṇṇuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟōṭum puṇarum muṟimi uṇarttiṉamiyiṟ ‘kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal’ eṉṉum peyart tāyiṟṟu. itu ‘meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala’ (eḻut. 103) eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, uyiriṉatu vikāramāy niṉṟa kuṟṟukaratti iruvaḻikkaṇṇum puṇark kiṉṟamiyiṉ, mēli ōttiṉōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu.
ittalic cūttiram, moḻimarapiṉakattu iruvaḻiya (36) eṉṟa kuṟṟukaram, itaṉakattu iṉittu moḻiyiṟuti varumeṉṟu avaṟṟiṟkup peyarum muṟiyun tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. appeyar peyar, ammuṟi muṟi, attoki toki. ‘toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟu’ (eḻut. 36) varumeṉṟu āṇṭuk kūṟiyavataṉi īṇṭu aintu vakippaṭutti, ataṉoṭu ‘neṭṭeḻut timparum’ (eḻut. 36) eṉṟatu oṉṟē yātaliṉ ataṉiyuṅ kūṭṭi aṟuvakitteṉṟār.
(i-ḷ.) īreḻuttorumoḻi - iraṇṭeḻuttāṉākiya oru moḻiyum, uyirttoṭar - uyirmēlvarum meyyit  toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, iṭittoṭar - iṭiyoṟṟu mēlvarum meyyit toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āytat toṭarmoḻi-āytamākiya eḻuttu mēlvarum meyyit toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, vaṉṟoṭar-valloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyit toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, meṉṟoṭar - melloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyit toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āyiru mūṉṟē-ākiya avvāṟu collumē, ukaraṅ kuṟuku iṭaṉ - ukaraṅ kuṟukivarum iṭam e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāku, varaku, teḷku, eḵku, kokku, kuraṅku eṉa varum.
itaṉi ēḻeṉṟu koḷvārkkup piṇṇākku, cuṇṇāmpu, āmaṇakku mutaliyaṉa muṭiyāmi uṇarka.	(1)
īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi vaṉtoṭarum meṉtoṭarum ātal
407.	avaṟṟuḷ
	īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi iṭittoṭa rākā.
iḵtu avvāṟaṉuḷ oṉṟaṟku, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ-avvāṟaṉuḷ, īroṟṟut toṭar moḻi-iraṇṭoṟṟu iṭikkaṇ toṭarntu niṟkuñ colliṟku iṭiyiṉa oṟṟu mutal niṉṟāl, iṭit toṭarākā - mēl iṭiyiṉan toṭarntu nillā, valliṉamum melliṉamun toṭarntu niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ārkku, īrkku, noympu, moympu eṉa varum.	(2)
āṟu īṟṟiṉum iruvaḻiyum ukaram arimāttiriyē peṟutal
408.	allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum
	ellā iṟutiyum ukara niliyum.
iḵtu ‘iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē’ (eḻut. 37) eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarmoḻikkaṇ arimāttiriyiṉuṅ kuṟukumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakki  ‘avviyal niliyu mēṉi mūṉṟē’ (eḻut. 12) eṉṟa vitiyē peṟumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉum - alvaḻiyic collumiṭattum, vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum - vēṟṟumip puṇarccik kaṇṇum, ellā iṟuti yum ukaram niliyum - āṟu īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum ukaran taṉ ari māttiri yip peṟṟu niṟkum e-ṟu.
varumoḻiyāṉallatu alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyum viḷaṅkāmi yiṉ, ‘allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum’ eṉavē irumoḻip puṇarcci eṉpatu peṟṟām. ivvirumoḻikkaṭ paḻiya arimāttiri peṟṟē niṟkumeṉṟār.
aṉṟi irumoḻip puṇarccikkaṇ oru māttiri peṟumeṉ ṟārkkup paṉmoḻip puṇarcciyākiya ceyyuḷilakkaṇattu kuṟṟuka rattāṉ nērpaci niripaci kōṭalum, avaṟṟāṉ aṟupatu vañciccīr kōṭalum, pattoṉpatiṉāyirat tirunūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟoru toṭi kōṭalum ilavāy muṟṟiyalukaramākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭuta liṉ māṟukoḷak kūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟan taṅkumeṉṟu uṇarka.
e-ṭu:	nākukaṭitu, varakukaṭitu, nākukaṭumi, varakukaṭumi eṉa varum.
ivi tam arimāttiri peṟṟaṉa. ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
iṉi, itu ‘mālyāṟupōntu kālcurantupāyntu’ eṉat toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇum arimāttiri peṟṟatu eṉṉākkāl vañciccīriṉṟāmāṟu uṇarka.	(3)
kuṟṟukaram arimāttiriyiṉum kuṟukiniṟkumiṭaṉ
409.	valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻit
	tolli iyaṟki niliyalum urittē.
itu muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ arimāttiri peṟum eṉṟataṉi vilakki ‘iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉum’ (eḻut. 37) eṉṟataṉāṉ ari māttiriyiṉum kuṟukumeṉṟu āṇṭu vitittatu īṇṭu valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇē varumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) valloṟṟut toṭar moḻi - valloṟṟut toṭar moḻikkuṟṟukaram, valleḻuttu varuvaḻi-valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, tolli iyaṟki niliyalum urittu- ‘iṭippaṭiṟ kuṟukum’ (eḻut. 37) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiya arimāttiri yiṉuṅ kuṟuki niṟkum eṉṟa iyalpilē niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
ummi etirmaṟi.
e-ṭu:	kokkukkaṭitu kokkukkaṭumi eṉa ari māttiriyiṟ kuṟintavāṟu, kuraṅkukaṭitu eṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟoṭu paṭuttuc cevikaruviyāka uṇarka.
muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattu ‘ukara niṟiyum’ eṉṟu pāṭam ōti, ataṟku ukaram arimāttiriyiṟ ciṟitu mikku niṟkumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, iccūttirattiṟkup paḻiya arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar.	(4)
kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻik kaṇṇum varumāṟu
410.	yakaram varuvaḻi ikaraṅ kuṟukum
	ukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu.
itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattu varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) yakaram varuvaḻi ukarakkiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu - yakara mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu nilimoḻik kuṟṟukara veḻuttu muṟṟat tōṉṟātu, ikaraṅ kuṟukum - āṇṭu ōr ikaram vantu arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nākiyātu, varakiyātu, teḷkiyātu, eḵkiyātu, kokkiyātu, kuraṅkiyātu eṉavarum.
‘tuvara’ eṉṟār, āṟu īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum ukaraṅ keṭumeṉṟaṟku.	(5)
īreḻuttumoḻi uyirttoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṭaṟavoṟṟu iraṭṭutal
411.	īreḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum
	vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ oṟṟiṭi iṉamikat
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭum valleḻuttu mikuti.
itu muṟkūṟiya āṟaṉuḷ muṉṉar niṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) īreḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum -  īreḻut torumoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟkum uyirttoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟkum, vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyāyiṉ, iṉoṟṟu iṭimika - iṉamākiya oṟṟu iṭiyilē mika, valleḻuttu mikuti tōṟṟam vēṇṭum - valleḻuttu mikuti tōṉṟi muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṭṭukkāl, cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, muyiṟṟuk kāl ciṉi tali puṟam eṉavum varum. kayiṟṟuppuṟam, vayiṟṟuttī eṉpaṉavumām.
‘tōṟṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ēṉikkaṇattum immuṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	yāṭṭuñāṟci niṇam maṇi vāl ataḷ eṉavum, muyiṟṟu ñāṟci niṇam muṭṭi valimi aṭi āṭṭam eṉavum varum.	(6)
avaṟṟuḷ oṟṟiṭi mikātaṉavum uḷavātal
412.	oṟṟiṭi iṉamikā moḻiyumā ruḷavē
	attiṟat tilli valleḻuttu mikalē.
iḵtu eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṟṟu iṭi iṉam mikā moḻiyumāruḷa - muṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṉuḷ iṉavoṟṟu iṭi mikku muṭiyāta moḻikaḷum uḷa, val leḻuttu mikal attiṟattilli - valloṟṟu mikku muṭital akkūṟṟu ḷilli e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nākukāl cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, varakukatir ciṉi tāḷ patar eṉavum varum.
‘attiṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupiṟku eytiya cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟavaḻi, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
e-ṭu:	yāṭṭiṉkāl, muyiṟṟiṉkāl, nākiṉkāl  varakiṉkatir eṉa varum.
‘attiṟam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉikkaṇattum oṟṟiṭi mikāmi koḷka. nākuñāṟci; nīṭci, māṭci, valimi eṉa oṭṭuka.	(7)
iṭiyoṟṟu āytat toṭark kuṟṟukara īṟukaḷ
iyalpāy muṭital
413.	iṭiyoṟṟut toṭarum āytat toṭarum
	naṭiyā yiyala eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu iṭiniṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum (406) muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṭiyoṟṟut toṭarum āytattoṭarum - iṭi yoṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, naṭi ā iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar - naṭipeṟa naṭakku miṭattu muṟkūṟiya avviyalpu muṭipiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	teḷkukāl, ciṟi tali puṟam eṉavum, eḵku kaṭumi, ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum varum.	(8)
vaṉtoṭar meṉtoṭark kuṟṟukara īṟukaḷ vali mikalum, meṉtoṭark kuṟṟukarac colliṉ meli valiyākat tiritalum
414.	vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
	vanta valleḻut toṟṟiṭi mikumē
	melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi melloṟ ṟellām
	valloṟ ṟiṟuti kiḷiyoṟ ṟākum.
itu piṉṉiṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum (406) muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - vaṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukarīṟum, meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, vanta valleḻuttu oṟṟu iṭimikum - varumoḻiyāy vanta valleḻuttiṉatu oṟṟu iṭiyilē mikku muṭiyum, melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi melloṟṟellām - avviraṇṭaṉuḷ mel loṟṟut toṭarmoḻikkaṇniṉṟa melloṟṟellām, iṟuti lalloṟṟu iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa valloṟṟum kiḷiyoṟṟuākum - kiḷiyākiya valloṟṟumāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
iṟuti valloṟṟu varutalāvatu, kuṟṟukaram ēṟiniṉṟa valloṟ ṟuttāṉē muṉṉarvantu niṟṟalām. kiḷivalloṟṟu varutalāvatu, ṇakārattiṟku ṭakāramum, ṉakārattiṟku ṟakāramum, puṇarcciyum piṟappum nōkkik kiḷiyāmātaliṉ, avi muṉṉar vantu niṟṟalām.
e-ṭu:	kokkukkāl ciṟaku tali puṟam, kuraṅkukkāl cevi tali puṟam, kurakkukkāl cevi tali puṟam, eṭkukkuṭṭi cevi tali puṟam, eṟpukkāṭu curam tali puṟam eṉa varum.
aṟputtaḷi eṉpatu, aṉpiṉāṟ ceyta taḷiyeṉa vēṟṟumiyum, aṉpākiya taḷiyeṉa alvaḻiyumām.
‘vanta’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivviraṇṭaṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.
e-ṭu:	kokkiṉkāl, kuraṅkiṉkāl eṉa varum.
‘ellām’ eṉṟataṉāṟ paṟampiṟpāri, kuṟumpiṟcāṉṟār eṉa melloṟṟut tiriyāmiyuṅ koḷka.
‘oṟṟeṉṟa’ mikutiyāṉ, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum kurakku ñāṟci niṇam mukam viral ukir eṉa melloṟṟut tirintu varumāṟu koḷka. cilappatikāram eṉpatum atu.
vaṉṟoṭarmoḻi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ varutal, ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum.	(9)
kuṟṟukara īṟṟu marappeyar ammuccāriyi peṟutal
415.	marappeyark kiḷavikku ammē cāriyi.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu; valleḻuttu vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) marappeyark kiḷavikku ammē cāriyi - kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu marappeyarkku varuñ cāriyi ammuc cāriyi e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tēkkaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
kamukaṅkāy, teṅkaṅkāy, cīḻkampul, kampampul, payaṟṟaṅkāy eṉṟāṟ pōlum pulliṉiyum marameṉa aṭakki, māṟukoḷak kūṟaleṉat taḻīikkoṇṭa citiveṉpatām ic cūttirameṉṟu uṇarka.	(10)
cila marappeyaratu melloṟṟu valiyāmi
416.	melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyaru muḷavē.
itu meṉṟoṭar moḻikku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyarum uḷa - melloṟṟu val loṟṟākat tiriyātu melloṟṟāy muṭiyum marappeyarum uḷa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puṉkaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kuruntaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum varum.
‘valiyā marappeyarumuḷa’ eṉavē, valikkum marappeyarum uḷaveṉṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vēppaṅkōṭu, kaṭappaṅkāy, īccaṅkuli eṉa varum.	(11)
ammuccāriyi peṟum kuṟṟukara īṟukaḷ
417.	īreḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟut toṭarum
	ammiṭi varaṟku muriyavi uḷavē
	ammara poḻukum moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu īreḻut torumoḻikkum vaṉṟoṭarmoḻikkum eytātatu eytuvittatu, muṉṉar eytiyataṉi vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) īreḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟut toṭarum - īreḻut torumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarīṟum vaṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarīṟum, am iṭi varaṟkum uriyavi uḷa - muṉ muṭittup pōnta muṭipukaḷaṉṟi ammuccāriyi iṭiyē vantu muṭitaṟku uriyaṉavum uḷa; yāṇ ṭeṉiṉ, ammarapu oḻukum moḻivayiṉāṉa - avvilakkaṇam naṭakkum moḻiyiṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ēṟaṅkōḷ (cīvaka. 485), cūtampōr, vaṭṭampōr, puṟṟam paḻañcōṟu eṉa varum. 
ummi etirmaṟiyātaliṉ, ammuppeṟātaṉa nākukāl, kokkukkāl eṉa muṉṉark kāṭṭiṉavēyām.
‘ammarapoḻukum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, aracakkaṉṉi, muracakkaṭippu eṉa akkum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttum; aracavāḻkki, muraca vāḻkki eṉa akkuk koṭuttum muṭikka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē iruṭṭattuk koṇṭāṉ, viḷakkattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttum; mayilāppiṟ koṟṟaṉ, paṟampiṟpāri eṉa iṉ koṭuttum; kariyataṉkōṭu, neṭiyataṉ kōṭu eṉa aṉkoṭuttum muṭikka.	(12)
akkuc cāriyi peṟum kuṟṟukara īṟu
418.	oṟṟunili tiriyātu akkoṭu varūum
	akkiḷi moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṟṟu nili tiriyātu akkoṭum varūum - oṟṟu muṉṉiṉṟa nilitiriyātu akkuccāriyiyōṭum piṟa cāriyiyoṭum varum, akkiḷi moḻi uḷa eṉa moḻipa - akkiḷiyāṉa coṟkaḷ uḷa eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar e-ṟu.
itaṟku ummiyi akkoṭum eṉa muṉṉar māṟuka.
e-ṭu:	kuṉṟakkūki, maṉṟappeṇṇi eṉavarum. ummiyāṟ koṅkattuḻavu, vaṅkattuvāṇikam eṉa attum peṟṟaṉa.
‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟu nilitiriyā atikārattukkaṇ varuñ cāriyikku iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. ‘akkiḷi’ yeṉṟār, iraṇṭu cāriyi toṭarntu muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉṟaṟku. pārppaṉakkuḻavi, cēri, tōṭṭam, piḷḷi eṉa aṉṉum akkum vantaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku uṭimi virikka. pārppiṉuṭkuḻavi eṉṟumām. pārppāṉākiyakuḻavi eṉṟāl īṇṭu muṭiyāteṉṟu uṇarka. pārppaṉa makaṉ, pārppaṉa vaṉiti eṉpaṉavum pārppāṉ cāti uṇarttiṉa.	(13)
kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇup peyar aṉcāriyi peṟumāṟu
419.	eṇṇuppeyark kiḷavi urupiyal niliyum.
itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyaroṭu poruṭpeyar muṭikkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇup peyark kiḷavi - eṇṇuppeyarākiya coṟkaḷ poruṭpeyaroṭu puṇarumiṭattu, urupiyal niliyum - urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aṉpeṟṟup puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉṟaṉkāyam, iraṇṭaṉkāyam, cukku, tōri payaṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉṟaṉāṟkoṇṭa kāyameṉa viriyum.
varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇuṅ koḷka. oṉṟaṉñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉa varum. mēlic cūttirattu ‘nili’ (eḻut. 418) eṉṟataṉāṉ, iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(14)
‘vaṇṭu’, ‘peṇṭu’ ivaṟṟiṉ cāriyippēṟu
420.	vaṇṭum peṇṭum iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
itu meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟa muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vaṇṭum peṇṭum iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - vaṇṭeṉṉuñ collum peṇṭeṉṉuñ collum iṉcāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vaṇṭiṉkāl peṇṭiṉkāl eṉa varum. itaṟku muṟkūṟiya nili (418) eṉṟa ilēciṉāl valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(15)
‘peṇṭu’ aṉṉum peṟutal
421.	peṇṭeṉ kiḷavik(ku) aṉṉum variyār.
itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) peṇṭu eṉ kiḷavikku aṉṉum variyār - peṇṭeṉ ṉuñ coṟku iṉṉēyaṉṟi aṉcāriyi varutaliyum nīkkār āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	peṇṭaṉki, cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.	(16)
‘yātu’, cuṭṭumutal āytattoṭark kuṟṟukara īṟukaḷiṉ muṭipu
422.	yāteṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
	āyta iṟutiyum urupiyal niliyum.
iḵtu īreḻuttorumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattuḷ oṉṟaṟkum, cuṭṭu mutalākiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum vēṟumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) yātu eṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum - yāteṉṉum īṟuñ cuṭṭeḻuttu mutalākiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum, urupiyal niliyum - urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyal piṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aṉ peṟṟuc cuṭṭu mutaliṟuti āytaṅ keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yātaṉkōṭu, ataṉkōṭu, itaṉkōṭu, utaṉkōṭu; cevi, tali, puṟam eṉa varum.
āytaṅkeṭāmuṉṉē aṉṉiṉ akarattik kuṟṟukarattiṉ mēl ēṟṟuka; āytaṅkeṭṭāl atu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ.	(17)
uyirvaruvaḻi, cuṭṭumutal āytattoṭark
kuṟṟukarattiṉ alvaḻimuṭipu
423.	muṉuyir varumiṭat tāytap puḷḷi
	maṉṉal vēṇṭum alvaḻi yāṉa.
itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutikku oruvaḻi alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉuyir varumiṭattu - cuṭṭu mutalākiya āytat toṭar moḻik kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṉmuṉṉē uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, āytap puḷḷi maṉṉal vēṇṭum - āytavoṟṟu muṉpōlak keṭātu nili peṟṟu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ, alvaḻiyāṉa - alvaḻikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉa niṟutti; aṭi āṭi ili īyam ural ūrti eḻu ēṇi aiyam oṭukkam ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.
‘muṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ivviti koḷka. aḵtaṭivu, aḵtoṭṭam eṉa oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, ēṉi ilakkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu aṟintu muṭikka.	(18)
ēṉikkaṇam varuvaḻi ataṉ muṭipu
424.	ēṉimuṉ variṉē tāṉili iṉṟē.
itu mēlavaṟṟiṟkup piṟakaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ēṉi muṉvariṉ - muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉar uyirkkaṇa mallaṉa varumāyiṉ, tāṉ niliyiṉṟu - avvāytaṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atukaṭitu itukaṭitu utukaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(19)
kuṟṟukara īṟukaḷ alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
425.	allatu kiḷappiṉ ellā moḻiyuñ
	colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟki yākum.
iḵtu āṟu īṟṟukkuṟṟukarattiṟkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉ - alvaḻiyic collumiṭattu, ellā moḻiyum - āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaramum, colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟki yākum - mēl āciriyaṉ kūṟiya kuṇattiyuṭiya iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nākukaṭitu, varakukaṭitu, teḷkukaṭitu, eḵkukaṭitu, kuraṅku kaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum. ēṉikkaṇattuk kaṇ ‘niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyal pākum’ (eḻut. 144) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
‘ellāmoḻiyum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, viṉiccollum viṉik kuṟippuc collum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kiṭantatu kutiri, karitu kutiri eṉa varum.
‘colliya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtoki vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟumikku valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭitalum, iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟu mikku muṭitalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	karaṭṭukkāṉam, kuruṭṭukkōḻi, tiruṭṭuppuliyaṉ, kaḷiṟṟuppaṉṟi, veḷiṟṟuppaṉi, eyiṟṟuppal eṉavum; vaṟaṭṭāṭu, kuruṭṭerutu eṉavum varum.
‘paṇpiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟākātu aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ōr yāṭṭi yāṉi, aiyāṭṭi yerutu eṉavum; aṟṟik kūttar, iṟṟikkūttar eṉavum; maṉṟittūti maṉṟip pāṉi, paṇṭic cāṉṟār eṉavum varum.	(20)
avaṟṟuḷ vaṉtoṭar īṟu vali mikutal
426.	valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu av vāṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
(i-ḷ.) valloṟṟut toṭar moḻi valleḻuttu mikum - valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟu valleḻuttu varuvaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kokkukkaṭitu, pākkukkaṭitu, paṭṭukkaṭitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.	(21)
valleḻuttu mikappeṟum meṉtoṭark kuṟṟukara moḻikaḷ
427.	cuṭṭuccuṉi nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
	yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
	āyiyal tiriyā valleḻut tiyaṟki.
iḵtu av vāṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉkaṇ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭap poruḷ uṇara niṉṟa iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭuc ciṉi nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - cuṭṭākiya ciṉiyeḻuttu nīṇṭa meṉṟoṭark kuṟṟukara īṟum, yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - yāveṉṉum viṉā mutalākiya meṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, valleḻuttiyaṟki ā iyal tiriyā - valleḻuttup peṟṟu muṭiyun taṉmiyākiya avviyalpiṉ tiriyātu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, īṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, ūṅkukkoṇ ṭāṉ, yāṅkukkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyi ṉāṉ eṉa varum.
‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉiyeccam iyalpāyum, vaṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉiyeccam mikkum muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	iruntukoṇṭāṉ, āṇṭuceṉṟāṉ, tantutīrntāṉ, vantupōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; cettukkiṭantāṉ, ceṟṟuc ceytāṉ, uyttukkoṇṭāṉ, naṭṭuppōṉāṉ eṉavum varum.	(22)
iyalpumuṭipu peṟum meṉtoṭar īṟu
428.	yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpu mākum.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, valleḻuttup peṟutalē aṉṟi iyalpā meṉṟaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpumākum  - avaṟṟuḷ yāveṉṉum viṉāviyuṭiya col muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi iyalpāyum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṅkukoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
iḵtu eppaṭiyeṉṉum viṉāpporuḷi uṇarttiṟṟu. ummiyāṉ mikkumuṭitalē valiyuṭittu. ēkāram pirinili.	(23)
melloṟṟu valiyāk kuṟṟukara īṟṟu moḻikaḷ
429.	annāṉ moḻiyun tannili tiriyā.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻic ceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) annāṉmoḻiyum - cuṭṭumutaṉ mūṉṟum yāmutaṉ moḻiyu mākiya annāṉku moḻiyum, tam nili tiriyā - tam melloṟṟāya taṉmi tirintu valloṟṟākātu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muṟkāṭṭiyavē.
‘tannili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, melloṟṟut tiriyātu valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa piṟavuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu: aṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, iṅkukkoṇṭāṉ, uṅkukkoṇ ṭāṉ, eṅkukkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyi ṉāṉ eṉa varum.
iṉi muṉṉar yāmoḻi eṉṉātu viṉā eṉṟataṉāṉ, ēḻāvataṉ kālap poruṭṭākiya piṟavum iyalpāy muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	muntu koṇṭāṉ, paṇṭu koṇṭāṉ, iṉṟu koṇ ṭāṉ, aṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, eṉṟu koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(24)
uṇṭu eṉum viṉikkuṟippu muṭiyumāṟu
430.	uṇṭeṉ kiḷavi uṇmi ceppiṉ
	munti iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum
	mēṉili yoṟṟē ḷakāra mātalum
	āmuṟi yiraṇṭum urimiyu muṭittē
	valleḻuttu varūuṅ kāli yāṉa.
itu meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuḷ viṉikkuṟippāy niṉṟatoru coṟpaṇpi uṇarttuṅkāl vēṟumuṭipu peṟutal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uṇṭeṉ kiḷavi uṇmi ceppiṉ - uṇṭeṉṉuñ col viṉikkuṟippi yuṇarttātu oru poruḷ tōṉṟuṅkāl tōṉṟi atu keṭuntuṇiyum uṇṭāy niṟkiṉṟa taṉmiyākiya paṇpi uṇartti niṟkumāyiṉ, munti iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum - muṉṉar niṉṟa kuṟṟukaram tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum, mēṉili oṟṟē ḷakāra mātalum - ataṟku mēl niṉṟa ṇakāra oṟṟu ḷakāra oṟṟātalumākiya, ā muṟi iraṇṭum urimiyum uṭittu-ammuṟi yiṉiyuṭiya iraṇṭu niliyum urittu; aḵtu urittaṉṟi muṉṉar niṉṟa niliyilē kēṭun tiripum iṉṟi niṟṟalum uṭittu, valleḻuttu varūuṅ kāliyāṉa - valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻiyāy varuṅkālattu e-ṟu.
valleḻuttu atikārattāṉ vārā niṟpa ‘valleḻuttu varūuṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, avvirumuṭipum uḷavām paṇpi yuṇarttum pakaram varumoḻikkaṇṇē variṉ eṉpatūum, ēṉik kacatakkaḷilum iyalpukaṇattilum uṇṭeṉa niṉṟu viṉikkuṟippāyuñ ciṟupāṉmi paṇpāyum niṟkumeṉpatūuṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	uḷporuḷ, uṇṭuporuḷ eṉa varum. itu poruḷic cuṭṭātu uṇmit taṉmip paṇpi īṇṭu uṇarttiṟṟu. iṉi, uṇṭukāṇam, uṇṭucākkāṭu, uṇṭu tāmari, uṇṭuñāṉam; nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭi, āṭi eṉa varuvaṉavellāṅ kēṭun tiripumiṉṟi viṉikkuṟippāyuñ ciṟupāṉmi paṇpāyum niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu viṉiyiyaluḷ viṉikkuṟippu ōtumvaḻi uṇarka.
uḷporuḷeṉpatu paṇputtoki muṭipaṉṟōveṉiṉ, aḵtu ōci oṟṟumipaṭac collum vaḻiyatu pōlum. iḵtu ōci iṭiyaṟavupaṭac collum vaḻiyateṉka.	(25)
iruperuntici alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu
431.	irutici puṇariṉ ēyiṭi varumē.
itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟut ticippeyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iru tici puṇariṉ - iraṇṭu perunticiyun tammiṟ puṇariṉ, ē iṭi varum - ēyeṉṉuñ cāriyi iṭi niṉṟu puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	teṟkēvaṭakku, vaṭakkēteṟku ivi ummit toki.	(26)
perunticikaḷoṭu kōṇattici puṇartal
432.	tiripuvēṟu kiḷappiṉ oṟṟum ukaramuṅ
	keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar
	oṟṟumey tirintu ṉakāra mākum
	teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅ kāli yāṉa.
itu perunticikaḷoṭu kōṇatticikaḷip puṇarttaliṉ, eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) tiripu vēṟu kiḷappiṉ - apperun ticikaḷoṭu kōṇat ticikaḷi vēṟākap puṇarkku miṭattu, oṟṟum ukaramuṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar - avvukaram ēṟi niṉṟa oṟṟum avvīṟṟu ukaramuṅ keṭṭumuṭital vēṇṭumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar, teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅkāli - avi teṟkeṉṉun ticiyoṭu puṇaruṅkālattu, āṉa oṟṟu mey tirintu ṉakāramākum - atticikkup porunti niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivutirintu ṉakara oṟṟāy niṟkum e-ṟu.
‘tirintu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, vaṭakku eṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa kakara oṟṟuk keṭuttu muṭittuk koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vaṭakiḻakku, vaṭamēṟku, teṉkiḻakku, teṉmēṟku eṉa varum.
‘vēṟu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ticippeyaroṭu poruṭpeyar variṉum immuṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	vaṭakaṭal, vaṭacuram, ‘vaṭavēṅkaṭam, teṉkumari’ (pāyiram), teṉcuram, teṉṉilaṅki eṉa varum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyir keṭṭun tirintum mey keṭṭum muṭivaṉavum uḷa, ticippeyar muṉṉarp poruṭpeyar vantuḻi yeṉṟu uṇarka. kiḻakku eṉpatu, kari kūri eṉpavaṟṟoṭu puṇarumiṭattuk kīḻkari kīḻkūri eṉa nilimoḻiyiṟuti ukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu ataṉmēl niṉṟa kakara oṟṟum ḻakarattiṉ akaramuṅkeṭṭu mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭintaṉa. mēṟkukari, kūri, mīkari mīkūri eṉa nilimoḻi īṟṟu ukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu ataṉmēl niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟuṅ keṭṭu ēkāram īkāramāki muṭintaṉa. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē. mēṉmāṭu mēlpāl mēliccēri eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavuñ ceykiyaṟintu muṭikka.	(27)
pattu, mutal eṭṭu eṇkaḷoṭu puṇarumāṟu
433.	oṉṟumuta lāka eṭṭa ṉiṟuti
	ellā veṇṇum pattaṉ muṉvariṟ
	kuṟṟiya lukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	muṟṟiṉ varūum iraṇṭalaṅ kaṭiyē.
niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaramum poruṭ peyarkkaṇ puṇarumāṟu uṇartti, iṉi avvīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar muṭikkiṉṟār. iḵtu avaṟṟuṭ patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyarōṭu eṇṇuppeyar vantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
