(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭaṉ iṟuti ellā eṇṇum - oṉṟeṉṉum eṇ mutalāka eṭṭeṉṉum eṇṇīṟāyuḷḷa ellā eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, pattaṉmuṉ variṉ - patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyariṉ muṉvariṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭum - appat teṉṉuñ colliṟ kuṟṟiyalukaran tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyoṭuṅ keṭum, iraṇṭalaṅkaṭi muṟṟa iṉvarūum - āṇṭu iraṇṭallāta eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṭattu muṭiya iṉcāriyi iṭivantu puṇarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṉoṉṟu, patiṉmūṉṟu, patiṉāṉku, patiṉintu, patiṉāṟu, patiṉēḻu, patiṉeṭṭu eṉa varum.
nilimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ, piṟaveṇṇiṉ muṉṉarp piṟa peyar vantuḻiyum iṉpeṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṉkūṟu, oṉpatiṉpāl eṉa varum.
‘muṟṟa’ eṉpataṉāṟ, patiṉāṉkeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram varumoḻikkaṭ karuviceytu keṭuttu muṭikka.	(28)
avveṇṇuppeyar ‘iraṇṭu’ varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
434.	pattaṉoṟ ṟukkeṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal
	otta teṉpa iraṇṭuvaru kāli.
itu mēl iṉ peṟāteṉṟu vilakkiya iraṇṭaṟkup piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) pattaṉ oṟṟuk keṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal - patteṉṉuñ collil niṉṟa takara oṟṟukkeṭa ṉakara oṟṟu iraṭṭittu varutal, iraṇṭu varukāli ottateṉpa - iraṇṭeṉṉum eṇ varuṅkālattiṟ poruntiṟ ṟeṉpar āciriyar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉa varum.
‘kuṟṟiya lukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭum’ (eḻut. 433) eṉṟa viti itaṟkum mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟkuṅ koḷka.	(29)
‘āyiram’ varuvaḻi, atu muṭiyumāṟu
435.	āyiram variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu.
iḵtu āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar variṉ varum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu - muṟkūṟiya pattaṉ muṉṉar oṉṟu mutaliyaṉavēyaṉṟi āyirameṉṉu meṇ vantālum īṟukeṭṭu iṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum iyalpiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṉāyiram eṉa varum.
ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu.	(30)
niṟiyum aḷavum varuvaḻi, atu muṭiyumāṟu
436.	niṟiyu maḷavum varūuṅ kāliyum
	kuṟiyā tākum iṉṉeṉ cāriyi.
iḵtu eṇṇuppeyaroṭu niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum puṇarkiṉṟa puṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) niṟiyum aḷavum varūuṅ kāliyum - muṟkūṟiya patteṉ pataṉ muṉṉar niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varuṅkālattum, iṉṉeṉ cāriyi kuṟiyātākum - avviṉṉeṉṉuñ cāriyi kuṟiyātu vantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṉkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum; patiṉkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi eṉavum; patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku eṉavum varum.
‘kuṟiyātākum’ eṉpataṉāṟ, poruṭpeyarum eṇṇup peyarum niṟippeyarum vantuḻiyum iṉ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñ ceyki ceytu muṭikka.
e-ṭu:	patiṟṟu vēli, yāṇṭu, aṭukku, muḻam eṉavum, patiṉ tiṅkaḷ eṉavum, patiṟṟuttoṭi eṉavum varum. patiṟ ṟoṉṟu eṉpatupōla iraṇṭumutaṟ pattaḷavum oṭṭuka.
ivvīṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal ‘aḷavākumoḻimutal’ (eḻut. 121) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘niliiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭikka, ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutalum ukaram varutalum valleḻuttup peṟutalum ‘oṉṟu mutalāka’ (eḻut. 433) eṉpataṉuḷ ‘muṟṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.	(31)
oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ eṇṇup peyar,
‘pattu’ varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
437.	oṉṟumuta loṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar
	niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa āytam
	vantiṭi niliyum iyaṟkit teṉpa
	kūṟiya iyaṟki kuṟṟiya lukaram
	āṟa ṉiṟuti alvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇṇuppeyaroṭu patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ iṟutimuṉṉar - oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu īṟākak kūṟukiṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, niṉṟa pattaṉ oṟṟukkeṭa - varumoḻiyāka vantuniṉṟa patteṉṉuñ colliṉatu takara oṟṟukkeṭa, āytam vantu iṭi niliyum iyaṟkit teṉpa - āyta māṉatu vantu iṭiyē nilipeṟum iyalpi yuṭitteṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar, āṟaṉ iṟuti alvaḻiyāṉa - avaṟṟuḷ āṟeṉṉum īṟallāta viṭattu, kuṟṟiyalukaram kūṟiya iyaṟki - kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟkūṟiya iyaṟkiyāy meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
iṅṅaṉam varumāṟu mēṟcūttiraṅkaḷuṭ kāṭṭutum.
‘vantu’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, āytamākat tiriyātu takara oṟṟuk keṭṭu orupatu eṉṟu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.	(32)
oṉṟu iraṇṭu, oru iru - eṉat tirital
438.	mutalī reṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakara mākum
	ukaram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa.
itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiṉ cilavaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻicceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalī reṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakaram ākum - avaṟṟiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa iraṇṭeṇṇiṉuṭiya ṉakara oṟṟum ṇakara oṟṟum rakara oṟṟākat tirintu niṟkum, āvayiṉāṉa ukaram varutal - avviṭattu ukaram varuka e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupaḵtu irupaḵtu eṉa varum.
oṉṟeṉpataṉ īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, ṉakara oṟṟiṉi rakara oṟṟākki, ukaramēṟṟi, oruveṉa niṟutti, niṉṟa patteṉpataṉ takara oṟṟuk keṭuttu, āytamākkip, paḵteṉa varuvittu, orupaḵtu eṉa muṭikka. irupaḵtiyum ivvāṟē muṭikka. mēl varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuñ cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kūṟuñ ciṟappuviti oḻintavaṟṟiṟku ituvē muṭipākak koḷka.	(33)
439.	iṭinili rakaram iraṇṭeṉ eṇṇiṟku
	naṭimaruṅ kiṉṟē poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) iraṇṭeṉ eṇṇiṟku iṭinili rakaram - iraṇ ṭeṉṉu meṇṇiṟku iṭiniṉṟa rakaram, poruḷvayiṉāṉa - ammoḻi poruḷpeṟum iṭattu, naṭimaruṅkiṉṟu - naṭakkum iṭamiṉṟik keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	irupaḵtu eṉa varum.
itaṟku rakara uyirmey itaṉāṟkeṭuttu ēṉiya kūṟiyavāṟē kūṭṭi muṭikka. ‘poruḷ’ eṉavē, eṇṇallāp peyaruṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	irukaṭal, iruviṉi, irupiṟappu eṉa varum.	(34)
mūṉṟum āṟum, muṉ aṟu - eṉat tirital
440.	mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇum āṟeṉṉum eṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aṟu eṉak kuṟukkip paḵtu eṉa varuvittu, aṟupaḵtu eṉa muṭikka.	(35)
mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram pakāram ātal
441.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē pakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟē pakāramākum - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakāra oṟṟup pakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muppaḵtu eṉa varum.	(36)
nāṉkaṉ ṉakāram ṟakāram ātal
442.	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē ṟakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ oṟṟē ṟakāramākum - nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ṟakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāṟpaḵtu eṉa varum.	(37)
aintaṉ nakāram makāram ātal
443.	ainta ṉoṟṟē makāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉoṟṟē makāramākum - ainteṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu makara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aimpaḵtu eṉa varum.
ēḻu kuṟṟiyalukara īṟaṉmi urupiyaluṭ kāṇka.	(38)
eṭṭaṉ ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal
444.	eṭṭa ṉoṟṟē ṇakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) eṭṭa ṉoṟṟē ṇakāramākum - eṭṭeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṭakara oṟṟu ṇakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum.	(39)
oṉpaḵtu ‘pattu’ varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
445.	oṉpāṉ okaramicit takaram oṟṟum
	munti yoṟṟē ṇakāram iraṭṭum
	paḵteṉ kiḷavi āytapaka raṅkeṭa
	niṟṟal vēṇṭum ūkārak kiḷavi
	oṟṟiya takaram ṟakara mākum.
iḵtu eytātatu eytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ okaramicit takaram oṟṟum - oṉpatu eṉa niṟuttip paḵtu eṉa varuvittu muṭikkuṅkāl, nilimoḻiyākiya oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉatu okarattiṟku muṉṉāka oru takara oṟṟut tōṉṟi niṟkum, munti oṟṟē ṇakāram iraṭṭum - muṉ coṉṉa okarattiṟku muṉṉar niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ṇakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, paḵteṉ kiḷavi āyta pakaraṅkeṭa - varumoḻiyākiya paḵteṉṉuñ col taṉkaṇ āytamum pakaramuṅ keṭṭuppōka, ūkārak kiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - nili moḻiyiṉ iraṭṭiya ṇakarattiṉ piṉṉar ūkāramākiya eḻuttu vantu niṟṟali āciriyaṉ virumpum, oṟṟiya takaram ṟakara mākum - varumoḻiyākiya patteṉpataṉ īṟṟiṉmēlēṟiya ukaraṅ keṭātu pirintuniṟpa oṟṟāy niṉṟa takaram ṟakara oṟṟāy niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	toṇṇūṟu eṉa varum.
itaṉi oṟṟāy vantu niṉṟa takara oṟṟiṉmēl nilimoḻi okaratti ēṟṟit tovvākki, ṇakara oṟṟu iraṭṭi ataṉmēl varumoḻikkaṭ pakaramum āytamuṅkeṭa vanta ūkāramēṟṟit toṇṇū ṟākkip, ‘pakaravāytam’ eṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, nilimoḻik kaṭ pakaramum āytamuṅ keṭuttuk, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, varumoḻi iṟutit takara oṟṟut tirintu niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa ukaramēṟṟit toṇṇūṟeṉa muṭikka.	(40)
oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ eṇkaḷōṭu aḷavuniṟippeyarkaḷ muṭiyumāṟu
446.	aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum niṟiyiṉ kiḷaviyum
	kiḷanta iyala tōṉṟuṅ kāli.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉeṇkaḷōṭu aḷavup peyarum niṟippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum niṟiyiṉ kiḷaviyun tōṉṟuṅ kāli - muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷantaṟiyappaṭum aḷavup peyarum niṟippeyarum vantu tōṉṟuṅ kālattu, kiḷanta iyala-āṟaṉ īṟu alvaḻik kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu, mutalīreṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakāramāy ukaram vantu, iṭinili rakara miraṇṭiṟkuk keṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki, nāṉkaṉoṟṟu vaṉkaṇattu ṟakaramāy, eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakaramāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orukalam, irukalam; cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi eṉavum; orukaḻañcu, irukaḻañcu, kaḵcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa muṉṉar muṭittum. ivi mutalīreṇṇiṉ ceyki.
‘tōṉṟuṅ kāli’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ivveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarka ḷellām ivvitiyum piṟavitiyum eytuvittu muṭittuk koḷka. oru mūṉṟu orunāṉku, irumūṉṟu irunāṉku, orukāl irukāl, oru muntiriki irumuntiriki, orumukkāl iru mukkāl eṉpaṉa piṟavuṅ koṇarntu oṭṭuka.
iṉip piṟaviti eytuvaṉa: ōroṉṟu, ōriraṇṭu, ōrintu, ōrāṟu, ōrēḻu, ōreṭṭu, ōroṉpatu eṉavum; īroṉṟu, īriraṇṭu, īrintu, īrāṟu, īrēḻu, īreṭṭu, īroṉpatu eṉavum; mūvoṉṟu, mūviraṇṭu, mūvintu, mūvāṟu, mūvēḻu, mūveṭṭu, mūvoṉpatu eṉavum; ‘mutalī reṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir’ (eḻut. 455) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ uyirkku eytiya piṟavitiyum, ‘mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉili nīṭalu murittu’ (eḻut. 457) eṉṟa piṟavitiyum peṟṟup, piṟaceykikaḷum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
nāloṉṟu, nāliraṇṭu, nālintu, nālāṟu, nālēḻu, nāleṭṭu, nāloṉpatu eṉpaṉa ‘nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum’ (eḻut. 453) eṉṟa vitipeṟṟu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum ivvāṟēyaṉṟi aḷavum niṟiyumaṉṟi varuvaṉavellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka.	(41)
mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram vanta valloṟ ṟātal
447.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum.
itu mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vantatokkum - mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉka ṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu varumoḻiyāy vanta aḷavup peyar niṟippeyar kaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta valloṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāyt tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mukkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum; muk kaḻañcu, kaḵcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
‘nāṉka ṉoṟṟē ṟakāra mākum’ (eḻut. 442) eṉṟa muṉṉi māṭṭēṟu niṟṟaliṉ, nāṟkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum; nāṟ kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam, eṉavum varum.	(42)
aintaṉ nakāram, vanta valloṟṟiṟkēṟṟa meliyākat tirital
448.	ainta ṉoṟṟē melleḻut tākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉoṟṟē melleḻuttākum - aintāvataṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttukku ēṟṟa melleḻuttākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aiṅkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi eṉavum; aiṅkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. ēkāram īṟṟaci.	(43)
ittiripukaḷ vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻiyē eṉṟal
449.	kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūuṅ kāli.
itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṟkum aintaṟkum varumoḻi variyaṟuk kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūuṅ kāli - mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu vantatoppatūum aintaṉoṟṟu melleḻuttāvatūum avvaḷavup peyar niṟippeyar oṉpatiṉum vaṉkaṇamākiya kacatapakkaḷ mutaṉmoḻi yāy vanta iṭattu e-ṟu.
atu muṉṉark kāṭṭiṉām.
‘āṟu neṭumutal kuṟukum’ (eḻut. 440) eṉṟa māṭṭēṟṟāṉē āṟu neṭumutal kuṟuki niṉṟatu.
e-ṭu:	aṟukalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi eṉavum; aṟukaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa mēṟ kāṭṭutum.
ēḻ kuṟṟukara īṟaṉmiyiṉ māṭṭēṟu ēlātāyiṟṟu.	(44)
meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum varumvaḻiyum,
‘eṭṭu’ eṇ - eṉavē niṟṟal
450.	namava eṉṉum mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi
	akaram variṉum eṭṭaṉmu ṉiyalpē.
itu vēṇṭātu kūṟi vēṇṭiyatu muṭikkiṉṟatu, ‘ñanamayava’ (eḻut. 144) mutaliya cūttirattuṭ kūṟiyavaṟṟik kūṟutaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) eṭṭaṉmuṉ - eṭṭeṉpataṉ muṉṉar, namava eṉṉum mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi akaram variṉum - aḷavuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar meṉkaṇattu iraṇṭum iṭikkaṇattu oṉṟumākiya namava eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉoṭu porunti uyirkkaṇattu akaram variṉum, ummiyāṉ uyirkkaṇattu ukaram variṉum kūṟāta valleḻuttukkaḷ variṉum, iyalpu - muṟkūṟiyavāṟē ṭakāram ṇakāramāy vēṟoru vikāramiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
namavaveṉṉum mūṉṟum vantāṟpōla, akaram variṉum eṉpatu poruḷ.
e-ṭu:	eṇṇāḻi, maṇṭi vaṭṭi eṉavum; eṇṇakal, eṇ ṇuḻakku eṉavum, eṇkalam, cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavum varum.
‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ’ vaṉkaṇattu niṟippeyaruṅ koḷka. eṇkaḻañcu; toṭi, palam eṉa varum.
ivvēṇṭā kūṟalāṉ, eṇṇakal eṉak kuṟṟukara īṟāyk kēṭuntirivum peṟṟu uyirvarumoḻiyāṉa toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka.	(45)
nama - varuvaḻi, aintu mūṉṟu ivaṟṟiṉ oṟṟu, vanta oṟṟātal
451.	aintum mūṉṟum namavaru kāli
	vanta tokkum oṟṟiyal niliyē.
ituvum mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvā muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aintum mūṉṟum nama varukāli - ainteṉṉum eṇṇum mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇum nakara mutaṉmoḻiyum makara mutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, oṟṟiyal nili - nilimoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟu naṭakkum nilimi kūṟiṉ, vantatu okkum - mēṟkūṟiyavāṟē makaramum pakaramum ākātu varumoḻi mutal vanta oṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ainnāḻi, aimmaṇṭi, munnāḻi, mummaṇṭi eṉa varum.
‘mūṉṟum aintum’ eṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, nāṉāḻi nāṉmaṇṭi eṉpuḻi nilimoḻi ṉakaram ṟakaramākātu niṉṟavāṟē niṉṟu muṭitalum, varumoḻi mutaṉiṉṟa nakaram ṉakaramāyt tiriya nilimoḻi ṉakaraṅ keṭutaluṅ koḷka.	(46)
vakaram varuvaḻi, mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram vakāramātal
452.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakaram varuvaḻit
	tōṉṟiya vakarat turuvā kummē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu, vakaram varuvaḻi - vakaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, tōṉṟiya vakarattu uruvākum - avvarumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiya vakarattiṉ vaṭivāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muvvaṭṭi eṉa varum.
‘tōṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, mutal nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟiṉṟi mūvaṭṭi eṉṟumām.	(47)
nāṉkaṉ ṉakāram lakāramātal
453.	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉoṟṟu - nāṉkām eṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu, lakāramākum - vakara mutaṉmoḻi vantāl lakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nālvaṭṭi eṉa varum.	(48)
aintaṉ nakāram keṭutal
454.	ainta ṉoṟṟē muntiyatu keṭumē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aintaṉoṟṟu-aintām eṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu, muntiyatu keṭum-vakara mutaṉmoḻi vantāl muṉṉiṉṟa vaṭivu keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aivaṭṭi eṉa varum.
‘munti’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ, nakara oṟṟuk keṭātu vakara oṟṟākat tirintu aivvaṭṭiyeṉac ciṟupāṉmi varum.	(49)
uyir varuvaḻi, mutal īreṇṇum ōr, īr - ātal
455.	mutalī reṇṇiṉmuṉ uyirvaru kālit
	tavaleṉa moḻipa ukarak kiḷavi
	mutaṉili nīṭa lāvayi ṉāṉa.
itu māṭṭēṟṟāṉ eytiya ukarattiṟkuk kēṭu kūṟi mutal nīḷka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalīreṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir varukāli - oru iru eṉa muṭintu niṉṟa iraṇṭeṇṇiṉ muṉṉar uyir mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi yāy varuṅkālattu, ukarakkiḷavi tavaleṉa moḻipa - nilimoḻiyukara mākiya eḻuttuk keṭutalāmeṉṟu colluvar pulavar, āvayiṉāṉa mutaṉili nīṭal - avviraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ nīṇṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōrakal, īrakal, ōruḻakku, īruḻakku eṉa varum.	(50)
mūṉṟu nāṉku aintu ivaṟṟiṉ oṟṟu, vakaram varuvaḻik kūṟiya iyalpiṉa ātal
456.	mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyum
	tōṉṟiya vakarat tiyaṟki yākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyum - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇum, nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇum ainteṉṉum eṇṇum, tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟkiyākum - muṉṉart tōṉṟi niṉṟa vakaram varumoḻikkuk kūṟiya iyalpāka mūṉṟiṉkaṇ vakara oṟṟāyum, nāṉkiṉkaṇ lakara oṟṟāyum, aintiṉkaṇ oṟṟukkeṭṭum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muvvakal, muvvuḻakku eṉa varum. itaṟkut ‘tōṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. nālakal, nāluḻakku, aiyakal, aiyuḻakku eṉa varum.
‘tōṉṟiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, mēl mūṉṟeṉpatu mutal nīṇṭa iṭattu nilimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟuk keṭuttukkoḷka. ‘iyaṟki’ eṉṟata ṉāṉ, toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. ‘mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē’ (eḻut. 452) mutaliya mūṉṟu cūttiramum koṇarntu muṭikka.	(51)
‘uḻakku’ varuvaḻi, neṭumutal kuṟukiya ‘mūṉṟu’ nīṇṭu muṭital
457.	mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉili nīṭalu murittē
	uḻakkeṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkat tāṉa.
itu muṉṉark kuṟukumeṉṟataṉi nīṇṭu muṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉnili nīṭalum urittu - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa eḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu, aḵtiyāṇ ṭeṉiṉ, uḻakku eṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkattāṉa - uḻakkeṉṉuñ col muṭiyum vaḻakkiṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mūvuḻakku eṉa varum.
‘vaḻakkattāṉa’ eṉpataṉāṉ, akaleṉ kiḷavikku mutaṉili nīṭaluṅ koḷka. mūvakal eṉa varum. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, nilimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟuk keṭukka.
mūḻakku mūḻākku eṉṉum marūumuṭivu ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭi yāṉ muṭikka.	(52)
neṭumutal kuṟukiya ‘āṟu’ nīṇṭu muṭital
458.	āṟeṉ kiḷavi mutalnī ṭummē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟeṉ kiḷavi - āṟeṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa variṉ, mutal nīṭum - muṉṉark kuṟuki niṉṟa mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘aṟu’ eṉṉātu ‘āṟu’ eṉṟār, tirintataṉtiripatu eṉṉum nayattāl.
e-ṭu:	āṟakal, āṟuḻakku eṉa varum.	(53)
aḷavu niṟippeyar varuvaḻi, ‘oṉpatu’ ‘iṉ’ peṟutal
459.	oṉpā ṉiṟuti uruvunili tiriyā(tu)
	iṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyi moḻiyē.
itu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭātu niṉṟu iṉ peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ iṟuti uruvunili tiriyātu - aḷavum niṟiyum varuvaḻi oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟukaran taṉ vaṭivu nili tiriyātu niṉṟu, cāriyi moḻi iṉpeṟal vēṇṭum - cāriyic collākiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṉkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
‘cāriyi moḻi’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉṉōṭu ukaramum valleḻut tum koṭuttuc ceyki ceytu muṭikka. oṉpatiṟṟukkalam, cāṭi eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
‘uruvu’ eṉpataṉāṉ, oṉpatiṟṟeṉa oṟṟiraṭṭutal ellāvaṟ ṟiṟkum koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, oṉpatiṉāḻi yeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakarak kēṭuṅ koḷka. ‘aḷavāku moḻi mutal’ (eḻut. 121) eṉpataṉkaṇ, ‘niliiya’eṉṉum ilēcāṉ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka.	(54)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇkaḷoṭu ‘nūṟu’ puṇarumāṟu
460.	nūṟumuṉ variṉuṅ kūṟiya iyalpē.
iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu nūṟeṉpataṉip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉ - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, nūṟuvariṉum - nūṟeṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar vantālum, kūṟiya iyalpu - mēṟ patteṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumvaḻik kūṟiya iyalpu eyti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
atu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki, mutalīreṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaramāy ukaram peṟṟu, iṭinili rakaram iraṇṭaṉkaṭ keṭṭu muṭitalām.
e-ṭu:	orunūṟu, irunūṟu, aṟunūṟu, eṇṇūṟu eṉa varum.
ivi māṭṭēṟṟāṉ muṭintaṉa. māṭṭēṟu ovvātaṉa mēṟkūṟi muṭippa.	(55)
mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram nakāramātal
461.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāra mākum.
itu māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāramākum - mūṉṟām eṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakaravoṟṟu nakaravoṟṟākum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	munnūṟu eṉa varum.	(56)
nāṉku aintu - ivaṟṟiṉ oṟṟut tiriyāmi
462.	nāṉkum aintum oṟṟumey tiriyā.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu mey tiriyā-nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇum ainteṉṉum eṇṇum tammoṟṟukkaḷ nili tiriyātu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāṉūṟu, ainnūṟu eṉa varum.
‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nāṉūṟu eṉpuḻi varumoḻi nakarattuḷ ūkāram pirittu, ‘laṉa veṉa varūum’ (eḻut. 149) eṉpataṉāṉ ṉakara voṟṟākki, ūkāramēṟṟi, nilimoḻi ṉakaraṅkeṭuttuk koḷka.	(57)
oṉpatu ‘nūṟu’ varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
463.	oṉpāṉ mutaṉili muntukiḷan taṟṟē
	munti yoṟṟē ḷakāram iraṭṭum
	nūṟeṉ kiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa
	ūā vākum iyaṟkit teṉpa
	āyiṭi varutal ikāra rakāram
	īṟumey keṭuttu makara moṟṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ mutaṉili muntu kiḷantaṟṟu - oṉpateṉṉu meṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa okaram mēṟ patteṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumvaḻik kūṟiyavāṟu pōla oru takaram oṟṟi ataṉmēl ēṟimuṭiyum, munti oṟṟē ḷakāram iraṭṭum - avvokarattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ḷakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, nūṟeṉkiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa ū āvākum iyaṟkit teṉpa - varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉṉum eṇṇup peyar nakāramākiya meykeṭa ataṉmēl ēṟiya ūkāram ākāramām iyalpiyuṭitteṉpar pulavar, āyiṭi ikāra rakāram varutal - ammoḻiyiṭi ōr ikaramum rakaramum varuka, īṟu mey keṭuttu makaram oṟṟum - īṟākiya kuṟṟukarattiṉiyum aḵtu ēṟiniṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉiyum keṭuttu oru makara oṟṟu vantu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘mey’ eṉpataṉāṉ, nilimoḻikkaṭ pakaraṅ keṭukka.
e-ṭu:	toḷḷāyiram eṉa varum.
itaṉi oṉpateṉṉum okarattiṉ muṉṉar vanta takara oṟṟiṉ mēlē okarattiyēṟṟip, pakaraṅ keṭuttuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟiṉi iraṇṭu ḷakara oṟṟākki, nūṟeṉpataṉ nakaraṅkeṭuttu, ūkāram ākāramākki ḷakarattiṉ mēlēṟṟi, ikaramum rakaramum varuvittu, vikārappaṭṭa uyirākiya ākārattiṉmuṉ uṭampaṭu mey yakāram varuvittu, ṟakara ukaraṅ keṭuttu, makara oṟṟu varuvittu muṭikka.	(58)
oṉṟu, iraṇṭu - eṇkaḷoṭu ‘āyiram’ muṭiyumāṟu
464.	āyirak kiḷavi varūuṅ kāli
	mutalī reṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭumē.
iḵtu avvoṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyirakkiḷavi varūuṅ kāli - āyirameṉṉuñ col oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ varuṅkālattu, mutal īreṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭum - oru iru eṉṉum iraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṭ peṟṟu niṉṟa ukaraṅ keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
‘ukaraṅ keṭum’ eṉavē, ēṉiyaṉa muṉṉark kūṟiyavāṟē niṟṟal peṟṟām.
e-ṭu:	orāyiram, irāyiram eṉa varum.	(59)
avi or, īr - eṉa uyir nīṭalum ātal
465.	mutaṉili nīṭiṉum māṉa milli.
iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutaṉili nīṭiṉum māṉamilli - ammutalīreṇṇiṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa okāra ikāraṅkaḷ nīṇṭumuṭiyiṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ōrāyiram, īrāyiram eṉa varum.	(60)
mūṉṟaṉ ṉakāram vakāramātal
466.	mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakāra mākum.
itu mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇ āyirattoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vakāramākum - mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu vakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	muvvāyiram eṉa varum.
muṉṉarc cūttirattu ‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, itaṉi mutaṉili nīṭṭi vakara oṟṟuk keṭuttu mūvāyiram eṉavum muṭikka.	(61)
nāṉkaṉ ṉakāram lakāramātal
467.	nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
itu nāṉkeṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉoṟṟē lakāramākum - nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nālāyiram eṉa varum.	(62)
aintaṉ nakāram yakāramātal
468.	ainta ṉoṟṟē yakāra mākum.
iḵtu ainteṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
i-ḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē yakāramākum - ainteṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu yakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aiyāyiram eṉa varum.	(63)
mutal kuṟuki niṉṟa ‘āṟu’ īṟṟu muṟṟukaram keṭṭu muṭital
469.	āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaram
	īṟumey oḻiyak keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu āṟeṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram - āṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram neṭumutal kuṟuki aṟuveṉa muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ, mey oḻiya īṟukeṭutal vēṇṭum - atu tāṉēṟiya meyyākiya ṟakara oṟṟukkeṭātuniṟpa muṟṟukaramākiya īṟu tāṉ keṭṭup puṇartali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aṟāyiram eṉa varum.
muṉṉar ‘neṭumutal kuṟukum’ (eḻut. 440) eṉṟavaḻi aṟuveṉa niṉṟa muṟṟukarattiṟkē īṇṭuk kēṭu kūṟiṉāreṉpatu peṟṟām. eṉṉi? kuṟṟiyalukaramāyiṉ ēṟimuṭitaliṉ. itu kuṟṟukaran tirintu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu muṭipu kūṟiṉār.
muṟṟiyalukaram ‘īṟumeyyoḻiyak keṭum’ eṉavē, kuṟṟukaraṅ keṭātu ēṟimuṭiyu meṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṟ peṟutum.
e-ṭu:	āṟāyiram eṉa varum.
‘maruṅku’ eṉṟataṉāṟ piṟa poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟukātu niṉṟu muṭital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	‘āṟākuvatē’ (col. 80) eṉa varum.	(64)
‘oṉpatu’ iṉcāriyi peṟutal
470.	oṉpā ṉiṟuti uruvunili tiriyā(tu)
	iṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyi marapē.
iḵtu oṉpateṉṉum eṇ ataṉoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉpāṉ iṟuti - oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟukaram, uruvunili tiriyātu - taṉ vaṭivunili tirintu keṭātē, cāriyi marapu iṉ peṟal vēṇṭum - cāriyiyākiya marapiṉiyuṭiya iṉpeṟṟu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉa varum.
‘uruvu’ eṉṟum ‘nili’ eṉṟum ‘cāriyimarapu’ eṉṟum kūṟiya mikiyāṉ, āyiramallāta piṟaveṇṇiṉkaṇṇum poruṭ peyariṭat tum iṉṉum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum muṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	oṉpatiṟṟuk kōṭi, oṉpatiṟṟoṉpatu, oṉpatiṟṟut taṭakki (pari. 3 : 39), oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu eṉa varum.
iṉṉum ivvilēcāṉē, vēṟoru muṭipiṉmiyiṟ kūṟā toḻinta eṇṇāyiram eṉṟavaḻi oṟṟiraṭṭutalum, īṇṭuk kūṟiya vaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutaluṅ koḷka. ‘aḷavāku moḻi mutal’ (eḻut. 121) eṉpataṉuḷ, ‘niliiya’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka.	(65)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇkaḷoṭu ‘nūṟāyiram’ muṭiyumāṟu
471.	nūṟā yiramuṉ varūuṅ kāli
	nūṟa ṉiyaṟki mutaṉilik kiḷavi.
iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu nūṟeṉṉum eṇ aṭiyaṭutta āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟāyiram muṉ varūuṅ kāli - nūṟāyiram eṉṉum aṭiyaṭuttamoḻi oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ varumoḻiyāy varuṅ kālattu, mutaṉilik kiḷavi nūṟaṉ iyaṟki - oṉṟeṉṉum mutaṉilikkiḷavi oṉṟumuṉ nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇoṭu muṭintāṟ pōla vikārameyti muṭiyum e-ṟu.
eṉavē vaḻinilik kiḷaviyākiya iraṇṭu mutaliya eṇkaḷ vikārameytiyum, eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	oru nūṟāyiram eṉa varum. ēṉiyaṉa irunūṟāyiram iraṇṭunūṟāyiram, munnūṟāyiram mūṉṟunūṟāyiram, nāṉūṟāyiram nāṉkunūṟāyiram, ainnūṟāyiram aintu nūṟāyiram aṟunūṟāyiram āṟunūṟāyiram, eṇṇūṟā yiram eṭṭunūṟāyiram, oṉpatu nūṟāyiram eṉa varum.
ivvikārappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, mutalīreṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaramākki ukaram varuvittu, mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukki, mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu nakāramākki, nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu mey tiriyā vākki, eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakāramākki, ilēcukaḷāṟ koṇṭa ceykikaḷil vēṇṭuvaṉavuṅ koṇarntu muṭikka.
‘ēṟpuḻikkōṭal’ eṉpataṉāṉ, toḷḷāyirameṉṟa muṭipi ṉoṭu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟatēṉum avvāṟu muṭiyāteṉṟu koḷka. ‘muṉ’ eṉpataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyi peṟṟu oṉpatiṉūṟāyiram eṉṟumām. ‘nili’ eṉpataṉāṉ, mūṉṟum āṟum iyalpāka muṭivuḻi neṭumutal kuṟukāmi koḷka.	(66)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇṇuppeyar varuvaḻi, ‘nūṟu’ muṭiyumāṟu
472.	nūṟeṉ kiḷavi oṉṟumutal oṉpāṟku
	īṟuciṉi yoḻiya iṉavoṟṟu mikumē.
itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi - nūṟeṉṉum eṇṇup peyar, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, īṟu ciṉiyoḻiya - īṟākiya kuṟṟukaran taṉṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyoṭuṅ keṭātuniṟpa, iṉa oṟṟu mikum - acciṉikku iṉamākiya ṟakara oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa varum. iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu aḷavuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
‘īṟuciṉi’ eṉṟu ōtiya mikiyāṉ, nūṟeṉpataṉoṭu piṟa eṇṇum, piṟaporuṭpeyarum, ivvitiyum, piṟavitiyum eyti muṭital koḷka. nūṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟukkōṭi, nūṟṟut toṇṇūṟu eṉavum; nūṟṟukkuṟi, ‘nūṟṟitaḻttāmari’ (aiṅkuṟu. 20), nūṟṟukkāṇam, nūṟṟukkāṉmaṇṭapam eṉavum iṉa oṟṟu mikkaṉa koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, irunūṟṟoṉṟu iraṇṭunūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa nūṟu aṭi yaṭutta vaḻiyuṅkoḷka.	(67)
orupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa varuvaḻiyum, ‘nūṟu’ aṟṟātal
473.	aviyūr pattiṉum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu annūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟumutal eṭṭu eṇkaḷ aṭiyaṭuttavaḻip puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) avi ūrpattiṉum - annūṟu eṉpatu niṉṟu muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal eṭṭu eṇkaḷi ūrntu vanta patteṉpataṉoṭu puṇaru miṭattum, attoḻiṟṟākum - īṟu ciṉiyoḻiya iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟorupaḵtu; irupaḵtu, muppaḵtu, nāṟpaḵtu, aimpaḵtu, aṟupaḵtu, eḻupaḵtu, eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum.
maṟṟu, nūṟṟoṉpatu avi ūrappaṭṭu vantatu aṉmi uṇarka. ‘ākum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, orunūṟṟorupaḵtu irunūṟṟorupaḵtu eṉa nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttu muṭiyum muṭipuṅ koḷka.	(68)
aḷavu niṟippeyar varuvaḻiyum aṟṟātal
474.	aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyā
	kuṟṟiya lukaramum valleḻut tiyaṟkiyum
	muṟkiḷan taṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu nūṟu eṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyātu - nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉ tiriyātu iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum, kuṟṟiyalukaramum valleḻut tiyaṟkiyum - avviṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaraṅ keṭāmiyum iṉa oṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭarmoḻiyāy niṟṟaliṉ varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum, muṟkiḷantaṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar - ‘valloṟṟut toṭar moḻi valleḻuttu mikumē’ (eḻut. 426) eṉa vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkuk kūṟiya taṉmiyavāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nūṟṟukkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
‘tiriyā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nūṟeṉpatu aṭiyaṭutta vaḻiyum ivviti koḷka. aḵtu orunūṟṟukkalam irunūṟṟukkalam eṉa varum.	(69)
orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷ,
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉoṭu puṇartal
475.	oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
	oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟku oṟṟiṭi mikumē
	niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi - oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākap patteṉṉum eṇ ēṟi orucollāki niṉṟa orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷ, oṉṟumutal oṉpāṟku - oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ varu moḻiyāy vantu puṇarum iṭattu, niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum-paḵteṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭṭu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ, oṟṟu iṭimikum - āṇṭu iṉa oṟṟākiya oru takara oṟṟu iṭimikku muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupattoṉṟu, irupattoṉṟu, orupattiraṇṭu, irupat tiraṇṭu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
ivaṟṟuḷ orupattoṉṟu orupattiraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇkaḷ atikārattāl niṉṟa nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aṭuttu varumeṉṟu uṇarka.	(70)
‘āyiram’ varuvaḻi, avveṇkaḷ ‘iṉ’ peṟutal
476.	āyiram variṉē iṉṉeṉ cāriyi
	āvayi ṉoṟṟiṭi mikuta lilli.
iḵtu orupaḵtu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu āyirattip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āyiram variṉ iṉṉeṉ cāriyi - avvoṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi āyirattoṭu puṇarum iṭattu iṉ cāriyi peṟum, āvayiṉ oṟṟu iṭi mikutal illi - avviṭattut takara oṟṟu iṭi vantu mikātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupatiṉāyiram irupatiṉāyiram eṉa eṇpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka. ivi nūṟṟorupatiṉāyiram eṉavum varum.
‘āvayiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, nūṟāyirattorupattīrāyiram eṉṟāṟ pōla attup peṟutalum piṟavuṅ koḷka.	(71)
aḷavum niṟiyum varuvaḻiyum ‘iṉ’ tōṉṟutal
477.	aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyā.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi muṉṉar aḷavup peyarum niṟippeyarum puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyā - orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum vantāl oṟṟu iṭimikātu iṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	orupatiṉkalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; orupatiṉkaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟiṟku nūṟu aṭiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka.
‘tiriyā’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, orupatiṟṟukkalam irupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu iraṭṭu talum, ukaramum valleḻuttup peṟutaluṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, orupatiṉāḻi eṉṟavaḻi varumoḻi nakaran tirintuḻi nilimoḻi ṉakarakkēṭuṅ koḷka.
‘aḷavum niṟiyum ataṉō raṉṉa’ eṉṟu pāṭam ōtuvār, muṉṉarc cūttirattu ‘āvayiṉ’ eṉṟataṉāṉum, ataṉ muṉṉarc cūttirattu ‘niṉṟa’ eṉṟataṉāṉum ivaṟṟi muṭippār.	(72)
vaṉkaṇam meṉkaṇam iṭikkaṇam varuvaḻi,
oṉṟu ‘oru’ ātal
478.	mutaṉili eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum
	ñanamat tōṉṟiṉum yavavan tiyiyiṉum
	mutaṉili yiyaṟki eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷoṭu poruṭ peyarip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutaṉili eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum - oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, ñanama tōṉṟiṉum - ñanamakkaḷākiya melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉum, yava vantu iyiyiṉum - yavakkaḷākiya iṭiyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, mutaṉili iyaṟki eṉmaṉār pulavar - avvoṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉeytiya muṭipu nilimi eyti muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
eṉavē, vaḻiniliyeṇṇākiya iraṇṭu mutalākiya eṇkaḷ ammūṉṟu kaṇamum mutaṉmoḻiyāyvariṉ, mutaṉili muṭipāki vikāram eytiyum, eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.
e-ṭu:	orukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum; irukal, iraṇṭukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum oṭṭuka.
ivveṇkaḷiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu, mutalīreṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakāramāka ukaram vantatu. irukal mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṭinili rakāraṅ keṭukka. mukkal mūṉṟukal; cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. itaṟku neṭu mutal kuṟukki ‘mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum’ (eḻut. 447) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka.
muṉṉar, eṇṇuppeyarum aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum varuvaḻikkūṟiya vikāraṅkaḷiṟ, poruṭpeyarkkum ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu muṭittu, ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ‘nili’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟuttirittu muṭikka. avi, mūṉṟaṟkum aintaṟkum ñakaram varuvaḻi ñakara oṟṟātalum, mūṉṟaṟku yakaram varuvaḻi vakara oṟṟātalumām.
e-ṭu:	nāṟkal nāṉkukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi; nāṉñāṇ nāṉkuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; aiṅkal aintukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi; aiññāṇ aintuñāṇ nūl maṇi; aiyāḻ aintuyāḻ aivaṭṭu aintuvaṭṭu; aṟukal āṟukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; eṇkal eṭṭukkal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi; eṇñāṇ eṭṭuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; oṉpatukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉpatiṉkal eṉac ceṉṟatēṉum vaḻakkiṉmiyiṉ oḻikka.
iṉṉum māṭṭēṟiṉṟi varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟ kellām muṭipu ‘nili’ yeṉṟataṉāṉ muṭikka.	(73)
uyirkkaṇamum yāvum varuvaḻi, aḵtu ‘ōr’ ātal
479.	ataṉili uyirkkum yāvaru kāli
	mutaṉili okaram ōvā kummē
	rakarat tukaran tuvarak keṭumē.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu, poruṭpeyaruḷ uyir mutalmoḻi muṭiyumāṟum, mēṟkūṟiya yakāram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutaṉilikku - oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ tiripākiya oru eṉṉum eṇṇiṟku, uyirum yāvum varukāli - uyir mutaṉmoḻiyum yāmutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ataṉ nili - ammutaṉiliyiṉ taṉmi ivvāṟām; okaram ōvākum - okaram ōkāramāy nīḷum; rakarattu ukaran tuvarak keṭum - rakarattu mēṉiṉṟa ukaram muṟṟak keṭṭu muṭiyum e-ṟu.
nāṉkāvataṉi mutaṉiliyōṭu kūṭṭi, ataṉkaṇ niṉṟa ummiyi uyiroṭum yāvoṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. eṉavē vaḻiniliyeṇkaḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu muṟkūṟiyavāṟē iruvāṟṟāṉum muṭiyum.
e-ṭu:	ōraṭi, ōrākam, ōrili, ōrīṭṭam, ōruli, ōrūcal, ōreḻu, ōrēṭu, ōriyam, ōroḻuṅku, ōrōli ōruviyam eṉa varum.
kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu, mutaleṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakaramākkuka. ōryāḻ, ōryāṉi eṉa varum.
‘tuvara’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, iraṇṭeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ikaratti nīṭṭi, rakarattuḷ ukarattik keṭuttu, īraci īryāṉi eṉavum, mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ṉakaravoṟṟuk keṭuttu, mūvaci mūyāṉi eṉavum muṭikka. ivi ceyyuṇ muṭipu. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iṅṅaṉam varuvaṉa piṟavum aṟintu muṭittuk koḷka.	(74)
‘mā’ varuvaḻi, iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ eṇkaḷ tiriyumāṟu
480.	iraṇṭumuta loṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar
	vaḻaṅkiyal māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
	makara aḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē.
iḵtu iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavu mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum uriya māveṉpatu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ  iṟuti muṉṉar - iraṇṭeṉṉu meṇ mutalāka oṉpateṉṉumeṇ īṟāka niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vaḻaṅku iyal mā eṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ - vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇē naṭanta aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uriya mā veṉṉuñ col varumoḻiyāy variṉ, makara aḷavoṭu nikaralum urittu - avveṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tantu puṇarkkappaṭum maṇṭiyeṉṉum aḷavup peyarōṭu ottu vikārappaṭṭu muṭitalum urittu; ummiyāṉ vikārappaṭātu iyalpāy muṭitalum urittu e-ṟu.
‘vaḻakkiyal’ vaḻaṅkiyaleṉa vikāram. ‘makaraaḷavu’ makara mutaṉ moḻiyākiya aḷavup peyareṉap paṇputtoki. aḵtu ‘aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum’ (eḻut. 446) eṉpataṉuḷ, oru maṇṭi eṉa muṭittatām.
e-ṭu:	irumā, mummā, nāṉmā, aimmā, aṟumā, eṇmā, oṉpatiṉmā eṉa muṉṉark kūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ vikārap paṭutti muṭikka.
iṉi, ummiyāṉ vikārappaṭuttātu iraṇṭumā mūṉṟumā nāṉkumā aintumā āṟumā eṭṭumā oṉpatumā eṉavum muṭikka.
puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ ‘aḷavu niṟiyum’ (eḻut. 389) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ, ēḻneṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram vantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟiṉār. ataṉāṉ, īṇṭu eḻumā eṉa muṭikka. ēḻmāveṉa muṭital vaḻakkiṉṟu.
‘iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉ’ eṉṟu eṭuttōtiṉamiyiṉ, oṉṟaṟku orumāveṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi, oṉṟumā veṉṉum muṭipu illi yāyiṟṟu.
‘vaḻaṅkiyaṉmā’ eṉṟār, vilaṅku māvi nīkkutaṟku.	(75)
puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ eñci niṉṟa ceyyuḷmuṭipu
481.	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
	ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟic
	ceyyuḷ toṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa niliyum
	vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.
itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ oḻintu niṉṟa ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) laṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉ - lakāra ṉakāra meṉṟu kūṟa varukiṉṟa puḷḷi īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭa - ummeṉṉuñ cāriyiyum keḻuveṉṉum cāriyiyum uṭpaṭa, piṟavum aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭit tōṉṟi - piṟacāriyiyum appeṟṟippaṭṭa marapiṉiyuṭiya moḻiyiṭattē tōṉṟi, ceyyuḷ toṭarvayiṉ mey peṟa niliyum - ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷit toṭarpu paṭuttik kūṟumiṭattup poruḷpaṭa niṟkum, vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷ vayiṉāṉa - vēṟṟumiyik kuṟitta poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum pōlum’. itaṟku ummeṉṉuñ cāriyiyiṉ makaratti ‘ammiṉiṟuti’ (eḻut. 129) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ ‘taṉmey eṉṟataṉāṟ piṟa cāriyiyun tiriyumeṉa nakara oṟṟākki nilimoḻi lakara oṟṟiṉ mēl uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. villun tiṅkaḷum pōlumeṉpataṟku, villiṭittiṅkaḷ pōlumeṉa ēḻaṉurupu virittup poruḷurikka. ‘kalkeḻu kāṉavar nalkuṟu makaḷē’ (kuṟun. 71); itaṟkuk kallik keḻīiyiṉa eṉa urikka. ‘mānitikkiḻavaṉum pōṉm’ (akam. 66); itaṟkuk kiḻavaṉip pōṉmeṉa urikka. ivvummi ciṟap paṉṟu.‘kāṉkeḻunāṭaṉ’ (akam. 98) itaṟkuk kāṉikkeḻīiya eṉa urikka.
iṉi ‘aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyiṭi’ eṉṟataṉāṟ, ‘keḻu’ eṉṟatu piṟa colliṭattē ‘paṇikeḻu peruntiṟaṟ palvēṉ maṉṉar’ (maturik. 234), ‘tuṟikeḻu mānti’ (naṟ. 35) eṉa iyalpāka varuvaṉavum; ‘vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar’ (akam. 17) ‘payaṅkeḻu māmaḻi’ (puṟam. 266) eṉa nilimoḻi yīṟṟeḻuttut tiriya varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, iccāriyiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekara nīṭciyuṅ koḷka. ‘pūkkēḻ toṭali nuṭaṅka veḻuntu’ (akam. 28) ‘tuṟi kēḻūraṉ koṭumi nāṇi’ (aiṅkuṟu. 11) ivaṟṟiṟku, iraṇṭāvatum ēṟpuḻi mūṉṟā vatum virikka. ‘ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi’ (akam. 80) eṉpuḻik keḻu veṉṉum uriccol ‘eḻuttup pirinticittal’ (col. 395) eṉpataṉāṉ nīṇṭateṉṟu uṇarka. ‘mey’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ‘pūkkēḻeṉ’ (naṟ. 10) puḻi valloṟṟu mikutal koḷka. iṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr cey yuṭkaṭ piṟacāriyi peṟṟu vikāraṅkaḷ eyti muṭivaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām iccūttiramē vitiyāka muṭittuk koḷka.	(76)
ivvatikārattup puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ ivi eṉal
482.	uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yākik
	kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum iciyiṉum tōṉṟi
	neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟiccoṟ kiḷaviyum
	uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi āyiru maruṅkiṉ
	aimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum
	ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyiṉ
	meyyoruṅ kiyalun toḻiltoku moḻiyum
	tammiyal kiḷappiṉ tammuṉ tāmvarūum
	eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉavi yellām maruviṉ pāttiya
	puṇariyal niliyiṭi uṇarat tōṉṟā.
iḵtu ivvatikārattāṟ puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ iviyeṉa avaṟṟi eṭuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki - kūṟuṅkāl uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāka niṟkum collāki, kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum iciyiṉum tōṉṟi - kuṟippiṉāṉum paṇpiṉāṉum iciyiṉāṉum piṟantu, neṟip paṭa vārāk kuṟic coṟkiḷaviyum - oruvaḻippaṭa vārāta coṟṟaṉmi kuṟinta coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi āyiru maruṅkiṉ - uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇiyeṉṉum avviraṇṭiṭattum uḷavākiya, aimpālaṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum - oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu pala eṉṉum aintu pāliṉiyum aṟitaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya paṇpu koḷpeyar tokun  tokiccollum, ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉum kiḷaviyiṉ - ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum peyareccac coṟkaḷiṉuṭiya, mey oruṅku iyalun toḻil tokumoḻiyum - kālaṅkāṭṭum ummum akaramum oru coṟkaṇṇē cēra naṭakkum puṭipeyarcci tokku niṟkuñ coṟkaḷum, tam iyal kiḷappiṉ-tamatu taṉmi kūṟumiṭattu, tammuṉ tāmvarūum eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭa - niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyumāy vārātu tammuṉṉart tāmē vantu niṟkum eṇṇup peyariṉatu tokutiyum uḷappaṭa, aṉṉa piṟavum ellām - attaṉmiyākiya piṟavumellām, maruviṉ pāttiya -  ulakattu maruvi naṭanta vaḻakkiṉatu pakutiyit tam ilakkaṇamākavuṭiya, puṇariyal nili iṭi yuṇarat tōṉṟā - oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarutal naṭanta taṉmi iṭam viḷaṅkat tōṉṟā e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaṇ viṇṇa viṇittatu, viṇviṇittatu ivi kuṟippuriccol; āṭi veḷḷaviḷarttatu, veḷviḷart tatu ivi paṇpuriccol; kaṭal olla volittatu, ollolittatu ivi iciyuriccol. ‘ollolinīr pāyvatē pōlun tuṟivaṉ’ eṉṟār ceyyuṭkaṇṇum.
ivi uyirīṟāyum puḷḷiyīṟāyum niṟṟaliṉ oṉṟaṉkaṇ aṭakka lākāmiyiṉ ‘neṟippaṭavārā’ eṉṟār. viṇṇaviṇittatu teṟipputtōṉṟat teṟittateṉṟum, viṇviṇittatu teṟipput teṟittateṉṟum ām. ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam niṟṟaliṉ taṉmi kuṟinta collāyiṟṟu. ‘viṉiyē kuṟippē’ (col. 258) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya ‘eṉa’ eṉpataṉi ivaṟṟoṭu kūṭṭiyavaḻi iṭiccollātaliṉ, viṇṇeṉa viṇittatu eṉap puṇarkkappaṭu māṟu uṇarka.
iṉik karumpārppāṉ, karumpārppaṉi, karumpārppār, karuṅ kutiri, karuṅkutirikaḷ eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuṭ kariyaṉākiya pārppāṉ, kariyaḷākiya pārppaṉi, kariyarākiya pārppār, kariyatākiya kutiri, kariyaṉavākiya kutirikaḷ eṉa aimpāliṉiyum uṇarttum paṇpu koḷpeyar tokkavāṟu kāṇka. ivaṟṟuṭ karumi eṉṉum paṇpup peyar tokkatēṟ karumiyākiya pārppāṉeṉa virittal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam viriyāmiyiṟ paṇpukoḷpeyar tokkateṉṟu uṇarka.
veṟṟili, veṟṟuppili, veṟṟaṭi, veṟṟeṉattoṭuttal eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟuḷ, veṟuvitākiyili yeṉpatu, pākkuṅ kōṭṭunūṟuṅ kūṭātatāya paṇpuṇarttiya īṟu tokutaliṉ maruviṉ pāttiyatāy niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu. veṟuvitākiya uppili yeṉṟatu, ciṟitum uppiliyeṉa niṉṟatu. ēṉiyavum aṉṉa. iṅṅaṉam aimpālun tokuttaṟku uriya mutaṉiliyātaliṟ puṇarttalākāmi kūṟiṉār.
iṉi, āṭaraṅku, ceykuṉṟu, puṇarpoḻutu, arivāḷ, kol yāṉi, celcelavu eṉa, nilam mutalākiya peyareccan tokka viṉit tokikaḷi virikkuṅkāl, āṭiṉavaraṅku eṉac ‘ceyta’ eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta akara īṟu iṟappu uṇarttiyum, āṭā niṉṟavaraṅku, āṭumaraṅku eṉac ‘ceyyum’ eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta ummīṟu nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttiyum, avaṟṟāṉāya puṭi peyarcciyit tōṟṟuvittu iraṇṭu peyareccamum oru coṟkaṇ oruṅku tokku niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉi oru peyareccattiṉkaṇ aṭakkip puṇarkkalākāmiyiṟ, puṇarkkalākāteṉṟār.
ummiṟuti nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttumāṟu, ‘viṉiyiṉ tokuti’ (col. 415) eṉṉum eccaviyaṟ cūttirattuṭ kūṟutum. iv um īṟu iraṇṭu kālamum oruṅkuṇarttutaṟ ciṟappu nōkkic ‘ceyta’ eṉpataṉi āciriyar muṟkūṟārāyiṉar.
iṉip pattu eṉa niṟuttip, patteṉat tantu puṇarkkappaṭātu, pappatteṉavum, paḵpatteṉavum vaḻaṅkumāṟu uṇarka. orōvoṉ ṟeṉpatum atu. atutāṉē ōroṉṟōroṉṟākak koṭu eṉṟāṟ puṇarkkappaṭum.
iṉi ‘aṉṉapiṟavum’ eṉṟataṉāṉē, uṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻi uṇ eṉṉum mutaṉiliyuṅ, kālaṅkāṭṭum ṭakaramum, iṭaṉum pālum uṇarttum āṉum oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarkkappaṭā, avi niṟuttacolluṅ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyum aṉmiyiṉ. kariyaṉ eṉpuḻik, karu eṉa niṟutti aṉ eṉat tantu puṇarkkappaṭātu, aḵtu iṉṉa ṉeṉṉum poruḷ tarutaliṉ. ēṉi viṉiccoṟkaḷum ivvāṟē pirittup puṇarkkalākāmi uṇarka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻik koḷ eṉpataṉi eṉa eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarkkap paṭāmiyum, ūraṉ veṟpaṉ mutaliya viṉippeyarkaḷum, piṟavum puṇarkkappaṭāmiyum koḷka.
ivvāciriyar puṇarkkappaṭā veṉṟa ic coṟkaḷiyum, vaṭanūṟkaṇ muṭitta aṉakaṉ aṉapāyaṉ akaḷaṅkaṉ mutaliya vaṭacoṟ kaḷiyum piṉṉuḷḷōr muṭittal mutaṉūloṭu ‘māṟukoḷak kūṟalām’ eṉṟu uṇarka.	(77)
atikārap puṟaṉaṭi
483.	kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum
	vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
	viḷampiya iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ
	vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅki ṉuṇarntaṉar oḻukkal
	naṉmati nāṭṭat teṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu ivvatikārattu eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟi yellām itaṉāṉē muṭikka eṉa atikārap puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum - muṉṉar eṭuttōtap paṭṭaṉa allāta coṟkaḷ ceyyuḷiṭattut tirintu muṭivaṉavum, vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum-nālvaki vaḻakkum naṭakku miṭattu maruvutaloṭu tirintu muṭivaṉavum, viḷampiya iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ-muṉṉark kūṟiya ilakkaṇa muṟimiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟumāyiṉ avaṟṟi, naṉmati nāṭṭattu-nalla aṟi viṉatu ārāycciyāṉē, vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ-vaḻakku muṭintu naṭakku miṭattē, uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal eṉmaṉār pulavar-muṭipu vēṟu pāṭukaḷi aṟintu naṭāttuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	‘taṭavuttiri’ eṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum ‘taṭavunili’ (puṟam. 140) eṉa ukaram peṟṟum akara īṟṟu uriccol vantatu. atavattaṅkaṉi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ akara īṟu attup peṟṟatu. ‘kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ’ eṉa akara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉart takaraṅ koṭukka.
‘naṟavaṅ kaṇṇi naṟpōrc cempiyaṉ
kurava nīṭiya koṉṟiyaṅ kāṉal’
eṉa ākāra iṟuti kuṟiyataṉiṟutic ciṉikeṭṭu iruvaḻiyum ammup peṟṟaṉa.
‘muḷavumāt tolicciya pinniṇap piḷavip
piṇavunāy muṭukkiya taṭiyoṭu virii’	(malipaṭu. 176)
eṉa avvīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟāta muṭipupeṟṟaṉa. ‘tiṇvār vicitta muḻavo ṭākuḷi’ (malipaṭu. 3), ‘cuṟaveṟi mīṉ’, ‘iravaḻaṅku ciṟuneṟi’ (akam. 318) ivi ukaram peṟāmal vantaṉa. ‘kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam poṟikkali’ (akam. 97) eṉa ikarīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟaṉa. ‘tīyiṉaṉṉa voṇceṅ kāntaḷ’ (malipaṭu. 145) eṉa īkāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṉ peṟṟatu. ‘nalloḻukkaṅ kākkun tiruvattavar’ (nālaṭi. 457) eṉa ukara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attuppeṟṟatu. ‘ēppeṟṟa māṉpiṇi pōl’ (cīvaka. 2945) eṉa ēkāra īṟu vēṟ ṟumikkaṇ ekaram peṟātu vantatu. ‘kittuṇṭām pōḻtē’ (nālaṭi. 19), ‘kittillār nallar’ (nāṉmaṇik. 69) eṉavum, ‘puṉṉiyaṅkāṉal’ (akam. 80), ‘mulliyan toṭiyal’ eṉavum, aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attum ammum peṟṟaṉa. ‘aṇṇal kōyil vaṇṇamē’ (cīvaka. 126) eṉa ōkāra īṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟatu.
iṉi ‘añcevi niṟiya vāliṉa’ (mullip. 89) eṉa alvaḻikkaṭ kakaramum akaramuṅkeṭṭaṉa. ‘maravam pāvi vayiṟārap paruki’ ‘maravanākam vaṇaṅki māṟkaṇam’ eṉa iruvaḻiyum makaram vikārappaṭṭu ammup peṟṟaṉa. ‘kāretir kāṉam pāṭiṉēmāka’ (puṟam. 144), ‘poṉṉan tikiri muṉcamat turuṭṭi’ (puṟam. 368) ‘poṉṉaṅ kuvaṭṭiṟ poliveyti’ eṉa ṉakara īṟu iruvaḻiyum ammuppeṟṟaṉa. ‘vērpiṇi vetirattuk kālporu naralici’ (naṟ. 62) eṉa rakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attup peṟṟatu. ‘nāvalan taṇpoḻil’ (perumpāṇ. 465), ‘kāṉalam peruntuṟi’ (aiṅkuṟu. 158) eṉa lakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammup peṟṟaṉa. ‘neytalañ ciṟupaṟi’ iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu. ‘āyiṭi yirupē rāṇmi ceyta pūcal’ (kuṟun. 43) eṉpuḻi āyiṭi yeṉpatu urupātaliṉ ‘nīṭavarutal’ (eḻut. 208) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyātu nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu. temmuṉi eṉat tevveṉpuḻi vakaraṅ keṭṭu makara oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭintatu. ‘a’ eṉṉuñ cuṭṭu ‘aṉṟi yaṉittum’ eṉat tirintatu. ‘mutirkōṅkiṉ muki’ (kuṟiñcikkali. 20) eṉavum, ‘kāymāṇṭa teṅkiṉ paḻam’ (cīvaka. 31) eṉavum, kuṟṟukara īṟu iṉ peṟutaluṅ koḷka. ‘toṇṭutaliyiṭṭa pattukkuṟi’ (tol. poruḷ. 413) eṉa ṇakaram iraṭṭātu takara oṟṟu ṭakara oṟṟāyk kuṟṟiyalukaram ēṟi muṭintatu.
iṅṅaṉañ ceyyuḷuṭ piṟavum tirivaṉa uḷavēṉum ip puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka.
arumaruntāṉeṉpatu rakaravukaraṅ keṭṭu arumantāṉeṉa muṭintatu. cōḻaṉāṭu cōṇāṭu eṉa aṉ keṭṭu muṭintatu. pāṇṭi nāṭum atu. toṇṭimāṉāṭu toṇṭi nāṭu eṉa īṟṟeḻuttuc cila keṭṭu muṭintatu. maliyamāṉāṭu malāṭu eṉa mutaleḻuttoḻintaṉa palavuṅ keṭṭu muṭintatu. potuvil eṉpatu potiyileṉa ukaran tirintu ikaramāy yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu muṭintatu. piṟavum ivvāṟē tirintu maruvi vaḻaṅkuvaṉa ellām ippuṟaṉaṭiyāṉ amittuk koḷka.	(78)
kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
eḻuttatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruri muṟṟiṟṟu.

eḻuttatikāra iyalkaḷiṉ amippu
‘nūliṉ marapu moḻimarapu nuṇpiṟappu
mēlip puṇarcci tokimarapu - pālām
urupiyaliṉ piṉṉuyir puḷḷi mayakkam
terivariya kuṟṟukarañ ceppu.’

eḻuttatikāra nūṟpākkaḷiṉ toki
‘eḻuttati kārattuc cūttiraṅkaḷ ellām
oḻukkiya oṉpatōt tuḷḷum - vaḻukkiṉṟi
nāṉūṟ ṟirunāṟpāṉ mūṉṟeṉṟu nāvalarkaḷ
mēṉūṟṟu vittār virittu.’

pāyira mēṟkōḷ niral
